Import direction (Plan 2's, unchanged): each source directory imports only from those before it — `src/ports`, `src/git`, `src/sec`, `src/data`, `src/board`, `src/holds`, `src/engine`; `src/daemon` and `src/entry` may import everything. Plan 3's new `src/holds` modules never import `src/engine` (engine facts arrive as parameters: maps, predicates, the `Fs`/`Clock` ports, structural types declared in holds); `src/board` never imports `src/holds` (ops.ts re-declares the hold unions; a unit test pins them equal). The cross-plan order (X-110: `src/ports → src/text → src/i18n → src/git → src/sec → src/data → src/regions → src/board → src/since → src/holds → src/claims → src/engine → src/claude → …`) keeps every Plan 3 direction; Task 17's `src/holds/revertCs.ts` takes `EngineCore` as the structural `RevertHost` it declares, never by importing `src/engine`, and Task 19's edit of `src/since/report.ts` imports only `src/board`. Every module has one responsibility, as listed. Unit tests live in `test/unit/`, real-git tests in `test/integration/`, SIM tests in `test/integration/sim.*`, each named in its task.

## Interfaces (the contract every task implements exactly)

Plan 1 and Plan 2 names are used as their contracts state them (`GitRepo`, `Oid`, `OidOrAbsent`, `ABSENT`, `TreeMap`, `TreeEntry`, `mergeFile`, `MergeFileResult`, `mergeTree`, `Clock`, `Anchor`, `Random`, `ulid`, `Fs`, `Trace`, `Log`, `Json`, `PeerId`, `crashpoint`, `ScopeClass`, `Scope`, `ScopeInputs`, `DeferReason`, `IncomingCs`, `writeGroups`, `HoldRecord`, `HoldKind`, `Residence`, `HoldState`, `Offer`, `BlobMap`, `HoldBlobs`, `Guard`, `AgeAnchor`, `heldSide`, `isTerminal`, `WHOLE_PATH_GUARDS`, `TERMINAL_STATES`, `hasRestoreMarkerRow`, `isBinaryBytes`, `rows`, `form`, `canon`, `stagedRead`, `StageDir`, `hashRouteB`, `Snapshotter`, `Materialized`, `TickResult`, `Exclusion`, `DiskEq`, `Writer`, `WriteLocalResult`, `StateStore`, `StateView`, `Transition`, `Meta`, `FoldState`, `OwnRejection`, `Outbox`, `PushState`, `OpDraft`, `EventOp`, `eventCid`, `BoardBuild`, `buildBoardCommit`, `CsTrailer`, `liveCommitTrailers`, `parseWriter`, `EngineCore`, `IterationCtx`, `prepareIteration`, `ApplyReport`, `applyStep`, `publishStep`, `PublishReport`, `prePushAsserts`, `PrePushInput`, `Engine`, `EngineInspection`, `StatusInputs`, `computeStatus`, `restart`, `lostStateRebuild`, `startDispatch`, `runDaemon`, `DaemonArgs`, `realEngine`, `PROFILES`, `GeneratorProfile`, `Oracle`, `SimContext`, `exitOracles`, `makeCores`, `makeEngines`). Everything below is new or changed. A comment after a member is part of the contract; a `/** … */` line names the spec ids it implements.

### Trace, deps

```ts
// src/ports/trace.ts — changed and added variants (Task 1 declares all; each owning task emits its own). No durations, no wall times (TST-03).
export type TraceEvent =
  /* …Plan 2 variants other than 'hold' and 'apply', unchanged… */
  | { kind: 'hold'; peer: string; id: string; holdKind: 'PATCH' | 'PIN' | 'INCOMING'; guard: string; guards: readonly string[];
      paths: readonly string[]; residence: 'ON_DISK' | 'OFF_DISK' | null; state: string; rev: number; localOnly: boolean }   // every creation and every transition (guard = first guard or '')
  | { kind: 'apply'; peer: string; tip: string; written: readonly string[]; deferred: readonly string[];
      groups: ReadonlyArray<{ paths: readonly string[]; outcome: 'written' | 'deferred'; reason: string | null }>;
      notApplicable: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; reason: string }>; mDisk: string | null }                        // INV-GROUP input (Task 6); groups[].reason is the group-level closureReason (R3-31)
  | { kind: 'mask'; peer: string; sDisk: string; aTree: string; registry: string | null; partial: readonly string[]; withheld: readonly string[]; conflicted: readonly string[] }   // each S_pub computation, i.e. once per materialization (maskStep, Task 12; no re-MASK, R3-62); registry = the registry commit maskEntries read (INV-MASK input, Task 14)
  | { kind: 'compose'; peer: string; tip: string; aTree: string; registry: string | null; pTree: string;
      pairs: ReadonlyArray<{ csId: string; R: string; A: string; order: number; paths: readonly string[] }>;        // every journaled ready pair at this composition (composed or not)
      groups: ReadonlyArray<{ key: string; csIds: readonly string[]; paths: readonly string[]; pins: readonly string[]; ready: boolean }>;   // ready = the engine's readiness (R3-63)
      withheldWhole: ReadonlyArray<{ key: string; paths: readonly string[] }> }   // publishStep after every composeP whose P tree is built, CAS re-merges and recompositions included, before ENG-41; a T0 trial composition of a t0-held group emits none (R3-63) (Task 10; INV-MASK and O-ATOMIC-PUB input, Task 14)
  | { kind: 'mirror'; peer: string; bnew: string; holds: ReadonlyArray<{ id: string; rev: number; localOnly: boolean; held: number }> }   // hold ops a pushed Bnew carries (Task 4)
  | { kind: 'discard'; peer: string; id: string; rev: number; held: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; blob: string | null }> }               // RESOLVED_DISCARD (Task 11; INV-NOLOSS)
  | { kind: 'repair'; peer: string; path: string; from: string; to: string }                                                           // DAT-08 (Task 7)
  | { kind: 'form_violation'; peer: string; path: string; commit: string }                                                            // DAT-11 (Task 6)
  | { kind: 'edit_check'; peer: string; cs: string; verdict: 'pass' | 'fail' | 'incomplete' }                                         // HLD-67 (Task 10; O-EDITCHECK input for Plan 5)
  | { kind: 'mass'; peer: string; entered: boolean; paths: number };                                                                   // HLD-30 entry / HLD-31 lift (Task 8)

// src/engine/deps.ts (changed)
export type MutantId = 'M05' | 'M06' | 'M07' | 'M12' | 'M17' | 'M18' | 'M19' | 'M20' | 'M25' | 'M28' | 'M33' | 'M38' | 'M39';   // R-29 + R3-50; one site each (Task 1 widens the union; sites: M19/M25/M28 Task 2, M38/M39 Task 6, M33 Task 8)
export interface AttributionPort {
  removalAttributed(path: string): boolean; shrinkAttributed(path: string): boolean;   // Plan 2 (R-25)
  observedEdit(path: string): boolean;     // HLD-26 (a): the delta lies in an Edit|Write|MultiEdit|NotebookEdit hook window of a live seat (Plan 5 binds)
  hookAttributed(path: string): boolean;   // DAT-39: the delta lies in any seat hook window (Plan 5 binds)
}
export const humanOnlyAttribution: AttributionPort;   // removal false, shrink true, observedEdit false, hookAttributed false
export interface EngineDeps {
  /* …Plan 2 fields… */
  editCheck: EditCheckPort; t0Regions: T0RegionsPort; gate: PublishGatePort; symbols: SymbolPort;   // Task 10 (R3-03, R3-04, R3-07); production, SIM and tools/real/two-clone.ts bind plan3Ports() (grammarlessEditCheck, passT0Regions, openGate, noSymbols)
}
```

```ts
// src/data/formCheck.ts (DAT-11; 03-data §3.2, §3.10; R3-32)
export interface FormViolation { path: string; commit: Oid; }
/** formCheckIncoming over LA_C..TIP, decided per path on (A, TIP): a text, unfiltered path present in A and TIP violates iff form(TIP[p]) ∉ {form(A[p])} ∪ {form(X[p]) : X second parent of a base-move commit on the chain that changed p}; commit = the newest commit that set TIP[p]. */
export function formCheckIncoming(repo: GitRepo, laC: Oid, tip: Oid, A: TreeMap, tipMap: TreeMap, textUnfiltered: (path: string) => boolean): Promise<FormViolation[]>;

// src/data/churn.ts (DAT-39; 03-data §3.8, §3.10; R3-35)
export const CHURN_SNAPSHOTS = 3;
export const CHURN_REWRITE_RATIO = 0.2;
export interface ChurnObservation { attributed: boolean; rewrittenRatio: number; binary: boolean; }
/** churnVerdict: the newest ≥ 3 consecutive observations all unattributed and each rewriting ≥ 20 % of rows (or binary) → 'generated?'. Pure. */
export function churnVerdict(history: readonly ChurnObservation[]): 'none' | 'generated?';
/** Share of prev's rows not matched in next (Myers matched rows, endings ignored — linediff). */
export function rewrittenRatio(prev: Buffer, next: Buffer): number;

// src/data/scope.ts (changed; HLD-03, DAT-31; R3-12, R3-21)
```

### Board

```ts
// src/board/ops.ts (changed; glossary §7, PRO-05, PRO-21; R3-24)
export interface HoldOpGuard { name: string; class: 'PREDICATE' | 'DECISION'; owner: string; }
export interface HoldOp {
  id: string; type: 'hold';
  hold_id: string; rev: number; prev_rev: number | null;
  kind: 'PATCH' | 'PIN' | 'INCOMING'; residence: 'ON_DISK' | 'OFF_DISK' | null;
  state: 'ACTIVE' | 'CONFLICTED' | 'PARKED' | 'SUBSUMED' | 'GONE' | 'RESOLVED_PUBLISH' | 'RESOLVED_DISCARD';
  offer: 'none' | 'pending_leftover' | 'offered' | 'accepted' | 'returned';
  guards: HoldOpGuard[]; paths: string[]; origin: string; cs_ids: string[]; peer_authors: string[]; local_only: boolean;
}
// OpaqueOp.type loses 'hold'; Op gains HoldOp; OpDraft follows. parseOpLine validates a hold op field by field (ULID hold_id, rev ≥ 1, prev_rev null or < rev,
// validPathSyntax paths sorted and unique, paths empty only with a host-reject guard — R3-11, cs_ids validId('cs'), peer_authors hex128). PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES gains 'hold' (name kept).
/** The parser's value sets, pinned equal to record.ts's HoldKind, HoldState, Offer and Residence by a unit test (board imports nothing from holds; Task 3). */
export const HOLD_OP_KINDS: ReadonlySet<string>;
export const HOLD_OP_STATES: ReadonlySet<string>;
export const HOLD_OP_OFFERS: ReadonlySet<string>;
export const HOLD_OP_RESIDENCES: ReadonlySet<string>;
/** PRO-06: a `hold` op or draft with prev_rev === null is record-creating (R3-25); Task 4 withholds these at the 5,000-record cap. */
export function isHoldCreation(d: OpDraft | Op): boolean;

// src/board/fold.ts (changed; PRO-06, PRO-21, PRO-24, PRO-27; R3-24, R3-26, R3-27)
export interface FoldedHold {   // FoldState.holds[hold_id]: the latest op's fields except id/type/prev_rev, plus owner and terminal_height
  rev: number; kind: HoldOp['kind']; residence: HoldOp['residence']; state: HoldOp['state']; offer: HoldOp['offer'];
  guards: HoldOpGuard[]; paths: string[]; origin: string; cs_ids: string[]; peer_authors: string[]; local_only: boolean;
  owner: PeerId; terminal_height?: number;
}
// FoldState.holds: Record<string, FoldedHold> (was Record<string, Json>).
export type OwnRejectionReason = 'generic' | 'type' | 'prev_rev' | 'validator' | 'held_subtree' | 'local_only_stub';
export interface OwnRejection { opId: string; type: OpType | 'unknown'; reason: OwnRejectionReason; holdId: string | null; foldedRev: number | null; }
/** PRO-21 validity: writer = owner (the creating op's writer), prev_rev = folded rev (null on create), rev > prev_rev, local_only ⇒ no held/<id>/ in the carrying tree, non-terminal ∧ ¬local_only ∧ paths ≠ [] ⇒ held/<id>/ present (BoardDraft.heldIds); effect: store, terminal_height on entering a terminal state; record_count includes holds (PRO-06). */
// FoldStepResult gains compactedHolds: string[] — PRO-27: at h ≡ 0 (mod 256) holds with terminal_height ≤ h − 512 leave state.json; the writer removes their held/ subtrees in that commit.
/** PRO-27: the hold ids compaction drops at height h ([] unless h ≡ 0 mod 256), equal to foldCommit's compactedHolds there; the writer of the commit at h removes their held/ subtrees (Task 4). */
export function holdsCompactedAt(s: FoldState, h: number): string[];
// class Folder gains (Task 3): takeHoldRejections(): OwnRejection[];   // drains the own `hold` rejections foldTo found since the last call, oldest first;
//   non-hold rejections stay in FoldOutcome.ownRejections only. Task 4's mirrorSync acts on them (R3-27) without editing core.ts.

// src/board/boardCommit.ts (changed; HLD-04, PRO-02, PRO-27)
export interface HeldBlob { stage: 'h0' | 'h1' | 's1' | 's2' | 's3'; path: string; oid: Oid; }
export interface BoardBuild { /* …Plan 2 fields… */ held?: { put: ReadonlyMap<string, readonly HeldBlob[]>; remove: ReadonlySet<string> }; }   // put replaces or adds held/<id>/, remove drops it; every other held/ entry is carried by oid
/** held/<id>/<stage>/<repo path> subtree, nested mktree (R-15); refuses an empty list. */
export function buildHeldTree(repo: GitRepo, blobs: readonly HeldBlob[]): Promise<Oid>;
/** The held/<id>/ entries of a board commit's tree; null when absent. */
export function readHeld(repo: GitRepo, board: Oid, holdId: string): Promise<HeldBlob[] | null>;
/** Every held/ subtree of a board commit from one `ls-tree -r` (Task 3; R3-26 "once per fetched board tip"). The engine reads it only through its cached wrapper heldOfTip (publish.ts). */
export function readHeldAll(repo: GitRepo, board: Oid): Promise<Map<string, HeldBlob[]>>;

// src/board/outbox.ts (changed; HLD-10, PRO-06, PRO-09; R3-25)
// class Outbox gains:
//   replaceHold(draft: Omit<HoldOp, 'id'>): void;          // an earlier draft of the same hold_id without a seq is replaced (one pending op per record); else appended
//   pendingHold(holdId: string): Omit<HoldOp, 'id'> | null; // the newest pending (unlanded) draft or op of that record
// and widens (Task 4; both new parameters optional, so Plan 2 callers are unchanged; Plan 2's Room type is not exported):
//   take(states, self, alloc, room: { maxOps: number; maxBytes: number; withhold?: (d: OpDraft) => boolean })   // withheld drafts stay unallocated, later drafts keep seq order
//   pendingAllowed(states, withhold?: (d: OpDraft) => boolean): boolean;                                         // false when only withheld drafts remain
//   — PRO-06's "withhold record-creating ops, keep ending ops flowing" with withhold = isHoldCreation while the fold is at the record cap.
```

### Holds: records, codes, offers, ports, signals

```ts
// src/holds/record.ts (changed; glossary §9 schema unchanged; HLD-03, HLD-05, 05 §2; R3-09, R3-12)
export type GuardClass = 'PREDICATE' | 'DECISION';
export type ReasonKey = 'answer' | 'bundle' | 'checks' | 'unstable' | 'incoming' | 'form' | 'markers' | 'local' | 'revert' | 'conflict' | 'git' | 'user' | 'wake-scope';
export type GuardName = 'claim' | 'dependency' | 't0' | 'unreadable' | 'unstable' | 'lock' | 'unmaterializable' | 'eol-bom' | 'eol-form' | 'markers'
  | 'local-only-conflict' | 'filter-failure' | 'secret-scan' | 'revert' | 'conflict' | 'claim-breach' | 'base-move' | 'join' | 'restart' | 'phantom'
  | 'host-reject' | 'portability' | 'generated?' | 'collision' | 'quarantine-review' | 'wake-scope';
export interface GuardRow { name: GuardName; cls: GuardClass; owner: string; key: ReasonKey; wholePath: boolean; humanRelease: boolean; takesArg: boolean; }
/** 05-holds Table 3 in row order, owner column verbatim (HLD-05). */
export const GUARD_TABLE: readonly GuardRow[];
/** Row of `<name>[:<arg>]`; null for a name outside Table 3 (an unknown DECISION guard: never released or transitioned here, HLD-05). */
export function guardRow(full: string): GuardRow | null;
/** A Table 3 guard with its fixed class and owner; throws on an unknown name or a malformed argument (05 §2 grammar; `generated?:exclude`, `collision:keep`). */
export function makeGuard(full: string): Guard;
export function guardArg(g: Guard): string | null;
export function isUnknownGuard(g: Guard): boolean;
/** {REASON} key (05 §2): 'local' for a local_only record, else the first DECISION guard in Table 3 row order, else the first PREDICATE; dependency → 'answer' when its target is a SHADOW or a claim PIN, else 'bundle'; null for an empty guard set; unknown names rank after every Table 3 row, so a record whose only guards are unknown gets 'user' (Task 1). */
export function reasonKey(r: HoldRecord, lookup: (id: string) => HoldRecord | undefined): ReasonKey | null;
export type LocalOnlyCause = 'filter-failure' | 'secret-scan' | 'secret-like' | 'not-live' | 'marker-companion';
/** HLD-03 / DAT-31: why the record is local_only at this rev ([] = it is not); secret-like per the U10 list in src/data/scope.ts. A LOCAL_ONLY scope class counts as
 *  'not-live' only when otherLocalOnly(p) — another non-terminal ON_DISK local_only record (id ≠ r.id) covers p, computed by the registry — else as LIVE (r's own effect). */
export function localOnlyCauses(r: Pick<HoldRecord, 'paths' | 'guards'>, scopeOf: (path: string) => ScopeClass, isCompanion: boolean, otherLocalOnly: (path: string) => boolean): LocalOnlyCause[];
/** 05 §2 whole-path guards: eol-bom, generated?, portability, markers, and every guard of a local_only record. */
export function hasWholePathGuard(r: HoldRecord): boolean;
// Plan2Guard and PLAN2_GUARD_CLASS remain, derived from GUARD_TABLE. record.ts's validation is unchanged except: guard names may be any text (unknown names load), INCOMING keeps "ON_DISK/ACTIVE or terminal", the pathless host-reject PIN of Task 14 stays the only empty-path record.

// src/holds/codes.ts (05 §1, §2; 03-data §2; glossary §19)
/** Every new code Plan 3 raises into status.json or the log (L-CODES input for Plan 7), plus HOLD_NOT_SHARED, HOLD_RETURNED, HOLD_PIN_CONTEXT and QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY,
 *  declared for Plans 5–7 with no Plan 3 raise site (R3-65). */
export const HOLD_CODES: readonly string[];   // HOLD_CREATED, HOLD_NOT_SHARED, HOLD_CONFLICTED, HOLD_SUBSUMED, HOLD_GONE, HOLD_PARKED, HOLD_AUTO_PARKED, HOLD_RESTORED, HOLD_RETURNED, HOLD_REVERT_HELD, HOLD_PIN_CONTEXT, HOLD_RESTORE_CONFLICT, HOLD_MASK_INVALID, HOLD_DIVERGED_REGIONS, HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE, HOLD_NOT_OWNER, HOLD_ALREADY_RESOLVED, HOLD_DISK_CHANGED, HOLD_FROZEN, HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN, HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE, HOLD_NOT_PARKABLE, HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED, CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE, CS_GROUP_WAITING, CS_READY_CANCELLED, CS_NO_CHECKS_CONFIGURED, INCOMING_WAITING, INCOMING_COLLISION, INCOMING_UNMATERIALIZABLE, RESTORE_MARKERS, RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY, QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY, EOL_MIXED_HELD, EOL_REPAIRED, PATH_NOT_PORTABLE, GENERATED_SUSPECT, BOARD_FULL
export type ResolveRefusal = 'HOLD_NOT_OWNER' | 'HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN' | 'HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE' | 'HOLD_NOT_PARKABLE' | 'HOLD_RESTORE_CONFLICT'
  | 'HOLD_DISK_CHANGED' | 'HOLD_FROZEN';   // resolve() never returns RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY or QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY: a seat actor gets claude_refused (R3-54, R3-65)
/** 05 §1 next command per refusal (texts are Plan 7's). */
export const NEXT_COMMAND: Readonly<Record<ResolveRefusal | 'RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY' | 'QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY' | 'STOP_HOLDS_NOT_DURABLE' | 'UNINSTALL_HOLDS' | 'HOLD_MASK_INVALID', string>>;

// src/holds/offers.ts (HLD-09; R3-06)
export type OfferEvent = 'born_leftover' | 'promote' | 'handoff' | 'k6_timer' | 'accepted' | 'declined' | 'released' | 'presume_absent'
  | 'holder_seat_end' | 'request_returned' | 'grant_ended';
/** HLD-09: none|returned → pending_leftover (born_leftover); pending_leftover → offered (promote, handoff, k6_timer; never while wake-scope holds); offered → accepted; offered → returned (declined, released, presume_absent, holder_seat_end, request_returned); accepted → returned (grant_ended only); otherwise null. Pure. */
export function offerNext(r: Pick<HoldRecord, 'offer' | 'guards'>, ev: OfferEvent): Offer | null;
export const DECLINES_TO_HUMAN = 2;   // HLD-09: at 2 folded declines of one (requester, claim_id, H1) the item goes to the requester's human

// src/holds/signals.ts (06-claude §3.12.1 signal set; HLD-34, HLD-36; glossary §25 F2; R3-05)
export type SeatActivity = 'IDLE' | 'ENDED' | 'MID_TURN' | 'AWAITING_PERMISSION';
export type CsSignal =
  | { kind: 'effect'; seat: string; agent: string | null; toolUseId: string; paths: readonly string[]; turnOrigin: 'prompt' | 'wake' }   // an attributed file effect (CCI-27); never split below one tool_use
  | { kind: 'prompt'; seat: string; humanOrigin: boolean; nonce: boolean }                                    // a nonce prompt is never a boundary and promotes nothing
  | { kind: 'stop' | 'stop_failure' | 'session_end' | 'seat_end'; seat: string }
  | { kind: 'subagent_stop'; seat: string; agent: string }
  | { kind: 'checkpoint'; seat: string; source: 'edit' | 'green' | 'tool'; candSeq: number; paths: readonly string[] }
  | { kind: 'checks_run'; seat: string; ok: boolean }                                                         // a completed checks.checkpoint-matching call (HLD-37 `checks`; R3-43; a 05/06 gap: Plan 5 feeds it)
  | { kind: 'idle_10min'; seat: string; activity: SeatActivity; txnOpen: boolean }                             // the HLD-34 fallback close input
  | { kind: 'activity'; seat: string; activity: SeatActivity };                                               // the HLD-36 recovered-CS close input

// src/holds/ports.ts (R3-03, R3-04, R3-07)
/** rev 2.5 (X-08, Task 10): fail and incomplete gain entries? — 04-regions §3.20's failed entries, ordered by check, path, line; an entry whose reason is no_snapshot,
 *  merge_conflict or no_merge carries no check number. Plan 3 reads only check, path, line and reason; the element stays an inline type (no Plan 3 name), and Plan 4 narrows
 *  it to its EditCheckEntry (assignable). */
export type EditCheckVerdict = { verdict: 'pass' }
  | { verdict: 'fail'; failed: ReadonlyArray<1 | 2 | 3 | 4>; entries?: ReadonlyArray<{ check?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path: string; line?: number; reason: string }> }
  | { verdict: 'incomplete'; entries?: ReadonlyArray<{ check?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path: string; line?: number; reason: string }> };
/** 04-regions editCheck(A, R, TIP, cs) as a port (REG-36); trees as commits or trees, restricted to the CS's paths; incomplete = fail (HLD-67). */
export interface EditCheckPort { check(repo: GitRepo, a: Oid, r: Oid, tip: Oid, csPaths: readonly string[]): Promise<EditCheckVerdict>; }
/** Plan 3 binding: checks 1–3 pass (no grammar, glossary §27.1); check 4's size clause (TIP[p] non-empty ⇒ merged M[p] neither absent, empty nor < 50 % of TIP's stored size, 04-regions §3.20). */
export const grammarlessEditCheck: EditCheckPort;
/** rev 2.5 (X-07, Task 10): the fail form gains findings? (first finding first); the T0 slot's open-CS drop reads findings[0] for last_check{kind: 'stale'}. Plan 3 fills it
 *  for item 3 from t0Shrink ({check: 4, path} per shrunk path, in path order); passT0Regions never fails. Plan 4 widens the element to T0Finding (+ reason, symbol?), assignable;
 *  src/holds never imports src/regions. */
export type T0Verdict = { pass: true }
  | { pass: false; paths: string[]; item: 1 | 2 | 3; findings?: ReadonlyArray<{ check: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path: string; line?: number }> }
  | { incomplete: true };
/** HLD-50 items 1–2 (REG-24 regionTripwire relative form, REG-27 interfaceCheck over the merged tree); Plan 4 binds. */
export interface T0RegionsPort { check(repo: GitRepo, tip: Oid, out: Oid, paths: readonly string[]): Promise<T0Verdict>; }
export const passT0Regions: T0RegionsPort;   // always { pass: true }
/** HLD-50 item 3: paths going from non-empty to empty, or shrinking > 50 %, without attribution (R-25 human rule via `attributed`). */
export function t0Shrink(repo: GitRepo, tip: TreeMap, out: TreeMap, paths: readonly string[], attributed: (path: string) => boolean): Promise<string[]>;
/** B1 (REG-20) over every hunk of diff(A[p], R[p]) of a ready group at the exact leased tip, and the `claim` PREDICATE. */
export interface PublishGatePort {
  gate(repo: GitRepo, tip: Oid, A: TreeMap, R: TreeMap, paths: readonly string[], csIds: readonly string[]): Promise<{ block: null } | { block: { claimId: string } }>;
  claimStillBlocks(r: HoldRecord): Promise<boolean>;
}
export const openGate: PublishGatePort;      // never blocks; claimStillBlocks → true (fail closed; Plan 3 creates no claim record)
/** HLD-48/HLD-49 symbol branch (REG symbol delta, 05 §8 Q2); Plan 4 binds. */
export interface SymbolPort { referencesHeldOnly(repo: GitRepo, held: HoldRecord, csR: TreeMap, A: TreeMap): Promise<boolean>; }
export const noSymbols: SymbolPort;          // always false
/** The one Plan 3 binding of the four ports: { editCheck: grammarlessEditCheck, t0Regions: passT0Regions, gate: openGate, symbols: noSymbols }; src/daemon/run.ts,
 *  tools/real/two-clone.ts, test/sim/engineAdapter.ts and both fixtures spread it into EngineDeps (Task 10). */
export function plan3Ports(): { editCheck: EditCheckPort; t0Regions: T0RegionsPort; gate: PublishGatePort; symbols: SymbolPort };
```

```ts
// src/holds/lastCheck.ts (rev 2.5; glossary §29.8, 05 §2 `last_check`, HLD-67 step 4, 10 §3.5.1; X-05, X-06; Task 10). Memory only: no serializer, no I/O, no log.
// Plan 3 owns the store (roadmap row 3); the member set is Plan 5's (X-05). One instance per ChangeSets, created by its constructor; never part of CsTable, the
// cs/<enc(b)>.json cache, a state commit, the registry, status.json, the board or a log; empty after a restart.
export type LastCheckKind = 'fail' | 'conflict' | 'incomplete' | 'stale' | 'skipped';
export type SkipReason = 'shrink' | 'slow_read' | 'base_moved' | 'batch';   // 06 §3.8.4 skips 3–6 (written by Plan 5's editCheckpoints.ts through recordFor)
export interface LastCheck { edit_n: number; kind: LastCheckKind; check?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path?: string; line?: number; skip?: SkipReason; more: number; }
export class LastCheckStore {
  record(csId: string, e: LastCheck): void;                                       // keeps the first failed check of the newest edit_n; an older edit_n is ignored
  get(csId: string): LastCheck | null;
  pass(csId: string): { ready: number | null; shared: number | null };            // last_pass; `ready` follows the journal (cleared by a stale drop or an HLD-41 withdrawal)
  clearCs(csId: string): void;                                                    // the CS closed (called inside ChangeSets)
  noteCandidate(csId: string, candSeq: number, editN: number): void;             // edit_n of a cand_seq (Plan 5's EditCheckpoints notes first; see ChangeSets.candidate)
  recordFor(csId: string, candSeq: number, e: Omit<LastCheck, 'edit_n'>): void;  // edit_n from the noted pair; an unknown candSeq records nothing
  passedFor(csId: string, candSeq: number): void;                                 // sets last_pass.ready; clears last_check when its edit_n is lower
  sharedFor(csId: string, candSeq: number): void;                                 // sets last_pass.shared
}
// The clear of last_pass.ready on an HLD-41 withdrawal is done inside src/holds without a ninth member (a non-member helper exported by lastCheck.ts, used by changesets.ts).
```

### Holds: REBASE, registry, MASK, R1, observed writes

```ts
// src/holds/rebase.ts (HLD-13, HLD-14; R3-18, R3-19)
export type RebaseOutcome = { ok: true; h0p: BlobMap; h1p: BlobMap } | { ok: false; conflicted: string[] };
/** A merge-file exit outside 0…127 (Plan 2's mergeFile rc −1: a missing or corrupt object, a killed git) is an operator error, never a conflict
 *  (HLD-14's "rc ≠ 0 means conflict" is read as rc 1…127). REBASE and MASK throw it and cache nothing; Task 2 maps it to HOLD_MASK_INVALID (05 F1). (Task 1) */
export class HoldOperatorError extends Error { readonly op: 'rebase' | 'mask' | 'reapply'; readonly path: string; readonly holdId: string; }
/** REBASE(r, p): H1'[p] = mf(A[p], H0[p], H1[p]) single-step from the ORIGINAL pair ((s1, s2) for a stage record), H0' = A; rc 1…127 = conflict (any other rc: HoldOperatorError);
 *  whole-path rule (R3-18): when p is not `mergeable` (binary or filtered) or any of A[p], H0[p], H1[p] is ABSENT,
 *  H1'[p] = H1[p] iff A[p] == H0[p] or A[p] == H1[p], else conflict, and no merge-file runs; outputs route (c) -w; cached by (r.id, r.rev, A[p]). */
export class RebaseCache {
  constructor(repo: GitRepo, mergeable: (path: string) => boolean);
  rebase(r: HoldRecord, A: TreeMap): Promise<RebaseOutcome>;
  /** HLD-07: h1 equals, or equals the REBASE of, r's held side on every path. */
  converges(r: HoldRecord, A: TreeMap, h1: BlobMap): Promise<boolean>;
  clear(): void;   // bounds memory; stale keys simply miss
}

// src/holds/registry.ts (changed; HLD-01…HLD-07, HLD-09, HLD-62 bookkeeping, 05 §2 age anchor; R3-09…R3-14, R3-22, R3-27)
export interface NewRecord {
  kind: HoldKind; residence: Residence; state: 'ACTIVE' | 'CONFLICTED' | 'PARKED';
  paths: string[]; blobs: HoldBlobs;
  guard: string | null;          // `<name>[:<arg>]` from Table 3; null only for an OFF_DISK PARKED birth (R3-10)
  origin: string; cs_ids: string[]; displaced_by: string | null;
  local_only?: boolean;          // ignored since Plan 3: recomputed (HLD-03, R3-12)
  offer?: Offer;                 // default 'none'; 'pending_leftover' at a SHADOW/K2/K3/K6 birth (HLD-09)
  peer_authors?: string[];       // default []
  companionOf?: string;          // a marker companion's parent (05 §3.4 item 3)
}
export interface RegistryDeps {
  repo: GitRepo; ref: string; random: Random; clock: Clock; peer: PeerId; trace: Trace; log: Log;   // Plan 2
  scopeOf: () => (path: string) => ScopeClass;   // the current iteration's scope (R3-12)
  A: () => TreeMap;                              // the current A (HLD-07 convergence)
  rebase: RebaseCache;
}
export interface HoldOutcome { id: string; created: boolean; attachedGuard: string | null; conflictedExisting: boolean; }
export type HoldPatch = Partial<Pick<HoldRecord, 'residence' | 'state' | 'blobs' | 'guards' | 'offer' | 'declines' | 'displaced_by' | 'paths' | 'cs_ids' | 'peer_authors'>>;
export type MirrorRejectReason = 'validator' | 'held_subtree' | 'local_only_stub' | 'generic' | 'type';   // no prev_rev value: a prev_rev rejection is a race and always re-drafts, never recorded (R3-27; Task 4); generic/type recorded like validator (R3-70)
/** A PARK, RESTORE or ON_DISK --discard between its registry commit and its final transition (record before displacement, HLD-16): settled at restart by DISK_EQ (settleHoldOps).
 *  (Task 1 declares it; Task 5 uses park/restore, Task 11 discard with final = { state: 'RESOLVED_DISCARD' }) */
export interface InflightOp {
  op: 'park' | 'restore' | 'discard';
  entries: Array<{ path: string; expected: OidOrAbsent; target: OidOrAbsent }>;   // expected = the pre-image per path, target = the write
  final: HoldPatch;                                                                // the closing transition, committed after WRITTEN
  explicit: boolean;                                                               // a restore marks the record explicit when it completes
}
export interface RegistryLocal {   // registry tree blob local.json (R3-13); never mirrored; a Plan 2 tree without local.json loads as all-empty
  companions: Record<string, string>;
  explicit: string[];
  rejected: Record<string, { key: string; reason: MirrorRejectReason }>;
  inflight: Record<string, InflightOp>;   // hold id → its interrupted-operator bookkeeping (Task 1)
  keepSyncing: string[];                  // sorted, unique: paths whose `generated?` record was answered "keep syncing" (--publish); churnStep never re-asks for them (DAT-39); Task 1 declares it (a local.json
                                          // without it loads with []), Task 11 adds markKeepSyncing and churnStep's use
}
export class HoldRegistry {
  // Plan 2: constructor(d: RegistryDeps), load(), all(), nonTerminal(), get(id), maskingRecords() — meanings kept; load() also reads local.json
  /** HLD-05 for every Table 3 guard and kind INCOMING; = (await holdOutcome(n)).id. */
  hold(n: NewRecord): Promise<string>;
  /** HLD-07: attach to an equal or REBASE-converging record of the same kind and paths (new rev); a stage call on held paths → that record CONFLICTED with stages; R-02 stage dedupe kept; else one commit with a new ULID record.
   *  Order: the stage branch (R-02 dedupe, then K4 onto an ON_DISK PATCH/PIN of the same path set), then equal/converging non-terminal records in ULID order (first match wins), then a new record;
   *  a guard-less call attaches only by Plan 2's exact dedupe and never when companionOf is set; an ON_DISK call attached to an OFF_DISK record moves it ON_DISK in the same rev
   *  (OFF_DISK CONFLICTED → ON_DISK CONFLICTED), its age restarting — an attach that left the bytes unmasked on disk would publish them (Task 1). */
  holdOutcome(n: NewRecord): Promise<HoldOutcome>;
  /** One rev, one commit; a terminal state sets residence null; local_only recomputed; refuses a terminal record and the release of an unknown guard (HLD-05). */
  transition(id: string, patch: HoldPatch): Promise<HoldRecord>;
  /** Several creations and transitions in ONE registry commit (markers park, park-all, R1 batches); `local` edits local.json in the same commit (Task 1; Task 5's PARK/RESTORE bookkeeping). */
  commitMany(creates: readonly NewRecord[], patches: ReadonlyArray<{ id: string; patch: HoldPatch }>,
    local?: { setInflight?: Readonly<Record<string, InflightOp>>; clearInflight?: readonly string[]; explicit?: readonly string[] }): Promise<{ created: string[]; records: HoldRecord[] }>;
  addGuard(id: string, full: string): Promise<HoldRecord>;
  /** Releases one (record, guard) pair (HLD-07 AND); unknown guards refuse. */
  releaseGuard(id: string, full: string): Promise<HoldRecord>;
  /** HLD-09 through offerNext; null on an illegal event; 'declined' also increments declines. */
  offerEvent(id: string, ev: OfferEvent): Promise<HoldRecord | null>;
  /** 05 §2: one commit re-persisting every non-terminal record's accumulated Δmono (clean exit); no op. */
  flushAgeAnchors(): Promise<number>;
  /** max(Δwall since anchor_wall_ms, mono_accum_ms + Δmono of this run) (INV-CLOCK). */
  ageMs(r: HoldRecord): number;
  byPath(path: string): readonly HoldRecord[];
  companionsOf(id: string): readonly HoldRecord[];
  isCompanion(id: string): boolean;
  local(): Readonly<RegistryLocal>;
  markExplicit(id: string): Promise<void>;
  markMirrorRejected(id: string, key: string, reason: MirrorRejectReason): Promise<void>;
  clearMirrorRejected(id: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Adds the record's paths to local.keepSyncing in one registry commit; the record must carry `generated?` or `generated?:exclude` (Task 11). */
  markKeepSyncing(id: string): Promise<void>;
  head(): Oid | null;
}

// src/holds/mask.ts (changed; HLD-15, HLD-19, HLD-21, DAT-20, DAT-23, DAT-35; R3-18…R3-21, R3-50)
export interface MaskInputs { A: TreeMap; scope: (path: string) => ScopeClass; records: readonly HoldRecord[]; mergeable: (path: string) => boolean; }   // + mergeable: text and unfiltered at EPOCH_BASE
export interface MaskOptions { m19?: boolean; m25?: boolean; m28?: boolean; }   // mutant flags the engine sets from EngineDeps.testMutants
export interface MaskResult {
  entries: Map<string, TreeEntry>;       // MASK(X); a masked path that A lacks is absent
  withheld: ReadonlySet<string>;         // whole path := A[p] (steps 1–4, binary/filtered PATCH, step-5 conflict)
  partial: ReadonlySet<string>;          // step 5 applied
  conflicted: string[];                  // record ids whose step-5 merge had rc ≠ 0 → the caller transitions them to CONFLICTED
}
/** MASK(X), 05 §3.4 item 2 steps 1–6; step 5: each ON_DISK ACTIVE PATCH with guards, ULID order, x := mf(x, H1'[p], H0'[p]) from x = X[p]; no registry write; a merge-file error throws HoldOperatorError{op: 'mask'}. */
export function maskEntries(repo: GitRepo, X: TreeMap, inp: MaskInputs, cache: RebaseCache, o?: MaskOptions): Promise<MaskResult>;
/** Steps 1–4 reason of a path, 'patch' when a step-5 record covers it (sync; Plan 2 semantics kept). */
export function maskReason(path: string, inp: MaskInputs): MaskReason;
/** HLD-21: per path, 1-based inclusive stored-form rows of diff(MASK_PATCH(S_disk[p]), S_disk[p]); reads existing blobs only; a held pure deletion after S_disk row k is the zero-length range [k + 1, k] (diff -U0's "+k,0"), passed through by Engine.heldRanges (Task 2).
 *  Rows are computed in-process (matchedRows over stored-form rows); no `git diff -U0`/`--unified=0` (L-HOLD-STORES, Task 8, allows it only in src/holds/revert.ts). The same holds for assertSPub and assertP. */
export function heldRanges(repo: GitRepo, sDisk: TreeMap, sPub: MaskResult): Promise<Map<string, Array<readonly [number, number]>>>;
export type MaskAssert = { ok: true } | { ok: false; clause: 'reapply' | 'revert_line' | 'p_class' | 'p_local_only' | 'p_withheld' | 'p_range'; path: string };
/** HLD-19 on S_pub: re-applying every ON_DISK PATCH (mf(S_pub, H0', H1'), reverse ULID) reproduces S_disk except withheld paths; diff(A, S_pub) holds no line of an ON_DISK revert record's H0'→H1' change. Rows in-process, no -U0 (see heldRanges). */
export function assertSPub(repo: GitRepo, sDisk: TreeMap, sPub: MaskResult, inp: MaskInputs, cache: RebaseCache): Promise<MaskAssert>;
/** HLD-19 on P (HLD-42): no QUARANTINED/SAVE_ONLY/UNMANAGED path, no local_only record path, no withheld path and no held range differs from A. Rows in-process, no -U0 (see heldRanges). */
export function assertP(repo: GitRepo, P: TreeMap, inp: MaskInputs): Promise<MaskAssert>;   // Task 2 fix round 1: the ranges parameter is removed

// src/holds/evaluate.ts (HLD-08, Algorithm R1, HLD-29; R3-17)
export type R1Verdict = 'CONFLICTED' | 'SUBSUMED' | 'RESOLVED_PUBLISH' | 'GONE' | 'ACTIVE' | 'HELD_BY_MARKERS';
/** R1 for an ON_DISK record: CONFLICTED (REBASE or MASK conflict; a PIN's REBASE(H1)) > SUBSUMED (H1' == A ∀p; RESOLVED_PUBLISH when explicit) > GONE (PATCH: mf(S_disk, H1', H0') == S_disk ∀p for r alone; PIN: S_disk == A ∀p) > ACTIVE; while `markers` holds → HELD_BY_MARKERS; a marker companion → GONE once its markers released. */
export function evaluateOnDisk(r: HoldRecord, A: TreeMap, sDisk: TreeMap, cache: RebaseCache, explicit: boolean, companion: boolean): Promise<R1Verdict>;
/** OFF_DISK records only when A[p] changed on one of their paths: one REBASE from the original pair; H1' == A everywhere → SUBSUMED (RESOLVED_PUBLISH when explicit); else null (never GONE-evaluated, INV-HLD-OFFDISK). */
export function evaluateOffDisk(r: HoldRecord, A: TreeMap, cache: RebaseCache, explicit: boolean): Promise<'SUBSUMED' | 'RESOLVED_PUBLISH' | null>;

// src/holds/observed.ts (HLD-26 (b); R3-36)
/** Every Plan 3 writeLocal/writeLocalGroup write after WRITTEN (park, markers park, S6 and the stop park, restore, the collision take, --discard, the repair; R3-36), by path and written blob; in memory. */
export class ObservedWrites {
  record(path: string, written: OidOrAbsent): void;
  /** True (and forgotten) when the snapshot delta of path lands exactly on a recorded write. */
  consume(path: string, next: OidOrAbsent): boolean;
  /** consume without forgetting: the breaker reads it at detection ticks, the revert detector consumes at the materialization (Task 8). */
  peek(path: string, next: OidOrAbsent): boolean;
}
```

### Holds: mirror, incoming, detectors

```ts
// src/holds/mirror.ts (HLD-04, HLD-10, HLD-62, PRO-21 writer side, PRO-24; R3-24…R3-29)
/** glossary §7 projection; a local_only record → stub (paths kept, no oids). */
export function projectOp(r: HoldRecord, prevRev: number | null): Omit<HoldOp, 'id'>;
/** held/<id>/<stage>/<path> blobs of a non-local_only record: every stage of its blob maps, no ABSENT side; [] for local_only (HLD-04). */
export function heldBlobs(r: HoldRecord): HeldBlob[];
/** Digest of the projection without rev/prev_rev plus heldBlobs(r) (R3-26). */
export function mirrorKey(r: HoldRecord): string;
/** HLD-10 + R3-25: creation, residence/state/offer/guard-set change or a held-side (h1/s2) change. */
export function mirrorWorthy(prev: HoldRecord | null, next: HoldRecord): boolean;
/** HLD-10/HLD-54, R3-29: a record created at once for a not-applicable path (INCOMING `unmaterializable`, `collision`, `quarantine-review`) drafts its creation op only at age ≥ 60 s;
 *  closure INCOMING records (`lock`, `eol-form`, `local-only-conflict`) and `unreadable`/`unstable` PINs are created only after 60 s of their condition, so their creation op is due at
 *  once, as for every other record (Task 4; otherwise they would wait 120 s). */
export function creationDue(r: HoldRecord, ageMs: number): boolean;
/** PRO-21 writer check (SEC-09 serializer form): only LIVE paths in a mirrored op and its held/, stub rule, 16 KiB line. */
export function checkMirror(r: HoldRecord, live: (path: string) => boolean): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: MirrorRejectReason };
/** HLD-62: folded entry equals the projection and the tip's held/<id> equals heldBlobs(r) (tipHeld null counts as [], so a record with paths [] — the pathless host-reject PIN — is durable
 *  when its folded entry equals the projection); local_only → false. */
export function isDurable(r: HoldRecord, folded: FoldedHold | undefined, tipHeld: readonly HeldBlob[] | null): boolean;

// src/holds/incoming.ts (HLD-32, HLD-53…HLD-55, DAT-11, DAT-29, DAT-33, SEC-32; R3-29…R3-31)
export const TRANSIENT_RECORD_MS = 60_000;
/** Δmono since a key was first seen in this daemon run; keys 'path:<p>' or 'closure:<sorted paths>' (R3-30). */
export class TransientClocks {
  constructor(clock: Clock);
  see(key: string): number;      // age in ms (0 at first sight)
  forget(key: string): void;
  ages(): ReadonlyMap<string, number>;
}
export type NotApplicableReason = 'collision' | 'unmaterializable' | 'quarantine';
export type ClosureReason = Exclude<DeferReason, 'closure' | 'collision' | 'unmaterializable' | 'quarantine'>;
export const INCOMING_GUARD_OF: Readonly<Record<ClosureReason, 'lock' | 'eol-form' | 'local-only-conflict'>>;   // R3-31: a closure's guard comes from its group-level reason, never 'closure'
/** R3-31's group-level reason of one deferred write group: the first of eol_form > local_only_conflict > any other reason among its entries (Task 6). Pure. */
export function closureReason(reasons: readonly DeferReason[]): ClosureReason;
export interface HoldCodeRaise { code: string; params: Record<string, Json>; }   // a message code incomingStep (or another src/holds step) asks the engine to raise
export interface IncomingInputs {
  A: TreeMap; tipMap: TreeMap;
  closures: ReadonlyArray<{ paths: readonly string[]; reason: ClosureReason }>;   // this loop's deferred write groups, one per group: ApplyReport.closures (R3-31)
  notApplicable: ReadonlyMap<string, NotApplicableReason>;
  rules: ReadonlyMap<string, PortRule>;                                         // the D7 rule of each `unmaterializable` path (ApplyReport.notApplicableRules)
  csOf: (path: string) => string | null;                                        // the incoming CS id of a deferred path (for INCOMING_WAITING {cs, path, reason}), from ApplyReport.incoming
  transient: ReadonlyMap<string, 'unreadable' | 'unstable'>;                   // publish-side exclusions of this tick
  privateBlob: (path: string) => OidOrAbsent;                                   // the blob the private index holds (h1 of a transient PIN)
}
/** One loop (HLD-53/54/55, HLD-32): not-applicable → INCOMING at once; closures ≥ 60 s → one INCOMING each; transient ≥ 60 s → one PIN, none when h0 and h1 are both ABSENT (R3-56); releases (SUBSUMED) when applied, TIP no longer changes the paths, or the condition cleared; h1 refreshed when TIP moves.
 *  A closure whose path set changes is a new closure for the 60 s rule (its 'closure:<sorted paths>' key changes); an INCOMING record already covering part of it widens
 *  (paths ∪, h1 from TIP) in a new rev instead of a second record being created (HLD-07; INV-GROUP's "one record per closure", which Task 14's oracle reads the same way).
 *  A closure clock is forgotten only when none of its paths is still deferred. A path failing validPathSyntax is no record's path: it stays carried by scope class (UNMANAGED, P2-CARRY).
 *  src/holds cannot reach EngineCore.holdCode, so the codes come back in `codes` (record ids exist only here): INCOMING_UNMATERIALIZABLE {id, path, rule}, INCOMING_COLLISION {id, path},
 *  INCOMING_WAITING {cs, path, reason} once per closure episode; the engine caller passes them to raiseHoldCodes (core.ts). (Task 6) */
export function incomingStep(reg: HoldRegistry, clocks: TransientClocks, inp: IncomingInputs): Promise<{ created: string[]; released: string[]; codes: HoldCodeRaise[] }>;

// src/holds/provenance.ts (HLD-25, ENG-36, ENG-37, glossary §6; R3-36)
export const PROV_RETENTION_MS = 1_209_600_000;   // 14 days, local time of append
export interface ProvEntry { commit: string; writer: string; cs_ids: string[]; t_local_ms: number; paths: Record<string, { add: string[]; del: string[] }>; }
/** 05 §2 non-trivial line: stored form without EOL, non-empty after removing whitespace, not only Unicode P*/S* characters. */
export function isNonTrivial(line: Buffer): boolean;
export function lineHash(line: Buffer): string;   // SHA-1 hex of the stored-form line without its EOL
export class ProvenanceJournal {
  constructor(d: { fs: Fs; file: string; repo: GitRepo; clock: Clock; peer: PeerId; log: Log });
  /** prov/<enc(b)>.jsonl, t_local_ms = rebuild time for rebuilt entries (Task 8):
   *  (i) a journal whose newest entry is an older first-parent ancestor of laC is advanced to laC ('loaded'; the append runs after the state CAS — for an apply after its apply.state
   *      crashpoint — so a kill at apply.state or inside the append leaves the journal one commit behind, R3-59);
   *  (ii) a corrupt file, any other mismatch, or a file missing at a restart is rebuilt over the first-parent chain (rebuildFrom, laC] (HLD-25: "≤ 14 days by commit order back to the newest
   *      commit applied before the retention cut, or the join point"; rebuildFrom null = the live chain's root, exclusive);
   *  (iii) genesis and join call load(tip, tip) (tip = liveCodeOf the new LA_B) right after attachState and before their first apply, which creates an empty journal (a joiner never rebuilds
   *      the team's history into fresh 14-day protection); a restart calls load(view.laC, await provRebuildFrom(core)) right after attachState and before resolveWriteIntent; the lost-state
   *      rebuild passes provRebuildFrom(core) too (R3-59).
   *  The journal keeps its load point (the laC of the last load); advance is a no-op before load() (R3-59). */
  load(laC: Oid, rebuildFrom: Oid | null): Promise<'loaded' | 'rebuilt'>;
  /** One entry per live commit in (max(old, load point), next] on next's first-parent chain (writer from Cowork-Writer; `branch:<X>` for base-move commits); prunes > 14 days; rebuilds over
   *  (rebuildFrom, next] when old is not on next's first-parent chain (a re-anchor; the caller passes provRebuildFrom(core)). Called only through core.ts's advanceProvenance (R3-59).
   *  (EngineCore.commit's existing callers keep their call shape; only applyStep passes { provenance: 'caller' }.) Row changes are computed in-process (matchedRows over stored-form rows, stripEnding for hashing; binary blobs split at LF the same way):
   *  the only `git diff -U0` calls in src/ are revertDetector's (text deltas) and taintOnlyH1's (L-HOLD-STORES). */
  advance(old: Oid, next: Oid, rebuildFrom: () => Promise<Oid | null>): Promise<number>;   // rebuildFrom is called only when a rebuild is needed (it reads the reflog)
  newest(): Oid | null;   // the commit of the newest entry (null for an empty journal); lostStateRebuild's advance-or-load choice (Task 8)
  addedBy(hash: string): readonly string[];
  deletedBy(hash: string): readonly string[];
}

// src/holds/revert.ts (HLD-18, HLD-26…HLD-29, 05 §3.6 revert detector; R3-36, R3-37)
export interface DetectorDelta { path: string; prev: OidOrAbsent; next: OidOrAbsent; binary: boolean; }   // observed deltas already removed by the caller
export interface TaintedPath { path: string; pin: boolean; }                                             // pin: binary or deletion → PIN
/** revertDetector: diff -U0 (prev, next) per delta; tainted when a removed non-trivial line was added, or an added one deleted, by another writer within retention; ws-equal pairs and hunks REBASE-matching a RESOLVED_PUBLISH record on p exempt; writes no object. */
export function revertDetector(repo: GitRepo, deltas: readonly DetectorDelta[], prov: ProvenanceJournal, self: PeerId, resolved: readonly HoldRecord[], cache: RebaseCache, A: TreeMap): Promise<TaintedPath[]>;
/** HLD-18, the single -U0 applier: A[p] plus only the -U0 hunks of diff(A[p], S_disk[p]) that contain a tainted line; output only a record's h1 (route (c) -w). */
export function taintOnlyH1(repo: GitRepo, path: string, a: OidOrAbsent, sDisk: OidOrAbsent, prov: ProvenanceJournal, self: PeerId): Promise<Oid>;
/** HLD-28: per tainted path a PATCH (H0 = A[p], H1 = taintOnlyH1) or PIN with guard revert, ON_DISK ACTIVE, origin 'human'; HLD-07 attach. */
export function holdReverts(reg: HoldRegistry, repo: GitRepo, tainted: readonly TaintedPath[], A: TreeMap, sDisk: TreeMap, prov: ProvenanceJournal, self: PeerId): Promise<string[]>;

// src/holds/breaker.ts (HLD-30; R3-38, R3-50)
export const MASS_WINDOW_MS = 30_000;
export const MASS_MIN_FILES = 10;
export const MASS_TRACKED_RATIO = 0.02;
export interface BreakerInput { path: string; deletion: boolean; observed: boolean; }
/** (a) unobserved-delta paths first seen within any 30 s Δmono window > max(10, 2 % of tracked) → 'window'; (b) an unobserved deletion of a file holding a non-trivial line another writer added → 'peer_lines'; index.lock and logs/HEAD never count. In memory. */
export class MassBreaker {
  constructor(clock: Clock);
  observe(dirty: readonly BreakerInput[], tracked: number, peerLinesIn: (path: string) => Promise<boolean>): Promise<'window' | 'peer_lines' | null>;
  reset(): void;
}
```

### Holds: change-sets

```ts
// src/holds/changesets.ts (changed; HLD-33…HLD-44, HLD-46/47 shapes, HLD-48, HLD-49, HLD-52, HLD-66 shape, HLD-67 steps 1/3/4; R3-05, R3-40…R3-44)
export type CsKind = 'human' | 'recovered' | 'seat';
export interface CsTableEntry {
  cs_id: string; kind: CsKind; seat: string | null; agent: string | null; origin: 'human' | 'prompt' | 'wake';
  paths: string[]; state: 'open' | 'closed'; cap_seq: number; publishes: number; checks: 'pass' | 'fail' | 'skipped'; covered: boolean;   // covered: the latest file effect is covered by a passing capture (HLD-39)
}
// checks: the result of the CS's latest checks_run signal before the capture — ok → 'pass', not ok → 'fail' — else 'skipped' (HLD-37, R3-43) (Task 9).
// covered (Task 9; the edit-check pass that sets it is Task 10's): true at a green or tool checkpoint and at a passing edit check on the CS's latest effect, false at any later
//   effect; a candidate's capture is passing iff every member CS is closed or covered after the verdict.
/** journal/keep/cs/table.json, canonical JSON (R3-40). Written only in state transitions (captures, closes, publishes; ENG-04). */
export interface CsTable {
  v: 1; next_order: number; seats: Record<string, { turn_seq: number }>; cs: CsTableEntry[]; orders: Record<string, number>;
  passing: Record<string, boolean>;   // per cs_id: its journaled ready pair came from a passing capture (05 §2); Plan 2 pairs read without a table count as passing (Task 9; Task 10's readiness reads it)
}
// ReadyPair keeps its Plan 2 shape; `order` is the peer-wide capture counter from CsTable.orders (newest passing capture wins). The first table (none journaled yet) gets
//   orders[cs_id] = n for every journaled pair and next_order = max(n) + 1 (1 when none), written in the first capturing state update (R3-40, Task 9).
// Capture shape (Task 9): one ready pair per member CS of the capturing group, each over that CS's own member paths, all with one shared order (next_order, then incremented);
//   a capture always journals a pair for a member CS that has none (a closed CS always has a ready pair, HLD-34) but replaces an unpublished passing pair only with another
//   passing one (HLD-39); a human or recovered CS gets its <peer_id>/human/<n> at its first capture and keeps it until published.
// Attribution in Plan 3 (Task 9): an `effect` signal's paths belong to that seat's CS for the deltas of the next observe(); every other changed path is unattributed (the human
//   CS, or the recovered CS while it is open); a `prompt` signal changes no CS; a checkpoint{source: 'edit'} is stored as a pending candidate (candSeq, paths) of the seat's CS.
export interface HeldPin { id: string; paths: readonly string[]; guards: readonly string[]; }   // non-terminal ON_DISK PIN with guards ("held PIN", glossary §3); declared by Task 9 (capture's `pins`), produced by heldPins (Task 10)
export interface PublicationGroup { key: string; csIds: string[]; paths: string[]; pins: string[]; ready: boolean; }   // HLD-38; key = the smallest cs_id
export interface Candidate { csId: string; candSeq: number; groupKey: string; pairs: ReadyPair[]; }                      // HLD-67 step 1, not journaled
export interface Composition {
  P: Map<string, TreeEntry>; csIds: string[]; csPaths: Map<string, string[]>; groups: PublicationGroup[];
  withheldWhole: Map<string, string[]>;   // group key → paths MASK steps 2–4 withheld on R (HLD-42 → dependency PIN)
}
// Plan 2's publish.ts declares a module-local `interface Composition { …; masked }`; Task 10, which exports this one and imports it into publish.ts, renames the local
//   interface to PublishComposition (it keeps `masked`), so the import and the local declaration never collide (TS2440).
export class ChangeSets {
  constructor(peer: PeerId, clock: Clock, fs?: Fs);   // fs: Plan 2 Task 15's P2-SCRATCHLOCK carry (core.ts passes core's Fs), kept unchanged
  /** Ready pairs + meta.cs_human_next + the journaled table + the cache (R3-69): journaled undefined keeps the current in-memory table (Plan 2's error-path restores; none at
   *  construction = Plan 2 behaviour); journaled null = no table journaled yet (order = n, R3-68); a journaled table always supplies orders, next_order, passing, publish counts, closed
   *  CSs and seats (so a seat's cs_ids never repeat within an epoch, HLD-33); cache supplies only open-CS membership and only when it names the current state commit — a null or stale
   *  cache re-forms every unpublished delta outside a ready pair as one `recovered` CS (HLD-36). attachCsTable (core.ts, Task 12a) passes readCsTable(repo, view) and readCsCache(core) (Task 9). */
  restore(ready: readonly ReadyPair[], nextN: number, journaled?: CsTable | null, cache?: CsTable | null): void;
  observe(changed: Iterable<string>): void;     // HLD-35: unattributed deltas → the human CS (the recovered CS while it is open)
  signal(s: CsSignal): void;                    // HLD-34: effects open/extend seat CSs (agent rules), boundaries close them; nonce prompts are no boundary
  done(): void;
  closeDue(): boolean;                          // a human/recovered/seat close, a pending green or tool checkpoint capture (Task 9), or a pending recapture is due (a group that no longer holds a held PIN and has a closed CS without an unpublished pair, Task 10)
  /** HLD-40 at a trigger: R[p] = S_disk[p], A_cap[p] = A[p] over every path of the GROUP of the closing CSs (held PINs excluded), one shared order, sparse trees; while no table is
   *  journaled, order = n and no multi-CS group forms (R3-68). Every read-tree into scratchIndex is preceded by resetScratchIndex (src/git/scratchIndex.ts, P2-SCRATCHLOCK).
   *  Plan 2's call shape is kept (R3-40): `pins` defaults to [] (Task 9 adds the parameter and a private group closure; Task 10 passes heldPins), and `pair` is the first closing
   *  CS's pair, so Plan 2's start.ts, loop.ts and test call sites compile unchanged (Task 9). */
  capture(repo: GitRepo, sDisk: TreeMap, A: TreeMap, scratchIndex: string, pins?: readonly HeldPin[]): Promise<{ pair: ReadyPair; pairs: ReadyPair[]; nextN: number } | null>;
  /** rev 2.5 (Task 10, X-05): when lastCheck holds no note for (csId, candSeq), notes it with editN = candSeq (Plan 3 has no edit-checkpoint counter; Plan 5's EditCheckpoints
   *  notes first, with 06 §3.8.4's edit_n, and its note wins). */
  candidate(repo: GitRepo, csId: string, candSeq: number, sDisk: TreeMap, A: TreeMap, scratchIndex: string, pins: readonly HeldPin[]): Promise<Candidate | null>;
  /** HLD-67 steps 3–4 / HLD-39: pass → the candidate's pairs replace the group's (newest passing wins) and lastCheck.passedFor(csId, candSeq); fail or incomplete → dropped,
   *  and the only other effect is the memory-only lastCheck.recordFor(csId, candSeq, …) (rev 2.5: D1, reconciliation X-06): kind 'conflict' when the first entry's reason is merge_conflict, 'incomplete'
   *  for an incomplete verdict or a first entry with reason no_snapshot or no_merge, else 'fail'; check/path/line from verdict.entries[0] (else check = failed[0]); more =
   *  entries.length − 1 (else failed.length − 1). No record, op, event, state change or pushed message. */
  settleCandidate(c: Candidate, verdict: EditCheckVerdict): readonly ReadyPair[] | null;
  /** HLD-41's four cases decided per GROUP (withdraw/cancel the whole group, never one pair). rev 2.5: a withdrawal clears last_pass.ready of each member seat CS (Task 10,
   *  10 §3.5.1 "follows the journal"); a clean HLD-41 rebase (glossary §29.2) of a pending revert CS's pair whose R[p] before the rebase equals the mark's current oid moves the
   *  mark to the new R[p] and reports the move in RebaseResult.marksMoved, so applyStep appends the reverts.jsonl line before its state update (Task 17, D2, §8 Q18 option (a)). */
  rebase(repo: GitRepo, changes: readonly RebaseChange[], scratchIndex: string): Promise<RebaseResult>;
  groups(pins: readonly HeldPin[]): PublicationGroup[];   // HLD-38 closure over open and ready CSs plus held PINs sharing a path; a group with a held PIN, or with a human/recovered CS while blocked() is non-empty, is not ready
  /** R3-53: the not-stageable non-portable paths present at this tick (portabilityStep hands them over at every tick; in memory, replaced each call; never CS member paths).
   *  While the set is non-empty every group holding a human or recovered CS (open, or ready and unpublished) is not ready — a readiness gate, not a PIN: never bundled,
   *  never a dependency target, no record (Task 10). */
  blockPaths(paths: ReadonlySet<string>): void;
  blocked(): ReadonlySet<string>;
  /** P (HLD-42): MASK(R) of the newest ready pair per path of every ready group with no held PIN, A[p] elsewhere; A_cap ≠ A → ReadyBaseMismatchError (ENG-39). Task 2 makes `mask` async; Task 10 replaces Plan 2's pinnedPaths with `pins`. */
  composeP(repo: GitRepo, A: TreeMap, mask: (X: TreeMap) => Promise<MaskResult>, pins: readonly HeldPin[]): Promise<Composition>;
  /** HLD-49 path bundle: a closing CS whose group holds `pin` journals no ready pair and joins it — the growth for the registry (paths ∪, cs_ids ∪, h1 from R; one transition) instead of ready pairs.
   *  When the group no longer holds a held PIN, closeDue() reports a recapture and the next materializing iteration captures the whole group (all members closed → passing),
   *  so the bundle publishes in one commit (HLD-52; Task 10). */
  bundle(repo: GitRepo, pin: HeldPin, pairs: readonly ReadyPair[]): Promise<{ paths: string[]; csIds: string[]; h1: BlobMap }>;
  /** rev 2.5: also lastCheck.sharedFor(csId, candSeq) for each published pair that came from a candidate (Task 10, X-05) and revertMarks.drop(csId) (Task 17, X-09). */
  published(csIds: readonly string[]): void;
  /** HLD-37 trailer of a CS in this publish: seq 1-based, state closed|checkpoint, origin, checks (R3-43). */
  trailer(csId: string, paths: number): CsTrailer;
  ready(): readonly ReadyPair[];
  counts(): { open: number; ready: number };
  openPaths(): ReadonlySet<string>;
  table(): CsTable;
  // rev 2.5 additions (cross-plan reconciliation 2026-10-08):
  readonly lastCheck: LastCheckStore;   // Task 10 (X-05): one per ChangeSets, created by the constructor; clearCs at every close of a seat CS; never in CsTable, the cache or a state commit
  readonly revertMarks: RevertMarks;    // Task 17 (X-09): the revert shrink marks (decision D2) of pending revert CSs (memory; re-derived at start from RevertsJournal, §8 Q18)
  /** Task 17 (X-09, HLD-68): a second human-class CS beside the open human CS (no new CsKind): `<peer>/human/<n>` with the next n of the per-peer counter, member paths
   *  exactly `paths`, closed at once (closeDue() true until it is captured, HLD-40 human close); returns its cs_id. */
  addRevertCs(paths: readonly string[]): string;
  /** Task 18 (X-11, FEAT-F14-04): Δmono until the open human CS (or the open recovered CS) reaches its 20 s quiet close, 0 when due, null when none is open; pure read. */
  quietLeftMs(): number | null;
}
/** Record constructors for later plans' creators (unit-tested only in Plan 3; R3-07): K2/K3/K6/K7/wake-scope whole-CS PIN over LIVE paths (H0 = A, H1 = R or disk), and the K1 SHADOW (OFF_DISK PARKED PATCH, offer pending_leftover). */
export function pinCs(guard: string, csIds: readonly string[], livePaths: readonly string[], A: TreeMap, h1: BlobMap, origin: string, offer: Offer): NewRecord;
export function shadowRecord(claimGuard: string, path: string, h0: OidOrAbsent, h1: OidOrAbsent, origin: string): NewRecord;
/** Non-terminal ON_DISK PIN records with a non-empty guard set (glossary §3 "held PIN", wake-scope included); replaces Plan 2's pinnedPaths(core) and PIN_GUARDS in publish.ts (compose,
 *  allowedPending and pendingCheap, R-18's otherPending) and is what captureStep passes as `pins` (Task 10). */
export function heldPins(reg: HoldRegistry): HeldPin[];
```
