### Holds: F10 revert kernel (rev 2.5, Task 17)

```ts
// src/holds/revertCs.ts (rev 2.5, F10; 05 Algorithm REV, HLD-68, §8 Q17, Q18; glossary §6 reverts.jsonl, §20 "Revert", §29.1 D2, §29.2, §29.3; X-09, X-10; Task 17).
// Roadmap row 3: the kernel lives in its own module, not src/holds/revert.ts (the HLD-27 detector's file, Task 8). It imports nothing from src/engine: engine facts arrive
// through RevertHost, a structural subset of EngineCore that EngineCore satisfies (Task 17 adds to core.ts any member the subset needs, never an import of src/engine).
// Plan 7's revert verb (preview/confirm, SEC-25, the daemon worker, RF_REVERT_* refusals, the test-only takeTheirs switch) and Plan 5's undo view are the callers.
export interface RevertHost {
  readonly repo: GitRepo; readonly peer: PeerId; readonly clock: Clock; readonly fs: Fs;
  readonly prov: ProvenanceJournal; readonly cs: ChangeSets; readonly holds: HoldRegistry; readonly observed: ObservedWrites;
  // + the current A and TIP maps, the current scope, the last materialized S_disk, and a DISK_EQ predicate (exact member names: Task 17's contract addition 1)
}
export const REVERT_MAX_PATHS = 2_000; export const REVERT_MAX_MERGES = 500; export const REVERT_MAX_MS = 30_000;   // glossary §20 "Revert"
export interface CsLocation { csId: string; epoch: string; label: string; commits: readonly Oid[]; }   // label = <cs_id> or <cs_id>@<epoch8>; commits in first-parent order (C(t))
/** Algorithm REV step 1 (HLD-33 epoch scope; FEAT-F10-02, -13). null = an unknown id (the live-chain grep finds no trailer). No FREEZE test: RF_REVERT_FROZEN is the verb's. */
export type LocateResult = { found: CsLocation } | { ambiguous: string[] } | { notOwn: { name: string } } | { tooOld: true } | { open: true }
  | { notShared: { hid: string | null } } | { noLast: true } | null;
export function locateCs(core: RevertHost, t: string, o: { mode: 'verb' | 'view' }): Promise<LocateResult>;
export type RevertSkip = 'NOT_LIVE' | 'BUSY' | 'MIXED' | 'RENAME_PAIR' | 'FILE_CHANGED_LATER' | 'TEAMMATE_LINE' | 'CHANGED_NOW';
export interface RevertResult {
  results: ReadonlyMap<string, Buffer | 'ABSENT'>;                                           // R_rev (X-09): result bytes in stored form, in memory — steps 1–7 write no object or file
  paths: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; del: number; add: number; disk: Oid | 'ABSENT' }>;    // per result path: rows removed/added, and the disk blob the preview digests
  kept: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; a: number; b: number; names: string[] }>;             // CHANGED_LATER regions (step 4(c)), with the later writers' display names
  skipped: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; key: RevertSkip }>;
}
/** Algorithm REV steps 2–7 (FEAT-F10-03…-06). The one `merge-file --ours` caller (step 4(c); L-HOLD-STORES, glossary §29.3); step 5's TEAMMATE_LINE backstop reads core.prov.
 *  Past REVERT_MAX_PATHS, REVERT_MAX_MERGES or REVERT_MAX_MS → {tooBig: true} (Plan 7's RF_REVERT_TOO_BIG); empty results → Plan 7's RF_REVERT_NOTHING. Never runs in a hook. */
export function revertInverse(core: RevertHost, loc: CsLocation, o: { mode: 'verb' | 'view' }): Promise<RevertResult | { tooBig: true }>;
/** Step 3's state part (verb mode): paths in an open or unpublished CS, under a non-terminal record, or with A[p] ≠ TIP[p]; revertInverse adds ¬DISK_EQ(p, A[p]) per path. */
export function busyPaths(core: RevertHost): ReadonlySet<string>;
/** Step 1's not-shared test (FEAT-F10-13): {hid} of a non-terminal record whose cs_ids contain csId, {hid: null} for a journaled ready pair, null when shared. A reason, not a
 *  code: Plan 7 maps it to its cli-area RF_REVERT_NOT_SHARED; HOLD_CODES stays 38 (X-10). */
export function revertNotShared(core: RevertHost, csId: string): { hid: string | null } | null;
/** Not open, no journaled ready pair, no non-terminal record naming it (Plan 6's X6-40 input). */
export function isFullyShared(core: RevertHost, csId: string): boolean;
/** View mode (FEAT-F10-10, CCI-68): Plan 5's RevertView shape, declared here; Plan 5's undoView.ts imports it (its local declaration is deleted). In-process -U3 diffs of stored
 *  forms for in-file text changes only; `human` names the change kind ('created' | 'deleted' | 'binary' | 'mode' | 'filtered') for Plan 5's T-SHOW_UNDO.human line; a path
 *  changed since the last materialization is skip CHANGED_NOW (§8 Q17 (a)); no object, no file, no last_check. */
export interface RevertView { kind: 'view' | 'notown' | 'summary_only' | 'unknown' | 'ambiguous'; summary: Json;
  paths: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; diff?: string; human?: string; skip?: 'MIXED' | 'FILE_CHANGED_LATER' | 'RENAME_PAIR' | 'NOT_LIVE' | 'TEAMMATE_LINE' | 'CHANGED_NOW' }>;
  kept: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; line: number }>; candidates?: readonly string[]; }
export function revertView(core: RevertHost, csId: string): Promise<RevertView>;
/** Algorithm REV step 8 after the verb's writeLocalGroup returned WRITTEN (its writes carry origin `revert:<of>`, `of` = the reverted CS's id, known before the write):
 *  ObservedWrites.record(p, written) per path (an observed delta, HLD-26: no revert record, no MASS), cs.addRevertCs(paths), revertMarks.set(cs, written), then
 *  RevertsJournal.append({cs, of, t_local_ms, written}) (fsynced); returns the new cs_id. */
export function openRevertCs(core: RevertHost, of: string, written: ReadonlyMap<string, Oid | 'ABSENT'>): Promise<string>;
export interface RevertLine { cs: string; of: string; t_local_ms: number; written: Record<string, Oid | 'ABSENT'>; }
/** cs/<enc(b)>.reverts.jsonl (glossary §6, §29.3): one line per confirmed revert, plus one per clean-rebase move of the mark (D2); pruned to 14 days at append; never pushed. */
export class RevertsJournal {
  constructor(fs: Fs, file: string, clock: Clock);
  append(e: RevertLine): Promise<void>;          // append + fsync, before the caller's next state update (ENG-36)
  lines(): Promise<RevertLine[]>;                 // a torn last line is dropped
  byCs(cs: string): Promise<RevertLine[]>;
  ofCs(cs: string): Promise<string | null>;       // the "revert of" label (every line of a cs carries the same `of`)
}
/** The revert shrink marks (glossary §29.1 decision D2, HLD-50 item 3): per pending revert CS, path → the oid its confirmed write produced (ABSENT for a deletion), carried forward only by a
 *  clean HLD-41 rebase whose R[p] before the rebase was the mark's oid; memory, held by ChangeSets.revertMarks. */
export class RevertMarks {
  set(cs: string, written: ReadonlyMap<string, Oid | 'ABSENT'>): void;
  covers(path: string, content: Oid | 'ABSENT'): boolean;                       // a pending mark holds exactly this content (t0Shrink's attributed; ENG-25's lift)
  moveOnCleanRebase(cs: string, path: string, before: Oid, after: Oid): boolean;   // moves only when the mark's oid for path === before
  current(cs: string): ReadonlyMap<string, Oid | 'ABSENT'> | null;
  drop(cs: string): void;                                                        // the CS published, its record turned terminal, or it left journal/ready/ unpublished
  pending(): readonly string[];
}
/** §8 Q18 at start and restart (after attachCsTable): for each journaled cs still pending (ready pair journaled, or in the cs_ids of a non-terminal record), per path the newest
 *  line of that cs whose oid equals the CS's journaled content (its ready pair's R[p], or the record's blob); a path with no matching line gets no mark. */
export function rederiveRevertMarks(core: RevertHost): Promise<void>;
```

### Engine

```ts
// src/engine/state.ts (changed; HLD-36, HLD-40; R3-40)
// journal/ready/<k> accepts human (<peer_id>/human/<n>) and seat (<seat_id>/<turn-seq>) cs_ids; ReadyPair.order comes from journal/keep/cs/table.json `orders`,
// from n for a human pair when no table exists; commit() refuses a ready pair whose order the table does not name once a table exists (the first table names every
// journaled pair, R3-40) and keeps Plan 2's order === n check while no table exists (R3-68; Plan 2's loop and start write no table before Tasks 12a/12b route their
// captures through captureStep, and Task 9's push-ok update writes the table keep entry only when one is journaled).
export function readCsTable(repo: GitRepo, view: StateView): Promise<CsTable | null>;
export function csTableKeep(repo: GitRepo, t: CsTable): Promise<readonly [string, Oid]>;   // ['cs', tree holding table.json] for Transition.keep

// src/engine/core.ts (changed)
// EngineCore gains (task that adds each):
//   readonly rebase: RebaseCache;          // Task 1 (HLD-14)
//   readonly observed: ObservedWrites;     // Task 5 (HLD-26 (b))
//   readonly transient: TransientClocks;   // Task 6 (HLD-32, HLD-54)
//   readonly prov: ProvenanceJournal;      // Task 8 (HLD-25)
//   readonly breaker: MassBreaker;         // Task 8 (HLD-30)
//   sPub: MaskResult | null;               // Task 12: MASK of the last materialized S_disk (glossary §3, ENG-28)
//   ranges: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>>;   // Task 12 (HLD-21)
//   holdCode: { code: string; params: Record<string, Json> } | null;          // Task 1: newest hold code for status (R-27); every later task sets it
//   maskInvalid: boolean;                  // Task 12: set by maskStep when assertSPub fails, cleared by the next passing assertSPub (an assertP failure never sets it: it blocks only
//                                          // that publishStep's code part, PublishReport.maskInvalid = true, R3-57); while set, publishStep sends no code commit but still sends the
//                                          // allowed board-only ops, PublishReport.maskInvalid = true (R3-20, ENG-28)
// RegistryDeps.scopeOf/A are bound to the current IterationCtx (Task 1). attachState stays synchronous; `async attachCsTable(core, view)` reads readCsTable(repo, view) and
//   readCsCache(core) and calls ChangeSets.restore(view.ready, view.meta.cs_human_next, journaled, cache), awaited right after every attachState call (Task 12a, R3-69).
// `async advanceProvenance(core, oldLaC)` (Task 8, R3-59) advances the provenance journal from oldLaC to view.laC: commit() calls it right after a CAS that moves laC (push ok, restart's
//   decision commit, a join's first-apply commit) unless the caller passes the commit option { provenance: 'caller' }; applyStep passes that option and calls it right after
//   crashpoint('apply.state') and setCurrentA; lostStateRebuild (restart.ts, which writes the state through state.rebuild/create without commit()) calls it after writeLostState and
//   attachState — prov.advance(prov.newest(), tip, …) when prov.newest() is a first-parent ancestor of tip, else prov.load(tip, await provRebuildFrom(core)).
//   EngineCore.commit(t: Transition, o?: { provenance?: 'commit' | 'caller' }) — the option is the only signature change (default 'commit').
// scopeInputs.localOnlyPaths, scopeInputs.localOnlyReason and ConflictCtx.localOnly read non-terminal ON_DISK local_only records (Task 2, R3-21); tickSkip(core) adds non-terminal collision records' paths (Task 6, DAT-29).
// allowedPending (the governor's `pending` input, Plan 2) counts code as pending only when some group of core.cs.groups(heldPins(core.holds)) is ready and has an unpublished
//   pair — the predicate publishPending uses — so a not-ready group never holds the head of the governor queue (HLD-39; Task 10); from Task 12a it also answers false for code
//   while EngineCore.maskInvalid or massResumePending(core) holds (publishStep would skip the code part then).
export interface PrepareOptions { materialize: boolean | 'auto'; forceFetch: boolean; holdPaths?: ReadonlySet<string>; }   // + holdPaths: a hold-record occasion (PARK, RESTORE, a record from disk bytes) materializes these paths (W6 (c)) (Task 5)
/** Writes the newest code to core.holdCode and every code to the daemon log (R-27); the one way a src/holds step's HoldCodeRaise list reaches status (incomingStep's codes; Task 6). */
export function raiseHoldCodes(core: EngineCore, codes: readonly HoldCodeRaise[]): void;
/** The live code of the laB of the oldest entry still in the state ref's reflog (ENG-02 writes every state update with --create-reflog; the first entry is the genesis/join
 *  StateStore.create: L0 at genesis, TIP at a join — not its laC, which is L_b at a join, R3-59); null when none remains; no meta field is added (ENG-05). (Task 8) */
export function stateJoinPoint(core: EngineCore): Promise<Oid | null>;
/** HLD-25's rebuild start: the laC of the newest state-ref reflog entry whose local timestamp (this PC's clock, INV-CLOCK) is older than clock.wallMs() − PROV_RETENTION_MS,
 *  else stateJoinPoint(core). Restart, the lost-state rebuild and a re-anchor's rebuild pass it to ProvenanceJournal.load/advance (Task 8, R3-36, R3-59). */
export function provRebuildFrom(core: EngineCore): Promise<Oid | null>;
/** R3-59: ProvenanceJournal.advance(oldLaC, view.laC, () => provRebuildFrom(core)) after a laC-moving state CAS (by commit() by default; by applyStep after apply.state). (Task 8) */
export function advanceProvenance(core: EngineCore, oldLaC: Oid): Promise<void>;
/** R3-69: readCsTable + readCsCache → core.cs.restore(view.ready, view.meta.cs_human_next, journaled, cache); awaited after every attachState call. (Task 12a) */
export function attachCsTable(core: EngineCore, view: StateView): Promise<void>;

// src/engine/holdOps.ts (HLD-08, HLD-16, HLD-17, 05 §3.4 items 3–4, DAT-29, DAT-45; R3-15, R3-16, R3-18, R3-23)
export type ParkOutcome = { kind: 'PARKED'; companion: string | null } | { kind: 'REFRESHED'; rev: number } | { kind: 'LOCKED' } | { kind: 'MISMATCH' }
  | { kind: 'FROZEN' } | { kind: 'POSTPONED'; why: 'push_intent' | 'stranded' | 'deferred' } | { kind: 'NOT_PARKABLE' };
/** HLD-16: target per path (r's step-5 MASK alone, else A[p]); held-side refresh first for PIN/CONFLICTED/whole-path records (REFRESHED when it must become durable first); writeLocalGroup all or none; OFF_DISK, PARKED (CONFLICTED kept), displaced_by; markers → markersPark; local_only/INCOMING → NOT_PARKABLE.
 *  After WRITTEN, per path: ObservedWrites.record(p, written) and Snapshotter.setEntry(p, written entry), then one snap-only state transition (snap := editTreeIndexOnly(snap, p → written))
 *  before the registry's final commit (R3-36, R3-60: the park family's writes are attributed in this run and after a restart); markersPark does the same. */
export function park(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, id: string, o: { humanEvidence: boolean; displacedBy: string | null; refreshMustBeDurable: boolean }): Promise<ParkOutcome>;
/** 05 §3.4 item 3 in R3-15's order. */
export function markersPark(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, id: string, o: { humanEvidence: boolean; displacedBy: string | null }): Promise<ParkOutcome>;
export type RestoreOutcome = { kind: 'RESTORED'; markers: boolean } | { kind: 'REFUSED'; code: 'HOLD_RESTORE_CONFLICT' | 'HOLD_DISK_CHANGED' | 'HOLD_FROZEN' | 'RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY' }
  | { kind: 'LOCKED' } | { kind: 'POSTPONED'; why: 'tip_write_pending' | 'push_intent' | 'stranded' };
/** HLD-17/DAT-45: disk[p] by staged-copy read (record content); merge-file --diff3 -L cowork:disk -L cowork:base -L cowork:held; rc 0 → writeLocal(p, D, canon(result, ref(p))) (DAT-45 step 1, R3-16; canon's output by route (c));
 *  rc > 0 or a marker row → the merged result written as is, guard markers + RESTORE_MARKERS; binary per R3-18; DAT §3.9 cases 2–3; ON_DISK, displacement guard released, ObservedWrites.record
 *  (no setEntry and no snap transition: the restored change is a new observed change of the human CS, HLD-57 (e)). REFUSED RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY is this operator's outcome only:
 *  resolve() maps it to {kind: 'claude_refused', reason: 'binary'} and never surfaces it as a code (R3-54, R3-65). */
export function restore(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, id: string, o: { humanEvidence: boolean; actor: 'human' | 'claude' }): Promise<RestoreOutcome>;
/** DAT-29 take-team: staged copy → route (b) -w → PATCH h0 ABSENT, h1 captured, OFF_DISK PARKED, no guard; then writeLocal(p, captured, TIP[p]) and, after WRITTEN, ObservedWrites.record (no setEntry: p is skipped by every snapshot while the collision record lives); CAS failure keeps the collision record;
 *  on any writer result other than WRITTEN the capture record ends GONE in one commit (its bytes are still on disk), mirroring R3-15's failed-companion rule; oracleOffDisk exempts this
 *  OFF_DISK → GONE ending (R3-67) (Task 5). */
export function collisionTake(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, id: string): Promise<'WRITTEN' | 'HOLD_DISK_CHANGED' | 'LOCKED' | 'POSTPONED'>;
/** HLD-08 after every apply step: R1 on ON_DISK records, the OFF_DISK check on records whose A[p] changed, MASK conflicts → CONFLICTED; one commitMany; HOLD_CONFLICTED/SUBSUMED/GONE to status/log. */
export function evaluateRecords(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, changedA: ReadonlySet<string>, maskConflicted: readonly string[]): Promise<string[]>;
/** Restart: settle an interrupted markers park by observation (R3-15); the first step of settleHoldOps. */
export function settleMarkersParks(core: EngineCore): Promise<void>;
/** Restart's one settlement entry point, run by restart on both of its paths before the first publish — after resolveWriteIntent/resolvePushIntent on the normal path and after
 *  lostStateRebuild and attachState on the lost-state path (Task 5): settleMarkersParks, then every local().inflight entry by DISK_EQ (all targets → commit R3-60's snap transition for a
 *  park or discard, then its final patch and clear it, with explicit for a restore and the daemon log line and `discard` trace for a discard; all expected → clear only; neither → clear,
 *  leave the record, log HOLD_DISK_CHANGED {hid}), every guard-less OFF_DISK PARKED capture whose path still has a non-terminal collision record and whose disk still DISK_EQ its h1
 *  (→ GONE, R3-67), and every ON_DISK CONFLICTED stage record whose disk DISK_EQ s3[p] with A[p] = s3[p] (the TIP write completed → OFF_DISK with displaced_by/peer_authors). Plan 2's
 *  restart decision (restart.ts ~588), which moves a K4 record OFF_DISK itself when a crashed apply's write is found complete, sets displaced_by and peer_authors in that same transition
 *  through the apply.ts helper (Task 5). Without settlement a crash after a completed PARK write and before its registry commit would leave an ON_DISK record that R1 then ends GONE (INV-NOLOSS). */
export function settleHoldOps(core: EngineCore): Promise<void>;
// Operator origins passed to Writer.writeLocal/writeLocalGroup (ENG-37): park:<hold_id>, markers_park:<hold_id>, restore:<hold_id>, take:<hold_id> (Task 5), discard:<hold_id> (Task 11),
//   repair:<path> (Task 7); every one is followed after WRITTEN by ObservedWrites.record (R3-36).
//   (rev 2.5, additive, X-09: revert:<of> — Plan 7's confirmed `/cowork revert` writeLocalGroup, `of` = the reverted CS's id; Task 17's openRevertCs makes the ObservedWrites.record
//   calls after WRITTEN. Tasks 5, 7 and 11 build nothing new for it.)

// src/engine/snapshot.ts (changed; DAT-08, glossary §26.1 W1/W6; R3-34, R3-36) — class Snapshotter gains (Task 5; Task 7's repair also uses it):
//   setEntry(path: string, entry: TreeEntry): Promise<void>;   // treeEdit.ts's editIndexOnly (update-index --cacheinfo, INDEX_ONLY config) on the private index for one path (the park family's and
//                                                              // --discard's written blob, the repair's S_disk[p]); writes no object; never a private copy of the helper
// and Materialized gains (Task 8): prevMap: TreeMap;   // the private-index entries, as they were before this materialization, of every path it re-added or removed (missing = absent):
//   the revert detector's prev[p] (05 §3.6 step 1); nothing else in Plan 2 keeps it once materialize has edited the private index.
// src/engine/apply.ts (changed) — ApplyReport gains (Task 5): changedA: string[];   // paths whose A[p] this apply changed (input of R1's OFF_DISK check in recordStep, HLD-08)
//   and (Task 6): closures: Array<{ paths: string[]; reason: ClosureReason }>;        // one entry per deferred write group (R3-31): applyStep regroups WriteGroupsResult.deferred's
//                                                                                     // per-(group, reason) entries — paths = their union, reason = closureReason(their reasons); IncomingInputs.closures' source
//                 notApplicableRules: Map<string, PortRule>;                          // the D7 rule of each `unmaterializable` path (portable()); IncomingInputs.rules' source
//                 incoming: IncomingCs[];                                             // this apply's incoming CSs (the trailers it read); IncomingInputs.csOf's source
// notApplicableStill (core.ts, Plan 2's P2-NA-TRIGGER re-check) returns a collision path to the apply trigger once DISK_EQ(p, TIP[p]) holds (R3-33; Task 6).

// src/engine/dataRecords.ts (DAT-08, DAT-32, DAT-33, DAT-39; R3-34, R3-35)
// Calling rule (Task 7): repairStep, portabilityStep and churnStep run on every iteration that ran a detection tick (it.tick !== null) — DAT-33 and DAT-39 count snapshots (R3-34's
//   reading) and F-DAT-04's generator keeps the human CS open, so no capture would ever materialize the path. Each writes an object only at a W6 occasion it owns (a portability or
//   generated? record's content is the staged copy hashed with route (b) -w, glossary §26.1 W6 (c)); repairStep writes the disk only in an iteration whose it.mat materialized p.
//   Per-core memory (quiet-tick counters, repair episodes, churn histories) lives in a module-level WeakMap<EngineCore, …>, so no EngineCore member is added.
/** DAT-08 repair per R3-34; trace 'repair'. */
export function repairStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<string[]>;
/** Paths whose DAT-08 repair condition holds (two unchanged detection ticks after the materialization that showed S_raw[p] ≠ S_disk[p], R3-34) and whose write waits for an iteration
 *  that materializes them; iterate() puts the set into the next iteration's PrepareOptions.holdPaths. A pure read of the module's per-core state (Task 7). */
export function repairDue(core: EngineCore): ReadonlySet<string>;
/** DAT-33 publish side: a NOT_ADMITTED 'portability' new path that is stageable() → local_only ON_DISK PATCH 'portability' (h1 = staged copy, stub), PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {id, path, rule};
 *  released when D7 passes. A NOT_ADMITTED 'portability' path that is not stageable() (R3-53) → no record, no CS membership (no cs.observe), PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {path, rule} (no id)
 *  to status and the log once per (path, episode), and the path in `unrecorded`; from Task 10 on it also calls core.cs.blockPaths(new Set(unrecorded)) at every tick. */
export function portabilityStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<{ created: string[]; released: string[]; unrecorded: string[] }>;
/** DAT-39: churnVerdict per changed LIVE path → ON_DISK PATCH 'generated?' (GENERATED_SUSPECT); not again while a non-terminal record over p carries generated? or generated?:exclude,
 *  or while p is in local().keepSyncing (the keep-syncing answer, Task 11); SUBSUMED when p becomes UNMANAGED. */
export function churnStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<{ created: string[]; released: string[] }>;

// src/engine/detect.ts (HLD-27, HLD-28, HLD-30, ENG-14; R3-37, R3-38)
/** Every detection tick: the breaker on the tick's dirty set (paths only); a trip enters MASS. */
export function breakerTick(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<'window' | 'peer_lines' | null>;
/** Every newly materialized S_disk, before any merge or capture: deltas from the private-index entries, observed ones dropped (ObservedWrites, AttributionPort.observedEdit), revertDetector, holdReverts. */
export function revertStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<string[]>;
/** HLD-30 entry: FREEZE(MASS), journal/keep/snap_pre_freeze = the last materialized S_disk tree, one event{kind: freeze, reason_code: MASS}, FROZEN; trace 'mass'. */
export function enterMass(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, why: 'window' | 'peer_lines'): Promise<void>;

// src/engine/resume.ts (R-28 extended; ENG-45, HLD-31; R3-38, R3-39)
export interface ResumeReport { nonretryable: boolean; hostRejectReleased: string | null; massLifted: boolean; }
/** One state CAS while no daemon runs: clears NONRETRYABLE and the streak, releases the host-reject guard of meta.nonretryable.hold_id — or, when that record is the pathless PIN
 *  (R3-11), ends it SUBSUMED with its guards unchanged (host-reject kept, no releaseGuard; its terminal op carries the guard), hostRejectReleased naming it either way — and removes MASS
 *  (keeping snap_pre_freeze); when it lifts MASS it drafts one event{kind: unfreeze, reason_code: MASS} (Plan 2 R-09's freeze/unfreeze pair; Task 8).
 *  When the record meta.nonretryable.hold_id names is already terminal (R1 ended it GONE, a --discard ended it RESOLVED_DISCARD) or missing, resume makes no registry change
 *  (hostRejectReleased null) and still clears meta.nonretryable and the streak in its one state CAS (Task 8).
 *  Transition.keep = view.keep with every entry unchanged (snap_pre_freeze kept). `daemon.js resume` takes daemon.lock for its whole run (R3-02). */
export function resume(core: EngineCore): Promise<ResumeReport>;
/** HLD-31 at the first materializing iteration with snap_pre_freeze kept and MASS unset: revertDetector over diff(snap_pre_freeze, S_disk) as unobserved deltas, the untainted remainder into one human CS, the keep entry dropped in the same update; returns the residue ids, or null when nothing was pending.
 *  Logs the residue as UNFROZEN {reason: 'MASS', residue: [<ids>]} (Plan 2's code for a lifted freeze, log only; Task 8). */
export function massResumeStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<string[] | null>;
/** journal/keep/snap_pre_freeze is kept and MASS is not in freeze_reasons (R3-38's "post-resume batch pending" flag). The predicate and breakerTick's silence while it is true are
 *  Task 8's; iterate() materializing while it is true and publishStep skipping its code part until massResumeStep has run (HLD-31 "before anything publishes afterwards") are Task 12a's. */
export function massResumePending(core: EngineCore): boolean;

// src/engine/csStep.ts (ENG-13 engine side, HLD-34, HLD-36, HLD-40, HLD-67; R3-05, R3-40, R3-41)
/** Queues a signal into the CS table; true when it asks for an out-of-cycle iteration (a boundary, or an edit checkpoint once its paths are 300 ms quiet).
 *  Synchronous (it cannot lstat): Engine.signal (async) waits for the 300 ms quiet period of the hook-named paths before the iteration (01-engine test 55; Task 9). */
export function queueSignal(core: EngineCore, s: CsSignal): boolean;
/** Captures every due group from the materialized S_disk (HLD-40) and settles candidates through EngineDeps.editCheck (HLD-67, trace 'edit_check'), in the iteration's state update with the CS table.
 *  Transition.keep is view.keep with only the 'cs' entry replaced; captures run only when it.mat is non-null. P2-STRANDED-SKIP carried (R3-23): while core.writer.strandedPaths() is
 *  non-empty it returns {captured: [], candidates: []} and journals nothing; closing captures run only when captureDue(core) holds (whatever else made the iteration materialize). */
export function captureStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<{ captured: string[]; candidates: Array<{ csId: string; verdict: EditCheckVerdict['verdict'] }> }>;
/** cs/<enc(b)>.json cache, {state: <state commit oid>, table}, temp file + rename through Fs: written at every membership change; used at restart only when it names the current state commit (HLD-36).
 *  The caller awaits it after every queueSignal that changed seat membership (Engine.signal) and, from Task 12 on, after every iteration that committed state (Task 9). */
export function writeCsCache(core: EngineCore): Promise<void>;
export function readCsCache(core: EngineCore): Promise<CsTable | null>;

// src/engine/resolve.ts (HLD-57, HLD-58, HLD-59, HLD-64 copy-restore, Table 4, DAT-29, DAT-33, DAT-39; R3-02, R3-09, R3-28)
export type ResolveVerb = 'publish' | 'discard' | 'park' | 'restore' | 'handoff';
export interface ResolveOptions { humanEvidence: boolean; actor: 'human' | { seat: string; agent: string | null }; }
export type ClaudeRestoreReason = 'not_owner' | 'binary' | 'quarantine' | 'state';   // R3-54: reasons, never message codes; Plan 5 maps them to 06-claude §3.16.3's MCP codes
export type ResolveResult =
  | { kind: 'done'; code: string; id: string; created: string | null }   // code e.g. HOLD_PARKED, HOLD_RESTORED, RESTORE_MARKERS, HOLD_ALREADY_RESOLVED; created = a copy-restore or collision-capture record
  | { kind: 'refused'; code: ResolveRefusal; params: Record<string, Json> }
  | { kind: 'claude_refused'; reason: ClaudeRestoreReason }              // a seat actor's restore that HLD-59 does not admit (R3-54); nothing changed, nothing written to status or log as a code
  | { kind: 'locked'; paths: string[] };                                  // the writer met a holder without delete sharing: nothing changed
/** Table 4 with HLD-57 (a)–(e) in order: owner (HLD-64 copy-restore exception), board fold first (terminal or accepted → HOLD_ALREADY_RESOLVED), fold-level rev CAS (the op's prev_rev
 *  follows mirrorSync's rule: the rev of r's newest allocated pending op, else the folded rev), writes only through writeLocal, a --restore output observed as a new change (e); the Claude form refused with HOLD_FROZEN under any FREEZE reason.
 *  Combinations Table 4 and 05 §1 leave open (Task 11): --restore of an ON_DISK record → done HOLD_RESTORED, no write; --park of an OFF_DISK record → done HOLD_PARKED, no write; an id neither
 *  the registry nor the fold names → HOLD_NOT_OWNER; a Claude restore outside HLD-59's states → {kind: 'claude_refused', reason} (R3-54); a seat actor with any verb but restore → TypeError (no MCP tool reaches it, SEC-27);
 *  --discard of an ON_DISK CONFLICTED record (a MASK conflict, or a K4 whose TIP write is pending) writes A[p], as PARK does (its step-5 merge conflicts by definition; Table 4's "MASK result" is MASK step 4's A[p]).
 *  An ON_DISK --discard is an operator write like PARK: it commits inflight[r] = {op: 'discard', final: {state: 'RESOLVED_DISCARD'}} before writeLocal(Group) with origin discard:<id>,
 *  and after WRITTEN calls ObservedWrites.record(p, written) and Snapshotter.setEntry(p, written entry), commits R3-60's snap transition, then the final patch with the inflight cleared.
 *  --publish on a secret-scan or filter-failure record → HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN (the D1/D2/D3 classification and the per-path filter re-run are Plan 7's; fail closed). --publish on a generated?
 *  record calls markKeepSyncing before releasing the guard. Copy-restore (HLD-64): the copy is born OFF_DISK in the foreign record's state (PARKED or CONFLICTED) with its kind, paths and guards
 *  (the first through hold, the rest through addGuard; none → a guard-less OFF_DISK PARKED copy), origin 'human', displaced_by null, blobs from the fetched board's held/<id>/ (readHeld), then RESTOREd. */
export function resolve(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, id: string, verb: ResolveVerb, o: ResolveOptions): Promise<ResolveResult>;
/** HLD-59 admissibility of cowork_resolve(id,"restore") for a seat; null = allowed (Plan 5 binds the tool and maps the reason, R3-54). */
export function claudeRestoreRefusal(r: HoldRecord, seat: string, binary: (path: string) => boolean): ClaudeRestoreReason | null;

// src/engine/escalation.ts (HLD-60, HLD-61, HLD-63 primitives; R3-45)
export const REVERT_EVENT_AFTER_MS = 600_000;
export const S6_AFTER_MS = 86_400_000;
/** HLD-60 on the owner's PC: HOLD_CREATED/HOLD_CONFLICTED for every record created since its last call (then HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE {hid, path} again for a binary-path revert PIN, so
 *  status ends on it, R3-70); one event{kind: health, reason_code: HOLD_REVERT_HELD, refs: [hold_id]} per revert record at age ≥ 10 min, unless the fold or the outbox already holds it (INV-ONE-EVENT). */
export function escalationStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<void>;
/** HLD-61 S6 per R3-45: ON_DISK PATCH/PIN, local_only false, not INCOMING, age ≥ 24 h, durable, no open-CS path, no FREEZE, no kept push intent → park (markers → markersPark); HOLD_AUTO_PARKED. */
export function s6Step(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<string[]>;
export interface DurabilityReport { notDurable: string[]; localOnly: string[]; onDisk: string[]; offDisk: string[]; }
export function durabilityReport(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<DurabilityReport>;
/** HLD-63 step 3 for Plan 7's stop: PARK every ON_DISK PATCH/PIN with local_only false, displaced_by = leaveOpId; never called by drain. */
export function parkAllForStop(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, leaveOpId: string): Promise<{ parked: string[]; companions: string[]; failed: string[] }>;
/** HLD-63 --discard-holds: each id through Table 4 --discard (RESOLVED_DISCARD, mirrored; each write attributed with origin discard:<id> and R3-60's snap transition). */
export function discardHolds(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, ids: readonly string[]): Promise<string[]>;

// src/engine/holdSteps.ts (01-engine §3.5 rows D, M, R, T; ENG-14, ENG-28; R3-19, R3-20, R3-46)
/** Step 3 D: transient clocks, breakerTick; repairStep, portabilityStep and churnStep on every iteration with a tick (Task 7's calling rule); on a materialization (it.mat non-null):
 *  massResumeStep, then revertStep, and Plan 2's R-07 eol-bom creation on a canon refusal (its one site moves here) — all before captureStep and any merge (Task 12). */
export function detectStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<{ records: string[]; mass: boolean }>;
/** Step 4 M (materializing iterations only): S_pub := maskEntries(S_disk), conflicted records → CONFLICTED, assertSPub, held ranges; trace 'mask' (with registry), once per materialization;
 *  false = HOLD_MASK_INVALID (EngineCore.maskInvalid set until the next passing assertSPub: no code publish meanwhile, R3-57). */
export function maskStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<boolean>;
/** Step 5 R after the writes — the only R1 call site in the loop: evaluateRecords(apply.changedA, maskStep's `conflicted` ids), then the PREDICATE guards (markers, eol-bom, dependency,
 *  claim; lock/eol-form/local-only-conflict/unmaterializable/unreadable/unstable through incomingStep; t0 stays with publishStep's T0 slot). No re-MASK: S_pub, core.ranges and the
 *  'mask' event change only at the next maskStep (R3-62).
 *  Task 12: the R-07 eol-bom release (a later snapshot passes canon) moves here; markers releases when no path's last materialized S_disk blob has a restore-marker row; claim releases
 *  when EngineDeps.gate.claimStillBlocks(r) answers false; then releaseDependencyGuards (Task 10) and incomingStep (Task 6). */
export function recordStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, apply: ApplyReport): Promise<void>;
/** Timers T: escalationStep, s6Step, the recovered CS's 20 s quiet timer, CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE (300 s after the oldest uncovered effect, once per CS) and CS_GROUP_WAITING (a group with a ready
 *  member unpublished for 10 min, once per group episode), to status and log only (R-27). The seat CS fallback close arrives as the idle_10min signal (Plan 5). */
export function timerStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<void>;
/** HLD-24 status inputs (R3-46): notConverged = Plan 2's not_converged entries, unchanged (P2-CARRY carried paths, push_unresolved, phantom, quarantine review, round-trip exclusions,
 *  pause/FREEZE blocked_publish) ∪ the Plan 3 additions (ON_DISK record paths, deferred and not-applicable paths, R3-53 paths and the groups its gate keeps back, R3-56's
 *  record-less transient exclusions older than 60 s); it extends Plan 2's list, never replaces it. */
export function holdStatusInputs(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Pick<StatusInputs, 'counts' | 'notConverged' | 'diverged' | 'lastEvent'>;

// src/engine/assertions.ts (changed; ENG-41 (i), (j), DAT-46; R3-20)
export interface PrePushInput {
  /* …Plan 2 fields except heldEntriesAdded… */
  masked: (p: string) => boolean;   // withheld whole by a record (MASK steps 2–4 or a step-5 conflict): withheld.has(p) && maskReason(p) !== 'path_class' (Task 2, R3-20); path classes keep
                                    // heldPathInP's 'INV-MASK' letter; step-5 partial paths are assertP's. publishStep computes it itself — a steps 2–4 reason of maskReason(p, inp),
                                    // a 'patch' path that is not mergeable (binary/filtered, R3-18), or a path of a record its own maskEntries runs reported `conflicted` —
                                    // independent of the MaskResult a (possibly stubbed) composeP callback returns (Task 2)
  heldAdded: ReadonlyArray<{ holdId: string; path: string; stage: HeldBlob['stage']; oid: Oid; localOnly: boolean; live: boolean }>;   // (j): non-local_only, LIVE, no restore-marker row unless the blob is in tree(TIP)
}

// src/engine/publish.ts (changed; 01-engine §3.12, Algorithm P, ENG-39…ENG-41, HLD-10, HLD-19, HLD-38…HLD-52; R3-04, R3-07, R3-20, R3-25, R3-43, R3-44)
// publishStep: mirror sync (draft ops for records whose mirror key is new, R3-26) → groups → B1 slot (EngineDeps.gate) → composeP → dependency PINs for withheldWhole (HLD-42)
// → assertP → OUT and conflicts → T0 (t0Shrink + EngineDeps.t0Regions; K7 PIN or open-CS drop, back to compose) → Bnew with ops and held/ (BoardBuild.held) → ENG-41 → push;
// a landed push marks groups published and joins closing CSs to held PINs (bundles). Every composeP whose P tree is built (CAS re-merges and recompositions included) emits one 'compose'
// trace event before ENG-41 (Task 10), except a T0 trial composition (R3-63). The t0 PREDICATE is evaluated in the T0 slot: a group whose only held PIN carries only t0 is composed into a
// trial OUT each window (no 'compose' event); T0 passing releases the guard in a registry commit and P is recomposed (one 'compose' event, the one preceding Bnew), and the group publishes
// in that window (Table 3 "T0 passes on a fresh OUT"; Task 10). EOL_MIXED_HELD {id, path} goes to status and the log the first time an eol-bom record withholds a path of a ready group,
// once per record id (DAT-06; Task 10). The code part is skipped while EngineCore.maskInvalid or massResumePending(core) (both Task 12a) and, for its own window only, after an assertP
// failure (R3-57). The R-18 throttle event's otherPending (pendingCheap) uses the same ready-and-unpublished group predicate as allowedPending; PIN_GUARDS and pinnedPaths are deleted (Task 10).
// PublishReport gains (maskInvalid: Task 2; mirrored: Task 4; dependencyPins, k7, dropped: Task 10):
export interface PublishReport { /* …Plan 2 fields… */ mirrored: string[]; dependencyPins: string[]; k7: string[]; dropped: string[]; maskInvalid: boolean; }
/** Runs first in publishStep and at start and restart (Task 12b): drains Folder.takeHoldRejections() (R3-27), then drafts projectOp(r, prevRev) through Outbox.replaceHold for every record
 *  whose mirrorKey differs from both its folded entry (with the tip's held/<id>/ from heldOfTip) and its pending draft and that local.rejected does not hold with that key, subject to
 *  creationDue and checkMirror; prevRev = the rev of r's newest allocated pending op, else its folded rev, else null. A terminal record gets an op only while the fold holds a non-terminal
 *  entry for its id or the record has a pending or allocated op; a terminal record with neither (released before its creation op was drafted, R3-29, or dropped from the board by PRO-27
 *  compaction) never gets one (R3-26). Before drafting a record, mirrorSync reads (cat-file) each of its heldBlobs; a blob that cannot be read (05 §6 "Registry blob corrupt") leaves the
 *  record undrafted and raises HOLD_MASK_INVALID; the window still sends the other allowed board ops (R3-20; with nothing else pending it pushes nothing, 05 F1).
 *  Bnew's held.put holds heldBlobs(r) of every non-local_only record whose op the take placed and whose heldBlobs is non-empty (no put for the pathless host-reject PIN, R3-56); held.remove
 *  holds every record turned local_only whose held/<id>/ the tip still has, plus holdsCompactedAt(fold at tip, tip height + 1); PrePushInput.heldAdded lists exactly the puts.
 *  PRO-06: while the take withholds a hold creation (withhold = isHoldCreation at the 5,000-record cap), mirrorSync drafts one event{kind: health, reason_code: BOARD_FULL} once per episode
 *  (from the first withheld creation until the folded record_count is below the cap), deduplicated against the fold and the outbox by eventCid (INV-ONE-EVENT); a health event is not
 *  record-creating in 02-protocol §3.1's list, so the cap does not withhold it. HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED and BOARD_FULL go to holdCode and the log (Task 4). */
export function mirrorSync(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<{ drafted: string[]; rejected: string[] }>;
/** The board tip's held/ subtrees, cached by board oid (one readHeldAll per new tip, R3-26; Task 4). */
export function heldOfTip(core: EngineCore, board: Oid): Promise<ReadonlyMap<string, readonly HeldBlob[]>>;
/** The dependency:<id> PREDICATE: releases each such guard whose target record is terminal, no longer withholds a path of the PIN's group (guard set empty and not CONFLICTED), or carries
 *  generated?:exclude; returns the ids it changed. Called by recordStep (Task 12) and by --discard on a generated? record (Task 11). (Task 10) */
export function releaseDependencyGuards(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Promise<string[]>;

// src/engine/status.ts (changed; HLD-24, ENG-57; R3-46)
export interface StatusInputs { /* …Plan 2 fields… */ diverged: { n: number; oldestAgeS: number } | null; noChecksConfigured: boolean; }
// diverged (Task 12): n = non-terminal ON_DISK records with a non-empty guard set, oldestAgeS = the largest ageMs among them ÷ 1,000, rounded down; null when n = 0.
// noChecksConfigured = TeamConfig.checks.checkpoint is empty.
// computeStatus (R3-61): Plan 2's precedence and rungs are kept; in 'ok' the message is BRANCH_REWRITTEN, else HOST_THROTTLED, else lastEvent, else CS_NO_CHECKS_CONFIGURED, else null;
//   in 'degraded' with no specific Plan 2 degraded condition outranking the generic not-converged one, the message is lastEvent (fed by core.holdCode) when set, else
//   HOLD_DIVERGED_REGIONS {n, age} when diverged.n > 0, else Plan 2's degraded code.

// src/engine/loop.ts (changed; 01-engine §3.5, ENG-13; R3-02, R3-05, R3-39, R3-45)
export interface EngineInspection {
  /* …Plan 2 fields… */
  sPub: TreeMap | null; lastP: TreeMap | null; registryHead: Oid | null;
  pendingAgeMs: Record<string, number>; csTable: CsTable;
  mirror: Record<string, { durable: boolean; rejected: MirrorRejectReason | null }>;
  ranges: Record<string, Array<readonly [number, number]>>;
  holdAgeMs: Record<string, number>;   // HoldRegistry.ageMs of every non-terminal record, by id (holdTimersSettled's input, Task 13) (Task 12a)
  restoreOffers: string[];   // this peer's OFF_DISK PARKED record ids, empty until the start's first apply step has run with nothing applicable left (HLD-64); when it first becomes
                             // non-empty after a start the daemon logs HOLD_PARKED {hids, next: '/cowork resolve <id> --restore'} once (Task 12b)
}
// class Engine gains:
//   resolve(id: string, verb: ResolveVerb, o: ResolveOptions): Promise<ResolveResult>;   // between iterations, never overlapping iterate(); fresh fetch first
//   resume(): Promise<ResumeReport>;                                                     // resumeAfterNonretryable() delegates here (returns report.nonretryable)
//   signal(s: CsSignal): Promise<void>;                                                  // queueSignal; an out-of-cycle iteration when asked (ENG-13 (b)/(c)), at most one in flight; resolves after
//                                                                                        // queueSignal, the writeCsCache of a membership-changing signal and, when an out-of-cycle iteration was
//                                                                                        // asked, after that iteration has started (its 300 ms quiet period of the hook-named paths included)
//   durabilityReport(): Promise<DurabilityReport>;
//   parkAllForStop(leaveOpId: string): Promise<{ parked: string[]; companions: string[]; failed: string[] }>;
//   heldRanges(path: string): ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>;                  // HLD-21 (Plan 5's G1)
//   (no unacked(seat): X-13 — HLD-23's unacknowledged items come from Plan 5's TurnItems.unacked, the one source)
//   flowInputs(): EngineFlowInputs;                                                      // Task 18 (X-11): F14's engine inputs, memory only
//   hasShadow(path: string): boolean;                                                    // Task 18 (X-11): stage O9's test, see src/engine/flowInputs.ts
// iterate() order (Task 12a; Plan 2 Task 17's steps kept in place, the hold steps inserted):
//   step-0 gates (the suspend rule, index.lock, observeEpoch/FREEZE(EPOCH), the lease-idle rule)
//   → prepareIteration(core, n, {materialize, forceFetch, holdPaths}) (it already calls observe(); never call it again)
//   → incident branch (enterIncident / keep the incident, status, stop when prepareIteration returns ok:false with why 'incident'; clearIncidentIfHealed)
//   → R-08 branch classification → resolvePushIntent(core, it) while view.intent.kind === 'push' (P2-PUSHINTENT-FIRST)
//   → detectStep → maskStep → captureStep → applyStep → recordStep → publishStep (or, when publishStep is not reached, the iteration fetch's readHttpThrottle signal to the governor)
//   → timerStep → Writer.sweepRing → status + writeCsCache.
//   (Task 2 first changes Plan 2's step-4 call site to the async maskEntries plus one commitMany of its conflicted ids; Task 12a replaces it with maskStep and routes Plan 2's capture
//   call through captureStep.)
//   materialize = ((<a pending edit checkpoint>) || massResumePending(core)) && writer.strandedPaths().length === 0 ? true : 'auto'
//     ('auto' is Plan 2's captureDue(core) or an admissible apply; pending green/tool checkpoints reach it through ChangeSets.closeDue, Task 9);
//   holdPaths = repairDue(core) ∪ the paths of S6 candidates due this loop (12b); writeCsCache(core) after every iteration that committed state (Task 12a).
// drain(): flushAgeAnchors, never parks (HLD-63; Task 12b).

// src/engine/flowInputs.ts (rev 2.5, F14; 10 §3.5.1, UX-33, FEAT-F14-04, -05, -13; roadmap row 3's O2/O3/O7/I2 inputs; X-11; Task 18). Memory only: built from the last
// iteration's in-memory state with no git process, no Fs call, no snapshot, materialization, ref, registry or board write (UX-33). Plan 5's flow handler reads it once per
// request; Plan 7's pure flowEntries consumes it beside Plan 5's ClaudeFlowInputs (seats, activity, PULL_MODE, read sets, the defer tags fence/read_lease/txn/conflicted_leased,
// TXN-open instants, edit checks in flight), which this type does not carry. A field flowEntries needs and this list lacks is added here (Plan 3 reviewer), never by a fallback.
export interface EngineFlowInputs {
  nowMono: number; loopPeriodMs: number; tipHeight: number | null; fetchedAgeMs: number | null;
  frozen: boolean; paused: boolean; B: number;                                                        // any FREEZE reason; any publish pause; the effective budget B_eff
  dirtyPaths: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; firstSeenMono: number }>;                                 // disk ≠ A as of the last snapshot (no newer read)
  incomingPaths: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; writer: PeerId; name: string }>;                       // A ≠ TIP; writer and Cowork-Writer display name kept from the last apply
  pushIntentPaths: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; sinceMono: number }>;                                 // a kept push intent's paths
  exclusions: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; reason: string; firstSeenMono: number; setAtStart: boolean }>;   // snapshot and transient exclusions, first seen this run
  deferred: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; reason: string; firstSeenMono: number; setAtStart: boolean }>;     // apply-side deferrals (TransientClocks)
  notApplicable: ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; reason: NotApplicableReason }>;
  pendingFirstSeenMono: number | null; lastApplyMono: number | null; quietLeftMs: number | null;       // quietLeftMs = ChangeSets.quietLeftMs()
  cs: ReadonlyArray<CsTableEntry & { openAtStart: boolean }>;                                          // the CS table's rows (seat, human, recovered)
  groups: readonly PublicationGroup[]; heldPins: readonly HeldPin[];
  holds: ReadonlyArray<{ id: string; kind: HoldKind; residence: Residence; state: HoldState; guards: readonly string[]; paths: readonly string[];
    cs_ids: readonly string[]; local_only: boolean; ageMs: number }>;                                  // the hold registry's non-terminal rows (incl. INCOMING)
}
export function buildFlowInputs(core: EngineCore): EngineFlowInputs;
/** Stage O9 (10 §3.5.1): true when `path` is a member of an open seat CS that a non-terminal SHADOW record names in its cs_ids. X-11's per-path form (Plan 7's draft names it
 *  hasShadow(csId); X-11 rules the path form). */
export function hasShadow(core: EngineCore, path: string): boolean;
// src/board/governor.ts (Task 18): Governor.waitDetail(): { ms: number; term: 'window' | 'own_spacing' | 'peer_cap' | 'team_guard' | 'in_flight' | 'backoff' } — read-only (no
//   attempt counted, no anchor, level or timer moved), the first blocking term in FEAT-F14-13's display priority (not mayAttempt's evaluation order); {ms: 0, term: 'window'}
//   when nothing blocks; backoff = ENG-44's transient backoff.

// src/engine/start.ts, restart.ts, recover.ts (changed)
// startDispatch/restart: prov.load — load(tip, tip) at genesis and join right after attachState and before the first apply (an empty journal), load(view.laC, await provRebuildFrom(core))
// at a restart right after attachState and before resolveWriteIntent (Task 8, R3-59); attachCsTable after every attachState (Task 12a, R3-69); settleHoldOps on both restart paths (Task 5);
// (Task 12b:) registry load before the first publish (HLD-01, HLD-64), captureLocalWork through captureStep (R3-40), mirrorSync re-mirrors non-durable records,
// PARKED records offered for RESTORE (EngineInspection.restoreOffers) only after TIP has been applied; restart/lost-state 'restart' records mirrored like any record.
// recover re-anchor (Task 4): the outbox the re-anchor rebuilds drops every `hold` draft of the old epoch, and the hold mirrors re-drafted from the registry against the folded rev at V
// (projectOp, new epoch) go into the same lostStateRebuild(…, metaOverrides).outbox, so one state update carries them (PRO-25; Plan 2's 5-parameter lostStateRebuild).

// src/daemon/run.ts (changed; R3-02, R3-39)
export type DaemonCommand = 'run' | 'recover' | 'resume' | 'resolve';
export interface DaemonArgs { /* …Plan 2 fields… */ id: string | null; verb: Exclude<ResolveVerb, 'handoff'> | null; }
// `node dist/daemon.js resolve --repo <p> --branch <b> --id <hold_id> --publish|--discard|--park|--restore`: takes daemon.lock exclusively for its whole run (R-26's dead-pid
// replacement included; a live holder → refusal, exit 1), runs restart(core) first (intent resolution, own leftovers, settleHoldOps; StartRefused mapped as Plan 2 Task 17's run maps it),
// then one fresh fetch and fold, Engine.resolve with humanEvidence = true, exit 0 done (HOLD_ALREADY_RESOLVED included), 1 refusal or lock (code + next command), 2 internal error (R3-02).
// `resume` now also lifts MASS and releases host-reject (ResumeReport printed); it also takes daemon.lock exclusively for its whole run (it writes the registry too).
// Task 10 binds plan3Ports() into the production EngineDeps here (and test/sim/engineAdapter.ts, tools/real/two-clone.ts and both fixtures the same), so the build stays green
// before Tasks 11 and 13 edit these files again.
```

### Test helpers, SIM and tools

```ts

//   runSim copies platform into PeerOptions.platform. afterEveryOp oracles also run after every quiescence round and every one of the 40 extra rounds (Task 13; Plan 2's six
//   only gain checks). SimOptions.settled?: (ctx: SimContext) => Promise<boolean> — T* is declared only when Plan 2's K-round rule holds and settled answers true; an omitted
//   `settled` defaults to holdTimersSettled (no hold timer due within the next 40 rounds; it answers true at once for a peer that is not a realEngine peer), so every profile and
//   Plan 2's realEngine SIM tests get it (R3-37: edits/faults runs may now hold revert records) (Task 13). Failure artifacts gain one bundle of refs/cowork-local/* per peer (Task 16).

//   per-peer Trace wrapper; the events themselves still carry no time (TST-03). oracleGroup reads it for its 10 s clause.

// test/sim/peers.ts (changed; Task 13)

// test/sim/engineAdapter.ts (changed)
export interface RealEngineOptions { /* …Plan 2 fields… */ platform?: (peerIndex: number) => NodeJS.Platform; }
// realEngine: EngineDeps.platform = opts.platform?.(index) ?? peer.opts.platform ?? process.platform; EngineDeps.editCheck/t0Regions/gate/symbols = the Plan 3 bindings (Task 10, Task 13).
export function realEngineOf(sim: SimEngine): Engine | null;   // the live Engine of a realEngine peer; null before its first step and between a KilledAt and the restart (Task 13)
export function faultFsOf(sim: SimEngine): FaultFs;            // that peer's FaultFs (lock ops, planted faults) (Task 13)

// test/sim/markers.ts (TST-08; R3-48)
export class MarkerModel {
  constructor(run: string);
  mint(peer: string): string;                                                    // CWM_<run>_<n>_<peer>
  place(bytes: Buffer, marker: string, at: number, mode: 'line' | 'suffix'): Buffer;   // keeps BOM, the file's EOL and cp949 bytes intact
  noteEdit(peer: string, path: string, added: readonly string[], removed: readonly string[], informed: boolean, peerHad: (m: string) => boolean, synced?: boolean): void;   // retires only informed removals by a peer whose A or disk held the marker; synced false (default true) registers added markers as exempt (R3-48)
  exempt(): ReadonlyMap<string, { peer: string; path: string }>;                // markers written to a path that was not synced at the write (temp, secret-like without a record, locally excluded, NOT_ADMITTED without a record): no obligation (R3-48)
  noteDiscard(holdId: string, markers: readonly string[]): void;
  live(): ReadonlyMap<string, { peer: string; path: string }>;
  discarded(): ReadonlyMap<string, string>;   // marker → hold id of the RESOLVED_DISCARD that accounted for it (noteDiscard) (Task 13)
  noteLateWrite(marker: string): void;        // the marker was written through a .old handle (write_during_apply 'old_handle')
  lateWrites(): ReadonlySet<string>;          // INV-NOLOSS: such a marker must be in a hold record by the next check
  retired(): ReadonlySet<string>;             // markers retired by an informed removal and not re-added since (R3-48's resurrection clause, R3-64; Task 15)
}
export function markerRun(seed: string): string;        // the seed without characters outside [0-9A-Za-z] ('holds-12' → 'holds12')
export function findMarkers(bytes: Buffer): string[];   // every /CWM_[0-9A-Za-z]+_[0-9]+_[0-9A-Za-z]+/ match in byte order (latin1 scan; markers are ASCII)

// test/sim/generators.ts (changed; 11-testing §3.5 F-HLD-1, F-ED-2…F-ED-5, F-FS-1, F-FS-4, F-FS-5, F-PATH-1, F-PATH-2; R3-47)
export interface GeneratorProfile {   // name gains 'holds'; + steps; the weights key widens (Task 13)
  readonly name: 'edits' | 'faults' | 'holds'; readonly engineWeight: number;
  readonly steps: number;             // op budget run-seeds uses without --steps: edits 40, faults 40, holds 60
  readonly weights: Readonly<Partial<Record<HoldsEditKind | HoldsFaultKind, number>>>;
}
export type HoldsEditKind = EditKind | 'edit_peer_line' | 'stale_save' | 'rewind' | 'formatter' | 'collide_ignored' | 'nonportable_name'
  | 'write_during_apply';             // F-ED-4 (concurrent write during an apply, at the apply.beforePhase2 yield point)
export type HoldsFaultKind = FaultKind | 'lock_share_read' | 'lock_share_none' | 'unlock' | 'age_24h' | 'resolve' | 'resume' | 'gc_prune' | 'nonconforming_push';
// PROFILES gains 'holds'; PROFILES.edits and PROFILES.faults gain steps: 40 and are otherwise unchanged.
export function seedFilesPlan3(random: Random): Record<string, Buffer>;   // seedFilesPlan2 + a cp949 file, a binary file, a 1.3 MB BOM+CRLF file, an ignored-pattern .gitignore, form/flip.txt, fmt/m00.py…fmt/m23.py
export function holdsGenerator(profile: GeneratorProfile, markers: MarkerModel): OpGenerator;
export function editGenerator(profile: GeneratorProfile, markers?: MarkerModel): OpGenerator;   // + markers: every written edit carries one fresh marker (R3-49 runs INV-NOLOSS on every profile)
export interface HoldsOpSpec { kind: HoldsEditKind | HoldsFaultKind; peer: number; params?: Readonly<Record<string, Json>>; }   // params per kind: Task 13 Notes, "Ops"
export type ScriptStep = HoldsOpSpec | { iterate: number } | { rounds: number } | { advance: number };
export function holdsOp(spec: HoldsOpSpec, markers: MarkerModel): SimOp;                        // one catalogue op with fixed parameters (holdsGenerator draws the same ops)
export function scriptedGenerator(steps: readonly ScriptStep[], markers: MarkerModel): OpGenerator;   // one ScriptStep per harness step, then null
export const HOLDS_PEER_PROFILE: (index: number) => { autocrlf: boolean; withFilter: boolean; platform?: NodeJS.Platform };   // p0 plain, p1 autocrlf, p2 platform 'win32'; no filter
export interface HoldsSetup { tuple: SeedTuple; factory: EngineFactory; options: SimOptions; markers: MarkerModel; }
/** The only composer of the `holds` profile (run-seeds, run-shard and every holds SIM test call it; R3-47). */
export function holdsSetup(seed: string, o?: { mutants?: ReadonlySet<MutantId>; weights?: GeneratorProfile['weights']; crashRate?: number;
  steps?: number; peers?: number; seedFiles?: Record<string, Buffer>; script?: readonly ScriptStep[] }): HoldsSetup;

export interface RegistrySnapshot { head: Oid | null; records: ReadonlyMap<string, HoldRecord>; local: Readonly<RegistryLocal> | null; }   // local.json as written (one shape with registry.ts)
export function readRegistry(git: GitRunner, peerDir: string, branch: string, at?: Oid | null): Promise<RegistrySnapshot>;   // refs/cowork-local/<enc(b)>/holds (or commit `at`) via cat-file + parseRecord
export function treeEntries(git: GitRunner, repoDir: string, treeish: string): Promise<Map<string, { mode: string; oid: Oid }>>;   // ls-tree -r -z

export function boardHolds(git: GitRunner, repoDir: string, board: Oid): Promise<{ holds: Record<string, FoldedHold>; held: Map<string, HeldBlob[]> }>;   // Plan 2 Folder + Task 3 readHeld over that repo's objects
export function simScope(git: GitRunner, repoDir: string, epochBase: Oid, records: readonly HoldRecord[]): Promise<(path: string, mode: string, size: number) => ScopeClass>;   // the D5 rows the SIM reaches; throws 'unsupported scope input' on an EPOCH_BASE .gitattributes or a merge driver
export function readStatus(peerDir: string): Promise<StatusJson | null>;   // <gitdir>/cowork/status.json

// test/sim/teamWriter.ts (Task 13) — R3-47's test-only non-conforming writer (I-DAT-13 shape); CoreFixture.pushTeamChange's body moves here

/** One live commit storing the given bytes verbatim (route (c), no canon; null = delete) with Cowork-CS/Cowork-CS-Paths trailers and its board commit, leased; commit dates = wallMs. */
export function teamPush(git: GitRunner, remoteUrl: string, refs: RefNames, w: TeamWriter, changes: Readonly<Record<string, Buffer | null>>, o: { csId?: string; wallMs: number }): Promise<{ board: Oid; live: Oid }>;

// test/sim/oraclesHolds.ts (11-testing §3.4; R3-49, R3-50)
export function oracleMask(): Oracle;          // INV-MASK every loop that materialized or changed A/registry: test-side MASK of S_disk and of each ready unheld group's R equals EngineInspection.sPub / the publish pTree; no masked path differs from A; re-applying ON_DISK records reproduces S_disk
export function oracleGroup(): Oracle;         // INV-GROUP after every apply: per incoming group applicable paths all M_disk or all unchanged; one INCOMING record per closure from 60 s; one per not-applicable path by X's next iteration; ≤ 1 apply per 10 s per worktree (TraceRecorder.monoAt); closures over LIVE paths only
export function oracleReady(): Oracle;         // O-READY
export function oracleAnd(): Oracle;           // O-AND
export function oracleOffDisk(): Oracle;       // O-OFFDISK
export function oracleLiveRecord(): Oracle;    // O-LIVE-RECORD
export function oracleAtomicPub(): Oracle;     // O-ATOMIC-PUB with exceptions (a)–(c), human CSs
export function oracleNoSilent(): Oracle;      // INV-NOSILENT status half: every hold, deferral, not-converged path and MASS in status.json within 2 loops
// Test-side operators (Task 14): file-form `git merge-file -p` on temp files through ctx.git (which adds MERGE_FLAGS); ABSENT = empty file; outputs hashed with
// `hash-object --stdin --no-filters` without -w; never an engine function. Exported for the unit tests.
export function testRebase(git: GitRunner, scratch: string, repoDir: string, r: HoldRecord, A: ReadonlyMap<string, { mode: string; oid: Oid }>): Promise<{ ok: true; h0p: Map<string, Oid | null>; h1p: Map<string, Oid | null> } | { ok: false }>;
export function testMask(git: GitRunner, scratch: string, repoDir: string, X: ReadonlyMap<string, { mode: string; oid: Oid }>, A: ReadonlyMap<string, { mode: string; oid: Oid }>,
  records: readonly HoldRecord[], scopeOf: (path: string, mode: string, size: number) => ScopeClass, binary: (path: string) => boolean): Promise<{ entries: Map<string, { mode: string; oid: Oid } | null>; withheld: Set<string>; partial: Set<string>; conflicted: string[] }>;

// test/sim/oracleNoloss.ts (TST-07, TST-08, 05 §5; R3-48, R3-49)
export function oracleNoloss(markers: MarkerModel, ns?: RefNamespace): Oracle;   // afterEveryOp (incremental) + atQuiescence (full scan)
export function oracleHeldSecret(ns?: RefNamespace): Oracle;                      // no restore-marker row and no local_only held byte in any object of the bare remote
export type NolossLocation = 'live' | 'saved' | 'held' | 'registry' | 'local_only_disk' | 'author_disk' | 'discard';   // 'saved' stays vacuous until Plan 7; 'author_disk' only while the path is in an open CS or a captured unpublished pair of the author (R3-48) (Task 15)
/** The first location holding marker m at this step in Task 15's search order, or null; `cache` carries the per-tree marker index and the pre-mirror windows of R3-48 between calls. */
export function locateMarker(ctx: SimContext, ns: RefNamespace, m: string, owner: { peer: string; path: string }, cache: unknown): Promise<{ at: NolossLocation; detail: string } | null>;

// test/sim/oracles.ts (changed in Task 16 only, so Tasks 14 and 15 never edit the same file)
/** X-112: one options object, so the landed one-argument form stays valid and Plans 4–7 append their keys (claims?: {model: ClaimsModel}, claude?, coord?, lifecycle?) here. */
export interface ExitOracleOptions { holds?: { markers: MarkerModel }; }
export function exitOracles(ns?: RefNamespace, o?: ExitOracleOptions): Oracle[];   // Plan 2's six; with o.holds, plus the ten Plan 3 oracles above
//   no o.holds: [FF, OID, FIRSTPARENT, BASE, PUSHED-IS-MERGE, CONVERGE] (Plan 2, unchanged); with o.holds = {markers}: those six, then oracleMask, oracleGroup, oracleReady, oracleAnd,
//   oracleOffDisk, oracleLiveRecord, oracleAtomicPub, oracleNoSilent, oracleNoloss(markers, ns), oracleHeldSecret(ns) (Task 16)
export function oracleState(): Oracle;   // O-STATE (afterEveryOp): each peer's refs/cowork-local/<enc(b)>/state moves only along its reflog CAS chain (entry k's old = entry k−1's new) and LA_C / LA_B stay on the first-parent chains of their previous values, across KilledAt and restart (Task 16)
export function oracleReach(): Oracle;   // O-REACH (afterEveryOp after a gc, atQuiescence): every oid named by a peer's state commit tree (A, snap, journal/ready/*/{R,A}, t/P, keep/*), its registry tree and every record's blob maps exists (cat-file -e) (Task 16)
```

**CLIs.**
- `tsx tools/sim/run-seeds.ts --profile edits|faults|holds …` (Plan 2's flags unchanged); `npm run sim:exit:holds` = `run-seeds --profile holds --seeds 1000 --parallel 8`. `--steps` defaults to `PROFILES[profile].steps`; every profile runs with a `MarkerModel` and `exitOracles(ns, {holds: {markers}})`; `--mutant <Mnn>` (repeatable, test builds; `RealEngineOptions.mutants`) unless Plan 2's run-seeds already landed a mutant selector, which is then used instead; the report line gains `{profile, steps, kills, kinds: Record<kind, count>}`; `tools/sim/run-shard.ts` takes the same `--profile` (Task 16).
- `npm run test:sim` = `npm test` with `TEST_SIM_FULL=1`: every multi-seed SIM integration test runs all its seeds (the fuzz all 1,000 trials) instead of its first (R3-55); `sim-exit.yml` runs it once per OS (Task 16).
- `node dist/daemon.js resolve --repo <path> --branch <b> --id <hold_id> --publish|--discard|--park|--restore` (R3-02); `node dist/daemon.js resume --repo <path> --branch <b>` now also lifts MASS and releases `host-reject` (R3-39).

**Lint rule added** (`tools/lint/rules.ts`; keyed on Plan 1's `argvOf` and source text, an approximation like L-OBJ-WRITE):

| Rule | Fails on | Source |
|---|---|---|
| L-HOLD-STORES | a string naming `refs/cowork-held/`, `refs/worktree/cowork/`, `cowork/quarantine/` or `cowork/conflicts/` anywhere in `src/`; an argv containing `-U0` or `--unified=0` outside `src/holds/revert.ts`; a `merge-file` argv with `--union` or `--theirs`, or with `--ours` outside the function `revertInverse` in `src/holds/revertCs.ts` (rev 2.5: the two forbidden-list exceptions are `taintOnlyH1()` for `-U0` and `revertInverse()` for `--ours`, glossary §29.3, X-09); the literal `state=wip` or a hold kind `SNAPSHOT`/`TRANSFORM` in `src/`; a `diff`/`merge-file` call in `src/holds/revert.ts` outside the functions `revertDetector` and `taintOnlyH1` | 05 HLD-12, 11-testing §3.10 L-HOLD-STORES, 05 U2 (rev 2.5), glossary §29.3 |

## Tasks

Waves (tasks in one wave touch disjoint files and may run in parallel; every task waits for the waves before it). Every task is gated on R3-01 (merged Plan 2); tasks marked **[P2 contract files]** edit files that exist only as Plan 2 contract when this plan is written.

- Wave A: Task 1
- Wave B: Task 2 ‖ Task 3
- Wave C: Task 4 ‖ Task 5
- Wave D: Task 6 ‖ Task 7
- Wave E: Task 8 ‖ Task 9
- Wave F: Task 10
- Wave G: Task 11
- Wave H1: Task 12a
- Wave H2: Task 12b
- Wave H3: Task 17 (rev 2.5, F10 revert kernel; after Tasks 10, 11 and 12b, whose files it extends)
- Wave H4: Task 18 (rev 2.5, F14 inputs; after Task 12a and Task 17 — both edit `changesets.ts`)
- Wave I: Task 13 (it may run beside Waves H3/H4: its files — `test/sim/*`, `test/sim/teamWriter.ts`, `test/sim/markers.ts`, `test/sim/holdsView.ts`, `pushTeamChange`'s body in `test/helpers/coreFixture.ts` — are in neither Task 17's nor Task 18's list; a task that finds an overlap waits)
- Wave J: Task 14 ‖ Task 15
- Wave K: Task 16
- Gated, outside the order (rev 2.5): Task 19 once the Plan 2 follow-up F04-a (F-P2-1) has merged and Task 12b is done; follow-up 3-V once Plan 8a's Task 6 and its Task 16 `viz:golden` script have merged and Task 12b is done. Neither touches an engine or SIM file, so Task 16's exit artifact stands when they merge after it.

Task 12 is split into Task 12a (loop placement, S_pub, status, checkpoints) and Task 12b (escalation, S6, stop primitives, start/restart, drain); 12b depends on 12a. A reference elsewhere in this plan to "Task 12" (outside the Global Constraints carried from Plan 2, whose task numbers are Plan 2's) means Task 12a, except where it concerns `escalation.ts` (`escalationStep`, `s6Step`, `durabilityReport`, `parkAllForStop`, `discardHolds`), S6, the stop primitives, `drain`, `start.ts`, `restart.ts` or `EngineInspection.restoreOffers`, which are Task 12b's.

Every task: TDD (failing test first, then code), real git against `file://` bare remotes (never mocked), SIM tests deterministic by seed, `npm run ci` green before its commit, one commit per green step ending with the Co-Authored-By line.

Executing tasks and the 2026-10-08 reconciliation: Tasks 1–3 (and Tasks 4, 5 and 7, dispatched the same afternoon) are not rewritten by the reconciliation. Its follow-ups for this plan are F-P3-1 (Task 10's rev 2.5 contract: `last_check`, verdict widenings, `PortsOverride`), F-P3-2 (Task 17), F-P3-3 (Task 18), F-P3-4 (Task 19), F-P3-5 (follow-up 3-V and the `verdicts.test.ts` `hold` flip), F-P3-6 (Task 8's L-HOLD-STORES exception), F-P3-7 (Task 12a without `Engine.unacked`; Task 12b's `HOLD_REVERT_HELD` viewer code), F-P3-8 (Task 16: `exitOracles` options, the X-113 seed rule, the crashpoint catalogue) and F-P3-9 (ledger entries, the controller's); every one of them is a task that has not started or a new task.

### Task 1: Guard table, registry widening and REBASE

**Covers:** A1, A2, A4, A5, A6, A8, A9, A10a, B1, B2, B3, C, C1, D2, D6a, G1, M1a, M2, N, CS-32, CS-40, CS-41, D-25, T-09 (union), X-03 (types); HLD-01…HLD-07, HLD-09 (table), HLD-13, HLD-14; R3-09…R3-14. Depends on: Plan 2 merged through its Task 14 fix round, its Task 15 round 2 and its Task 16 round 1, with feat/p3-holds rebased onto them (R3-01, R3-66; re-baseline check first); also declares every Plan 3 `TraceEvent` variant, the `MutantId` union, the `AttributionPort` members and `EngineCore.holdCode`, so later parallel tasks never edit those shared lines.
