**Tests:**

*Re-baseline (R3-01)*
- `"every Plan 2 name of Tasks 1–14 the Plan 3 contract cites exists with its cited signature"` — a `tsc`-checked file imports each Plan 1/Plan 2 name of the §Interfaces preamble list that Plan 2 Tasks 1–14 produce (and the Plan 2 names this task's Consumes line adds) and assigns each to the type the Plan 2 contract (plus ledger rulings) states; at run time it also asserts that `parseRecord` accepts the pathless `host-reject` PIN of Task 14's fix round (`kind: 'PIN'`, `paths: []`, `origin: 'daemon'`) and refuses `paths: []` with any other guard, and that `test/crashpoints.json` lists `registry.cas`. The names of Plan 2 Tasks 15–20 (`Engine`, `EngineInspection`, `restart`, `lostStateRebuild`, `startDispatch`, `runDaemon`, `DaemonArgs`, `realEngine`, `PROFILES`, `exitOracles`, `makeEngines`, `Engine.resumeAfterNonretryable`) are Task 2's first test, so Task 1 can start once Plan 2's Task 14 has merged (R3-01). A failing assertion stops the task for a controller ruling (R3-01).

*Guard table and record helpers*
- `"GUARD_TABLE is 05-holds Table 3 in row order with class, owner, key and flags"` — exactly these 26 rows, in this order, as `(name, cls, owner, key, wholePath, humanRelease, takesArg)`: `claim P HLD/REG answer – – arg`; `dependency P HLD bundle – – arg`; `t0 P HLD/REG checks`; `unreadable P HLD/ENG unstable`; `unstable P HLD/ENG unstable`; `lock P HLD/ENG incoming`; `unmaterializable P DAT incoming`; `eol-bom P DAT form whole`; `eol-form P DAT form`; `markers P DAT/HLD markers whole`; `local-only-conflict P HLD incoming`; `filter-failure D DAT/SEC local human`; `secret-scan D "SEC (local human, glossary §16)" local human`; `revert D HLD revert human`; `conflict D DAT conflict human`; `claim-breach D DAT/COO conflict human`; `base-move D ENG git`; `join D LIF user human`; `restart D ENG git human`; `phantom D ENG user human`; `host-reject D ENG user`; `portability D DAT user whole`; `generated? D DAT user whole human arg`; `collision D DAT user human arg`; `quarantine-review D "SEC (local human)" user`; `wake-scope D "local human" wake-scope human` (P = PREDICATE, D = DECISION; "human" = `humanRelease`, Table 3 names a human `--publish` among its releases; "arg" = `takesArg`) (C, CS-32, R3-09, HLD-05).
- `"Plan2Guard and PLAN2_GUARD_CLASS are views of GUARD_TABLE"` — the six Plan 2 names keep their Plan 2 classes (`eol-bom` PREDICATE, the rest DECISION) and every Plan 2 owner string equals the table's (R3-09).
- `"makeGuard applies the 05 §2 argument grammar"` — accepts `dependency:01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV`, `claim:abcdefghij234567` (16 lowercase base32), `generated?`, `generated?:exclude`, `collision`, `collision:keep`, `lock`; throws a TypeError for `dependency` (no arg), `dependency:x`, `claim:ABCDEFGHIJ234567`, `generated?:keep`, `collision:take`, `markers:1`, `lock:x`, `snapshot`, `transform`, `` (empty); `guardArg` returns `exclude` for `generated?:exclude` and null for `lock`; each made guard carries the row's class and owner (B2, HLD-05).
- `"a guard name outside Table 3 loads and is unknown"` — `parseRecord` accepts a record whose guard is `{name: 'frobnicate', class: 'DECISION', owner: 'XYZ'}`; `guardRow('frobnicate')` is null and `isUnknownGuard` true; `makeGuard('frobnicate')` throws (A8, HLD-05).
- `"reasonKey follows 05 §2"` — a `local_only` record with `revert` → `local`; guards `[lock, revert]` → `revert`; guards stored as `[conflict, revert]` → `revert` (row 14 precedes row 15, record order ignored); `[markers, eol-bom]` → `form` (row 8 before row 10); `[dependency:<id of a PIN carrying claim:abcdefghij234567>]` → `answer`; `[dependency:<id of a PATCH carrying claim:…>]` (a SHADOW) → `answer`; `[dependency:<id of a revert PATCH>]` and `[dependency:<unknown id>]` → `bundle`; `[]` → null; only `frobnicate` → `user` (unknown names rank after every Table 3 row); fast-check over 500 random subsets of Table 3 names: the key of the lowest-row DECISION guard, else of the lowest-row PREDICATE guard (C1, 05 U7 second sentence).
- `"localOnlyCauses recognises every HLD-03 cause"` — `.env` → `['secret-like']`; `config/secrets.yml` → `['secret-like']`; `src/a.ts` with `filter-failure` → `['filter-failure']`, with `secret-scan` → `['secret-scan']`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (scope QUARANTINED) → `['not-live']`; `.gitignore` (SAVE_ONLY) → `['not-live']`; a path whose scope is `LOCAL_ONLY` only through this record (row 9, its own effect; `otherLocalOnly` false) → `[]`, and the same path `LOCAL_ONLY` through another record's `secret-scan` (`otherLocalOnly` true) → `['not-live']`, so the co-located record mirrors as a stub instead of being rejected by `checkMirror`; `isCompanion = true` over `src/a.ts` → `['marker-companion']`; `src/a.ts` LIVE without guards → `[]`; a two-path record `[.env, src/a.ts]` → `['secret-like']` (A6, HLD-03, R3-12).
- `"hasWholePathGuard: eol-bom, generated?, generated?:exclude, portability, markers and every guard of a local_only record"` — `revert`, `conflict`, `dependency:<id>` alone are not; a `local_only` record with only `revert` is (B3).
- `"TERMINAL_STATES is HOLDS_ONLY_TERMINAL"` — exactly `SUBSUMED, GONE, RESOLVED_PUBLISH, RESOLVED_DISCARD` (B3).
- `"nothing of the 05 §1 deferred list exists"` — type-level: `HoldKind` is exactly `'PATCH' | 'PIN' | 'INCOMING'` (no `SNAPSHOT`, no `TRANSFORM`); `GuardName` has no `override`, `adopt`; runtime: `HoldRegistry.prototype` has no `adopt`, `override`, `export` or `fork` member; `OfferEvent` has no cycle or fork event; `HOLD_CODES` has no `HOLD_NOT_DURABLE` (A10a).

*Codes*
- `"HOLD_CODES is the Plan 3 code list"` — exactly the 38 codes of the §Interfaces comment (including the 4 R3-65 declares for Plans 5–7, and `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY` as `restore()`'s operator-level outcome), in that order, no duplicate, each matching `^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,47}$`, none containing `VIOLATION`, no `HOLD_NOT_DURABLE`, and it contains the 03-data codes `EOL_MIXED_HELD`, `EOL_REPAIRED`, `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE`, `INCOMING_COLLISION`, `INCOMING_UNMATERIALIZABLE`, `RESTORE_MARKERS`, `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY`, `GENERATED_SUSPECT`, `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY` (N, D-25).
- `"NEXT_COMMAND matches the 05 §1 refusal table"` — `HOLD_NOT_OWNER` → `/cowork show <id>`; `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN` → `wait, cowork_request go-first, or --handoff`; `HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE` → `/cowork resolve <id> --publish|--discard`; `HOLD_NOT_PARKABLE` → `—`; `HOLD_RESTORE_CONFLICT` → `/cowork show <id>`; `HOLD_DISK_CHANGED` → `repeat the command`; `HOLD_FROZEN` → `/cowork status`; `STOP_HOLDS_NOT_DURABLE` and `UNINSTALL_HOLDS` → `--discard-holds=<ids> --confirm=<id>`; `HOLD_MASK_INVALID` → `/cowork doctor`; `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY` → `/cowork resolve <id> --restore` (03-data §2); `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY` → `/cowork review <id> --accept` (SEC-33); every `ResolveRefusal` has an entry, and `ResolveRefusal` holds neither `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY` nor `QUARANTINE_HUMAN_ONLY` (type-level; R3-65) (N).

*Offers (HLD-09)*
- `"offerNext implements the HLD-09 table and nothing else"` — over all 5 offers × 11 events: exactly `none+born_leftover`, `returned+born_leftover` → `pending_leftover`; `pending_leftover+{promote, handoff, k6_timer}` → `offered`; `offered+accepted` → `accepted`; `offered+{declined, released, presume_absent, holder_seat_end, request_returned}` → `returned`; `accepted+grant_ended` → `returned`; the other 43 pairs → null (e.g. `none+promote`, `offered+grant_ended`, `accepted+declined`, `returned+accepted`) (G1, CS-40, HLD-09, 05 U5 offer part).
- `"a record carrying wake-scope is not promoted while that guard holds"` — `pending_leftover` with guards `[wake-scope]`: `promote`, `handoff`, `k6_timer` → null; after `releaseGuard(id, 'wake-scope')` the same `promote` → `offered` (G1, HLD-09).
- `"offerEvent writes one rev per legal event and counts declines"` — `offerEvent(id, 'born_leftover')` then `'promote'` then `'declined'`: offers `pending_leftover`, `offered`, `returned`, `declines` 0, 0, 1, three commits; an illegal `offerEvent(id, 'accepted')` on `returned` returns null and leaves the registry head unchanged; `DECLINES_TO_HUMAN` is 2 (G1, CS-40).
- `"an acknowledgment and the automatic re-attempt never touch the record"` — `OfferEvent` has no acknowledgment or re-attempt member; an `offered` record keeps `rev` and `offer` while the test advances the clock 30 min (G1, 05 U5 "re-attempt emits no op").

*REBASE (notation of the Notes)*
- `"REBASE is single-step from the original pair"` — r: `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`; `A = T9{8:eight}` → `{ok: true, h1p: T9{2:two,8:eight}, h0p: T9{8:eight}}`; a second call with `A = T9{8:EIGHT}` gives `T9{2:two,8:EIGHT}` and its `merge-file` argv names `h0`'s and `h1`'s oids, never the first result's (D2, HLD-13, HLD-14).
- `"REBASE reports a conflict on an adjacent-line team edit"` — `A = T9{3:three}` → `{ok: false, conflicted: ['src/t9.txt']}` (rc 1, measured) (D2, HLD-14).
- `"REBASE of a stage record uses (s1, s2)"` — `s1 = T9`, `s2 = T9{2:two}`, `s3 = T9{2:TWO}`, `A = T9{8:eight}` → `h1p = T9{2:two,8:eight}` (D2).
- `"REBASE is cached by (id, rev, A[p])"` — a second `rebase(r, A)` makes no git call (spy runner); a new rev of r, or `A[p]` changed, recomputes; `clear()` forgets every entry (HLD-14).
- `"REBASE outputs are route (c) blobs in the object store"` — `h1p`'s oid equals `hash-object --stdin --no-filters` of its bytes and `cat-file -e` succeeds before `rebase` returns (HLD-03 route (c); R3-19 basis).
- `"binary, filtered and ABSENT sides use the whole-path rule and run no merge-file"` — binary path (NUL at byte 10) `h0 = B0`, `h1 = B1`: `A = B0` → `h1p = B1`; `A = B1` → `h1p = B1`; `A = B2` → conflict; the same for a path with `filter=crypt` at `EPOCH_BASE`; a deletion `h1 = ABSENT` with `A = h0` → `h1p = ABSENT`, with `A ≠ h0` → conflict; a creation `h0 = ABSENT` with `A` ABSENT → `h1p = h1`; the spy runner sees no `merge-file` call in any of these (HLD-13; clarification in Contract additions).
- `"REBASE's base is A[p], never tree(LA_C)[p]"` — a pending path whose state `A[p] = T9` while `tree(LA_C)[p] = T9{8:eight}`: `rebase(r, A)` gives `T9{2:two}`; a test-local operator that takes `tree(LA_C)[p]` gives `T9{2:two,8:eight}` and the test asserts they differ (05 U1 REBASE row, HLD-13).
- `"a merge-file error is not a conflict"` — after the loose object of r's `h1` is overwritten with garbage, `rebase(r, A)` throws `HoldOperatorError` with `op: 'rebase'`, the path and r's id, and caches nothing (contract addition).
- `"converges: equal, REBASE-converging and diverging held sides"` — r: `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`; with `A = T9`: `converges(r, A, {p: T9{2:two}})` true; with `A = T9{8:eight}`: `{p: T9{2:two,8:eight}}` true, `{p: T9{2:TWO,8:eight}}` false; with `A = T9{3:three}` (REBASE conflict) always false (HLD-07, R3-14).
- `"no operator of this task reads claim state"` — a source scan of `rebase.ts`, `registry.ts`, `record.ts`, `offers.ts`, `codes.ts` finds no import of a claim, gate or fence module; a record carrying `claim:abcdefghij234567` rebases to the same `h1p` as the same record without it (D6a, HLD-06).

*Registry: births*
- `"hold() accepts every Table 3 guard with its fixed class and owner, and the INCOMING kind"` — one `hold()` per Table 3 row over `src/g<n>.txt` (PATCH ON_DISK ACTIVE, or PIN for `t0`, `unreadable`, `unstable`, `join`, `phantom`, `host-reject`; INCOMING ON_DISK ACTIVE for `lock`, `unmaterializable`, `eol-form`, `local-only-conflict`, `collision`, `quarantine-review`): each stored record's guard equals `makeGuard(<full guard>)`, where the full guard is the row's name except `claim:abcdefghij234567` and `dependency:<id of a record created first in this test>` (the two rows whose argument is required); a `filter-failure` record with `h1: {}` is accepted (A1, A8, C, CS-32, HLD-05).
- `"hold() refuses an unknown name, a malformed argument, a guard-less ON_DISK birth and an INCOMING record outside ON_DISK ACTIVE"` — each throws a TypeError and the registry head is unchanged (A8, R3-10).
- `"guard-less births are exactly the OFF_DISK PARKED ones"` — a marker companion (`companionOf: r.id`), a ring late-write PATCH, a collision-take capture (`h0` ABSENT) and an HLD-64 copy-restore copy of a guard-less foreign record (origin `human`) are each accepted with `guards: []`; `local().companions` maps the companion to r; the companion is never attached to anything (R3-10, R3-13).
- `"the pathless host-reject PIN is the only empty-path record"` — `hold({kind: 'PIN', paths: [], guard: 'host-reject', origin: 'daemon', cs_ids: [], …})` is accepted; `paths: []` with `phantom`, or a PATCH with `host-reject`, throws (R3-11).
- `"records round-trip every glossary §9 field"` — a record with `offer: 'offered'`, `declines: 1`, `displaced_by: '<P2>/<epoch>/7'`, `peer_authors: [P2]`, two `cs_ids`, stage blobs and guards `[dependency:<ulid>, revert]` serializes byte-identically after `load()`; its JSON key set is exactly glossary §9 (no line range, patch-id, pre/post-image hash or other extra field) (A1, A5, HLD-02).
- `"multi-path records keep per-path blob maps keyed exactly by their paths"` — a 3-path PIN with one ABSENT `h0` side: `h0`/`h1` keys equal the paths, `blobs/` names only the non-ABSENT oids, and the record's id is a ULID from the seeded Random (A5, HLD-02, HLD-04).

*Registry: transitions*
- `"transition() follows Table 1"` — accepted: ON_DISK ACTIVE→CONFLICTED, ON_DISK→OFF_DISK PARKED, ON_DISK CONFLICTED→OFF_DISK CONFLICTED, OFF_DISK PARKED→ON_DISK ACTIVE, any non-terminal→SUBSUMED/GONE/RESOLVED_PUBLISH/RESOLVED_DISCARD (residence set to null by the registry); refused (TypeError, head unchanged): OFF_DISK ACTIVE, ON_DISK PARKED, an INCOMING record to OFF_DISK, and any patch on a terminal record (A2, 05 U5 Table 1 part).
- `"one change, one id: an equal H1 attaches the new guard"` — r (PATCH `revert`, `h1 = X`); `holdOutcome` of the same kind, paths and `h1` with guard `join` → `{id: r.id, created: false, attachedGuard: 'join', conflictedExisting: false}`, r at rev 2 with guards `[revert, join]`, one commit; repeating it → `attachedGuard: null` and no commit (A9, HLD-07, CS-41, R3-14).
- `"a REBASE-converging H1 attaches and a diverging one creates a record"` — r: `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`; with current A `T9{8:eight}`, `hold({h0: A, h1: T9{2:two,8:eight}, guard: 'revert'})` returns r's id; `h1 = T9{2:TWO,8:eight}` returns a new id (A9, HLD-07, R3-14).
- `"a different kind or path set never attaches"` — a PIN over r's path with r's `h1`, and a PATCH over `[p, q]`, each create a record (HLD-07).
- `"an OFF_DISK record attached by an ON_DISK call moves back ON_DISK"` — r OFF_DISK PARKED; an ON_DISK ACTIVE `hold()` with r's kind, paths and `h1` → r ON_DISK ACTIVE with the new guard in one rev (an OFF_DISK CONFLICTED r becomes ON_DISK CONFLICTED); its age restarts (clarification in Contract additions; HLD-07, INV-HLD-OFFDISK).
- `"a K4 on a path set already held transitions the existing record"` — r: ON_DISK ACTIVE PATCH `revert` over `[src/f.py]`; `holdOutcome` of a stage record (`guard: 'conflict'`, `s1, s2, s3`) over `[src/f.py]` → `{id: r.id, conflictedExisting: true}`, r CONFLICTED with the stages as blobs and guards `[revert, conflict]`, one commit; the same K4 while r covers `[src/f.py, src/g.py]` creates a new record (05 P6 registry half, HLD-07, R3-14).
- `"R-02's stage dedupe is kept"` — a second stage call with the same kind, guard names, paths and `s2` but new `s1`/`s3` returns the same id with `s1`/`s3` replaced in one rev (R3-14).
- `"guards compose with AND"` — r with `[revert, join]`: `releaseGuard(r, 'revert')` → guards `[join]` (one rev) and r is still in `maskingRecords()`; `releaseGuard(r, 'join')` → `[]`; `releaseGuard` of a guard r does not carry throws (A9, HLD-07).
- `"U4 guard algebra with an unknown name"` — fast-check, 200 runs: a record with a random non-empty subset of Table 3 names plus `frobnicate`, then a random sequence of `releaseGuard`/`addGuard` calls: the guards are always the initial set plus adds minus releases, `releaseGuard(id, 'frobnicate')` always throws, and the record is in `maskingRecords()` whenever it is ON_DISK with a guard left (05 U4, HLD-05, HLD-07).
- `"a record carrying an unknown guard is never transitioned"` — `transition` with a `state`, `residence`, `guards` or `offer` patch and `offerEvent` throw a TypeError naming `frobnicate`; `flushAgeAnchors` still re-persists its age (HLD-05, R3-09).
- `"local_only is recomputed at every rev"` — `hold({paths: ['.env'], local_only: false, …})` stores `local_only: true`; a `src/a.ts` record gains `true` at the rev that `addGuard(id, 'secret-scan')` writes and `false` at the rev of `releaseGuard(id, 'secret-scan')`; with `scopeOf` switched to report `src/b.txt` SAVE_ONLY, the next transition of a `src/b.txt` record flips it to `true`; a marker companion is `true` at every rev; each flip shows in that rev's `hold` trace event (`localOnly`) (A6, HLD-03, R3-12, 05 U7 recomputation half).
- `"RegistryDeps.scopeOf and A follow the current iteration"` — before any iteration a `hold()` uses the start-time scope (EPOCH_BASE attributes and team config); after `prepareIteration` whose scope reports `src/x.txt` QUARANTINED, a record over it is `local_only`; `converges` inside `holdOutcome` reads `A` of that iteration (R3-12).

*Registry: chain, batch, local.json, age*
- `"commitMany writes several creations and transitions as one registry commit"` — 2 creations + 2 patches → the head advances by exactly one commit whose first parent is the old head, `registry.cas` is hit twice (before and after the CAS), 4 `hold` trace events follow; a refused entry (Table 1) writes nothing at all (A4, HLD-01).
- `"local.json keeps companions, explicit, rejected and inflight across load()"` — `markExplicit(a)`, `markMirrorRejected(b, 'k1', 'validator')`, a `commitMany` with `setInflight: {c: <park op>}` and `clearMirrorRejected(b)` each write one commit with no record rev change; a fresh `HoldRegistry` after `load()` returns the same `local()`; the blob is canonical JSON at the tree root next to `records/` and `blobs/`; a Plan 2 registry tree without `local.json` loads with an empty `local()` (R3-13; `inflight` per Contract additions).
- `"every registry transition is a new commit and the ref moves only by CAS with a reflog"` — after `hold`, `transition`, `commitMany`, `flushAgeAnchors` and `markExplicit`, `rev-list --first-parent` of `refs/cowork-local/<enc(b)>/holds` has one commit per call, the reflog one entry per call, and no ref under `refs/worktree/` exists; a CAS lost to a planted second writer throws `RegistryCasLostError` (A4, HLD-01).
- `"every blob any rev named survives git gc --prune=now"` — five transitions replacing `h1` each time, then `gc --prune=now`: `cat-file -e` succeeds for every `h1` ever named and for every `records/<id>.json` of the chain (A4, HLD-01, O-REACH shape).
- `"crashpoint registry.cas around a commitMany leaves the old or the new registry"` — kill (`KilledAt`) at the first hit: after a fresh `load()` the record set equals the old one; kill at the second hit: equals the new one; never a partial set, never a duplicated id (05 F7 registry part, A4).
- `"ageMs is max(Δwall, mono_accum + Δmono) and restarts on (re)entering ON_DISK"` — VirtualClock: create ON_DISK at wall W, mono 0; at +30 s `ageMs` = 30,000; a state transition at +40 s persists `mono_accum_ms` 40,000 with `since_rev` unchanged; a new registry (restart) with wall W + 60 s and mono 0 reads 60,000; with wall moved back 1 h it reads 40,000 (INV-CLOCK); OFF_DISK→ON_DISK at rev n sets `since_rev = n`, `mono_accum_ms = 0`, `anchor_wall_ms` = the wall at that transition (B1, 05 §2).
- `"flushAgeAnchors re-persists accumulated Δmono in one commit"` — two ON_DISK records aged 50 s and one terminal record → one commit, both records at rev + 1 with `mono_accum_ms` 50,000, returns 2; called again at once → returns 0 and writes nothing (B1).
- `"every creation and transition emits the widened hold trace event"` — fields `holdKind` (`INCOMING` included), `guard` (first guard or `''`), `guards`, `paths`, `residence` (null for terminal), `state`, `rev`, `localOnly` (X-03).

*Queries, types, core*
- `"byPath, companionsOf, isCompanion, local and head"` — `byPath('src/a.txt')` lists the non-terminal records naming it in ULID order (terminal ones excluded); `companionsOf(r)` lists r's marker companions; `isCompanion` is true only for them; `head()` equals the ref; every returned record is deeply frozen (M1a).
- `"the save-view facts come from registry queries"` — `nonTerminal()` split by `residence` gives the ON_DISK and OFF_DISK lists a save report needs, with each record's id, kind, paths, guards and origin; no query writes a commit (M2).
- `"TraceEvent declares every Plan 3 variant"` — a type-checked test builds one event of each kind `hold`, `apply` (with `groups`, `notApplicable`, `mDisk`), `mask` (with `registry`), `compose`, `mirror`, `discard`, `repair`, `form_violation`, `edit_check`, `mass`; `TraceRecorder.ofKind` returns each; Plan 2's `apply` emit now carries `groups: []`, `notApplicable: []`, `mDisk: null` (X-03).
- `"MutantId and AttributionPort widen"` — `MutantId` accepts `M19`, `M25`, `M28`, `M33`, `M38`, `M39` beside Plan 2's seven; `humanOnlyAttribution` answers `removalAttributed` false, `shrinkAttributed` true, `observedEdit` false, `hookAttributed` false (T-09 union).
- `"EngineCore gains rebase and holdCode"` — `createEngineCore` builds `rebase` with `mergeable(p) = pathIsText(p) && !pathIsFiltered(p)` and the registry with that cache; `holdCode` is null after build (X-03).
- `"Plan 2 hold call sites keep working with the widened registry"` — the Plan 2 Tasks 1–14 suites for the apply-side and publish-side K4, the `phantom` PIN, the ring late-write PATCH and the `host-reject` PIN pass; only their expected `local_only` comes from the registry now; the `eol-bom` record suite (Plan 2 Task 17, `loop.ts`) is Task 2's re-baseline (A8, R3-12, R3-66).

**Crashpoints:** none new; every registry commit of this task (`hold`, `transition`, `commitMany`, `flushAgeAnchors`, `markExplicit`, `markMirrorRejected`, `clearMirrorRejected`, `offerEvent`, `addGuard`, `releaseGuard`) passes the existing `registry.cas` call sites (immediately before and after the CAS `update-ref`). **Lint:** none (L-HOLD-STORES is Task 8; L-OBJ-WRITE already allows `repo.hashBytes`).

**Notes:**
- Test notation used by Tasks 1, 2 and 5: `T9` is the 9-line LF text `1\n2\n3\n4\n5\n6\n7\n8\n9\n` at `src/t9.txt`; `T9{2:two,6:six}` is `T9` with line 2 replaced by `two` and line 6 by `six`; `T9+{4:x1,x2}` inserts `x1\nx2\n` after line 4; `T9-{3}` deletes line 3. Edits that must merge are ≥ 2 unchanged lines apart (Global Constraints); `{2:…}` with `{3:…}` is the adjacent-line conflict. `P1`, `P2` are the peers of `makeCores`. `B0`…`B3` are distinct 64-byte binary blobs with a NUL at byte 10.
- `hold()` resolves `local_only` itself; `NewRecord.local_only` is accepted and ignored (R3-12). `scopeOf()` is the scope function of the newest prepared `IterationCtx`, else the start-time scope at `EPOCH_BASE`; `localOnlyCauses` treats a `LOCAL_ONLY` class as LIVE only when this record itself is the path's `local_only` cause — the registry passes `otherLocalOnly(p)` = another non-terminal ON_DISK `local_only` record (id ≠ r.id) covers p, and a `LOCAL_ONLY` path caused by another record counts as `'not-live'`.
- HLD-07 attach order: the stage-record branch (R-02 dedupe, then K4 onto an ON_DISK PATCH/PIN of the same path set), then equal/converging non-terminal records of the same kind and path set in ULID order (the first match wins), then a new record. A guard-less call attaches only by Plan 2's exact dedupe (same kind, paths and held side, no guards) and never when `companionOf` is set.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — `HoldOperatorError`; the whole-path REBASE rule on `RebaseCache` (R3-18); `InflightOp`, `RegistryLocal.inflight` and `commitMany(…, local?)`; `isSecretLike`; the rank of unknown guard names in `reasonKey`; the OFF_DISK → ON_DISK attach on `holdOutcome`.

**Covers:** C2, D1, D3, D8, I1, CS-19, CS-51, D-04, D-05, D-06, D-21; HLD-15, HLD-19, HLD-21, DAT-20, DAT-23, DAT-35, ENG-41 (i); TC-HLD-02 (step-5 half); mutant sites M19, M25, M28; R3-18…R3-21, R3-57. - Test: `test/unit/p3-baseline-engine.test.ts`, `test/integration/mask.p3.test.ts`, `test/integration/mask.bomcrlf.test.ts`, `test/integration/mask.assert.test.ts`, `test/integration/heldRanges.test.ts`, `test/integration/publish.mask.test.ts`, `test/integration/scope.localOnly.test.ts`, `test/unit/mask.prop.test.ts`

**Spec:** glossary §3 MASK, `S_pub`, P, held ranges, §21 INV-MASK; 05-holds §2 (held ranges, whole-path guard), §3.4 preamble and item 2, HLD-15, HLD-19, HLD-21, §6 row "Registry blob corrupt"; 03-data §2 (LOCAL_ONLY), §3.6 (D5 row 9), DAT-20, DAT-23, DAT-31, DAT-35; 01-engine ENG-28, ENG-41 (i); 11-testing §3.9 rows M19, M25, M28; rulings R3-18…R3-21.

**Tests:**

*Re-baseline, second half (R3-01)*
- `"every Plan 2 name of Tasks 15–20 the Plan 3 contract cites exists with its cited signature"` — a `tsc`-checked file imports `Engine`, `EngineInspection`, `restart`, `lostStateRebuild`, `startDispatch`, `runDaemon`, `DaemonArgs`, `realEngine`, `PROFILES`, `GeneratorProfile`, `Oracle`, `SimContext`, `exitOracles`, `makeEngines` and the other preamble names Plan 2 Tasks 15–20 produce, and assigns each to its contract type (`contract.md`'s text where it differs from the plan doc, header); at run time it asserts that `Engine.prototype.resumeAfterNonretryable` exists, and Plan 2 Task 17's `eol-bom` record suite passes with the widened registry (R3-66). The implementer also greps the merged Plan 2 Tasks 15–20 code and tests for `AttributionPort`, `RegistryDeps`, `DaemonArgs`, `StatusInputs`, `EngineInspection` and `ChangeSets` constructor literals and lists in the ledger, per literal, the Plan 3 task whose file edit widens it (Task 1 `AttributionPort`/`RegistryDeps`, Task 11 `DaemonArgs`, Task 12a `StatusInputs`/`EngineInspection`); a literal no task's file list covers stops the task for a controller ruling. A failing assertion stops the task for a controller ruling (R3-01).

*MASK steps (notation of Task 1's Notes; r is an ON_DISK ACTIVE PATCH `revert` unless stated)*
- `"steps 1–4 keep Plan 2's order and results through the async maskEntries"` — Plan 2's cases (path classes, ON_DISK `local_only`, whole-path guard, ON_DISK PIN with guards, ON_DISK CONFLICTED, OFF_DISK `claim-breach`) give the same entries, now from `await maskEntries(repo, X, inp, cache)`, each listed in `withheld` (D3, HLD-15).
- `"step 5 removes only the held change: an edit two lines away publishes"` — `A = T9`, r: `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`; `X = T9{2:two,6:six}` → `entries[p]` is `T9{6:six}` (a route (c) blob), `partial = {p}`, `withheld = {}`, `conflicted = []` (D3, D-04, HLD-15, 05 I1 second half, TC-HLD-02 second half).
- `"step 5 runs every guarded ON_DISK ACTIVE PATCH on p in ULID order from x = X[p]"` — r1 holds line 2, r2 (larger ULID) holds line 8; `X = T9{2:two,5:five,8:eight}` → `T9{5:five}`; the spy runner shows two `merge-file` calls, r1's oids first, the second taking the first output as `<current>` (D3).
- `"a step-5 conflict withholds the whole path and reports the record"` — `X = T9{2:two,3:three}` (an edit next to the held line) → `entries[p] = A[p]`, `withheld` has p, `conflicted = [r.id]`, and the registry head is unchanged (maskEntries writes no registry state) (HLD-15, VERIFIED d2, R3-19).
- `"step 5 uses REBASE: mutant M19 reverts a teammate's identical change"` — `A = T9{2:two}` (the team made the held change), r: `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`, `X = T9{2:two,6:six}`: `maskEntries` gives `T9{2:two,6:six}`; with `o.m19` (step 5 from the unrebased pair) it gives `T9{6:six}`, which differs from A on line 2 (M19, D-04, HLD-13).
- `"step 5 is a reverse merge: mutant M28 corrupts a file after a team insertion"` — `A = n1 n2 n3 + T9` (three lines inserted above line 1), r: `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`, `X = A` with its row 5 (`2`) → `two`: `maskEntries` gives exactly A; with `o.m28` (held rows replaced at their original row numbers) the output differs from A and still contains `two` (M28, D-04).
- `"step 1 resets quarantined paths: mutant M25 does not"` — `.github/workflows/ci.yml` with `X[p] ≠ A[p]` and no record: entries[p] = A[p]; with `o.m25` entries[p] = X[p] (M25, DAT-23, C2).
- `"path classes are not records"` — QUARANTINED `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, SAVE_ONLY `.gitignore`, UNMANAGED `vendor/lib.bin` (`cowork-sync=off`) and a LOCAL_ONLY `.env.local` (row 9) each differ in X and come back as `A[p]`, with no registry record created or required for the first three (C2, DAT-23).
- `"OFF_DISK records and INCOMING records mask nothing; an OFF_DISK claim-breach pins its path"` — an OFF_DISK PARKED revert PATCH, an OFF_DISK CONFLICTED K4 record and an INCOMING `lock` record over p leave `X[p]`; an OFF_DISK record carrying `claim-breach` gives `A[p]` (D3, INV-HLD-OFFDISK).
- `"an ON_DISK local_only record withholds its whole path; an OFF_DISK one masks nothing"` — a `.env` PATCH ON_DISK → `A[p]`, withheld; a marker companion (OFF_DISK PARKED, `local_only`) and a collision-take capture over p → `X[p]` (R3-21, D3 step 2).
- `"binary and filtered PATCH paths are withheld whole and never merged"` — `img/logo.png` (NUL at byte 10) and `secret.cfg` (`filter=crypt` at `EPOCH_BASE`) each with an ON_DISK ACTIVE `revert` PATCH → `A[p]`, listed in `withheld`, `maskReason` `'patch'`; the spy runner sees no `merge-file` naming their oids (R3-18, DAT-20, DAT-35, D-21).
- `"an ABSENT side in step 5 follows the whole-path rule"` — a held deletion (`h1` ABSENT) with `X` lacking p → entries[p] = A[p] (the deletion is withheld); with X holding new bytes at p → conflict, `A[p]`, r in `conflicted`; a held creation (`h0` ABSENT) with `X[p] = h1` and A lacking p → p absent from entries (D3; whole-path rule of Task 1's Contract additions).
- `"maskEntries writes no registry state and only route-(c) step-5 outputs"` — over the cases above the registry head never moves, and every loose object created during the call hashes to its own bytes under `hash-object --stdin --no-filters` (R3-19, D1).
- `"every MASK merge is merge-file -p --object-id with an explicit A-based base"` — every recorded argv starts `merge-file -p --object-id`, carries the MERGE_FLAGS `-c` pairs, has three 40/64-hex oid arguments and no `--ours`, `--union`, `--theirs`, `-X` or path argument (D1).
- `"MASK's base is A[p], never tree(LA_C)[p]"` — a pending path with `A[p] = T9` and `tree(LA_C)[p] = T9{3:three}` (TIP's version, not yet applied), r: `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`, `X = T9{2:two}` → `T9` with `conflicted = []`; a test-local MASK fed `tree(LA_C)` reports r conflicted and outputs `T9{3:three}`, a team version this peer never applied (05 U1 MASK row, D1).
- `"maskReason stays synchronous and names the first step"` — `path_class`, `local_only`, `whole_path_guard`, `pin`, `conflicted`, `'patch'` for a step-5 path (binary included), null otherwise (D3).
- `"a merge-file error in step 5 throws HoldOperatorError"` — r's `h1` loose object overwritten with garbage → `maskEntries` rejects with `HoldOperatorError{op: 'mask'}`; no entry map escapes (05 F1 basis).
- `"Review Focus 5: step 5 on the 1.3 MB BOM+CRLF fixture and a cp949 file"` — a peer with `core.autocrlf=true`, `A` = the fixture blob (Plan 1 `generateBigBomCrlf`, as Plan 2's `apply.bomcrlf` test builds it), r holding row 1000, `X` = A with rows 1000 and 2000 edited: the output differs from A only in row 2000, every other row (BOM in row 1, the CR of every row) is byte-identical to `X`'s; the same with a cp949 file (`b'\xc7\xd1\xb1\xdb'` rows), held row 3, edited row 7; every `merge-file` input and output is a stored-form blob (Review Focus 5, D-04, HLD-13).
- `"property: MASK(S_disk) is A plus every unheld local edit"` — fast-check, 100 runs (05 P1's ≥ 1,000-trial run is Task 14): a 20-line A, 1–4 local edits ≥ 2 lines apart, a random subset held by 1–3 records: the result equals A with exactly the unheld edits, and `assertSPub` passes (D3, D8).

*Held ranges (HLD-21)*
- `"heldRanges lists the stored-form S_disk rows of each step-5 path"` — `X = T9{2:two,6:six}` with r on line 2 → `{p: [[2, 2]]}`; r holding the insertion `T9+{4:x1,x2}` → `[[5, 6]]`; r holding the deletion `T9-{3}` → `[[3, 2]]` (the zero-length range after `S_disk` row 2); a PIN path, a withheld path and an unguarded PATCH give no entry; the loose-object count is unchanged by the call, and `gitCallsOf` over `heldRanges`, `assertSPub` and `assertP` shows no argv containing `-U0` or `--unified=0` (rows computed in-process; L-HOLD-STORES, Task 8) (I1, CS-51, HLD-21).

*HLD-19 assertions (R3-20)*
- `"assertSPub passes on maskEntries' own output"` — every MASK case above → `{ok: true}` (D8, HLD-19).
- `"assertSPub 'reapply' fails when S_pub differs from MASK on a step-5 path, and ignores withheld paths"` — S_pub[p] replaced by `T9{6:SIX}` → `{ok: false, clause: 'reapply', path: p}`; the same replacement on a withheld path → `{ok: true}` (D8, HLD-19).
- `"assertSPub 'revert_line' fails when diff(A, S_pub) carries a line of an ON_DISK revert record's change"` — S_pub[p] := X[p] (the held `two` left in) for a `revert` record → `reapply` holds (re-applying is idempotent) and the result is `{ok: false, clause: 'revert_line'}`; the same unmasked S_pub with a `join` PATCH instead passes `assertSPub` (only `revert` lines are an HLD-19 clause; Task 14's INV-MASK oracle compares S_pub with its own MASK) (D8, HLD-19).
- `"assertP fails on each planted P"` — `.github/workflows/ci.yml` differing from A → `p_class`; the path of an ON_DISK `local_only` record → `p_local_only`; a whole-withheld path → `p_withheld`; a step-5 path whose P blob still carries the held change (the reverse merge of r is clean but changes it) → `p_range`; P == MASK(R) → ok (CS-19, D8, D-05, HLD-19).
- `"ENG-41 (i) keeps meaning withheld whole by a record"` — `prePushAsserts` with `masked(p) = withheld.has(p) && maskReason(p) !== 'path_class'`: a step-5 path whose P differs from A only by an unheld edit passes (i); the same path with the held line in P passes (i) and fails `assertP` `p_range` (ENG-41 (i), D-05, R3-20).
- `"heldPathInP keeps Plan 2's letter split under the Plan 3 binding of masked"` — a quarantined `.claude/settings.json` that differs from A in P (it is in `withheld` by MASK step 1) yields `publish_assert:INV-MASK`, not `i`; the path of an ON_DISK `join` PIN that differs from A in P yields `i`; Plan 2's `publish.test.ts` case at lines 537–558 passes with only its stub made asynchronous (R3-20, ENG-41 (i), HLD-19).

*Publish and loop call sites*
- `"composeP takes the asynchronous mask"` — Plan 2's `composeP` suite runs with `mask = (X) => maskEntries(…)`; `ReadyBaseMismatchError` (M20 shape) is unchanged (R3-19).
- `"MASK conflicts at publish become CONFLICTED in one registry commit"` — a ready human CS over `src/t9.txt` whose R carries an edit adjacent to r's held line: publish transitions r to CONFLICTED in one `commitMany`, P carries `A[p]` for that path and the CS's other paths publish (HLD-15, R3-19).
- `"an assertP failure drops the code part and keeps board-only ops"` — `makeCores` with mutant M28 active: a teammate's commit inserted three lines above line 1 of `src/t9.txt` and was applied, r holds `two` (now row 5), a ready human CS over `src/t9.txt` edits row 11, and a pending `event` op is in the outbox: the positional mask leaves `two` in P, `assertP` fails `p_range`; no live commit lands, the board commit carries the event op only (from Task 4 on: the event op and r's creation `hold` op), `PublishReport.maskInvalid` is true, `core.holdCode.code` and the daemon log carry `HOLD_MASK_INVALID`, and no object reachable from `refs/cowork/*` holds S_disk[p]'s held line (R3-20, R3-57, HLD-19, CS-19, D8).
- `"05 F1: a corrupt registry blob means no push and S_disk never pushed"` — r's `h1` loose object overwritten with garbage, a ready CS over r's path and nothing in the outbox: `publishStep` makes zero push attempts, sets `maskInvalid`, raises `HOLD_MASK_INVALID` to `holdCode` and the log, and the remote's refs are unchanged (05 F1, D8, R3-20).
- `"the loop's step-4 S_pub uses the async MASK"` — `Engine.iterate` on a materializing iteration computes S_pub with `maskEntries` and transitions its `conflicted` records in one `commitMany`; a detection tick that reuses the last S_pub runs no `merge-file` (R3-19; loop call site per Contract additions).

*LOCAL_ONLY binding (R3-21)*
- `"a path of a non-terminal ON_DISK local_only record is LOCAL_ONLY"` — tracked `config/.env.local` (LIVE) with an ON_DISK `local_only` PATCH: `it.scope(p)` is `{cls: 'LOCAL_ONLY', reason: 'secret-like'}`; with a `secret-scan` guard the reason is `secret-scan`, with `filter-failure` it is `filter-failure`; a marker companion (OFF_DISK) and a terminal record leave the path LIVE; a SAVE_ONLY `.gitignore` covered by a `local_only` record stays SAVE_ONLY (one reason per path) (R3-21, D-06, DAT-23, DAT-31).
- `"ConflictCtx.localOnly is true exactly for paths of non-terminal ON_DISK local_only records"` — the apply's conflict context answers true for `config/.env.local` above and false for the OFF_DISK companion's path; the DEFER and its INCOMING record are Task 6's (R3-21, D-06).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** none (L-MERGE-OPTS already requires `--object-id`; L-HOLD-STORES is Task 8).

**Notes:**
- `maskEntries` applies `MaskOptions` only when the engine passes them from `EngineDeps.testMutants` (`m19 ← M19`, `m25 ← M25`, `m28 ← M28`); each flag has exactly one site (R-29 pattern).
- `assertSPub`'s `revert_line` clause and `assertP`'s `p_range` clause are both checked by the reverse merge of the record alone: the tree under test lacks r's held change iff `mf(x, H1'[p], H0'[p])` is clean and returns `x` unchanged. `assertP` checks every path that has held ranges in `ranges` plus every step-5 PATCH path of `inp.records`.
- `S_pub`'s own computation, held-range refresh and the `mask` trace event are wired by Task 12 (`maskStep`); this task only changes the call site Plan 2's loop already has.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — the step-4 `S_pub` call site in `loop.ts` (on `iterate()`; Flags item 2); `ScopeInputs.localOnlyReason`; the zero-length held range `[k + 1, k]` on `heldRanges`; `PublishReport.maskInvalid` (owned here); `PrePushInput.masked` = `withheld.has(p) && maskReason(p) !== 'path_class'` (R3-20); in-process rows for `heldRanges`, `assertSPub` and `assertP`.

### Task 3: Hold ops on the board — typed op, fold effect, held/ subtrees, compaction

**Covers:** A7, A10, F1, F4, F5 (fold side), F9 (classification); PRO-02, PRO-05, PRO-06, PRO-21, PRO-24 (fold reasons), PRO-27, HLD-04, HLD-11; T-PRO-05 and T-PRO-33 fold parts; R3-11, R3-24. Depends on: Task 1. Parallel with Task 2.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/board/ops.ts` (typed `HoldOpGuard`/`HoldOp`; `OpaqueOp` loses `'hold'`; field-by-field parse; `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES` gains `'hold'`), `src/board/fold.ts` (`FoldedHold`, typed `FoldState.holds`, PRO-21 validity and effect, `OwnRejectionReason`, widened `OwnRejection`, `terminal_height`, hold compaction, `FoldStepResult.compactedHolds`, `parseFold` of typed holds), `src/board/boardCommit.ts` (`HeldBlob`, `BoardBuild.held`, `buildHeldTree`, `readHeld`, `readHeldAll`), `test/helpers/foldBoard.ts` (the board generator also emits `hold` ops: valid chains, stale `prev_rev`, non-owner, stub violations), `test/unit/ops.test.ts`, `test/unit/fold.test.ts` (Plan 2's "`hold` folds as a no-op" cases replaced), `test/unit/outbox.test.ts` (the opaque `hold` draft `{type: 'hold', hold_id, paths}` at line 123 becomes a typed `HoldOp` draft with every glossary §7 field; Task 4 edits this file again one wave later)
- Test: `test/unit/ops.hold.test.ts`, `test/unit/fold.hold.test.ts`, `test/unit/fold.hold.prop.test.ts`, `test/integration/boardCommit.held.test.ts`, `test/integration/folder.hold.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 2 `OpType`, `Op`, `OpDraft`, `OpaqueOp`, `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES`, `parseOpLine`, `serializeOps`, `OP_MAX_LINE_BYTES`, `BoardCapError`, `FoldState`, `FoldStepResult`, `foldCommit`, `serializeFold`, `parseFold`, `OwnRejection`, `Folder`, `FoldOutcome`, `CHECKPOINT_EVERY`, `COMPACT_AGE`, `BoardBuild`, `BoardDraft`, `BoardCommitData`, `buildBoardCommit`, `readBoardCommit`, `BOARD_TREE_MAX_BYTES`, `GitRepo.mktree`, `validId`, `validPathSyntax`, `PeerId`, `generateBoard`, `genDraft`, `foldAll`, `honestCheckpoints`, `importBoard`, `makeRepoFixture`; Plan 2 `HoldKind`, `HoldState`, `Offer`, `Residence` (record.ts) and Task 1 `GUARD_TABLE` (in the union-pinning test only: `src/board` imports nothing from `src/holds`).
- Produces: `HoldOpGuard`, `HoldOp`, `Op` (+ `HoldOp`), `OpaqueOp` (without `'hold'`), `OpDraft`, `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES` (+ `'hold'`), `HOLD_OP_KINDS`, `HOLD_OP_STATES`, `HOLD_OP_OFFERS`, `HOLD_OP_RESIDENCES`, `isHoldCreation` (additions); `FoldedHold`, `FoldState.holds: Record<string, FoldedHold>`, `OwnRejectionReason`, `OwnRejection` (+ `holdId`, `foldedRev`), `FoldStepResult.compactedHolds`, `holdsCompactedAt`, `Folder.takeHoldRejections` (additions); `HeldBlob`, `BoardBuild.held`, `buildHeldTree`, `readHeld`, `readHeldAll` (addition).

**Spec:** glossary §3 (board commit, `held/<hold_id>/<stage>/<repo path>`), §7 (`hold` op), §20 (board caps), §21 INV-PRO-FOLD; 02-protocol §2 ("record"), §3.1 (caps, the board tree size), §3.3 (steps 3a–3c and 7, the `hold` row: "Store the latest record; set `terminal_height` when the state becomes terminal"), PRO-02, PRO-05, PRO-06, PRO-21, PRO-24 (fold reasons), PRO-27; 05-holds HLD-04, HLD-11; tests T-PRO-05 and T-PRO-33 (fold parts); rulings R3-11, R3-24.

**Tests:**
- `"parseOpLine types a hold op with exactly the glossary §7 fields"` — a line carrying every §7 field plus `blobs`, `owner`, `declines`, `displaced_by`, `age_anchor` and `text` parses to a `HoldOp` with exactly `id, type, hold_id, rev, prev_rev, kind, residence, state, offer, guards, paths, origin, cs_ids, peer_authors, local_only`; `guards` keep `{name, class, owner}` verbatim, an unknown name `frobnicate` included (F1, PRO-05, R3-24).
- `"parseOpLine refuses a hold op with a malformed field"` — each of these lines parses to null: `hold_id: 'x'`; `rev: 0`; `rev: 1.5`; `prev_rev: 2` with `rev: 2`; `prev_rev: 'a'`; `kind: 'SNAPSHOT'`; `residence: 'IN_RAM'`; `state: 'WIP'`; `offer: 'maybe'`; `paths: ['b.txt', 'a.txt']`; `paths: ['a.txt', 'a.txt']`; `paths: ['docs/12:30-notes.md']`; `cs_ids: ['cs-1']`; `peer_authors: ['ABC']`; a guard with class `MAYBE` or an empty name; `local_only: 'yes'`; `origin: ''` (F1, PRO-21).
- `"paths [] is valid only with a host-reject guard"` — `paths: []` with guards `[{host-reject, DECISION, ENG}]` parses; with `[{phantom, DECISION, ENG}]` or no guard it is null (R3-11).
- `"the board's hold unions equal record.ts's"` — type-level mutual assignability `HoldOp['kind'] ≡ HoldKind`, `HoldOp['state'] ≡ HoldState`, `HoldOp['offer'] ≡ Offer`, `HoldOp['residence'] ≡ Residence | null`, and at run time `HOLD_OP_KINDS`, `HOLD_OP_STATES`, `HOLD_OP_OFFERS`, `HOLD_OP_RESIDENCES` equal the record schema's sets (R3-24).
- `"PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES contains hold and OpaqueOp no longer does"` — a `hold` line now has a fold effect; `claim`, `release`, `request`, `request_status`, `answer`, `note`, `presume_absent` stay opaque no-ops (F4).
- `"serializeOps round-trips a hold op and refuses a hold line over 16,384 bytes"` — a 300-path op whose canonical line is 18 KB throws `BoardCapError` (PRO-06, 02 §3.1).
- `"isHoldCreation is true exactly for hold drafts with prev_rev null"` — create draft true, `prev_rev: 3` false, any non-hold draft false (F9).
- `"the fold stores the owner's latest op and derives owner from the creating op's writer"` — writer P1 creates (rev 1, `prev_rev` null) at height 10, then rev 3 (`prev_rev` 1) at height 11: `holds[id]` holds the rev-3 fields (no `id`, `type`, `prev_rev`) plus `owner: P1`; `record_count` rose by 1 at height 10 only (F4, PRO-21).
- `"T-PRO-05: a hold rev race applies the first op and rejects the second with prev_rev"` — P1's commit at height 12 carries rev 4 `prev_rev` 3 and its commit at height 13 carries rev 5 `prev_rev` 3: the first applies; the second is P1's own rejection `{type: 'hold', reason: 'prev_rev', holdId: id, foldedRev: 4}` (T-PRO-05, PRO-21, PRO-24 fold reasons).
- `"T-PRO-05: a non-owner hold op is a no-op told to its writer"` — P2 writes rev 4 `prev_rev` 3 for P1's record: `holds[id]` unchanged; P2's own rejection `{reason: 'type', holdId: id, foldedRev: 3}`; P1 sees no rejection (T-PRO-05, PRO-21).
- `"T-PRO-05: a local_only op carried with held/<id>/ is rejected local_only_stub"` — `BoardDraft.heldIds` contains id and the op has `local_only: true` → no-op, own rejection `local_only_stub` (T-PRO-05, PRO-21).
- `"T-PRO-05: a mirrored non-terminal record without held/<id>/ is rejected held_subtree"` — `local_only: false`, state ACTIVE, `paths: ['a.txt']`, `heldIds: []` → own rejection `held_subtree`; the same op with state `RESOLVED_DISCARD`, and the pathless `host-reject` PIN, fold valid with no subtree (T-PRO-05, PRO-21, R3-11).
- `"an own hold line that fails parsing is rejected validator with its hold_id"` — P1's own line with `paths: ['docs/12:30-notes.md']` and a valid `hold_id` → `{type: 'hold', reason: 'validator', holdId, foldedRev: <folded rev or null>}`; an unparsable `claim` line keeps Plan 2's `'type'` (PRO-24 fold reasons, F5 fold side).
- `"terminal_height is set by the op that enters a terminal state"` — rev 6 with state `SUBSUMED` at height 20 → `terminal_height: 20`; a non-terminal record has none (F4, PRO-21 effect).
- `"compaction drops terminal holds older than two checkpoints and never a non-terminal one"` — terminal at height 500 is dropped at height 1024 (500 ≤ 512); terminal at 513 is kept at 1024 and dropped at 1280; PARKED, `offer: 'offered'` and ACTIVE records created at height 10 are kept at 1024 and 1280; `compactedHolds` at 1024 is exactly the first id; `record_count` falls by the dropped count (A10, HLD-11, PRO-27).
- `"holdsCompactedAt predicts exactly what foldCommit drops"` — for 200 generated fold states and every height 1…2048, `holdsCompactedAt(s, h)` equals `foldCommit(s, draft at h).compactedHolds`, and is `[]` whenever `h mod 256 ≠ 0` (PRO-27; Contract additions).
- `"T-PRO-33 fold part: holds count toward the 5,000-record cap and compaction relieves it"` — a generated fold with 4,960 events newer than h − 512 and 40 terminal holds whose `terminal_height` ≤ h − 512 (h the next checkpoint height) has `record_count` 5,000; at the next checkpoint height it is 4,960 and `holds` holds none of the 40 (T-PRO-33; writer withholding is Task 4).
- `"INV-PRO-FOLD with holds: genesis and checkpoint folds agree and own rejections are identical"` — the hold-emitting board generator, 3 peers, 600 commits (two compaction heights): folding from genesis and from every honest checkpoint yields byte-identical `state.json`; two Folders of different peers report identical own-rejection sets per writer (F4, PRO-21, INV-PRO-FOLD).
- `"parseFold reads typed holds and refuses a malformed hold entry"` — `parseFold(serializeFold(s))` equals `s` with holds; an entry with `rev: 0`, an extra key, no `owner` or a non-integer `terminal_height` → null (F4).
- `"a local_only stub keeps its paths and no oid"` — the folded entry of a stub op has its `paths` and `local_only: true`, and no field of `FoldedHold` holds an object id (R3-24, F1).
- `"PRO-05: hold oids appear only as held/ entries"` — `HoldOp` and `FoldedHold` have no blob-id or text field (type test); a smuggled `blobs` field is dropped at parse (PRO-05).
- `"buildHeldTree builds held/<id>/<stage>/<repo path> as nested mktree trees"` — `[h0 src/deep/a.ts, h1 src/deep/a.ts, h1 README.md]` → `ls-tree -r` of the result lists exactly `h0/src/deep/a.ts`, `h1/README.md`, `h1/src/deep/a.ts` with their oids; `[]` throws a TypeError (A7, HLD-04, PRO-02).
- `"buildBoardCommit puts, replaces and removes held/<id>/ and carries the rest by oid"` — previous board with `held/A`, `held/B`: `held: {put: {B: new, C: new}, remove: {A}}` → `held/B` (new tree), `held/C`, no `held/A`; the next build without `held` carries `held/B` and `held/C` with identical tree oids; `heldIds` of each read-back commit match (PRO-02, PRO-27 writer side).
- `"readHeld and readHeldAll read back what buildHeldTree wrote"` — `readHeld(repo, board, 'C')` returns the three entries in stage/path order, an id without a subtree returns null, a board without `held/` returns null; `readHeldAll` returns every id's entries from one `ls-tree -r` call (spy runner) (A7; Contract additions).
- `"a board tree holds exactly ops.jsonl, protocol.json, checkpoint/state.json and held/"` — after puts and removes, `ls-tree` of the board commit names nothing else and no path outside `held/` (PRO-02).
- `"the writer refuses held/ subtrees that push the board tree over 5,000,000 bytes"` — puts whose tree objects under `held/` plus `ops.jsonl`, `protocol.json` and `checkpoint/state.json` exceed 5,000,000 bytes throw `BoardCapError` before `commit-tree`; Plan 2's reader rule (`board_too_large` on the top-level `held/` tree object alone) is unchanged (PRO-06).
- `"Folder.takeHoldRejections drains the own hold rejections folded since the last call"` — `foldTo` over three commits with two own hold rejections → `takeHoldRejections()` returns both, oldest first, then `[]`; non-hold rejections stay in `FoldOutcome.ownRejections` only (F5 fold side; Contract additions).
- `"the pathless host-reject PIN op of a board-only NONRETRYABLE folds valid"` — `paths: []`, `cs_ids: []`, origin `daemon`, no `held/` subtree → stored, counted in `record_count` (R3-11).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** none (the `held/` trees are metadata trees built by `mktree` in `src/board/boardCommit.ts`, already allow-listed by R-15).

**Notes:** Changing the `hold` fold effect changes checkpoint bytes, so every peer of a team must run a build with this task (the R-12 note); Plan 2 boards carry no `hold` op, so their checkpoints are unchanged. An own `hold` op that the generic checks of step 3b reject keeps Plan 2's `'generic'` reason (renumbered, PRO-24 first bullet).

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — `HOLD_OP_KINDS`, `HOLD_OP_STATES`, `HOLD_OP_OFFERS`, `HOLD_OP_RESIDENCES`, `isHoldCreation`; `holdsCompactedAt`, `Folder.takeHoldRejections`; `readHeldAll`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `HoldRegistry` (`all`, `nonTerminal`, `get`, `local`, `ageMs`, `transition`, `addGuard`, `releaseGuard`, `markMirrorRejected`, `clearMirrorRejected`, `flushAgeAnchors`), `HoldRecord`, `heldSide`, `MirrorRejectReason`, `HOLD_CODES`, `EngineCore.holdCode`; Task 3 `HoldOp`, `HoldOpGuard`, `FoldedHold`, `HeldBlob`, `BoardBuild.held`, `buildHeldTree`, `readHeldAll`, `holdsCompactedAt`, `isHoldCreation`, `Folder.takeHoldRejections`, `OwnRejection`; Task 2 `PublishReport.maskInvalid`; Plan 2 `Outbox`, `OpDraft`, `PushState`, `allowedInState`, `allowedInAll`, `publishStep`, `publishPending`, `PublishReport`, `prePushAsserts`, `PrePushInput`, `buildBoardCommit`, `readBoardCommit`, `serializeOps`, `OP_MAX_LINE_BYTES`, `EventOp`, `eventCid`, `hasRestoreMarkerRow`, `recover`, `lostStateRebuild`, `Engine`, `makeEngines`, `makeCores`, `createBareRemote` (`rejectNext`), `VirtualClock`, `TraceRecorder`.

**Spec:** glossary §7, §8 (FREEZE row: hold mirrors are board-only ops), §9 (`HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED`, LIVE-only rule), §20 (board caps), §25.2 item 1 (S6 waits for durability), item 9; 02-protocol §2.1 (`HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED` carrier and params), §3.1, §3.3 "Determinism" (re-anchor re-issue), PRO-06, PRO-09, PRO-21 (writer side), PRO-24, PRO-25, PRO-38, PRO-45; 05-holds HLD-10, HLD-62, HLD-64 (re-mirror), §7 F5, F6, B2; 03-data §2 (LIVE-only records), DAT-43, DAT-46; 01-engine ENG-41 (j); tests T-PRO-05 (own-op handling), T-PRO-13 (variant), T-PRO-16, TC-HLD-09 (mirror-rejection half); rulings R3-25…R3-29.

**Tests:**

*Pure (`mirror.ts`, `outbox.ts`, `assertions.ts`)*
- `"projectOp maps a record to the glossary §7 fields"` — a record with `rev` 4, `offer: 'offered'`, `declines` 1, `displaced_by` set, two `cs_ids` and guards `[revert, join]` → `{type: 'hold', hold_id, rev: 4, prev_rev: <given>, kind, residence, state, offer: 'offered', guards (verbatim objects), paths, origin, cs_ids, peer_authors, local_only}`; no `declines`, `displaced_by`, `blobs`, `age_anchor`, `owner`; a terminal record projects `residence: null` (HLD-10, R3-24 basis).
- `"heldBlobs lists every stage of a non-local_only record and never an ABSENT side"` — PATCH `h0 {a.txt: X, b.txt: ABSENT}`, `h1 {a.txt: Y, b.txt: Z}` → `[h0 a.txt X, h1 a.txt Y, h1 b.txt Z]`; a stage record → `s1`, `s2`, `s3` entries; a `local_only` record → `[]` (F3, HLD-04, D-12, D-19).
- `"mirrorKey ignores rev and prev_rev; mirrorWorthy follows R3-25"` — `mirrorWorthy` is true for a creation (`prev = null`), a residence, state, offer or guard-set change, an `h1` or `s2` change, and a `local_only` flip; false for a change of only `age_anchor`, only `declines`, only `displaced_by`, or only `rev` (R3-25, HLD-10).
- `"creationDue waits 60 s only for not-applicable INCOMING records"` — INCOMING `unmaterializable`, `collision`, `quarantine-review`: false at age 59,999 ms, true at 60,000; INCOMING `lock`, `eol-form`, `local-only-conflict` and PINs guarded `unreadable`/`unstable` (created only after their condition persisted 60 s): true at age 0; every other record: true at age 0 (R3-29, HLD-10; clarification in Contract additions).
- `"checkMirror is the PRO-21 writer check"` — a LIVE-path record → ok; a non-`local_only` record naming a path `live()` rejects → `{ok: false, reason: 'local_only_stub'}`; a `join` PIN over 300 paths `src/gen/f<nnn>-<40 × 'x'>.txt` (op line ≈ 18 KB) → `'validator'`; a `local_only` record over a quarantined path → ok (it mirrors as the stub) (PRO-21 writer side, R3-25).
- `"isDurable compares the folded entry and the tip's held/ with the record"` — equal projection and `tipHeld == heldBlobs(r)` → true; folded entry at an older rev with an identical projection (an age flush since) → true; folded `h1`-bearing record whose tip `held/` names another `h1` → false; folded `state` differs → false; `tipHeld` null for a non-`local_only` record with paths → false; the pathless `host-reject` PIN with a folded entry equal to its projection and `tipHeld` null → true; any `local_only` record → false (F6, HLD-62, R3-26).
- `"replaceHold keeps one pending op per record"` — two drafts for hold X before any seq → one entry, the newer; after `take` allocates a seq to it, a third `replaceHold` appends a new entry and `pendingHold(X)` returns that newest one (R3-25, PRO-09).
- `"take withholds the drafts withhold names and pendingAllowed ignores them"` — `withhold = isHoldCreation`: a creation draft stays unallocated while an update draft queued after it is taken (seq order otherwise kept); `pendingAllowed(states, withhold)` is false when only withheld drafts remain (PRO-06; Contract additions).
- `"ENG-41 (j) checks the real held/ additions"` — `heldAdded` entries: of a `local_only` record → fails `j`; on a path whose scope is not LIVE → fails; a blob containing `<<<<<<< cowork:disk` that is not a blob of `tree(TIP)` → fails; a blob whose row is `||||||| cowork:base\r\n` (a CRLF restore-marker row, Review Focus 5) → fails; the same blobs present in `tree(TIP)` → pass (DAT-46); clean entries → pass (ENG-41 (j), D-18, DAT-46).

*Engine (`makeCores`, `makeEngines`)*
- `"a new record is mirrored by the next publish with its held/ subtree and becomes durable"` — a `revert` PATCH on `src/t9.txt` made with `hold()`; the next `publishStep` (P == A, a board-only push) carries one hold op (`prev_rev` null) and `held/<id>/h0/src/t9.txt`, `held/<id>/h1/src/t9.txt`; trace `mirror` `{bnew, holds: [{id, rev: 1, localOnly: false, held: 2}]}`; `PublishReport.mirrored = [id]`; after the next fold `isDurable` is true (F3, F6, HLD-10, HLD-62).
- `"a durable record drafts nothing more; an age flush or a declines change keeps it durable"` — 10 further windows: zero hold ops; `flushAgeAnchors()` (rev + 1) and `transition(r, {declines: 2})` (no other field) draft nothing and `isDurable` stays true (R3-25, R3-26, HLD-10).
- `"a guard release mirrors exactly one op chained on the folded rev"` — `releaseGuard(r, 'join')` on a durable r at folded rev 2 → one op `{rev: 3, prev_rev: 2}` in the next window (F2, HLD-10).
- `"a held-side refresh replaces an unsent draft and chains on a sent one"` — while r's draft has no seq, a transition changing `h1` replaces it (one op in the next Bnew, carrying the newest `held/<id>/`); while r's op is allocated but its push was rejected, the next transition's op has `prev_rev` = that op's `rev` (R3-25).
- `"hold ops ride board-only pushes under FREEZE and the regionSpec VERSION pause and wait under the others"` — `freeze_reasons: ['MASS']` → a board-only push carries the op and no code; push state `version_board_only` → the same; `incident`, `nonretryable`, `offline` and `version_proto` → zero push attempts with the draft still pending, then the op lands in the first window after the state clears (F2, HLD-10, PRO-45).
- `"Review Focus 4: a record created while offline is mirrored in the first window after reconnect"` — fetch failing for 30 virtual minutes: the record is never durable and nothing is pushed; after reconnect the first window's Bnew carries its op and `isDurable` turns true after that fold (F6, Review Focus 4).
- `"05 F6: hold ops rejected '(failed)' twice and then accepted land in the third window with no host-reject record"` — `createBareRemote.rejectNext` twice with the leased ref unchanged; the registry gains no `host-reject` record; the third attempt lands the ops (05 F6, F2).
- `"every stale prev_rev rejection is re-drafted from the folded rev and never makes the record HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED"` — a test-written board commit with P1's writer id and next seq lands an op for r (rev 2, `prev_rev` 1) before P1's own pending op (rev 2, `prev_rev` 1) lands: the fold rejects P1's op `prev_rev` (folded rev 2); the next window carries exactly one re-drafted op `{prev_rev: 2}` (its `mirror` trace event lists r) and the log no line for it; a second forced `prev_rev` rejection (the same planting against the re-drafted op) re-drafts again `{prev_rev: 3}`; `local.rejected` never names r, `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED` is never raised, and r is durable after the third op folds; a planted rejection re-drafts at most once per folded rejection (two windows on the same board tip carry one re-draft) (R3-27, PRO-24, HLD-10, F5 engine side).
- `"a validator rejection is never re-issued and is told once"` — a test-written board commit with P1's writer id and next seq carries an op for r with `paths: ['docs/12:30-notes.md']`: the fold rejects it `validator`; `local.rejected[r] = {key: mirrorKey(r), reason: 'validator'}`; `holdCode` and the log carry `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED {hold_id: r, reason: 'validator'}` exactly once over 10 windows, in which no hold op for r is pushed; r stays ON_DISK with every blob in the registry and `isDurable` false; then `addGuard(r, 'join')` → the next window carries one op for r, and `local.rejected[r]` is cleared once it is durable (05 F5 non-S6 parts, PRO-24, PRO-38, R3-27, TC-HLD-09 mirror-rejection half, T-PRO-05 own-op handling).
- `"a restart does not re-issue a rejected key"` — after the rejection above, a fresh core (restart) runs `mirrorSync`: it drafts nothing for r and raises nothing again (R3-27).
- `"Review Focus 4: a PIN whose op line exceeds 16 KiB is a local rejection"` — the 300-path `join` PIN of `checkMirror`'s test: never in any Bnew, `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED {reason: 'validator'}` once, `local.rejected` set, `isDurable` false after 10 windows (Review Focus 4, R3-25, PRO-21 writer side).
- `"T-PRO-13 variant, durability half: a HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED record stays non-durable at 24 virtual hours"` — the rejected record and a mirrored record of the same age after a 24 h clock jump: `isDurable` false and true respectively (T-PRO-13 variant; the S6 half is Task 12b's).
- `"PRO-06: at 5,000 folded records hold creations are withheld while ending and updating ops land"` — a fold seeded at `record_count` 5,000: a new record's creation op stays in the outbox unallocated; another record's `releaseGuard` op and a terminal op land; `BOARD_FULL` goes to `holdCode` and the log once; `mirrorSync` drafts one `event{kind: health, reason_code: BOARD_FULL}` once for the episode (ten more windows at the cap draft no second one; a fold already holding that event's cid drafts none), and the event is not withheld (not record-creating in 02-protocol §3.1's list); after compaction relieves the cap the creation op lands and a later cap episode drafts a new event (PRO-06, R3-25, INV-ONE-EVENT).
- `"a local_only record mirrors as a content-free stub"` — a `.env` PATCH: its op has `local_only: true`, `paths: ['.env']`, Bnew has no `held/<id>/`, and the fold stores it without rejection (D-19, D-12, PRO-21 writer side).
- `"a record that turns local_only removes its held/<id>/ in the same Bnew"` — `src/a.txt` mirrored with `held/<id>/`, then `addGuard(r, 'secret-scan')`: the Bnew carrying the stub op lists `held.remove` for r and the fold accepts it (no `local_only_stub` rejection) (PRO-21 writer side, D-19).
- `"a K4 record mirrors its stages for a LIVE path and only a stub for a SAVE_ONLY path"` — an apply-side K4 on `src/a.txt` → `held/<id>/s1|s2|s3/src/a.txt`; a K4 on `.gitignore` (SAVE_ONLY) → stub, no `held/` entry (D-12, DAT-43, I-DAT-19 record half).
- `"a held/ blob with a restore-marker row never reaches the remote"` — a record whose `h1` (made with `hold()`) contains `||||||| cowork:base`: `publishStep` stops at letter (j) before any push; no object of the remote contains the row (D-18, DAT-46, ENG-41 (j)).
- `"RESOLVED_DISCARD is mirrored as one op and its held/ subtree stays until compaction"` — `transition(r, {state: 'RESOLVED_DISCARD'})` → one op with that state (a stub for a `local_only` r), and later Bnews carry `held/<id>/` by the same oid (R3-28).
- `"the Bnew at a compaction height removes the held/ subtrees of the holds the fold drops there"` — tip at height 1023 with a terminal hold of `terminal_height` 400: the peer's Bnew at 1024 has no `held/<that id>/`, and that fold's `compactedHolds` names it (PRO-27 writer side, F3).
- `"INCOMING creation ops wait for creationDue and a record released earlier emits nothing"` — an INCOMING `unmaterializable` record: no op at age 30 s, one op at 60 s; an INCOMING `collision` record transitioned to SUBSUMED at age 40 s: zero ops ever (R3-29, HLD-10).
- `"a terminal record with no folded entry and no pending or allocated op is never re-mirrored"` — (a) a terminal hold whose terminal op folded at height ≤ h − 512 is dropped by PRO-27 compaction at h ≡ 0 (mod 256): over 10 further windows and then a fresh core's `mirrorSync` (a restart), zero hold ops for it and `record_count` unchanged; (b) an INCOMING `collision` record ended SUBSUMED at age 40 s, then a restart: the fresh core's `mirrorSync` drafts nothing for it (R3-26, R3-29, INV-CONVERGE zero-push clause, T-PRO-16).
- `"the pathless host-reject PIN's creation and terminal ops carry no held/ put"` — a board-only NONRETRYABLE's pathless PIN: the Bnew carrying its creation op has no `held/<id>/` and `buildHeldTree` is never called with `[]`; after `transition(id, {state: 'SUBSUMED'})` (guards unchanged, as Task 8's `resume` will do) the Bnew carrying the terminal op has none either; both fold without an own rejection and `isDurable` is true after the first (R3-56, R3-11).
- `"a record whose held-side blob cannot be read is not drafted"` — r made with `hold()`, its `h1` loose object overwritten with garbage before the first `mirrorSync`: no hold op for r is drafted, `HOLD_MASK_INVALID` goes to `holdCode` and the log, an `event` op pending in the same window is still pushed board-only, and with nothing else pending the window makes zero push attempts (05 F1, 05 §6 "Registry blob corrupt", R3-20).
- `"HLD-64: a restart re-drafts every non-durable record before the first publish"` — one durable and one never-mirrored record; a fresh core's `mirrorSync` drafts exactly one op, for the second (HLD-64 re-mirror, R3-26).
- `"heldOfTip reads the tip's held/ tree once per board oid"` — two `mirrorSync` calls on the same tip make one `ls-tree -r` (spy); a new tip makes one more (R3-26).
- `"PRO-25: recover re-anchor re-drafts hold mirrors against the folded rev under the new epoch"` — an unlanded hold op of the old epoch; `recover` in re-anchor mode → the outbox written by the rebuild's single state update (`lostStateRebuild(…, metaOverrides)`) holds, per non-durable record, one hold draft whose `prev_rev` is the folded rev on the recovered chain, minted under the new epoch; no old-epoch `hold` draft and no op id of the old epoch remains (F8, PRO-25).
- `"05 B2: hold-related pushes stay at or below mirror-worthy transitions"` — `makeEngines` n = 2, one virtual hour: one permanent `revert` record plus a scripted transition every 5 min alternating `flushAgeAnchors` (not mirror-worthy) and `addGuard`/`releaseGuard` of `join` (6 mirror-worthy); pushes whose Bnew carries a hold op ≤ 7 (creation + 6) (05 B2, HLD-10).
- `"T-PRO-16 Plan 3 part: 90 minutes with one permanent PATCH and no edits give exactly one hold op, then zero pushes"` — `makeEngines` n = 2; the PATCH carries the non-escalating guard `generated?` (made with `hold()`; HLD-60's 10-minute event is for `revert` records only, and T-PRO-16's "one-time escalation event" is Task 12b's `HLD-60 revert row` test), so the test stays exact once Task 12b's escalation runs in `iterate()`; the creation mirror is the only op; after it, zero push attempts for the remaining time (T-PRO-16, PRO-38).

**Crashpoints:** none new (the mirror rides `publish.sent`/`publish.landed` of Plan 2). **Lint:** none.

**Notes:**
- `mirrorSync` runs first in `publishStep` (and Task 12b calls it at start and restart): it drains `Folder.takeHoldRejections()` (R3-27 handling), then for every record whose `mirrorKey` differs from both its folded entry (with the tip's `held/<id>/` from `heldOfTip`) and its pending draft, and is not `local.rejected` with that key, drafts `projectOp(r, prevRev)` through `replaceHold` — `prevRev` = the rev of r's newest allocated pending op, else its folded rev, else null — subject to `creationDue` and `checkMirror`. A terminal record gets an op only while the fold holds a non-terminal entry for its id or the record has a pending or allocated op; a terminal record with neither (released before its creation op was drafted, or compacted away by PRO-27) gets none (R3-26). Before drafting, `mirrorSync` reads each `heldBlobs(r)` blob; an unreadable one leaves r undrafted and raises `HOLD_MASK_INVALID` (05 F1). At the record cap it drafts the `BOARD_FULL` health event once per episode (§Interfaces `mirrorSync`).
- Bnew's `held.put` holds `heldBlobs(r)` of the current registry rev for every non-`local_only` record whose op the take placed and whose `heldBlobs` is non-empty (R3-56); `held.remove` holds every record turned `local_only` whose `held/<id>/` the tip still has, plus `holdsCompactedAt(fold at tip, tip height + 1)`; `heldAdded` for ENG-41 (j) lists exactly the puts.
- `HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED` and `BOARD_FULL` go to `core.holdCode` and the daemon log (R-27); seat delivery is Plan 5's.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — `mirrorSync` and `heldOfTip` (publish.ts); `Outbox.take(…, room.withhold)` and `Outbox.pendingAllowed(…, withhold)`; the `creationDue` rule (now as R3-29 states it); every `prev_rev` rejection re-drafted, never recorded (R3-27 as amended; Flags item 9 withdrawn).
