**Tests (notation of Task 1's Notes):**

*Algorithm R1 (HLD-08)*
- `"R1 is CONFLICTED > SUBSUMED > GONE > ACTIVE"` — r: ON_DISK ACTIVE PATCH `revert`, `h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9{2:two}`: `A = T9{3:three}` → CONFLICTED; `A = T9{2:two}` → SUBSUMED; `A = T9`, `S_disk = T9{6:six}` (the held change undone) → GONE; `A = T9`, `S_disk = T9{2:two,6:six}` → ACTIVE (E1, HLD-08, 05 U5 R1 part).
- `"a PIN is CONFLICTED when REBASE(H1) conflicts and GONE when S_disk equals A on every path"` — `join` PIN `h1 = T9{2:two}` with `A = T9{3:three}` → CONFLICTED; with `S_disk == A` on both of its paths → GONE (E1).
- `"an explicitly released record ends RESOLVED_PUBLISH instead of SUBSUMED"` — the same SUBSUMED case with `explicit = true` → RESOLVED_PUBLISH (E1, HLD-29).
- `"R3-17: a record with no guard left stays ACTIVE until SUBSUMED"` — r after a RESTORE released its last guard (guards `[]`, ON_DISK ACTIVE, in `local.explicit`), `S_disk` still carrying `two`: R1 step 3 uses `mf(S_disk[p], H1'[p], H0'[p])` for r alone → ACTIVE, not GONE; once A carries `two` → RESOLVED_PUBLISH (R3-17, E1).
- `"markers suspends R1 and a marker companion ends GONE once its markers releases"` — a record carrying `markers` → `HELD_BY_MARKERS` whatever A and `S_disk` are; a restored companion (ON_DISK, `markers`) → `HELD_BY_MARKERS`, and after `markers` releases → GONE (E1, D5).
- `"OFF_DISK records are checked only when A[p] changed and are never GONE"` — an OFF_DISK PARKED r over `src/t9.txt`: `evaluateRecords` with `changedA = {}` runs no git command for r (spy); with `changedA = {src/t9.txt}` and `A = T9{2:two}` → SUBSUMED (RESOLVED_PUBLISH when explicit); with `A = T9{8:eight}` → no transition; with the disk equal to A it is never GONE (E2, HLD-08, INV-HLD-OFFDISK).
- `"evaluateRecords writes one commitMany and raises HOLD_CONFLICTED, HOLD_SUBSUMED and HOLD_GONE"` — three ON_DISK records reaching the three verdicts plus one MASK-conflicted id → exactly one registry commit; the log has `HOLD_CONFLICTED {path, author, hid}`, `HOLD_SUBSUMED {hid, path}`, `HOLD_GONE {hid, path}` and the MASK-conflicted record is CONFLICTED; `holdCode` is the newest (HLD-08, E1).
- `"INCOMING records and records carrying an unknown guard are not evaluated"` — an INCOMING `lock` record whose paths applied and a `frobnicate` record that would be SUBSUMED keep their state (HLD-54 delegation, HLD-05).
- `"05 P3 holdcat X2: a conflict record is born OFF_DISK, a later local edit publishes within 2 loops, and --restore yields the HLD-17 result"` — same-line and adjacent-line variants on two `makeCores` peers: the apply-side K4 record is OFF_DISK after the verified write; a local edit 4 lines away is in the remote live tree within 2 test-loop rounds (prepare → apply → evaluateRecords → publish) after its human CS closes (advance 20 s, or `done()`; HLD-35); `restore(r)` writes `mf --diff3 (disk, s1, s2)` (E2, HLD-08, 05 P3).

*K4 displacement (Table 2, R3-22)*
- `"an apply-side K4 record gets displaced_by and peer_authors at its OFF_DISK move"` — P2 lands a same-line change through `pushTeamChange` (`Cowork-Writer: <P2>/<e>/<s>`); P1's K4 record, after the verified TIP write, is OFF_DISK CONFLICTED with `displaced_by = '<P2>/<e>/<s>'` (the newest live commit in `(LA_C, TIP]` that changed p) and `peer_authors = [P2]`, in one rev (A3, R3-22).
- `"a publish-side K4 record gets them at the next apply that writes TIP"` — P1's publish-side K4 record (ON_DISK CONFLICTED) gets the same two fields when P1's next apply writes TIP[p] (A3, R3-22).

*Observed writes, materialization*
- `"ObservedWrites consumes a recorded write exactly once"` — `record('a.txt', X)`; `consume('a.txt', Y)` false; `consume('a.txt', X)` true; again false; `consume('b.txt', X)` false (HLD-26 (b)).
- `"Snapshotter.setEntry sets one private-index entry with update-index --cacheinfo and writes no object"` — after `setEntry('src/a.txt', {mode: '100644', oid: <an existing blob>})`, `ls-files -s` of the private index shows that oid for `src/a.txt` and every other entry unchanged; the git calls between are one `update-index --cacheinfo` with the private `GIT_INDEX_FILE` and no `hash-object` or `add`; the loose-object count is unchanged (R3-34, R3-36, glossary §26.1 W6).
- `"every operator write is recorded as attributed, and a park's also survives a restart"` — after a WRITTEN `park`, `markersPark`, `restore` and `collisionTake`, `core.observed.peek(p, written)` is true for each written path; after the park and the markers park the private-index entry of p equals the written blob, `state:snap`'s entry of p equals it too (one snap-only state commit, before the registry's final commit, R3-60), the next detection tick lists p neither in `changed` nor in `dirty`, and the same holds after `restart(core)` on a fresh core (`ObservedWrites` empty; restart reseeds the private index from `state:snap`) (R3-36, R3-60, ENG-37, 01-engine §3.11).
- `"a kill between the park's snap transition and its final registry commit is completed by settleHoldOps with the snap entry kept"` — kill at the second `registry.cas` hit of the final commit: after `restart(core)` the record is OFF_DISK PARKED, the inflight entry is cleared, `state:snap[p]` is the written blob and the first tick lists p as unchanged (R3-60, HLD-16).
- `"holdPaths materializes exactly those paths and a detection tick writes no object"` — `prepareIteration(core, n, {materialize: 'auto', forceFetch: false, holdPaths: {src/t9.txt}})` → `it.mat.sDiskMap` has an object for p even with no capture or apply due; a later iteration without `holdPaths` and nothing due leaves the loose-object count unchanged (D-24, 01-engine §3.8 (c)).

*PARK (HLD-16)*
- `"PARK of an ACTIVE PATCH writes the step-5 MASK of r alone"` — r holds line 2, `S_disk = T9{2:two,6:six}` → `{kind: 'PARKED', companion: null}`, the disk DISK_EQ `T9{6:six}`, r OFF_DISK PARKED with `displaced_by = o.displacedBy` (null for `--park`), `HOLD_PARKED {path, hid}`; registry commits: one setting `inflight[r]`, one with the final patch and the inflight cleared (D4, HLD-16, H6 operator half).
- `"PARK of a PIN or CONFLICTED record refreshes the held side first and writes A[p]"` — a `join` PIN with `h1 = T9{2:two}` over `S_disk = T9{2:two,6:six}`: the first commit sets `h1 := S_disk[p]` and `inflight[r]` together, the disk becomes `T9`, r OFF_DISK PARKED; an ON_DISK CONFLICTED stage record refreshes `s2` and ends OFF_DISK CONFLICTED (D4, HLD-16).
- `"PARK with refreshMustBeDurable returns REFRESHED and writes nothing"` — the refresh changes `h1` → `{kind: 'REFRESHED', rev}`, disk unchanged, no inflight entry; the same call once that rev is durable parks (Task 12b's S6 decides durability) (D4, HLD-61 basis).
- `"PARK is all or none"` — a two-path PIN whose `b.txt` is held by a share-none holder → `LOCKED`, `a.txt` and `b.txt` byte-identical, r ON_DISK at the refresh rev, inflight cleared (HLD-16, Review Focus 2).
- `"Review Focus 2: PARK under a share-read and under a share-none holder reports LOCKED and changes nothing"` — both `FaultFs.holdLock` modes, `humanEvidence` true and false: `LOCKED`, disk unchanged, residence unchanged (H6 operator half, HLD-16).
- `"PARK refuses local_only and INCOMING records and skips the pathless host-reject PIN"` — `NOT_PARKABLE`, no registry commit (H6 operator half, HLD-16, R3-11).
- `"PARK of a record carrying markers is the markers park"` — `park()` returns `markersPark()`'s outcome and never refreshes the held side (H6 operator half, D5).
- `"PARK under FREEZE writes only with human evidence"` — `freeze_reasons: ['MASS']`: `humanEvidence: false` → `FROZEN`, nothing changes; `true` → PARKED (HLD-16).
- `"PARK, RESTORE and the collision take never write over a kept push intent or a stranded restore"` — `view.intent.kind === 'push'` → `POSTPONED push_intent` (take: `'POSTPONED'`); `Writer.strandedPaths()` non-empty → `POSTPONED stranded`; no registry commit in either case (R3-23).
- `"PARK whose disk changed since S_disk reports MISMATCH and keeps the residence"` — the file edited after materialization → `MISMATCH`, r ON_DISK, inflight cleared; the next loop's snapshot retries (HLD-16).

*Markers park (R3-15)*
- `"the markers park keeps the marker file in a local_only companion and writes A"` — r: a K4 record restored over an overlapping edit (rows on disk, guard `markers`); `markersPark(r)`: commit 1 creates r′ (PATCH, `h0 = A[p]`, `h1 = M[p]` = route (b) `-w` of the staged copy, byte-exact, OFF_DISK PARKED, no guard, `local_only`, `companionOf: r`); `writeLocalGroup(M → A)`; commit 2 releases `markers` on r and moves it OFF_DISK PARKED (an r still CONFLICTED would stay CONFLICTED); r's blobs are unchanged; `local().companions[r′] = r`; returns `{kind: 'PARKED', companion: r′}` (D5, D-17, B4, R3-15, 05 I11 second half).
- `"after a markers park no object reachable from refs/cowork/* contains a restore-marker row"` — `git rev-list --objects refs/cowork/*` then `cat-file -p` of every blob: no row matching `^(<{7}|\|{7}|>{7}) cowork:` (05 I11, TC-HLD-06 markers-park part, I-DAT-12, DAT-46).
- `"a failed markers park ends the companion GONE and leaves r unchanged"` — share-none holder → `LOCKED`: r′ GONE in one commit, r ON_DISK with `markers`, disk unchanged; the same for `MISMATCH` (Review Focus 2, R3-15).
- `"restoring a marker companion writes its rows back and adds markers"` — `restore(r′)` → the disk is byte-equal to M, r′ ON_DISK ACTIVE carrying `markers`, `RESTORE_MARKERS` logged (05 I11, I-DAT-12, D-17, H7 operator half).
- `"settleMarkersParks completes or undoes an interrupted markers park by observation"` — killed at the second `registry.cas` hit of commit 1 → after restart the rows are still on disk → r′ GONE, r unchanged; killed at `apply.group.phase2` (disk = A, commit 2 not written) → after restart DISK_EQ(p, A[p]) → commit 2 is written (`markers` released, r OFF_DISK) (R3-15, 05 F7).

*RESTORE (HLD-17, DAT-45)*
- `"a clean text RESTORE writes the merge and releases the displacement guard"` — K4 record OFF_DISK CONFLICTED `[conflict]`, `s1 = T9`, `s2 = T9{2:two}`, `s3 = A = T9{8:eight}`, disk = A: `merge-file -p --diff3 --object-id -L cowork:disk -L cowork:base -L cowork:held` gives `T9{2:two,8:eight}` → `writeLocal(p, disk, result)`; r ON_DISK ACTIVE with guards `[]`; `local().explicit` has r; `observed.consume(p, result)` is true; `HOLD_RESTORED {hid, path}`; returns `{kind: 'RESTORED', markers: false}` (D6, D-14, HLD-17, DAT-45, HLD-26 (b), H7 operator half).
- `"a conflicted text RESTORE writes cowork: markers and adds markers"` — disk `T9{2:TWO}`: rc 1; the written file has rows starting `<<<<<<< cowork:disk`, `||||||| cowork:base`, `=======`, `>>>>>>> cowork:held`; r gains `markers`; `RESTORE_MARKERS {id, path, rows: [2, 4, 6, 8]}` (the 1-based marker rows); `maskEntries` withholds p; returns `{kind: 'RESTORED', markers: true}` (D6, D-14, HLD-17, R3-16, DAT-45).
- `"Review Focus 5: a conflicted RESTORE on the 1.3 MB BOM+CRLF fixture under core.autocrlf=true writes CRLF marker rows that are detected and never leave this PC"` — a K4 record over the fixture whose local edit and TIP's edit overlap on row 1000: the restore writes `cowork:` marker rows ending in CRLF, `hasRestoreMarkerRow` detects them in the staged copy, `maskEntries` withholds the path, a following `publishStep` composes `P[p] = A[p]`, and `git rev-list --objects refs/cowork/*` plus `cat-file -p` finds no row matching `^(<{7}|\|{7}|>{7}) cowork:` with or without a trailing CR (Review Focus 5, DAT-46, I-DAT-12).
- `"every guard other than the displacement stays"` — an OFF_DISK PARKED `revert` PATCH restored → ON_DISK ACTIVE, guards `[revert]` (HLD-17).
- `"a clean RESTORE over a non-canonical disk writes canon(result, ref(p))"` — `core.autocrlf=true`, a UTF-8 BOM+CRLF file whose disk copy a Write left without its BOM (the DAT-08 repair has not run): the clean merge's raw result lacks the BOM, the written file and the blob `writeLocal` received equal `canon(result, ref(p))` (BOM back, stored form), and every new loose object hashes to itself under `hash-object --stdin --no-filters` (DAT-45 step 1, DAT-01, R3-16).
- `"a restore whose result has a restore-marker row adds markers even at rc 0"` — restoring a companion whose M merges cleanly onto the current disk still adds `markers` (DAT-45).
- `"05 I13 operator half: nothing publishes until the rows are gone, then the edit publishes and the record ends RESOLVED_PUBLISH"` — after the conflicted restore, `publishStep` composes `P[p] = A[p]`; the test removes the rows, checks `hasRestoreMarkerRow(disk)` is false and releases `markers` (standing in for Task 12's predicate step); the next publish lands the edit and `evaluateRecords` ends r RESOLVED_PUBLISH (05 I13, E1, HLD-29).
- `"binary RESTORE is human only and writes the held side whole only over the base or A"` — `s1 = B0`, `s2 = B1`, `A = B2`: disk `B0` → writes `B1`; disk `B2` → writes `B1`; disk `B3` → `{kind: 'REFUSED', code: 'HOLD_RESTORE_CONFLICT'}`, nothing written; `actor: 'claude'` → `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY` before any read (D-16, R3-18, 05 I13 binary, I-DAT-12 binary variants).
- `"deletions and recreations follow 03-data §3.9 cases 2–3"` — `s2` ABSENT, disk D present → `writeLocal(p, D, 'DELETE')`, the file is gone; D ABSENT and `s2` present → `writeLocal(p, ABSENT, s2)` creates it (D-15).
- `"RESTORE waits while the record is ON_DISK with its TIP write pending"` — an ON_DISK CONFLICTED K4 record before its verified write → `{kind: 'POSTPONED', why: 'tip_write_pending'}`, no write (D6, DAT-45).
- `"a Claude RESTORE under FREEZE is refused HOLD_FROZEN; the human verb writes"` — `freeze_reasons: ['MASS']`, `actor: 'claude'` → `HOLD_FROZEN`; `actor: 'human'`, `humanEvidence: true` → RESTORED (D-14, HLD-17).
- `"RESTORE under a share-none holder reports LOCKED and writes nothing"` — r stays OFF_DISK, inflight cleared (Review Focus 2, H7 operator half).
- `"Review Focus 5 / I-DAT-15: RESTORE keeps every byte outside the edited rows"` — `core.autocrlf=true`, the 1.3 MB BOM+CRLF fixture: K4 record with the local edit on row 1000 and TIP's on row 2000, clean restore: every row except 1000 is byte-identical to the disk before (BOM, CRs kept); the same with a cp949 file (rows 3 and 9) (Review Focus 5, I-DAT-15, D-14).
- `"TC-DAT-02 hold and restore leg: a cp949 file and a UTF-16LE working-tree-encoding file round-trip byte-exact through PARK and RESTORE"` — core level (`.gitattributes` at `EPOCH_BASE` naming `w16.txt working-tree-encoding=UTF-16LE`; the `holds` SIM profile carries no `.gitattributes`, Flags item 10): a `revert` PATCH over one edited row of each file, `park(r)` then `restore(r)`; after the restore each working file is byte-identical to its bytes before the park (BOM, encoding and endings included), every `merge-file` input and output is a stored-form blob, and `h0`/`h1` are the stored-form blobs of the staged-copy read (TC-DAT-02 hold/restore leg, D-14, Review Focus 5).

*Collision take (DAT-29 operator)*
- `"collisionTake captures the local file before writing the team version"` — an INCOMING `collision` record c over an ignored `.env` (local bytes L, TIP bytes T): capture k (PATCH, `h0` ABSENT, `h1` = route (b) `-w` of the staged copy, equal to L's clean blob, OFF_DISK PARKED, no guard, `local_only` because `.env` is secret-like, origin `human`) is committed before `writeLocal(.env, L, T)`; returns `'WRITTEN'`; c is unchanged (it ends at the next apply by R3-33) (DAT-29, D-24, D-26).
- `"a collision take whose disk changed keeps the collision record and ends the capture GONE"` — the file rewritten between the read and the writer's pre-image check (`FaultFs.interpose`) → `'HOLD_DISK_CHANGED'`, k GONE, c unchanged; a share-none holder → `'LOCKED'`, the same (Review Focus 2, DAT-29, R3-67).

*Record before displacement and crashes (Review Focus 3)*
- `"every displacing operator holds the bytes in a record before the disk changes"` — `FaultFs.interpose` before the first `.new` of a PIN PARK, a markers park, a collision take and an apply-side K4: at that moment a record of the registry head holds the disk's current clean blob as `h1`/`s2` (D-26, P-DAT-05 operator slice).
- `"Review Focus 3: kill between the registry commit and the disk write, gc --prune=now, restart"` — for PARK, markers park, collision take, RESTORE and the apply-side K4, kill (`KilledAt`) at the second `registry.cas` hit, at `apply.group.phase1` and at `apply.group.phase2`; then `git gc --prune=now` and `restart(core)` (which runs `settleHoldOps`): the registry is the old or the new commit, no record id is lost or duplicated, every blob any record names exists, and each pair is consistent — PARK: (old disk, r ON_DISK) or (target, r OFF_DISK PARKED); RESTORE: (old disk, r OFF_DISK) or (result, r ON_DISK with its final guards); markers park: as R3-15; take: (L, capture GONE, c open) or (T, capture OFF_DISK PARKED holding L); K4: (LOCAL, record ON_DISK CONFLICTED) or (TIP, record OFF_DISK with `displaced_by`) (Review Focus 3, 05 F7, HLD-16, R3-15).
- `"settleHoldOps settles inflight operators by DISK_EQ"` — an `inflight[r]` whose entries all DISK_EQ their targets → the final patch is committed and the entry cleared (with `explicit` for a restore, the snap transition for a park); all DISK_EQ their expected blobs → only cleared; neither → cleared, r left as is, `HOLD_DISK_CHANGED {hid}` logged (contract addition).
- `"settleHoldOps also runs on the lost-state restart path"` — a PARK killed at `apply.group.phase2` (disk = target, inflight set), then the state ref deleted: `restart(core)` takes the lost-state branch, and after `lostStateRebuild` and `attachState` `settleHoldOps` completes the PARK (r OFF_DISK PARKED, inflight cleared) before the first publish (contract addition, 05 F7).
- `"a K4 record moved OFF_DISK by Plan 2's restart decision carries displaced_by and peer_authors"` — an apply-side K4 killed after its TIP write and before the state update: Plan 2's restart decision finds the write complete and moves the record OFF_DISK; the record has `displaced_by = '<P2>/<e>/<s>'` and `peer_authors = [P2]` in that same rev (R3-22).

*Operator discipline*
- `"05 U1: REBASE, MASK, PARK and RESTORE on BOM+CRLF stored-form fixtures; a tree(LA_C) base fails"` — on the fixture with the held row 10, a team row 500 and a local row 900: each operator's output equals the expected stored-form blob; for a pending path (`A[p]` = the old version, `tree(LA_C)[p]` = TIP's unapplied edit next to the held row) a test-local copy of each operator taking `tree(LA_C)[p]` as its base yields a different blob or a spurious conflict (05 U1, HLD-13…HLD-17).
- `"05 B4: no git process names a working file during RESTORE, the markers park or a collision capture"` — spy runner: every file argument is under `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/stage/` or is an oid, `--path=<repo path>` appears only as the attribute path of `hash-object`, and no argv holds the work tree's absolute path of p (handle tracing is Plan 7's STRESS run) (05 B4, D-24, B4).
- `"every Plan 3 disk write goes through writeLocal with its operator's origin"` — the writer receives origins `park:<id>`, `markers_park:<id>`, `restore:<id>`, `take:<id>`; `FaultFs.calls` shows no other write under the work tree during these operators (ENG-37).

**Crashpoints:** none new; the tests kill at `registry.cas` (both call sites), `apply.group.phase1`, `apply.group.mid` (two-path groups) and `apply.group.phase2`, which every operator reaches through `writeLocalGroup`. **Lint:** none.

**Notes:**
- Every operator runs only in an iteration whose `it.mat` covers its paths (`prepareIteration` with `holdPaths`); `park`, `markersPark`, `restore` and `collisionTake` throw a TypeError otherwise (a caller bug, not a runtime outcome). `restore` on an ON_DISK record other than a K4 record whose TIP write is pending also throws (Table 4 applicability is Task 11's).
- PARK and RESTORE commit `inflight[r]` (expected = the pre-image per path, target = the write, final = the closing `HoldPatch`) before `writeLocalGroup`, and the final patch with the inflight entry cleared after `WRITTEN`; any other writer result clears the entry only. `evaluateRecords` is called by tests directly after `applyStep`; its loop call site is Task 12's `recordStep`.
- After `WRITTEN` every operator calls `core.observed.record(p, written)` for each written path; `park` and `markersPark` then call `core.snapshot.setEntry(p, written entry)` and commit the snap-only state transition `core.commit({snap: await editTreeIndexOnly(repo, fs, view.snap, edits, scratch index)})` before the registry's final commit (R3-36, R3-60). `restore`'s clean result is `canon(result, ref(p))` (R3-16, DAT-45 step 1); `restore` and `collisionTake` set no private-index entry and commit no snap transition.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — `settleHoldOps` (restart's settlement entry point on both restart paths; `settleMarkersParks` is its first step); the operator origins passed to `writeLocal`; a failed collision take ends its capture GONE (on `collisionTake`, R3-67); the attributed-write recording, `Snapshotter.setEntry` and the park family's snap transition (R3-36, R3-60); RESTORE's `canon` of a clean result.

### Task 6: Receive side — INCOMING records, 60 s transient records, formCheckIncoming, local-only conflicts, collisions

**Covers:** J1, K1, K2, K3, K4, K5, K6, K7, CS-43, CS-44, CS-50, D-02, D-03, D-08, D-11, D-13, D-22; HLD-32, HLD-53…HLD-55, DAT-11, DAT-29, DAT-33 (receive), SEC-32 (record); 05 I5, I6, I14, B1, TC-ENG-06, TC-ENG-15, TC-ENG-16, TC-HLD-05, I-DAT-08 (record half), I-DAT-13, P-DAT-02 (DEFER leg); mutant sites M38, M39; R3-29…R3-33, R3-56. Depends on: Tasks 1, 2, 4, 5. Parallel with Task 7.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/holds/incoming.ts`, `src/data/formCheck.ts`
- Modify: `src/engine/apply.ts` (`ApplyReport.closures`, regrouped per deferred write group with `closureReason`, R3-31; `ApplyReport.notApplicableRules`; `ApplyReport.incoming` (this apply's `IncomingCs` list, `IncomingInputs.csOf`'s source); `formCheckIncoming` before the merge, violating paths deferred `eol_form`; D8 step 0's DEFER deferred `local_only_conflict`; R3-33 `disk_equal` creations in the not-applicable check; mutant sites M38 and M39; the `apply` trace's `groups` (group-level `reason`), `notApplicable`, `mDisk`), `src/engine/core.ts` (`EngineCore.transient`; `raiseHoldCodes`; `tickSkip` adds the paths of non-terminal `collision` records; Plan 2's `notApplicableStill` (core.ts ~510, the P2-NA-TRIGGER re-check) returns a collision path to the apply trigger once `DISK_EQ(p, TIP[p])` holds, R3-33), `test/helpers/coreFixture.ts` (`pushTeamChange(…, {raw: true})`: bytes stored as given, no canon)
- Test: `test/unit/incoming.test.ts`, `test/integration/incoming.test.ts`, `test/integration/formCheck.test.ts`, `test/integration/receive.collision.test.ts`, `test/integration/receive.lock.test.ts`, `test/integration/receive.portability.test.ts`, `test/integration/receive.localOnly.test.ts`, `test/integration/receive.budget.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `HoldRegistry` (`hold`, `holdOutcome`, `transition`, `commitMany`, `nonTerminal`, `byPath`), `makeGuard`, `HOLD_CODES`, `EngineCore.holdCode`, `MutantId` (M38, M39); Task 2 `ConflictCtx.localOnly` binding; Task 4 `mirrorSync`, `creationDue`; Task 5 `collisionTake`, `ApplyReport.changedA`; Plan 2 `applyStep`, `applyNeeded`, `ApplyReport`, `APPLY_COALESCE_MS`, `writeGroups`, `WriteGroupsResult`, `IncomingCs`, `DeferReason`, `PortRule`, `Meta.pending`, `conflict`, `ConflictVerdict`, `form`, `isBinaryBytes`, `AttrSnapshot`, `GitRepo` (`firstParents`, `readCommit`, `readBlobs`), `parseCsTrailers`, `unmaterializableHere`, `portable`, `tickSkip`, `TickResult.excluded`, `Writer.applyGroups`, `restart`, `publishStep`, `prepareIteration`, `Clock`, `FaultFs.holdLock`, `makeCores`, `pushTeamChange`, `VirtualClock`, `TraceRecorder`, `KilledAt`; the shared helpers `diffPaths`, `sameTreeEntry`, `isRegularEntry` (`src/engine/treeDiff.ts`; `changedA` and the closure regrouping use them, never a copy).
- Produces: `TRANSIENT_RECORD_MS`, `TransientClocks`, `NotApplicableReason`, `ClosureReason`, `closureReason`, `INCOMING_GUARD_OF`, `HoldCodeRaise`, `IncomingInputs` (with `rules`, `csOf`), `incomingStep` (returning `codes`); `FormViolation`, `formCheckIncoming`; `EngineCore.transient`, `raiseHoldCodes`; `ApplyReport.closures`, `ApplyReport.notApplicableRules` and `ApplyReport.incoming` (additions); `CoreFixture.pushTeamChange` raw mode; trace `form_violation` and the widened `apply` emission.

**Spec:** glossary §9 (transient exception), §20 (transient exclusion 60 s, apply coalescing ≤ 1 per 10 s, hold escalation "INCOMING health stub at 60 s"), §21 INV-GROUP (rev 2.1), §26.1 W4; 05-holds §2 (transient exclusion, closure), §3.9, HLD-32, HLD-53, HLD-54, HLD-55, §7 I5, I6, I14, B1; 03-data §3.2 (incoming form check), §3.6 (DAT-29), §3.7 (DAT-33 receive side), §3.8 (lock deferral), §3.9 D8 step 0, §3.10 (`formCheckIncoming`), DAT-11, DAT-29, DAT-33, DAT-43; 09-security SEC-32 (the record); 01-engine §3.11 defer table, ENG-32, ENG-36 (`disk_equal`); 11-testing TC-ENG-06, TC-ENG-15, TC-ENG-16, TC-HLD-05, mutant rows M38, M39; 03-data I-DAT-08 (record half), I-DAT-13, P-DAT-02 (DEFER leg); rulings R3-29…R3-33.

**Tests:**

*Clocks and the guard map*
- `"TransientClocks.see returns Δmono since first sight; forget resets; a new instance starts at 0"` — VirtualClock: `see('path:a.txt')` 0, at +40 s 40,000; after `forget` the next `see` is 0; a fresh `TransientClocks` (a restart) returns 0 for every key (R3-30, HLD-32).
- `"INCOMING_GUARD_OF maps eol_form, local_only_conflict and every other apply-side reason"` — `eol_form` → `eol-form`, `local_only_conflict` → `local-only-conflict`; `locked`, `disk_low`, `unreadable`, `unstable`, `fence`, `read_lease`, `txn`, `conflicted_leased`, `mismatch`, `race`, `filter_failure` → `lock`; a closure's guard is that of its group-level reason, never `closure` (R3-31, HLD-54).
- `"closureReason picks eol_form, then local_only_conflict, then any other reason, and ignores closure"` — `[closure, locked]` → `locked`; `[locked, eol_form, closure]` → `eol_form`; `[local_only_conflict, disk_low]` → `local_only_conflict`; `[eol_form, local_only_conflict]` → `eol_form` (R3-31).
- `"ApplyReport.closures has one entry per deferred write group"` — an incoming CS over `a.txt`, `b.txt`, `c.txt` with `b.txt` under a share-read holder: Plan 2's `WriteGroupsResult.deferred` holds `{[b.txt], locked}` and `{[a.txt, c.txt], closure}`; `ApplyReport.closures` is exactly `[{paths: [a.txt, b.txt, c.txt], reason: 'locked'}]` and the `apply` trace's `groups` entry carries `reason: 'locked'`; a group with one `eol_form` and one `locked` path gets `reason: 'eol_form'` (R3-31, INV-GROUP).

*Not-applicable paths (HLD-53, HLD-54)*
- `"each not-applicable path gets its own INCOMING record at once"` — `notApplicable = {con.py: unmaterializable, .env: collision, .claude/settings.json: quarantine}` → the first `incomingStep` creates three INCOMING ON_DISK ACTIVE records with guards `unmaterializable`, `collision`, `quarantine-review`, `h0 = A[p]` (ABSENT for creations), `h1 = TIP[p]`; `.env` (secret-like) and the quarantined path (not LIVE) are `local_only`; `incomingStep` returns `codes` holding `INCOMING_UNMATERIALIZABLE {id, path: 'con.py', rule}` (the rule from `IncomingInputs.rules`, i.e. `ApplyReport.notApplicableRules`) and `INCOMING_COLLISION {id, path: '.env'}`, and after the test loop's `raiseHoldCodes(core, codes)` the log carries both and `holdCode` the newest; the rest of the CS is not deferred (K1, K5, K6, K7, HLD-53, HLD-54, SEC-32 record, D-08, D-11).
- `"a not-applicable record's creation op waits 60 s and one released earlier emits nothing"` — with Task 4's `mirrorSync` in the test loop: the `unmaterializable` record's op is drafted at age 60 s; a `collision` record ended SUBSUMED at 40 s produces no op ever (R3-29, HLD-10).
- `"a not-applicable record is released when TIP no longer contains p or p becomes materialisable"` — TIP deletes `con.py` → SUBSUMED; the case pair `README.md`/`readme.md` loses `readme.md` → `README.md` applies and its record ends SUBSUMED (K6, D-11, HLD-54).
- `"a quarantine-review record comes from an incoming change to a quarantined path"` — `pushTeamChange({'.claude/settings.json': …})`: apply leaves it unwritten (`quarantine`), one INCOMING `quarantine-review` record, `local_only`, mirrored only as a stub; its release is Plan 7's (K7, SEC-32 record, HLD-54).

*Closures (HLD-54, HLD-32 apply side)*
- `"a closure deferred less than 60 s creates no record and no board op"` — a 3-path incoming CS (`a.txt`, `b.txt`, `c.txt`) with `b.txt` under a share-read holder: for 59 virtual seconds `meta.pending` lists all three, the registry head does not move and the outbox holds no hold op (K2, HLD-32, INV-HLD-BUDGET).
- `"at 60 s one INCOMING record covers the whole closure"` — at the first `incomingStep` with the closure clock ≥ 60,000 ms: one record over `[a.txt, b.txt, c.txt]`, guard `lock`, `h0 = A`, `h1 =` TIP's blobs, ON_DISK ACTIVE; its creation op is drafted in the same loop (K2, CS-44, HLD-54, R3-31, D-22).
- `"TC-ENG-06: a locked 3-path group writes nothing; after unlock all three are written in one group with one state update"` — share-none holder on `b.txt`: no path written, the record created at 60 s; after unlock the next permitted apply writes the three in one group (`apply` trace `groups: [{paths: [a.txt, b.txt, c.txt], outcome: 'written', reason: null}]`), one state CAS, and `incomingStep` ends the record SUBSUMED; the board saw exactly two hold ops for it (creation and release) (Review Focus 2, TC-ENG-06, HLD-32, K2).
- `"mutant M38 writes the available paths of a locked group"` — the same setup with M38: `a.txt` and `c.txt` are written while `b.txt` is not; the test asserts that partial state (Task 14's INV-GROUP oracle kills it) (M38).
- `"a TIP move refreshes the INCOMING record and a grown closure widens it"` — TIP changes `a.txt` again → one new rev with the new `h1[a.txt]`; a further incoming CS touching `c.txt` and `d.txt` → the same record now covers `[a.txt, b.txt, c.txt, d.txt]` (one rev), never a second record (R3-31, K2).
- `"a closure record is released when TIP no longer changes its paths"` — the teammate reverts the change (TIP[p] == A[p] for every path) → SUBSUMED with no write (HLD-54).
- `"INCOMING_WAITING is raised while a closure is pending"` — `incomingStep`'s `codes` carry `INCOMING_WAITING {cs: <IncomingInputs.csOf('b.txt')>, path: 'b.txt', reason: 'locked'}` once per closure episode, and after `raiseHoldCodes` `holdCode` equals it and the log has one line per episode; the PreToolUse delivery is Plan 5's (CS-43 status side, HLD-53).
- `"the apply trace carries groups, notApplicable and mDisk"` — for the closure above and for 05 I14 below: `groups` lists each write group with its outcome and reason, `notApplicable` each not-applicable path with its reason, `mDisk` the tree oid of `M_disk` (INV-GROUP input, HLD-53).
- `"05 I5: a closure is all-new or all-old across kills, and six CSs landing together give exactly two applies"` — the share-read setup: kill at `apply.group.phase1` → after `restart` all three paths are old; kill at `apply.group.phase2` → all three are new; six `pushTeamChange` calls are landed with `advance(1_500)` between them (all six within 7.5 virtual s, before p0's second permitted apply under Plan 2's `APPLY_COALESCE_MS` = 10 s), then the test loop runs every 2.5 virtual s: exactly 2 applies (the first at the first loop after the first commit, covering the commits landed by then; the second at least 10 s Δmono later, covering the rest), no two written applies < 10 s Δmono apart, and every one of the six CSs applied within 10 s plus one loop after the last commit (05 I5, INV-GROUP).
- `"mutant M39 drops apply coalescing"` — two incoming CSs 3 s apart: two applies within 10 s Δmono with M39, one without (M39, glossary §20).
- `"05 I6: a deferred closure never blocks other closures"` — `f.py` held by an ON_DISK ACTIVE `revert` PATCH (made with `hold()`), `book.csv` under a share-none holder; teammate changes to `f.py` (4 lines from the held line), to `g.py` and to `book.csv` in three CSs: `f.py` (merged, the held line kept) and `g.py` land within 2 test-loop rounds, `book.csv` within 1 round after unlock (05 I6, INV-HLD-RECEIVE).
- `"05 I14: unmaterialisable and colliding paths of a CS do not defer the rest"` — `platform: 'win32'`, one incoming CS with `con.py`, `.env` (a local ignored file) and `src/x.txt`: `src/x.txt` applies in the same loop; `con.py` and `.env` are in `ApplyReport.notApplicable` with one INCOMING record each; `ApplyReport.closures` is empty (05 I14, K1).
- `"TC-ENG-15: each non-portable path gets its own INCOMING record and later applies continue"` — `platform: 'win32'`, a CS adding `con.py`, `trail.`, `README.md` and `readme.md` plus an edit of `src/a.txt`: `src/a.txt` applies; `con.py`, `trail.` and the later of the case pair get `unmaterializable` records naming their D7 rule; a later CS on `src/a.txt` applies; `a:b.txt` (fails SEC-35 syntax) is carried by scope class and gets no record (TC-ENG-15, DAT-33 receive side, D-11).

*Collisions (DAT-29)*
- `"TC-ENG-16 / I-DAT-08 record half: an incoming .env never overwrites a locally ignored .env"` — B's ignored `.env` stays byte-identical; one INCOMING `collision` record (`local_only` stub); `INCOMING_COLLISION {id, path: '.env'}`; the CS's other paths apply (TC-ENG-16, I-DAT-08, D-08, DAT-29).
- `"a collision record's path is skipped by every snapshot while it is non-terminal"` — `tickSkip(core)` contains `.env`; ten later ticks list `.env` neither as new nor as changed; no add/add K4 record; no P carries `.env` (K5, D-08, DAT-29).
- `"I-DAT-08 quiet-period variant: an untracked notes.txt created 100 ms before the add lands becomes a collision record"` — `notes.txt` (not ignored, mtime 100 ms old) when A's add of `notes.txt` is applied: a `collision` record; over the next 10 loops no K4 record and no push of B's `notes.txt` bytes (I-DAT-08, DAT-29).
- `"R3-33: a creation target equal to TIP's blob is applied with no write"` — local `.env` bytes equal TIP's `.env`: `A[.env] := TIP[.env]` with `A_set` cause `disk_equal`, the file's mtime unchanged, no `collision` record; after Task 5's `collisionTake` wrote TIP's bytes, the next apply does the same and the `collision` record ends SUBSUMED (R3-33, DAT-29).

*Incoming form check (DAT-11, R3-32)*
- `"formCheckIncoming flags a form change of a text unfiltered path, decided on (A, TIP)"` — `A[p]` CRLF and `TIP[p]` LF → `[{path: p, commit: <newest commit in (LA_C, TIP] that set TIP[p]>}]`; `TIP[p]` CRLF with an edited row → `[]`; a binary path and a `filter=crypt` path changing form → `[]`; a path absent in A or in TIP → `[]` (D-02, DAT-11, R3-32).
- `"a base-move second parent's form is allowed"` — a synthetic two-parent commit on the chain whose second parent X has `X[p]` LF: `TIP[p]` LF is not flagged; with X CRLF it is (D-02, R3-32).
- `"a form violation defers its write group at once and becomes an INCOMING eol-form record at 60 s"` — `meta.pending[p] = 'eol_form'` in the same loop, the CS's group (p plus its closure) deferred, every other group applied; trace `form_violation {path, commit}`; at 60 s one INCOMING `eol-form` record over the closure (D-03, K4, HLD-54).
- `"I-DAT-13: a non-conforming writer's LF blob for a CRLF path is held as eol-form and released when a conforming commit restores the form"` — `pushTeamChange({'src/w.cs': <LF bytes>}, {raw: true})` for a CRLF path plus a change to `src/v.cs`: peers apply `src/v.cs`, hold `src/w.cs` (record at 60 s); a later conforming commit restores CRLF → `src/w.cs` applies and the record ends SUBSUMED (I-DAT-13, DAT-11, D-03).

*Local-only conflicts (HLD-55, D8 step 0)*
- `"an incoming change that would displace a local_only path defers its closure"` — tracked `config/.env.local` (LIVE) with an ON_DISK `local_only` PATCH; an incoming same-line change in a CS that also changes `src/y.txt`: `conflict` returns `DEFER {reason: 'local_only_conflict'}`, the closure (`config/.env.local`, `src/y.txt`) is deferred, no record before 60 s; at 60 s one INCOMING `local-only-conflict` record over the closure (K3, CS-44, HLD-55, D-13, P-DAT-02 DEFER leg).
- `"the local-only-conflict record is released when the local_only record ends"` — the `local_only` record transitioned to RESOLVED_DISCARD (disk back to A): the next apply writes the closure and the INCOMING record ends SUBSUMED; the same when the `local_only` record stops being `local_only` (K3, HLD-55).

*Publish-side transient records (HLD-32)*
- `"an unreadable path makes no record for 59 s and one PIN at 60 s"` — `src/a.txt` under a share-none holder (staged reads fail): 59 s nothing; at 60 s one PIN, guard `unreadable`, `h0 = A[p]`, `h1 = privateBlob(p)` (the blob the private index holds), ON_DISK ACTIVE, `local_only` false; until then S_pub keeps `A[p]` or the previous blob (Plan 2) (J1, HLD-32, CS-50).
- `"an unstable secret-like path becomes a local_only PIN guarded unstable"` — a tracked (LIVE) `.env.dev` whose staged read stays unstable for 60 s → PIN `unstable`, `local_only` true by the secret-like pattern (stub) (J1, HLD-32, HLD-03).
- `"a transient record is released when the condition clears: one op at creation, one at release"` — the holder closes → SUBSUMED; the board saw exactly two hold ops for it (J1, HLD-32).
- `"an unreadable new file that was never staged gets no PIN"` — an untracked `src/new.txt` created under a share-none holder (staged reads fail, `A[p]` and `privateBlob(p)` both ABSENT): at 60 s and 120 s no record and no registry commit (its `not_converged` listing is Task 12a's T-10 test); nothing of it is published; after unlock the file is read and joins the human CS (R3-56, HLD-32).

*Budget*
- `"05 B1 / TC-HLD-05: thirty minutes with one permanent PATCH and one 40 s lock and no edits give exactly one push"` — `makeCores` n = 2 and a test loop every 2.5 virtual seconds (`prepareIteration` → `applyStep` → `incomingStep` → `publishStep`): on P1 a `generated?` PATCH made with `hold()` at t = 0 (a non-escalating guard: a `revert` record would add HLD-60's +10 min event, a second push, once Task 12b's escalation runs) and, at t = 10 min, a teammate change whose target P1's FaultFs holds share-read for 40 s; P1's push attempts over 30 min = 1 (the creation mirror); no INCOMING record ever exists on P1 (Review Focus 2, 05 B1, TC-HLD-05, HLD-32, INV-HLD-BUDGET).

**Crashpoints:** none new; 05 I5 kills at `apply.group.phase1` and `apply.group.phase2`. **Lint:** none.

**Notes:**
- `incomingStep` is called by the tests after `applyStep` with `closures = ApplyReport.closures`, `notApplicable = ApplyReport.notApplicable`, `rules = ApplyReport.notApplicableRules`, `csOf` from `ApplyReport.incoming` (the apply's `IncomingCs` list), `transient` from `TickResult.excluded` (`unreadable`, `unstable`) and `privateBlob` from the private index, followed by `raiseHoldCodes(core, result.codes)`; Task 12's `recordStep` is its loop call site and makes the same two calls. A closure clock is forgotten only when none of its paths is still deferred, so an apply coalesced away for 10 s keeps the ages.
- `.env` and similar secret-like INCOMING records are `local_only` by HLD-03 (Task 1 recomputes it); their creation op is the stub.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — `ApplyReport.closures` (one entry per deferred write group, R3-31) and `ApplyReport.notApplicableRules`; `ClosureReason`, `closureReason`; the changing-closure and clock rules on `incomingStep`; `IncomingInputs.rules`/`csOf`, `incomingStep`'s `codes` and `raiseHoldCodes`; records only for `validPathSyntax` paths (Flags item 8).

### Task 7: 03-data record creators — disk repair, portability, churn guard

**Covers:** K8, D-01, D-10, D-19 (portability stub), D-20; DAT-08, DAT-32, DAT-33 (publish side), DAT-39; I-DAT-04, I-DAT-09 (record half), I-DAT-20, F-DAT-04; R3-34, R3-35, R3-53 (the step half). Depends on: Tasks 1, 2, 4, 5. Parallel with Task 6.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/data/churn.ts`, `src/engine/dataRecords.ts`
- Test: `test/unit/churn.test.ts`, `test/integration/repair.test.ts`, `test/integration/portability.test.ts`, `test/integration/churn.test.ts`

Wave D disjointness: Task 7 edits no file Task 6 owns (`core.ts`, `apply.ts`, `incoming.ts`, `formCheck.ts`, `coreFixture.ts`); `Snapshotter.setEntry` is Task 5's (R3-36). The steps' per-core memory (quiet-tick counters and repair episodes, churn histories) lives in a module-level `WeakMap<EngineCore, …>` inside `dataRecords.ts`, so no `EngineCore` member is added.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 2 `EngineCore` (`holds`, `cs`, `writer`, `snapshot`, `stage`, `view`, `repo`, `deps.attribution`, `deps.log`, `deps.trace`, `deps.ports.fs`), `IterationCtx` (`tick`, `mat`, `A`, `scope`, `freeze`), `TickResult` (`changed`, `dirty`, `excluded`, `notAdmitted`), `DirtyEntry`, `Materialized` (`sRawMap`, `sDiskMap`, `canonRefused`), `Snapshotter.markClean`, Task 5's `Snapshotter.setEntry`, `Writer.writeLocal`, `Writer.strandedPaths`, `stagedRead`, `StageDir`, `stageable`, `hashRouteB`, `portable`, `form`, `rows`, `stripEnding`, `isBinaryBytes`, `matchedRows` (`src/data/linediff.ts`), `prepareIteration`, `publishStep`, `restart`, `ABSENT`; test helpers `makeCores`, `writeWork`, `readWork`, `workPath`, `gitCallsOf`, `generateBigBomCrlf`, `seededRandom`, `prep`, `captureReady`, `remoteCode`, `treeOf`, `LogRecorder`, `setCrashHandler`; Task 1 `HoldRegistry.hold`/`holdOutcome`/`transition`/`releaseGuard`/`byPath`/`nonTerminal`/`head`, `makeGuard`, `HOLD_CODES` (`EOL_REPAIRED`, `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE`, `GENERATED_SUSPECT`), `AttributionPort.hookAttributed`, `humanOnlyAttribution`, `EngineCore.holdCode`, `TraceEvent` `repair`; Task 2 `maskReason`, `MaskInputs`; Task 4 `projectOp` and the publish step's mirror sync (stub assertions only); Task 5 `PrepareOptions.holdPaths`, `evaluateRecords` (Tasks 4 and 5 merge in Wave C, before this task's wave; only tests and the `holdPaths` hand-off use them).
- Produces: `CHURN_SNAPSHOTS`, `CHURN_REWRITE_RATIO`, `ChurnObservation`, `churnVerdict`, `rewrittenRatio`; `repairStep`, `portabilityStep` (with `unrecorded`, R3-53), `churnStep`; `repairDue` (contract addition 1).

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — (1) `repairDue`; (2) the calling rule of `repairStep`, `portabilityStep` and `churnStep` (on `src/engine/dataRecords.ts`); (3) `portabilityStep`'s R3-53 branch and its `unrecorded` result. The keep-syncing suppression (`RegistryLocal.keepSyncing`, `churnStep` skipping those paths) is Task 11's, which edits this file again; before Task 11 a `local.json` loads with `keepSyncing = []`. The tests' "contract addition n" refers to these numbers.

**Spec:** 03-data §2 (records and the LIVE-only rule; codes `EOL_REPAIRED`, `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE`, `GENERATED_SUSPECT`), §3.2 "Disk repair", §3.7 "Publish side", §3.8 "Churn guard", §3.10 (`churnVerdict`); DAT-08, DAT-32, DAT-33 (publish side), DAT-39; glossary §9 (LIVE-only rule, content-free stub), §20 (data numbers: churn guard 3 snapshots × ≥ 20 % rows), §24 default 4, §25.2 item 2, §26.1 W1, W6; 05-holds Table 3 rows `portability` and `generated?`; 01-engine §3.11 (`writeLocal` users: "DAT-08 disk-form repair"); tests I-DAT-04, I-DAT-09 (record half), I-DAT-20 (record half), F-DAT-04; rulings R3-12, R3-17, R3-23, R3-34, R3-35.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/churn.test.ts`
- `"rewrittenRatio: identical rows give 0; a 10-row text with rows 3 and 7 replaced gives 0.2; every row replaced gives 1; the same rows with LF and with CRLF endings give 0"` — Myers matched rows with endings stripped (`matchedRows` over `stripEnding(rows(x))`) (DAT-39, D-20).
- `"rewrittenRatio of an empty prev is 1 when next has a row and 0 when both are empty"` — the 0/0 case (DAT-39).
- `"churnVerdict needs the newest 3 observations all unattributed and each at least 20 %"` — CHURN_SNAPSHOTS = 3, CHURN_REWRITE_RATIO = 0.2; histories (newest last) `[0.2, 0.2, 0.2]` → `'generated?'`; `[0.19, 0.5, 0.5]` → `'none'`; `[0.5, 0.5]` → `'none'`; `[0.5, {attributed: true, 0.5}, 0.5]` → `'none'`; `[{attributed: true, 0.9}, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3]` → `'generated?'` (DAT-39, glossary §20 data numbers).
- `"churnVerdict counts a binary observation whatever its ratio"` — three unattributed `{binary: true, rewrittenRatio: 0}` → `'generated?'` ("or the path is binary", DAT-39).

`test/integration/repair.test.ts` — `makeCores('t7-repair', {n: 1, autocrlf: [true], files: {'big.py': generateBigBomCrlf(26_436, seededRandom('t7-repair')), 'legacy.txt': <40 cp949 rows, CRLF, e.g. 0xB0 0xA1 0xB3 0xAA per row>, 'mixed.txt': <20 LF rows>}})` after `bootstrap()`; "a materializing iteration" is `prepareIteration(core, n, {materialize: true, forceFetch: false, holdPaths: repairDue(core)})` followed by `repairStep(core, it)`; "a tick" is the same with `materialize: false` after `advance(2_500)`.
- `"a Write that drops the BOM of the 1.3 MB BOM+CRLF fixture is repaired at the first materializing iteration after two unchanged ticks, byte-identical to the stored form"` — write `big.py` = fixture without its 3 BOM bytes; iteration 1 materializes (`S_raw[big.py] ≠ S_disk[big.py]`), writes nothing, `repairDue` empty; two unchanged ticks → `repairDue = {big.py}`; the next materializing iteration writes it: `readWork(big.py)` equals the original fixture bytes; trace `repair {path: 'big.py', from: <S_raw oid>, to: <S_disk oid>}`; the writer received origin `repair:big.py` and `core.observed.peek('big.py', <S_disk oid>)` is true; exactly one log line `EOL_REPAIRED {path: 'big.py', bom: true, eol: 'CRLF'}` (`bom`/`eol` = `form(A[big.py])`); `core.holdCode` unchanged (log only, R-27) (I-DAT-04, DAT-08, D-01, K8, R3-34, Review Focus 5).
- `"after the repair the next 100 idle ticks copy and hash nothing for big.py"` — 100 × (`advance(2_500)`, tick): `gitCallsOf` has no `hash-object` and no `add` naming `big.py`, `FaultFs.calls` has no `readFile` of `big.py`, and the next materialization's `sRawMap.get('big.py').oid` equals the repair's `S_disk` oid (I-DAT-04 "0 re-hashes per idle cycle over 100 cycles", DAT-08 "records p's new stat data against S_disk[p]", R3-34 `setEntry` + `markClean`).
- `"a cp949 CRLF file whose endings a Write flipped to LF is repaired back to CRLF with every multibyte byte unchanged"` — `legacy.txt` rewritten with LF; after the two quiet ticks and a materializing iteration the disk bytes equal the original; one `EOL_REPAIRED {path: 'legacy.txt', bom: false, eol: 'CRLF'}` (DAT-08, Review Focus 5).
- `"an edit during the two quiet ticks restarts the count"` — drop the BOM; materialize; one unchanged tick; change row 7 (BOM still missing): the tick shows `big.py` changed and `repairDue` stays empty; the next materialization starts a new count; after two unchanged ticks the repair writes a file that keeps the edited row 7 and has the BOM back (R3-34 "two consecutive detection ticks with p's stat and detection id unchanged").
- `"no repair while any FREEZE reason is set; the repair runs at the first materializing iteration after the lift"` — `core.commit({meta: {...view.meta, freeze_reasons: ['MASS']}})` with `big.py` due: no `writeLocal` call (no `rename` of `big.py` in `FaultFs.calls`), no `repair` trace, `repairDue` still lists it; after `freeze_reasons: []` the next materializing iteration repairs (DAT-08, R3-34 "never under FREEZE").
- `"a canon-refused path and a path held under eol-bom are never repaired"` — `mixed.txt` rewritten with rows 1–3 CRLF and the rest LF (canon refuses it, `canonRefused` holds it) and, separately, `legacy.txt` with its endings flipped while the test holds it under `hold({kind: 'PATCH', guard: 'eol-bom', …})`: over 5 materializing iterations neither path is in `repairDue`, no `writeLocal` names them, their disk bytes are unchanged (DAT-08 "MUST NOT repair a path held under eol-bom", R3-34).
- `"a repair is postponed while a push intent is kept and while a path is stranded"` — `core.commit({intent: {kind: 'push', …}})` with the state's own oids: `repairStep` writes nothing and `repairDue` still lists `big.py`; after `{kind: 'none'}` the next materializing iteration repairs; the same with `FaultFs` leaving one stranded restore (`Writer.strandedPaths()` non-empty) (R3-23).
- `"a share-read lock on big.py makes the repair's writeLocal LOCKED: nothing is written and big.py stays due until the lock is released"` — `FaultFs.holdLock('big.py', 'share-read')`; one materializing iteration: disk bytes unchanged, no trace, no log; release; the next materializing iteration repairs (ENG-37, Review Focus 2 shape).
- `"a writer MISMATCH writes and logs nothing, and a later BOM drop is a new episode that logs EOL_REPAIRED again"` — `FaultFs.interpose` on the writer's first read of `big.py` rewrites row 9 once: the write returns MISMATCH, no `repair` trace, no log line, `repairDue` empty until two more unchanged ticks after the next materialization; after the clean repair, dropping the BOM again yields a second `EOL_REPAIRED` (2 lines for 2 episodes) (DAT-08 "once per (seat, path, episode)", R3-34).
- `"a kill at apply.group.phase1 inside the repair's writeLocal leaves big.py with the BOM-less or the repaired bytes after restart, and the next due iteration completes the repair"` — `setCrashHandler` kills at `apply.group.phase1` reached through `writeLocal`'s journaled local intent; a new core and `restart(core)`: the disk is one of the two blobs (never torn); within two ticks and one materialization the repair completes (R3-52 shape, ENG-37, Review Focus 3 shape).

`test/integration/portability.test.ts` — the non-portable name is `con.py` on Linux and macOS hosts and a 100-syllable Hangul component plus `.txt` (304 UTF-8 bytes) on a win32 host; both fail D7 rule 2 and are creatable on the host that uses them.
- `"a human-created non-portable new file becomes one local_only ON_DISK PATCH with guard portability, h0 ABSENT and h1 the staged copy's blob, reported PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {id, path, rule: 2}"` — `writeWork(<name>, 'print(1)\n')`, `advance(2_500)`, tick, `portabilityStep`: `created = [id]`; the record has kind `PATCH`, residence `ON_DISK`, state `ACTIVE`, guards `[{name: 'portability', class: 'DECISION', owner: 'DAT'}]`, `local_only: true` (registry recompute: scope `NOT_ADMITTED` → cause `not-live`, R3-12), `blobs.h0[<name>] = ABSENT`, `blobs.h1[<name>]` equal to a test-side `git hash-object --path=<name>` of the same bytes, origin `'human'`, `cs_ids: []`; `core.holdCode = {code: 'PATH_NOT_PORTABLE', params: {id, path: <name>, rule: 2}}` and one log line (DAT-33, D-10, D-19, R3-35).
- `"ten more ticks, one of them after an edit of the file, create no second record and no registry commit"` — `holds.head()` unchanged; the record keeps its first `h1` (the later bytes stay on the author's disk) (DAT-33, HLD-07).
- `"portabilityStep adds the path to the open human CS, so the CS's capture lists it with R and A_cap both lacking it"` — edit `src/a.txt` and create the file in the same tick; after `advance(20_000)` `captureReady`: the pair's `paths` include both; `R` and `A` have no entry for the name (the group pin itself is Task 10's test) (R3-35).
- `"the record's mirror is a content-free stub: the next window's board commit has no held/<id>/ subtree and no tree entry naming the file, and projectOp carries local_only true and no oid"` — after one `publishStep`; `projectOp(record, null)` has `local_only: true`, `paths: [<name>]` and no oid field; 03-data §3.7's "a non-portable name never appears … in a hold op" conflicts with R3-24's stub keeping `paths[]` — R3-24 is followed and the 03-data sentence is flagged (D-19, I-DAT-09 record half).
- `"the non-portable file is never in a pushed live tree"` — whatever the window publishes, `treeOf(remoteCode)` has no entry for the name (DAT-32; that `src/a.txt` waits with it is Task 10's dependency-PIN test).
- `"renaming the file to a portable name ends the record GONE at the next tick and the new name publishes as an ordinary new path"` — rename `con.py` → `con_.py` (win32: the Hangul file → `ok.txt`); the next tick's `portabilityStep` returns it in `released`, the record is `GONE` (trace `hold` state `GONE`); after 20 s quiet the new name is in the next pushed live tree (I-DAT-09 record half, DAT-33 release "renamed, deleted").
- `"on a case-sensitive host readme.md beside tracked README.md is a rule-4 record; deleting README.md releases its guard and both changes publish in one commit"` — skipped on win32 and darwin; record `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {rule: 4}`; after `README.md` is deleted the next tick releases `portability` (guards `[]`, `local_only` recomputed `false` because the path is now LIVE, state `ACTIVE`, R3-17); one live commit carries the deletion and `readme.md`; after the next apply step `evaluateRecords` ends the record `SUBSUMED` (DAT-33 release "the colliding path removed").
- `"a dotgit-alias new name gets no record, is never read, and is reported PATH_NOT_PORTABLE without an id"` — `writeWork('git~1/x.txt', 'x\n')`: no record and no registry commit, `FaultFs.calls` has no `lstat` or `readFile` of the file (P2-DOTGIT; DAT-33 needs a staged read for `h1`); `portabilityStep` returns it in `unrecorded`, `core.holdCode = {code: 'PATH_NOT_PORTABLE', params: {path: 'git~1/x.txt', rule: <portable()'s rule>}}` with no `id`, one log line, and ten further ticks raise nothing more (one per episode) (R3-53).
- `"R3-53: renaming src/old.py to src/old:v2.py (a name that fails SEC-35) creates no record, reports PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {path, rule} without an id, and never puts the bad name into a CS"` — on Linux and macOS hosts (skipped on win32, which cannot create the name): `fs.rename` of the tracked `src/old.py` to `src/old:v2.py`, a tick, `portabilityStep`: `created = []`, `unrecorded = ['src/old:v2.py']`, `holds.head()` unchanged, `holdCode` `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {path: 'src/old:v2.py', rule: 2}`, `core.cs.openPaths()` holds `src/old.py` (the deletion) and not `src/old:v2.py`; that nothing of the human CS publishes until the name is fixed is Task 10's test (the readiness gate) (R3-53, DAT-32, DAT-33).
- `"an NFD-spelled new name is handled like a:b.txt"` — `writeWork(NFD_NAME, …)` with `NFD_NAME = 'nfd/한.txt'.normalize('NFD')` (the decomposed spelling, as Task 13's NFD name) on a host that keeps NFD names (skipped on darwin): no record, in `unrecorded`, `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {path, rule: 1}` (R3-53).

`test/integration/churn.test.ts` — `files: {'gen/api.ts': <50 rows 'export const v<i> = <i>;'>, 'src/use.ts': <10 rows>, 'big.py': generateBigBomCrlf(26_436, …)}`; "a qualifying rewrite" replaces 30 of the 50 rows of `gen/api.ts` with fresh text, then `advance(2_500)` and a tick-only iteration plus `churnStep`.
- `"a file rewritten wholesale at three consecutive ticks becomes one ON_DISK PATCH generated? with GENERATED_SUSPECT {id, path: 'gen/api.ts', excludeLine: '\"gen/api.ts\"'}"` — ticks 1–2 create nothing; tick 3 creates it: guards `[{name: 'generated?', class: 'DECISION', owner: 'DAT'}]`, `local_only: false`, `h0 = A[gen/api.ts]`, `h1` = a test-side `hash-object --path` of the tick-3 bytes, origin `'human'`; `core.holdCode` is `GENERATED_SUSPECT`; `it.mat` was `null` in all three iterations (DAT-39, D-20, R3-35, glossary §20 data numbers).
- `"two rewrites, an unchanged tick, then one rewrite create no record; three further consecutive rewrites do"` — a tick at which the path is clean clears its history; a tick that excludes it (`quiet`) neither extends nor clears it (DAT-39 "≥ 3 consecutive snapshots").
- `"rewriting 19 of 100 rows at 20 consecutive ticks creates no record; rewriting 20 of 100 creates one at the third"` — the 20 % boundary on a 100-row variant of the file (DAT-39, F-DAT-04).
- `"a binary file changed by one byte at three consecutive ticks becomes a record"` — `img.bin` with a NUL in its first 8,000 bytes (DAT-39 "or the path is binary").
- `"a change for which AttributionPort.hookAttributed answers true breaks the chain"` — a test attribution port answering `true` for the second change of `gen/api.ts` only: no record after tick 3, one after tick 5 (DAT-39 "each change with no hook attribution"; R3-35).
- `"while the record is non-terminal, or carries generated?:exclude, no second record is created"` — 10 more qualifying ticks leave `holds.head()` unchanged; the same after `transition(id, {guards: [makeGuard('generated?:exclude')]})` (DAT-39 "not again", F-DAT-04 one record).
- `"the record masks gen/api.ts whole and no pushed live tree carries a generated byte of it"` — `maskReason('gen/api.ts', inp) === 'whole_path_guard'`; a human CS holding `gen/api.ts` and `src/use.ts` closes after the generator stops and 20 s pass; whatever the window publishes, the remote live tree's `gen/api.ts` blob equals `A[gen/api.ts]` (the dependency PIN over `src/use.ts` is Task 10's test) (I-DAT-20 record half, DAT-39).
- `"a generated? record whose path the iteration scope reports UNMANAGED ends SUBSUMED"` — `churnStep(core, {...it, scope: (q) => q === 'gen/api.ts' ? {cls: 'UNMANAGED', reason: null} : it.scope(q)})` returns it in `released` and the record is `SUBSUMED` (implemented, unreachable before Plan 7's EPOCH_BASE change, R3-08) (DAT-39).
- `"F-DAT-04: a generator rewriting gen/api.ts every 2 s on two peers for 60 virtual s gives one generated? record per peer and the live chain never changes gen/api.ts"` — `makeCores({n: 2})`; 30 rounds of (both peers write a qualifying rewrite, `advance(2_000)`, each peer tick + `churnStep` + `publishStep`): each peer has exactly one non-terminal `generated?` record; the remote live tip's `gen/api.ts` equals `L0`'s blob after every round (F-DAT-04).
- `"a human typing with autosave into the 1.3 MB fixture never creates a generated? record"` — one row of `big.py` changed every 2 s for 30 ticks: no record (F-DAT-04 "small diffs").
- `"churn observation writes no object; the record's content is the only -w"` — over the ticks before the record `gitCallsOf` has no `hash-object -w` and no `add`; at creation exactly one route (b) `hash-object -w --path=gen/api.ts` of the stage copy (glossary §26.1 W6, R3-35).

**Crashpoints:** none new (record creations pass `registry.cas`; the repair's write reaches the writer's anchored steps through `writeLocal`'s journaled local intent). **Lint:** none.

**Notes:** `repairStep` candidates are LIVE, text, unfiltered paths of a materialization with `S_raw[p] ≠ S_disk[p]`, not in `canonRefused` and not under a non-terminal `eol-bom` record; the episode is keyed by p's lstat and `S_raw` id at that materialization; it skips (stays due) while `it.freeze` is non-empty, `view.intent.kind === 'push'` or `Writer.strandedPaths()` is non-empty (R3-23); its `writeLocal` carries origin `repair:<p>`; on `WRITTEN` it calls `core.observed.record(p, S_disk[p])`, `snapshot.setEntry(p, S_disk entry)` and then `snapshot.markClean(p, lstat, S_disk oid)` (R3-36; no snap transition: `state:snap` already holds `S_disk[p]`). `portabilityStep` handles every `tick.notAdmitted` entry with reason `portability` and computes the rule with `portable(p, others)` (others = A's paths ∪ the tick's other new paths): a `stageable()` path gets the DAT-33 record — the file is read through `stagedRead` into `StageDir` and hashed with `hashRouteB(…, true)`; an unstable read creates nothing this tick — and a path that is not `stageable()` gets R3-53's treatment (no read, no record, no CS membership, `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {path, rule}` once per episode keyed by the path's presence, listed in `unrecorded`). `churnStep` observes `tick.changed` LIVE paths with a `modify` dirty entry: next bytes = the tick's stage copy (an `Fs` read of `StageDir.abs(p)`, no git), prev bytes = the previous observation (kept in memory until the history clears) or, at the first observation, the private-index blob; a `new` or `remove` entry clears the history.

### Task 8: Provenance, the revert detector, the mass-change breaker and resume

**Covers:** A11, D7, CS-22, CS-23, CS-24, CS-25, CS-26, CS-27, CS-28, CS-29, CS-31, D-23, D-27; HLD-12, HLD-18, HLD-25…HLD-31, ENG-14, ENG-36/ENG-37 (provenance), ENG-45 (host-reject release); 05 U2, U3, P4, F3, I7, TC-HLD-03, TC-HLD-07, `HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE` (05 §1 binary row); lint L-HOLD-STORES (with rev 2.5's `revertInverse()` exception, glossary §29.3, 05 U2; X-09, follow-up F-P3-6 — the rule names the exception before Task 17 creates the function, so the real `src/` tree passes either way); mutant site M33; R3-36…R3-39, R3-59. Depends on: Tasks 2, 5, 6. Parallel with Task 9. **[P2 contract files]** (`loop.ts` delegation, `daemon/run.ts` resume, `restart.ts` load and lost-state advance, `start.ts` load at genesis and join).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/holds/provenance.ts`, `src/holds/revert.ts`, `src/holds/breaker.ts`, `src/engine/detect.ts`, `src/engine/resume.ts`
- Modify: `src/engine/core.ts` (`EngineCore.prov`, `EngineCore.breaker`, `stateJoinPoint`, `provRebuildFrom`, `advanceProvenance`; `commit()` calls `advanceProvenance` after a CAS that moves `laC` unless given `{provenance: 'caller'}`, R3-59), `src/engine/apply.ts` (`applyStep` commits with `{provenance: 'caller'}` and calls `advanceProvenance` right after `crashpoint('apply.state')` and `setCurrentA`; nothing else), `src/engine/start.ts` (genesis and join call `prov.load(tip, tip)` right after `attachState`, before their first apply; **[P2 contract file]**), `src/engine/restart.ts` (a restart calls `prov.load(view.laC, await provRebuildFrom(core))` right after `attachState(core, v)` and before `resolveWriteIntent`; after `writeLostState` and `attachState` in `lostStateRebuild`, keeping its 5-parameter signature: `prov.advance(prov.newest(), tip, …)` when the journal's newest entry is a first-parent ancestor of tip, else `prov.load(tip, await provRebuildFrom(core))`, R3-36, R3-59; **[P2 contract file]**), `test/helpers/coreFixture.ts` (`bootstrap()` loads the journal like genesis/join where it does not go through `startDispatch`), `src/engine/snapshot.ts` (`Materialized.prevMap`), `src/holds/observed.ts` (`ObservedWrites.peek`), `src/engine/loop.ts` (`Engine.resume()`; `resumeAfterNonretryable()` delegates), `src/daemon/run.ts` (`resume` takes `daemon.lock` for its whole run, lifts MASS, releases `host-reject`, prints `ResumeReport`), `tools/lint/rules.ts` (L-HOLD-STORES), `test/unit/lint.rules.test.ts`
- Test: `test/unit/provenance.test.ts`, `test/unit/breaker.test.ts`, `test/unit/claimfree.test.ts`, `test/integration/provenance.test.ts`, `test/integration/revert.test.ts`, `test/integration/revert.bomcrlf.test.ts`, `test/integration/mass.test.ts`, `test/integration/resume.test.ts`

Wave E disjointness: Task 9 edits `changesets.ts`, `signals.ts`, `csStep.ts`, `state.ts`, `publish.ts`, `test/helpers/publishHelpers.ts` and `test/integration/publish.test.ts`; Task 8 edits none of them (Task 8's `apply.ts`, `start.ts` and `coreFixture.ts` edits are outside Task 9's list).

**Tests:**

`test/unit/provenance.test.ts`
- `"isNonTrivial: '', '   \t', '});', '++' and '© ®' are trivial; 'x = 1', '  // ok' and '가' are not"` — no EOL in the input; Unicode P*/S* only counts as trivial (05 §2 non-trivial line, HLD-25).
- `"lineHash is the SHA-1 hex of a row without its EOL: the rows 'a\r\n' and 'a\n' both hash as 'a'"` — via `stripEnding(rows(x))` (HLD-25).

`test/integration/provenance.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 1})` with `src/a.txt` and teammate commits from `pushTeamChange`.
- `"advance appends one entry per live commit in (old, next] with the Cowork-Writer peer, the Cowork-CS ids and add/del hashes of non-trivial changed rows only"` — teammate commit c1 adds rows `TEAM = 1` and `}` to `src/a.txt`, c2 deletes row `three`; after the apply: two entries, c1 `{writer: <teammate peer>, cs_ids: [<its csId>], paths: {'src/a.txt': {add: [lineHash('TEAM = 1')], del: []}}}` (the trivial `}` is not listed), c2 `{del: [lineHash('three')]}` (HLD-25, CS-22).
- `"the journal advances after an apply, a landed push and a lost-state rebuild, and not after a commit that leaves laC unchanged"` — apply of a teammate commit: +1 entry, appended after the `apply.state` crashpoint (a crash handler at `apply.state` sees the journal without it); this peer's own publish landing (`applyPushOk`): +1 entry with `writer` = this peer; a capture-only state commit: +0; `lostStateRebuild(core, T, 'lost_state')` (which writes through `state.rebuild`/`create`, not `EngineCore.commit`): entries up to TIP, appended by `restart.ts`'s advance (R3-36, R3-59, ENG-36, ENG-37 provenance, CS-22).
- `"advance is a no-op before load() and never appends commits before the load point"` — a fresh `ProvenanceJournal`: `advance(c0, c2, …)` appends nothing; after `load(c1, c1)`, `advance(c0, c2, …)` appends only c2 (R3-59).
- `"a state ref deleted while the daemon runs: the lost-state rebuild fills the journal over (old LA_C, TIP], so a stale save of a teammate line added in that span is a revert record"` — this peer at LA_C = c0; the teammate lands c1 adding `TEAM_LS = 1` (not yet applied); the state ref is deleted (`update-ref -d`) while the core runs; the next iteration's lost-state rebuild moves `laC` to TIP = c1 and the journal gains c1's entry (`addedBy(lineHash('TEAM_LS = 1'))` names the teammate); a stale save that removes `TEAM_LS = 1` then gives one `revert` PATCH at the next materialization (R3-36, ENG-54, HLD-27).
- `"entries older than 14 days of local append time are pruned at the next append"` — `advance` 15 days of wall clock, one more teammate commit applied: the old entry is gone and `addedBy(lineHash('TEAM = 1'))` is `[]` (HLD-25, `PROV_RETENTION_MS = 1_209_600_000`).
- `"load rebuilds a missing or corrupt journal from the live first-parent chain back to provRebuildFrom with t_local_ms = the rebuild time, and the revertDetector verdicts are identical before and after"` — delete `prov/<enc(b)>.jsonl` → `load` returns `'rebuilt'`; replace its last line with `{"x"` → `'rebuilt'`; for a fixed set of three deltas (remove `TEAM = 1`, re-add `three`, edit an own row) `revertDetector` returns the same `TaintedPath[]` each time (05 F3, U3 "journal deleted and rebuilt", TC-HLD-03).
- `"a rebuild stops at the retention cut: a teammate line applied 20 local days ago stays unprotected after the journal is deleted"` — teammate commit c1 (adds `TEAM_OLD = 1`) applied, then 15 days of wall clock, teammate commit c2 (adds `TEAM_NEW = 1`) applied, then 5 more days; with the intact journal removing `TEAM_OLD = 1` gives no record (pruned) and removing `TEAM_NEW = 1` gives one; delete the journal and restart: `provRebuildFrom(core)` is c1 (the newest reflog entry older than 14 days), `load` rebuilds only c2's entry, and the same two removals give the same verdicts — no record for `TEAM_OLD`, one for `TEAM_NEW` (HLD-25 "≤ 14 days by commit order back to the newest commit applied before the retention cut", R3-36).
- `"a journal left one commit behind by a kill at apply.state is advanced by load, not rebuilt"` — `setCrashHandler` kills at `apply.state` during an apply of a teammate commit (the state CAS has landed; the append runs after the anchor, R3-59), and separately a `FaultFs.interpose` on the `appendFile` of `prov/<enc(b)>.jsonl` throws `KilledAt` inside the append; in both cases `restart(core)` calls `load(laC, await provRebuildFrom(core))`, which returns `'loaded'`, and the missing entry is present (contract addition 4, ENG-36, R3-36, R3-59, 11-testing §3.6 `apply.state`).
- `"stateJoinPoint is the live code of the oldest reflog entry's laB, and a rebuild never reaches past it"` — after `bootstrap()` and two applied teammate commits c1, c2, `stateJoinPoint(core)` is `L0` and so is `provRebuildFrom(core)` (no entry older than 14 days); on a joined peer (a state created with `laC = L_b`) `stateJoinPoint` is the TIP its join started from, not `L_b`; `load(laC, c1)` on a deleted journal rebuilds only c2's entry; `load(laC, laC)` creates an empty journal; after `git reflog expire --expire=now --all` both are `null` (contract addition 4, HLD-25 "or the join point", R3-59).
- `"a joiner's first apply and a restart never protect history from before their load point"` — peer 1 joins after the teammate's c1 (adds `TEAM_OLD = 1`): `startDispatch`'s join calls `load(tip, tip)` before its first apply, and after that apply the journal is empty (no c1 entry); a restart of peer 0 calls `load` right after `attachState` and before `resolveWriteIntent`, and the restart decision commit appends only commits after the load point (R3-59, HLD-25; the end-to-end join test is Task 12b's).
- `"advance computes row changes in-process: no git diff with -U0 or --unified=0 is spawned"` — `gitCallsOf` over three advances (L-HOLD-STORES, D-23).
- `"a commit carrying Cowork-Base is recorded with writer branch:<X>"` — a synthetic live commit with trailer `Cowork-Base: <X>` (no base move exists before Plan 7) (HLD-25).
- `"provenance depends only on the live chain: a commit on refs/heads/<b> that is not on the live chain adds and removes no entry"` — the save clause of U3 itself is Plan 7's (U3 "provenance survives a save", partial).

`test/unit/breaker.test.ts` — `MassBreaker` on a `VirtualClock`.
- `"tracked 100: 10 unobserved paths first seen within 30 s do not trip; the 11th trips 'window'"` — `MASS_MIN_FILES = 10` (HLD-30, CS-29).
- `"tracked 1,000: the threshold is 20 (2 %), so 20 paths do not trip and 21 do"` — `MASS_TRACKED_RATIO = 0.02` (HLD-30).
- `"only paths first seen inside one 30 s Δmono window count: 6 at t = 0 and 5 at t = 35 s do not trip; 6 at t = 0 and 5 at t = 25 s trip"` — `MASS_WINDOW_MS = 30_000` (glossary §20 MASS breaker window).
- `"observed paths never count, and a path seen again counts once"` — HLD-30 "unobserved deltas".
- `"an unobserved deletion whose peerLinesIn answers true trips 'peer_lines' with a single path; an observed deletion or peerLinesIn false does not"` — HLD-30 second trigger.
- `"'.git/index.lock' and '.git/logs/HEAD' never count: 15 such inputs do not trip"` — HLD-30 ("index.lock and logs/HEAD are NOT triggers").
- `"reset() forgets every first-seen time"` — 10 paths, `reset()`, 10 more: no trip.

`test/unit/claimfree.test.ts`
- `"the revert detector, MASK, REBASE, PARK, RESTORE and R1 modules import nothing that reads claim state"` — scans `src/holds/{revert,mask,rebase,evaluate,provenance}.ts` and `src/engine/{holdOps,detect}.ts`: no import specifier containing `claim` and no identifier `claims`; `FoldState.claims` is read nowhere in them (HLD-06, CS-28 structural form; 05 P5 runs in Plan 4).

`test/integration/revert.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 1})`, `f.py` = 60 rows `row_<i> = <i>`; the teammate (`pushTeamChange`) adds `TEAM = 1` after row 20 and deletes `row_30`; this peer's own earlier publish added `MINE = 1` after row 40; all applied. "Materialize and detect" is a materializing iteration followed by `revertStep(core, it)`.
- `"removing a teammate-added line is tainted; removing a pre-genesis line or a line this peer published is not"` — deleting the `TEAM = 1` row gives one `revert` PATCH (ON_DISK ACTIVE, origin `'human'`, `cs_ids: []`, `h0 = A[f.py]`); deleting `row_10` or `MINE = 1` gives none (HLD-27, CS-24, 05 P4 exp_a shape).
- `"re-adding a line a teammate deleted is tainted"` — a stale save that brings `row_30 = 30` back gives one `revert` PATCH (HLD-27 bidirectional, 05 P4 holdcat X3).
- `"a whitespace-only reindent of a teammate's line is exempt; a quote-style change of it is tainted"` — `TEAM = 1` → `TEAM =  1` (ws-equal pair) gives none; `print('a')` → `print("a")` on a teammate-added row gives one (HLD-27, U3 formatter rows).
- `"05 U3 / TC-HLD-03 table: modify, delete and re-add teammate lines through a human save, a /rewind restore, a Bash sed, a non-whitespace formatter, a whitespace-only formatter and a Claude Edit"` — 18 cells, each on a fresh fixture: the first four writers (an in-place write, a whole-file write of the pre-apply bytes, a truncate-and-write, a rename-over write, all unobserved) give taint yes for modify, delete and re-add; the whitespace-only formatter gives no; the Claude Edit (a test `AttributionPort` whose `observedEdit('f.py')` is `true`) gives no (HLD-26, HLD-27, U3, TC-HLD-03, CS-23).
- `"a delta landing exactly on an ObservedWrites entry is dropped and the entry consumed"` — `core.observed.record('f.py', <blob without TEAM>)`, then the disk holds that blob: no record, `consume` afterwards returns `false`; a further different delta of `f.py` is examined normally (HLD-26 (b), R3-36, ENG-37 provenance half).
- `"a teammate line appended more than 14 days ago of local time is no longer protected"` — `advance` 15 days of wall clock, then delete `TEAM = 1`: no record (HLD-25 retention, HLD-27 "within the retention window").
- `"a binary file whose teammate version is replaced becomes a PIN guard revert, and deleting a file holding a teammate line becomes a PIN with h1 ABSENT"` — `img.bin` (NUL in the first 8,000 bytes) set by the teammate, overwritten by a stale copy; `g.py` holding `TEAM_G = 1` deleted: two PINs, ON_DISK ACTIVE, guard `revert` (HLD-28 step 5, CS-25).
- `"a binary path is held only as a whole-file PIN and HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE {hid, path} goes to the daemon log once per record and to status"` — the `img.bin` record above: no PATCH, no line-level held range (`heldRanges` has no entry for it), `maskReason` withholds the whole path, the log carries `HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE` once over 10 further materializing iterations and `core.holdCode` names it (the order against `HOLD_CREATED` in the loop is Task 12b's `escalationStep` test, R3-70) (05 §1 binary row, 05 §2 code table, N, HLD-28 step 5).
- `"taintOnlyH1 applies only the tainted hunks: h1 is A with only the TEAM removal, while maskEntries' S_pub is A plus the untainted edit of row 50"` — one save deletes `TEAM = 1` and edits `row_50`; `h1` equals A with the TEAM row removed; `S_pub[f.py]` equals A with `row_50` edited and `TEAM = 1` present; `taintOnlyH1`'s output is used only as the record's `h1` (HLD-18, D7, CS-27, R3-37).
- `"revertStep runs only on an iteration that materialized: a tick-only iteration creates no record and writes no object, and the next materializing iteration creates it from that S_disk"` — the delete is saved, three tick-only iterations: no record, no `hash-object -w`; the materializing iteration creates it and the record's `h1` is computed against that iteration's `S_disk` (ENG-14, HLD-27, CS-24, 05 B3 stale-buffer variant).
- `"a second materialization with the same tainted hunks attaches to the same record"` — save the same TEAM removal plus an untainted `row_55` edit: `holdOutcome` reports `created: false`, one non-terminal record (HLD-07, HLD-28).
- `"a hunk covered by a RESOLVED_PUBLISH record on the path is exempt"` — the record of the TEAM removal is released the way `--publish` does it (`markExplicit`, `releaseGuard(id, 'revert')`, `cs.observe(['f.py'])`), captured, published, and `evaluateRecords` ends it `RESOLVED_PUBLISH`; this peer then re-adds `TEAM = 1` itself (its own add, untainted) and deletes it again: no new record (HLD-29, CS-26).

`test/integration/revert.bomcrlf.test.ts` — Review Focus 1. `makeCores({n: 1, autocrlf: [true], files: {'big.py': generateBigBomCrlf(26_436, seededRandom('t8-rf1'))}})`; the teammate adds row `TEAM_RF = 1` after row 1,000 and replaces row 5,000 with `ROW_5000_TEAM = 2`; applied before each case. "Publishes" means: `advance(20_000)`, `captureReady`, `publishStep` lands.

- `"a stale buffer (the pre-apply bytes plus an edit of row 20,000) saved in place gives one revert PATCH holding both teammate hunks; the row-20,000 edit publishes and the remote keeps TEAM_RF and ROW_5000_TEAM"` — Review Focus 1, 05 P4.
