- `"a second save that keeps the tainted hunks and adds an untainted edit attaches to the same record"` — one id, `created: false` (Review Focus 1, HLD-07).
- `"releasing the record the way --publish does publishes the hunks, R1 ends it RESOLVED_PUBLISH, and the revertDetector then stays quiet when the same hunks reappear"` — the stale-buffer record released (`markExplicit`, `releaseGuard`, `cs.observe`), published, `evaluateRecords` → `RESOLVED_PUBLISH`; this peer re-adds and removes `TEAM_RF = 1` again: no record (Review Focus 1, HLD-29; the Table 4 verb itself is Task 11's).
- `"INV-HLD-NOREVERT over the run: for every commit this peer pushed, a test-side recomputation finds no removed teammate line and no re-added teammate-deleted line unless covered by a RESOLVED_PUBLISH record"` — recomputed with the test's own row diff and its own read of the journal file, never `revertDetector` (05 P4).

`test/integration/mass.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 1})`, 200 tracked files `pkg/m<i>.py` of 20 rows plus `f.py` and `g.py`; the teammate's `TEAM = 1` row in `f.py` is applied; "the formatter" rewrites 180 of the `pkg/` files with quote-style changes in one tick; `breakerTick(core, it)` runs on every tick.
- `"a formatter rewriting 180 files in one tick enters FREEZE(MASS)"` — `freeze_reasons` contains `MASS`; `journal/keep/snap_pre_freeze` is the tree of the last materialized `S_disk` before the tick (`Snapshotter.last().sDisk`, else `view.snap`); the other keep entries are unchanged (Transition.keep is passed as `view.keep` plus the new entry); one `event{kind: 'freeze', reason_code: 'MASS'}` in the outbox; trace `mass {entered: true, paths: 180}` (HLD-30, CS-29, TC-HLD-07, R3-38).
- `"snap_pre_freeze stays reachable under git gc --prune=now"` — every blob of the kept tree is readable after gc (HLD-30, R3-38, ENG-04).
- `"while MASS is set nothing applies and no code publishes, and a revert record made at a capture's materialization mirrors through a board-only push with the freeze event"` — a teammate change arrives (not applied); a stale save deletes `TEAM = 1`; the human CS closes after 20 s and is captured (a materializing iteration, `revertStep` creates the `f.py` record); `publishStep` pushes a board commit carrying the freeze event and the record's `hold` op and no live commit (HLD-31, 01-engine test 25 shape, 05 I7).
- `"nothing auto-releases MASS: 720 iterations over 30 virtual minutes keep it set and push no code"` — HLD-31 ("a timeout never lifts it"), TC-HLD-07.
- `"mutant M33 lets the same formatter publish the 180 files without a freeze"` — `setMutants(p, ['M33'])`: `breakerTick` returns `null` (its one site) and the formatted files reach the live chain, which the zero-code-push assertion of the freeze test catches (TC-HLD-07, M33).
- `"an 11-file node_modules flood of admitted new files trips MASS with 3 tracked files"` — `node_modules/x<i>.js`, each under `sync.maxNewFileBytes`, no ignore rule (D-27, DAT-24 routing).
- `"an unobserved deletion of f.py, which holds the teammate's line, trips peer_lines; deleting g.py, whose lines are all pre-genesis, does not"` — `peerLinesIn` reads the private-index blob of the deleted path (HLD-30 second trigger).
- `"05 I7: formatter plus one stale revert — freeze, the record mirrors, resume, then the formatting publishes and the revert stays held"` — after the board-only window, `resume(core)` lifts MASS; the next materializing iteration runs `massResumeStep`: the `f.py` hunk attaches to the existing record (same id, HLD-07), the 181 changed LIVE paths form one human CS, `snap_pre_freeze` leaves the keep set in the same state update, and the log has `UNFROZEN {reason: 'MASS', residue: [<id>]}`; after 20 s one live commit carries the 180 formatted files and `f.py` equal to `A[f.py]`; the record stays ON_DISK ACTIVE (05 I7, HLD-31, CS-31, R3-38).
- `"a kill after massResumeStep's registry commit and before its state update re-runs the batch after restart with no duplicate record"` — kill at the second `registry.cas` call site; restart: `snap_pre_freeze` is still kept, `massResumeStep` runs again and the record count is unchanged (R3-38, HLD-07, 05 F7 shape).
- `"massResumePending is true from resume until massResumeStep's state update, and massResumeStep returns null when nothing is pending"` — contract addition 3 (the loop and publish uses of the predicate are Task 12a's test).
- `"breakerTick observes nothing while MASS is set or the post-resume batch is pending, so the formatted paths never re-trip MASS after resume"` — a restart between `resume` and the batch (fresh breaker memory) does not re-enter MASS (R3-38).

`test/integration/resume.test.ts`
- `"resume clears meta.nonretryable and the streak and releases the host-reject guard of meta.nonretryable.hold_id; the PIN's group publishes in the next window and R1 ends it SUBSUMED"` — Plan 2's setup: `wrapGit` rejects three pushes of the same publish with the board unchanged → one `host-reject` PIN over the CS's paths; `resume(core)` returns `{nonretryable: true, hostRejectReleased: <id>, massLifted: false}`; the next `publishStep` lands; `evaluateRecords` ends the record `SUBSUMED` (ENG-45, R3-39, Table 3 `host-reject`).
- `"three new rejections after resume add host-reject again to the same record"` — one id, a new rev, guard `host-reject` back (HLD-07, Table 3 "a new NONRETRYABLE classification of it adds the guard again").
- `"the pathless host-reject PIN of a board-only NONRETRYABLE ends SUBSUMED in resume's registry commit with host-reject kept"` — `resume(core)` returns `hostRejectReleased: <id>`; the record is SUBSUMED with guards still `[host-reject]` and `paths: []` (no `releaseGuard` call: a spy registry records none); its `records/<id>.json` re-parses with `parseRecord`; its terminal `hold` op parses with `parseOpLine` and folds without an own rejection (R3-11, R3-39, the Plan 2 Task 14 carry).
- `"resume lifts MASS, keeps snap_pre_freeze and drafts one event{kind: unfreeze, reason_code: MASS}"` — `ResumeReport.massLifted: true` (R3-38, contract addition 5).
- `"resume with nothing pending reports all false and makes no state or registry commit"` — R3-39.
- `"resume clears NONRETRYABLE when the host-reject record it names is already terminal or missing"` — the host-reject PIN ended GONE by R1 (the human reverted the edit, `S_disk == A`), and in a second fixture ended RESOLVED_DISCARD by `transition(id, {state: 'RESOLVED_DISCARD'})` (Task 11's verb), and in a third deleted from a scratch registry: `resume(core)` makes no registry commit, returns `hostRejectReleased: null`, and its one state CAS clears `meta.nonretryable` and the streak (R3-39, R-28).
- `"node dist/daemon.js resume refuses with exit 1 while a live daemon holds daemon.lock, takes the lock itself for its run (a dead pid's lock is replaced, R-26), and with no daemon prints the ResumeReport as one JSON line and exits 0; Engine.resumeAfterNonretryable() returns report.nonretryable"` — `runDaemon` in process with a capturing `DaemonIo` (R-28 extended, R3-02, R3-39).

`test/unit/lint.rules.test.ts` (modify)
- `"L-HOLD-STORES fails on a planted source naming refs/cowork-held/, refs/worktree/cowork/, cowork/quarantine/ or cowork/conflicts/"` — HLD-12, A11.
- `"L-HOLD-STORES fails when a second module calls git with -U0 or --unified=0, and when src/holds/revert.ts calls diff or merge-file outside revertDetector and taintOnlyH1"` — a planted `src/engine/x.ts` with `['diff', '-U0', a, b]` fails the build (05 U2, HLD-18, CS-27).
- `"L-HOLD-STORES fails on a merge-file argv with --union or --theirs anywhere and with --ours outside revertInverse() in src/holds/revertCs.ts, on the literal state=wip and on a hold kind 'SNAPSHOT' or 'TRANSFORM' in src/"` — planted sources: `--ours` inside a function `revertInverse` in `src/holds/revertCs.ts` passes, a second injected `--ours` caller (another function of that file, or `src/engine/x.ts`) fails the build (05 U2 rev 2.5, glossary §29.3, F-P3-6/X-09); HLD-12, D-23.
- `"L-HOLD-STORES passes on the real src/ tree"` — A11.

**Crashpoints:** none new (tests kill at `registry.cas` and `apply.state`). **Lint:** adds L-HOLD-STORES (§Interfaces table).

**Notes:** `revertStep` builds one `DetectorDelta` per LIVE path where `it.mat.prevMap[p]` (or ABSENT) differs from `it.mat.sDiskMap[p]` (or ABSENT), drops a delta when `core.observed.consume(p, next)` or `deps.attribution.observedEdit(p)` is true, passes the registry's RESOLVED_PUBLISH records as `resolved`, then calls `holdReverts`. Inside `revertDetector`: a text delta is `git diff -U0 --no-color --no-ext-diff <prev> <next>`; an ABSENT side means every row of the other side is added or removed (no git call); ws-equal pairs are paired greedily inside one hunk; a tainted hunk is exempt when a RESOLVED_PUBLISH record r on p rebases cleanly onto A (`cache.rebase(r, A).ok`) and every tainted row of the hunk is one r's own `H0[p] → H1[p]` change removes or adds. `breakerTick` feeds every `it.tick.dirty` path in `it.tick.changed` with `deletion = kind === 'remove'` and `observed = core.observed.peek(p, id ?? ABSENT) || deps.attribution.observedEdit(p)`, `tracked = it.A.size`; it returns `null` without observing under mutant M33, while MASS is set and while `massResumePending(core)`. `enterMass` and `resume` pass `Transition.keep` as `view.keep` with only `snap_pre_freeze` added or kept (the transition replaces the whole keep set).

---

### Task 9: Change-set table — human, recovered and seat CSs, signals, captures, journal, trailers

**Covers:** CS-01, CS-02, CS-03, CS-04, CS-05, CS-06, CS-08, CS-09 (engine side); HLD-33…HLD-37, HLD-40; 05 U6, U9, F2, I12 (trailer half); R3-05, R3-40, R3-43, R3-68, R3-69 (the `restore` semantics). Depends on: Tasks 2, 4, 6. Parallel with Task 8.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/holds/signals.ts`, `src/engine/csStep.ts`
- Modify: `src/holds/changesets.ts` (CS table, seat and recovered CSs, signals, group captures with a private group closure, `HeldPin`, `CsTableEntry.covered`, the first table's orders and the tableless `order = n` mode (R3-68), `restore(ready, nextN, journaled?, cache?)` (R3-69), pending green/tool checkpoints in `closeDue`, `resetScratchIndex` from `src/git/scratchIndex.ts` before every scratch `read-tree`, `trailer`, `published`, `table`), `src/engine/state.ts` (seat cs_ids in `journal/ready/`, `ReadyPair.order` from `journal/keep/cs/table.json`, Plan 2's `order === n` check kept while no table exists, `readCsTable`, `csTableKeep`), `src/engine/publish.ts` (1-based trailers from `cs.trailer`, the `Cowork-Seat` list, the CS table keep entry in the push-ok state update only when `readCsTable(repo, view)` is non-null, as `captureReady` does, until Task 12a routes every loop capture through `captureStep`; from Task 12a on it is written unconditionally), `test/helpers/publishHelpers.ts` (`captureReady` commits `csTableKeep(repo, core.cs.table())` with `keep = view.keep` and only the `cs` entry replaced whenever `readCsTable(repo, view)` is non-null, as `captureStep` does, so Tasks 7–11's tests may mix it with `captureStep`), `test/integration/publish.test.ts` (the trailer assertion at line 88 expects `seq: 1`, R3-43). `test/integration/apply.test.ts` (four `.pair` sites) and `test/integration/changesets.test.ts` compile unchanged because `capture` keeps Plan 2's call shape (R3-40).
- Test: `test/unit/changesets.table.test.ts`, `test/integration/cstable.test.ts`, `test/integration/recovered.test.ts`, `test/integration/trailers.test.ts`

Wave E disjointness: Task 9 does not edit `core.ts` (Task 12a adds `attachCsTable` after `attachState`, R3-69), `apply.ts`, `start.ts`, `coreFixture.ts`, `snapshot.ts`, `observed.ts`, `loop.ts`, `restart.ts` or `daemon/run.ts` (all but `core.ts` are Task 8's in this wave, and Task 8 also edits `core.ts`); `ChangeSets.capture` keeps Plan 2's call shape (`pins` optional, `pair` kept, R3-40), so Plan 2's `loop.ts` and `start.ts` compile and behave unchanged until Task 12a routes the loop's capture through `captureStep` and Task 12b does `start.ts`'s `captureLocalWork`; its tests call `ChangeSets.restore(…)`, `queueSignal` and `captureStep` directly.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 2 `ChangeSets` members, `ReadyPair`, `readyKey`, `HUMAN_CS_QUIET_MS`, `RebaseChange`, `StateStore`, `StateView`, `Transition`, `Meta.cs_human_next`, `CsTrailer`, `liveCommitTrailers`, `parseCsTrailers`, `encodeCsPaths`, `IncomingCs`, `validId`, `canonicalJson`, `enc`, `EngineCore` (`cs`, `commit`, `view`, `scratch('r')`, `repo`, `writer`, `deps.paths`, `deps.ports.fs`), `IterationCtx`, `captureDue`, `Writer.strandedPaths`, `prepareIteration`, `publishStep`, `applyPushOk`, `Fs`, `FaultFs`; test helpers `makeCores`, `writeWork`, `prep`, `captureReady`, `remoteCode`, `treeOf`, `gitCallsOf`, `VirtualClock`; Task 1 `TraceEvent`, `HoldRegistry` (spy on `offerEvent`); Task 2 the asynchronous `composeP` mask; Task 4 the publish step's mirror sync (unchanged); `resetScratchIndex`, `ScratchResetError` (`src/git/scratchIndex.ts`, P2-SCRATCHLOCK; never a private copy such as Plan 2 Task 15's `resetCaptureIndex`).
- Produces: `SeatActivity`, `CsSignal` (with `checks_run`); `CsKind`, `CsTableEntry` (with `covered`), `CsTable`, `HeldPin`; `ChangeSets.restore` (with the table), `observe`, `signal`, `done`, `closeDue`, `capture` (group form, Plan 2 call shape kept, `pins` defaulting to `[]`), `published`, `trailer`, `ready`, `counts`, `openPaths`, `table`; `readCsTable`, `csTableKeep`; `queueSignal`, `captureStep` (closing and checkpoint captures with P2-STRANDED-SKIP; candidates are Task 10's), `writeCsCache`, `readCsCache`; contract additions 1–8.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — (1) `ChangeSets.restore(ready, nextN, journaled?, cache?)` (R3-69; `attachCsTable`, its engine caller, is Task 12a's); (2) `CsTable.passing`; (3) the capture shape, (4) Plan 3 attribution and (5) `checks` from the latest `checks_run` signal (comments on `CsTable`); (6) the `cs/<enc(b)>.json` format and the `writeCsCache` callers; (7) `CsTableEntry.covered` with its green/tool-checkpoint rule and the later-effect reset, the `HeldPin` type and the private group closure `capture` uses (called with `pins = []` until Task 10 passes `heldPins`); (8) the first table's orders and `capture`'s kept call shape (R3-40), and `captureStep`'s P2-STRANDED-SKIP (R3-23). Task 10 keeps the edit-check pass that sets `covered`, the public `groups(pins)` with PIN exclusion and bundling. The tests' "contract addition n" refers to these numbers.

**Spec:** 05-holds §2 (passing capture), HLD-33…HLD-37, HLD-40, §8 Q16; glossary §3 (ready pair, cs_id), §4 (`journal/ready/`, `journal/keep/`), §5 (`Cowork-CS`, `Cowork-CS-Paths`, `Cowork-Seat`), §6 (`cs/<enc(b)>.json`), §25 F2; 06-claude §3.12.1 (the signal set); 01-engine ENG-04, ENG-13 (b) (engine side); tests 05 U6, U9, F2, I12 (trailer half); rulings R3-05, R3-40, R3-43.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/changesets.table.test.ts` — a `ChangeSets` on a `VirtualClock`; seat ids are 32 lowercase hex (`'a'.repeat(32)`, `'b'.repeat(32)`); signals are scripted (R3-05).
- `"seat cs_ids are <seat_id>/<turn-seq> counted from 1 per seat, including a subagent CS opening after the parent's stop; human cs_ids are <peer_id>/human/<n>"` — `effect(A)` → `A/1`; `stop(A)`; `effect(A)` → `A/2`; `effect(A, agent 'x')` while `A/2` is open → member of `A/2`; `stop(A)`; `effect(A, agent 'x')` → `A/3`; `effect(B)` → `B/1`; every id passes `validId('cs', …)` (HLD-33, CS-01).
- `"agent, session and prompt ids are CS-table attributes, never part of the cs_id"` — `A/3`'s entry has `agent: 'x'` (HLD-33).
- `"a seat CS opens at its first file effect, never at a prompt; it closes at stop, stop_failure, session_end and seat_end; idle_10min closes it only when the activity is IDLE or ENDED and no TXN is open"` — a table of signal → closed? including `idle_10min{MID_TURN}`, `idle_10min{AWAITING_PERMISSION}`, `idle_10min{IDLE, txnOpen: true}` → stays open (HLD-34, CS-02, R3-05).
- `"calls with an agent join the parent's open CS; after the parent's stop they form their own CS that closes only at that agent's subagent_stop or seat_end"` — HLD-34.
- `"all paths of one effect signal enter the same CS (never split below one tool_use)"` — HLD-34.
- `"05 U9: a nonce prompt mid-turn leaves the open CS, its cs_id and its origin unchanged and fires no offer event; at an idle seat the next effect with turnOrigin wake opens a new CS with origin wake; a prompt without a nonce changes no CS"` — a spy `HoldRegistry` records no `offerEvent` call (U9, CS-03, HLD-34, R3-06).
- `"unattributed deltas form the human CS, which closes 20 s Δmono after its last change or at done(); paths an effect claimed since the previous observe stay with the seat CS"` — HLD-35, CS-04, contract addition 4.
- `"a checkpoint{source: edit} names only seat CSs: the human CS and a recovered CS never get a pending candidate"` — HLD-35, HLD-36, U10 (human and recovered half).
- `"queueSignal asks for an out-of-cycle iteration exactly for stop, stop_failure, subagent_stop, session_end, seat_end, checkpoint (green, tool, edit) and an idle_10min that closes; never for effect, prompt, activity, checks_run or an idle_10min that keeps the CS open"` — CS-09 engine side, ENG-13 (b)/(c).

`test/integration/cstable.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 1})`; signals through `queueSignal(core, s)`; a capture is a materializing iteration plus `captureStep(core, it)`.
- `"a capture at a seat CS's stop journals one ready pair per member CS of the group, each over its own paths, with one shared order, R = S_disk unmasked and A_cap = A"` — `A/1` (effects on `a.py`, `b.py`) and the open human CS (`b.py`, `c.py`) form one group; at `stop(A)` two pairs land under `journal/ready/<base64url(cs_id)>/{R,A}` with equal `order`; `A/1`'s R has `S_disk[a.py]`, `S_disk[b.py]` even where an ON_DISK record masks them (R is not masked) (HLD-40, CS-08, contract addition 3).
- `"the capture's state update carries journal/keep/cs/table.json as canonical JSON and leaves the other keep entries unchanged; a state commit that captures nothing does not rewrite the table"` — R3-40, CS-05, ENG-04.
- `"readCsTable round-trips the table and csTableKeep names it 'cs'; StateStore.read accepts seat cs_ids in journal/ready and takes ReadyPair.order from the table; without a table a human pair's order is n"` — R3-40, CS-01 migration of `state.ts`.
- `"commit() refuses a ready pair whose order the table does not name once a table exists"` — R3-40.
- `"the first table names every Plan 2-style pair: a group capture after a human pair journaled without a table gets a strictly larger order and its commit is accepted"` — a pair `<peer>/human/1` journaled by Plan 2's path (no table, order = n = 1), then a seat group capture through `captureStep`: the written table has `orders['<peer>/human/1'] = 1`, `next_order` 3 after the capture, the new pairs' order 2, and `StateStore.commit` accepts the update; `captureReady` after that commits the table keep entry and is accepted too (R3-40, contract addition 8).
- `"capture keeps Plan 2's call shape"` — `capture(repo, sDisk, A, scratchIndex)` with no `pins` returns `{pair, pairs, nextN}` with `pair === pairs[0]` for a lone human CS, as Plan 2's callers read it (R3-40).
- `"without a journaled table a Plan 2-style capture keeps order = n and is accepted"` — a restart with `meta.cs_human_next = 5` and no journaled pair or table, then a Plan 2-style capture (the loop's call, no `captureStep`): the pair `<peer>/human/5` has order 5, no table is written and `StateStore.commit` accepts it; a later `captureStep` capture writes the first table with `orders['<peer>/human/5'] = 5` and `next_order` 6 before the capture (R3-68).
- `"a Plan 2-shape capture → publish → capture cycle with no captureStep call writes no table and every state update is accepted"` — Plan 2's loop capture, a landed `publishStep` (its push-ok update writes no `cs` keep entry while none is journaled) and a second Plan 2 capture: no state commit holds `journal/keep/cs/`, and `StateStore.commit` accepts all three (R3-40, R3-68).
- `"restore takes orders, next_order and passing from the journaled table and only open membership from the cache"` — a journaled table naming a non-passing pair of `A/1` and `next_order` 7, with (a) a fresh cache, (b) a stale cache and (c) no cache: in every case `passing[A/1]` stays false, the next capture's order is 7, and `restore(ready, n)` with `journaled` undefined (Plan 2's error-path call) keeps the in-memory table unchanged; only in (b) and (c) the unpublished deltas outside a ready pair form one `recovered` CS (R3-69, HLD-36).
- `"no capture is journaled while a restore is stranded"` — `Writer.strandedPaths()` non-empty (a `FaultFs` rule leaves one restore stranded), a human CS due to close and a materializing iteration: `captureStep` returns `{captured: [], candidates: []}` and the state commit is unchanged; after the stranded restore completes the next materializing iteration captures (R3-23, Plan 2's P2-STRANDED-SKIP, contract addition 8).
- `"a materialization that captureDue did not ask for captures nothing"` — an iteration materialized for a `holdPaths` occasion only while no CS close is due: `captureStep` journals no pair (R3-23).
- `"a non-passing capture never replaces an unpublished passing pair, and a CS without a pair always gets one"` — `A/1` captured at a green checkpoint (passing); the human CS of the same group (open, uncovered) closes later while `A/1` has a newer uncovered effect: `A/1`'s journaled pair keeps its oids and `passing[A/1]` stays `true`; the human CS gets a pair (contract addition 2, HLD-39, HLD-34 "a closed CS always has a ready pair").
- `"a newer passing capture of the same group supersedes the older one"` — two green checkpoints: the second capture's pairs replace the first's (HLD-40).
- `"edits after the trigger stay in the open CS and are not in the journaled pair"` — an effect after the green checkpoint's capture: the pair's R keeps the captured blob (HLD-40).
- `"a capture is where R is first written: ticks before it write no object and its iteration writes each dirty group blob once"` — `gitCallsOf`: no `hash-object -w`/`add` at the ticks; one materialization at the capture (HLD-40, glossary §26.1 W6, 05 B3 shape).
- `"writeCsCache after a membership-changing signal writes cs/<enc(b)>.json naming the current state commit with the open seat CS's members, and readCsCache returns it only when it names view.commit"` — `effect(A, ['a.py'])`, `writeCsCache`; the file's `state` is `view.commit` and its table lists `A/1` with paths `['a.py']`; after a later state commit without a rewrite `readCsCache` returns `null` (HLD-36, R3-40, contract addition 6).

`test/integration/recovered.test.ts`
- `"05 F2: with cs/<enc(b)>.json deleted mid-turn, every unpublished delta outside a ready pair forms one recovered CS with a human-class cs_id, and the journaled turn_seq keeps the seat's next CS at <seat>/<turn_seq + 1>"` — seat `A/2` open with effects on `a.py` (a signature change) and `b.py`; delete the cache; `restore(view.ready, meta.cs_human_next, await readCsTable(repo, view), null)`: one `recovered` CS over `a.py` and `b.py`; the next `effect(A)` opens `A/3` (F2, HLD-36, CS-05, contract addition 1).
- `"the recovered CS stays open while a seat reported MID_TURN or AWAITING_PERMISSION, closes 20 s after that seat's stop, and its capture publishes a.py and b.py in one commit"` — `activity{A, MID_TURN}` then 60 s quiet: still open; `stop(A)` then `advance(20_000)`: captured; one live commit holds both files (F2 "no half publish", HLD-36).
- `"with no seat activity the recovered CS closes after 20 s quiet like the human CS, and done() closes it at once"` — HLD-36 (the no-seat form, R3-05).
- `"a stale cache naming an older state commit is treated as lost; a fresh one restores the open seat CS with its members"` — HLD-36, R3-40.
- `"a seat effect after recovery opens a new seat CS covering only its own paths"` — HLD-36 ("Claude edit checkpoints after the loss open a new seat CS").

`test/integration/trailers.test.ts`
- `"05 U6: Cowork-CS trailers round-trip cs_id, state, seq, origin, checks and paths for a seat CS and a human CS"` — `<32 hex>/3 state=checkpoint seq=2 origin=wake checks=pass paths=2` and `<peer>/human/4 state=closed seq=1 origin=human checks=skipped paths=1` through `liveCommitTrailers` → commit message → `parseCsTrailers` (U6, HLD-37, CS-06).
- `"a CS with 257 paths carries no Cowork-CS-Paths and the receiver's IncomingCs covers the whole commit"` — U6, HLD-37.
- `"the first publish of a human CS carries seq=1 state=closed origin=human checks=skipped and Cowork-Seat: human"` — replaces Plan 2's ratified `seq=0` (R3-43, HLD-37).
- `"05 I12 trailer half: a seat CS published at a green checkpoint, then at a tool checkpoint, then at its stop carries seq=1 state=checkpoint checks=pass, seq=2 state=checkpoint checks=pass and seq=3 state=closed, with one Cowork-Seat line naming the seat"` — each capture followed by one landed `publishStep` (I12 trailer half, R3-43, contract addition 5).
- `"a stop that changed nothing since the last published capture makes no push"` — P == A for the CS's paths, no trailer-only commit (05 §8 Q16, I12).
- `"a CS of a wake-originated turn carries origin=wake and a prompt-originated one origin=prompt"` — HLD-34, HLD-37.
- `"checks follows the latest checks_run before the capture: green then failing then capture gives checks=fail, failing then green gives checks=pass, none gives checks=skipped"` — scripted `checks_run {ok: true}`, `checks_run {ok: false}`, then a green-checkpoint capture of the seat CS: the published `Cowork-CS` trailer carries `checks=fail` (HLD-37, R3-43, contract addition 5).
- `"a publish carrying two CSs carries one Cowork-CS per CS and one Cowork-Seat per contributing seat and human"` — glossary §5, HLD-37.
- `"published() raises the CS's publish count in the table written by the push-ok state update, and a closed, fully published CS leaves the table at that update"` — R3-40, Algorithm P step 7.

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** none.

**Notes:** `Transition.keep` replaces the whole keep set, so `captureStep` and the push-ok update pass `view.keep` with only the `cs` entry replaced. A pending green or tool checkpoint makes `closeDue()` (and so `captureDue(core)`) true until its capture runs, so the loop materializes for it (Task 12a). Captures run only when `it.mat` is non-null, `Writer.strandedPaths()` is empty and, for closing captures, `captureDue(core)` holds (R3-23; `captureDue(core)` drives the materialization until Task 12a adds the other occasions). The private group closure `capture` uses is HLD-38's over open and ready CSs plus the given `pins`; Task 10 makes it the public `groups(pins)`.

`test/integration/pinCs.test.ts`
- `"pinCs builds a PIN over the LIVE paths it is given with H0 = A, H1 = the given map, the guard, origin, cs_ids and offer; shadowRecord is an OFF_DISK PARKED PATCH with guard claim:<id>, offer pending_leftover and displaced_by null"` — HLD-46, HLD-47 constructors, CS-34, CS-35, R3-07.
- `"a CS editing src/a.py, .github/workflows/ci.yml (QUARANTINED) and .gitignore (SAVE_ONLY) pinned with pinCs over its LIVE paths records only src/a.py, and its op and held/ entries name only src/a.py"` — the other two stay on disk under MASK step 1 (TC-HLD-09 shape, HLD-03, CS-33 shared rule).
- `"a second hold of the same pinCs (same paths and h1) attaches to the first record"` — one id (HLD-07, CS-34 "repeat hits join the same record").

`test/integration/editcheck.test.ts` — scripted `EditCheckPort`s through `CoreOptions.ports`; a candidate is a `checkpoint{source: 'edit', candSeq, paths}` signal, a materializing iteration and `captureStep`.
- `"grammarlessEditCheck passes checks 1–3 and fails check 4 when a touched path whose TIP blob is non-empty merges to absent, empty or under 50 % of TIP's stored size"` — `merge-tree --merge-base=A R TIP` restricted to the CS's paths; a 1,000-byte TIP blob merged to 499 bytes → `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [4]}`; 500 bytes, growth and a path absent from TIP → `{verdict: 'pass'}` (R3-03, 04-regions §3.20, HLD-67 step 2's Plan 3 binding).
- `"a passing candidate of the CS's publication group becomes the group's ready pairs in one state update, and the port saw (A, R, TIP, the CS's own paths)"` — trace `edit_check {cs, verdict: 'pass'}`; the journaled pairs carry the candidate's R oids (HLD-67 steps 1 and 3, R3-41, CS-10).
- `"a failing candidate leaves the state commit, the registry head, the outbox and core.holdCode unchanged and its R blobs referenced by no ref"` — only the trace `edit_check {verdict: 'fail'}` and (rev 2.5, D1) the memory-only `core.cs.lastCheck` slot; `git for-each-ref` reaches none of the candidate blobs; `status.json`, the daemon log and the board carry nothing new (U10, HLD-67 step 4, ENG-13, 01-engine test 56 core, glossary §29.8).
- `"an incomplete verdict is a fail"` — U10, HLD-67 step 2.
- `"rev 2.5: a failing candidate writes last_check from its first entry — fail with check, path and line, conflict for merge_conflict, incomplete for an incomplete verdict or a no_snapshot/no_merge entry, more = the other entries — and a verdict without entries gives check = failed[0]"` — scripted ports: `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [2, 3], entries: [{check: 2, path: 'a.py', line: 14, reason: 'caller'}, {check: 3, path: 'b.py', line: 3, reason: 'unresolved'}]}` → `{kind: 'fail', check: 2, path: 'a.py', line: 14, more: 1}`; `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [], entries: [{path: 'a.py', reason: 'merge_conflict'}]}` → `{kind: 'conflict', path: 'a.py', more: 0}` with no `check`; `{verdict: 'incomplete'}` → `{kind: 'incomplete', more: 0}`; `grammarlessEditCheck`'s `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [4]}` → `{kind: 'fail', check: 4, more: 0}`; `edit_n` = candSeq unless the test noted another value first (X-05, X-06, X-08, HLD-67 step 4, 10 §3.5.1).
- `"rev 2.5: a later passing candidate with a higher edit_n clears last_check and sets last_pass.ready; its publish sets last_pass.shared; the CS's close clears the slot"` — 10 §3.5.1, glossary §29.8 "cleared when the CS closes or a passing capture with a higher edit_n lands".
- `"rev 2.5: last_check is never in the CS table, the cs/<enc(b)>.json cache, a state commit or the registry, and is empty after a restart"` — byte checks of `journal/keep/cs/table.json`, the cache file and the registry tree after a failing candidate; `restart(core)` → `lastCheck.get(cs)` null (glossary §29.8 "lost at daemon restart").
- `"rev 2.5: an HLD-41 withdrawal of a passing capture clears last_pass.ready"` — the readiness test's case (ii) fixture with a noted candidate (10 §3.5.1 "follows the journal").
- `"a fail after a pass leaves the earlier ready pair journaled and it publishes in the next window"` — U10, TC-HLD-11 (c) (the behaviour M47 breaks; its mutant site is Plan 5's, R3-50).
- `"an older candSeq never replaces a newer passing capture"` — candSeq 5 passes, then candSeq 4's verdict arrives as pass: the journaled pair stays candSeq 5's (HLD-67 step 3, glossary §27.1 "newest passing capture wins").
- `"a candidate whose group path cannot be read stably fails silently: no pair, no record, no message"` — `FaultFs` makes the staged read of `b.py` unstable during the candidate (HLD-67 step 1).
- `"a human CS and a recovered CS never call EditCheckPort"` — a counting port stays at 0 calls over their captures (U10, HLD-35, HLD-36).
- `"05 I12 Plan 3 part: edits 40 s apart with verdicts fail [2], pass, pass publish nothing after the first, both edits in one commit with state=checkpoint seq=1 after the second, seq=2 after the third, and state=closed at the stop only if something changed after the third capture"` — the edits are `a.py` (signature change), `b.py` (callers), `test_a.py`; the receiver's build clause is Plan 5's (I12, HLD-37, HLD-39, HLD-67, TC-HLD-11 (b)).
- `"TC-HLD-11 (g): a seat editing every 10 s for 10 virtual minutes with every check failing publishes nothing until a check passes or the turn ends"` — 60 failing candidates, no live commit; the first passing one publishes (TC-HLD-11 (g), HLD-12 "no time-capped WIP", CS-07).
- `"TC-HLD-11 (e): a human CS is captured only after 20 s quiet or done()"` — TC-HLD-11 (e), HLD-35, CS-07.

`test/integration/bundle.test.ts`
- `"05 I2 path bundle: a scripted gate blocking CS1 (pricing.py, orders.py) on claim abcdefghij234567 pins it; CS2 touching orders.py and reports.py joins the PIN at its close; when the claim releases one commit holds all three files"` — the gate makes one PIN `claim:abcdefghij234567` over CS1's LIVE paths (`H0 = A`, `H1 = R`, every group path published as `A[p]`, the disk untouched); CS2's close grows that record (paths ∪, `cs_ids` ∪, `h1` refreshed from R, one rev, one `hold` op) with no `dependency` guard naming the record itself and no ready pair for CS2; the live chain shows none of the three files changed while held; `releaseGuard(id, 'claim:abcdefghij234567')` stands in for the claim PREDICATE's release (`recordStep`'s `claimStillBlocks` check is Task 12a's), the group is re-captured and one live commit carries all three files with both `Cowork-CS` trailers (I2 path bundle, HLD-45 slot, HLD-49, HLD-52, CS-33, CS-37, CS-38, R3-44, contract addition 5).

`test/integration/publishGroups.test.ts`
- `"HLD-44: two independent ready groups publish in one push with one Cowork-CS trailer per CS, and a passing capture arriving before the window replaces the older one in that push"` — HLD-44, CS-17.
- `"05 I17 Plan 3 part: four peers whose seats pass an edit check every 10–30 virtual seconds for 10 virtual minutes make at most 12 push attempts in every 60 s window and never publish a partial capture"` — `makeCores({n: 4})` with the governor at B = 12; the p50 latency and the HTTP 429 fallback are the soak's and Plan 2's (I17, HLD-44, glossary §27.2).
- `"TC-HLD-08 at core level: every live commit of a 3-peer scripted run mixing seat and human CSs equals its parent plus whole group captures, never a strict subset of a group capture's hunks except exceptions (a)–(c)"` — a test-side check over every pushed commit (TC-HLD-08, O-ATOMIC-PUB, CS-21; the receiver-build clause is Plan 5's).
- `"Algorithm P order: groups, gate, composeP, dependency PINs, assertP, OUT, T0, and the gate gets the rebased R, never S_disk"` — scripted ports record their call order and arguments (CS-18, HLD-45 input, ENG-39).
- `"publishStep emits one compose trace event per built P tree, CAS re-merges and K7/drop/dependency recompositions included, naming the pairs, groups and withheldWhole that composeP used"` — a 2-group window with a dependency PIN, then a `wrapGit` lease loss: two `compose` events before the landed push, each with `tip`, `aTree`, `registry` = the registry head, `pTree` equal to the tree of the composed P, every journaled ready pair (`csId`, `R`, `A`, `order`, `paths`), the groups with their `pins` and the engine's `ready`, and the `withheldWhole` entry; no `compose` event in a window that composes nothing, and none for a T0 trial composition (Task 14's INV-MASK and O-ATOMIC-PUB input, X-03, R3-63).
- `"the throttle event's otherPending counts only ready, unheld groups"` — Plan 2's R-18 throttle path with one group held by a `join` PIN and one not-ready open group: `pendingCheap` reports no other pending code; with one ready unheld group it reports it (R-18, HLD-39).
- `"under openGate no record with a claim guard is created in a 3-peer human run"` — R3-07.

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** none.

**Notes:** `publishStep` order (header §Interfaces): mirror sync → `groups(heldPins)` with readiness from `CsTable.passing` and R3-53's blocked paths → B1 slot `EngineDeps.gate.gate(repo, tip, A, R, paths, csIds)` per ready group (a block → `hold(pinCs('claim:<id>', …))`, offer `pending_leftover` while a member CS is open) → `composeP` → `hold(pinCs('dependency:<id>', …))` per `withheldWhole` entry that leaves a LIVE path outside `local_only` record paths (none otherwise, R3-44), then recompose → `assertP` (Task 2) → OUT and conflicts → T0 (`t0Shrink` with `deps.attribution.shrinkAttributed`, then `t0Regions.check`) with K7 or drop, recompose without the group, each pass removing ≥ 1 group → Bnew → ENG-41 → push. Rev 2.5: a `t0Shrink` failure becomes `{pass: false, paths, item: 3, findings: [{check: 4, path}…]}` (path order) so the open-CS drop and the K7 path read one form; the drop calls `core.cs.lastCheck.recordFor(csId, candSeq, {kind: 'stale', …findings[0], more: findings.length − 1})` for the dropped capture's candidate (nothing when the capture was not a candidate); the `attributed` callback passed to `t0Shrink` is the one place Task 17 ORs the revert shrink mark (`core.cs.revertMarks.covers(p, OUT[p])`), so this task keeps it a single expression.

### Task 11: Resolve — Table 4 verbs, HLD-57 rules, collision and generated? answers, copy-restore, daemon subcommand

**Covers:** H1, H2, H3, H4, H5, H6, H7, H8, H9, F7 (copy-restore), D-09; HLD-57…HLD-59, HLD-64 (copy-restore), Table 4, the §1 refusal table, DAT-29 verbs, DAT-33 refusal, DAT-39 answers; 05 U5 (resolve parts), U8, I9 (copy-restore), I13, I15, TC-HLD-10, I-DAT-08 (verbs), I-DAT-09 (`--publish` clause), I-DAT-20 (exclude half), F-HLD-1 engine parts; R3-02, R3-06 (handoff), R3-28, R3-60 (`--discard`), R3-65. Depends on: Tasks 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 10 (Task 8's `revertStep` for the HLD-57 (e) test). **[P2 contract files]** (`loop.ts`, `daemon/run.ts`, `entry/daemon.ts`, `test/helpers/engineFixture.ts`).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/resolve.ts`
- Modify: `src/holds/registry.ts` (`HoldRegistry.markKeepSyncing`; `RegistryLocal.keepSyncing` is declared by Task 1), `src/engine/dataRecords.ts` (`churnStep` skips `keepSyncing` paths), `src/engine/loop.ts` (`Engine.resolve`), `src/daemon/run.ts` (`resolve` subcommand taking `daemon.lock` exclusively and running `restart(core)` first, `DaemonCommand`, `DaemonArgs.id`/`verb`, `parseDaemonArgs`), `src/entry/daemon.ts` (usage text), `test/helpers/engineFixture.ts` (`resolve(peer, id, verb)`)
- Test: `test/unit/claudeRestore.test.ts`, `test/integration/resolve.table.test.ts`, `test/integration/resolve.publish.test.ts`, `test/integration/resolve.discard.test.ts`, `test/integration/resolve.collision.test.ts`, `test/integration/resolve.copyrestore.test.ts`, `test/integration/daemon.resolve.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 2 `Writer.writeLocal`/`writeLocalGroup`, `prepareIteration` (`holdPaths`), `Engine`, `runDaemon`, `parseDaemonArgs`, `DaemonArgs`, `DaemonIo`, `FoldState.peers` (`status: 'left'`), `LeaveOp`, `Outbox.add`, `isBinaryBytes`; test helpers `makeCores`, `makeEngines`, `pushTeamChange`, `writeWork`, `readWork`, `prep`, `captureReady`, `remoteCode`, `treeOf`, `FaultFs.holdLock`, `FaultFs.interpose`; Task 1 `HoldRegistry` (`get`, `transition`, `commitMany`, `addGuard`, `releaseGuard`, `offerEvent`, `markExplicit`, `local`, `isCompanion`, `head`), `guardRow`, `GUARD_TABLE`, `isUnknownGuard`, `makeGuard`, `HOLD_CODES`, `ResolveRefusal`, `NEXT_COMMAND`, `offerNext`; Task 2 `maskEntries` (r's step-5 result alone), `MaskInputs`; Task 3 `FoldedHold`, `readHeld`; Task 4 the publish step's mirror sync (op `prev_rev` by `mirrorSync`'s rule), `isDurable`; Task 8 `revertStep`, `resume`; Task 5 `settleHoldOps` (with `InflightOp.op === 'discard'`); `Snapshotter.setEntry`, `editTreeIndexOnly` (R3-60); Task 5 `park`, `markersPark`, `restore`, `collisionTake`, `ObservedWrites.record`, `evaluateRecords`; Task 6 collision records, `tickSkip` collision paths, `incomingStep`; Task 7 `churnStep`, `portabilityStep`; Task 10 `releaseDependencyGuards`, `pinCs`, `shadowRecord`, `heldPins`.
- Produces: `ResolveVerb`, `ResolveOptions`, `ClaudeRestoreReason`, `ResolveResult` (with `claude_refused`), `resolve`, `claudeRestoreRefusal`; `Engine.resolve`; `DaemonCommand` `'resolve'`, `DaemonArgs.id`/`verb`; `EngineFixture.resolve`; contract additions 1–4.

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — (1) `RegistryLocal.keepSyncing`, `HoldRegistry.markKeepSyncing` and `churnStep`'s keep-syncing suppression in `dataRecords.ts`; (2) the verb combinations Table 4 leaves open (`--discard` of an ON_DISK CONFLICTED record writes `A[p]`), the ON_DISK `--discard` as an `inflight` operator write with origin `discard:<id>` and R3-60's snap transition, and the Claude restore's reasons (`ClaudeRestoreReason`, `claude_refused`, R3-54, superseding Flags item 5), (3) the `secret-scan`/`filter-failure` `--publish` refusal and (4) copy-restore (R3-10's fourth guard-less birth), all on `resolve`. The tests' "contract addition n" refers to these numbers.

**Spec:** 05-holds §1 refusal table, Table 3 (release columns), Table 4, HLD-09, HLD-17, HLD-29, HLD-57…HLD-59, HLD-64 (copy-restore); 03-data DAT-29 (verbs), DAT-33 (`--publish` refusal), DAT-39 (answers), DAT-45, §3.9 restore cases; glossary §15 (`/cowork resolve`), §24 defaults 4–5, §25.2 items 2–3; 11-testing F-HLD-1, TC-HLD-10, I-DAT-08; tests 05 U5 (resolve parts), U8, I9 (copy-restore), I13, I15, P6 (the no-op after accept); rulings R3-02, R3-06, R3-09, R3-10, R3-23, R3-28.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/claudeRestore.test.ts`
- `"claudeRestoreRefusal allows only a record whose origin is the calling seat in OFF_DISK PARKED or CONFLICTED with guard conflict, claim-breach or none, or a returned SHADOW, on text paths"` — `null` for origin `<seat>` and `<seat>/<agent>` with those states; `'not_owner'` for another seat's record; `'binary'` for a binary path; `'quarantine'` for a `quarantine-review` record; `'state'` for a `revert` record and for an ON_DISK record; no result is a message code, and `resolve()` with a seat actor returns `{kind: 'claude_refused', reason}` for each, writing nothing to `holdCode` or the log as a code (HLD-59, H3, DAT-45, R3-54, contract addition 2).

`test/integration/resolve.table.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 2})`; every record is created through Plan 3's own creators or Task 1's `hold()` with `humanEvidence: true` unless a row says Claude.
- `"05 U5 resolve part: every Table 4 row gives the listed result and next record state"` — one fixture per row, asserting the `ResolveResult`, the record's residence/state/offer/guards and the disk afterwards (Table 4, U5, H4, H5, H6, H7, H8):
  - ON_DISK `revert` PATCH: `--publish` → `done` (no write), guards `[]`, `local.explicit` has it, `RESOLVED_PUBLISH` after the publish lands and R1 runs; `--discard` → disk = r's step-5 MASK result, `RESOLVED_DISCARD`; `--park` → disk = the same MASK result, OFF_DISK PARKED, `HOLD_PARKED`; `--handoff` → refused `HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE`.
  - PARKED `revert` → `--restore` → ON_DISK ACTIVE, guards `[revert]` (the PARKED displacement released, every other guard kept), `HOLD_RESTORED`.
  - OFF_DISK CONFLICTED `conflict` (K4) → `--restore` → ON_DISK, `conflict` released, `HOLD_RESTORED`; `--discard` → state only, `RESOLVED_DISCARD`.
  - ON_DISK `join` PIN → `--publish` → released; `--discard` → disk `A[p]`; `--park` → OFF_DISK PARKED after the held-side refresh.
  - INCOMING `lock` → `--publish` → `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN`; `--park` → `HOLD_NOT_PARKABLE`; `--handoff` → `HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE`; `--discard` → `RESOLVED_DISCARD`.
  - ON_DISK `eol-bom` PATCH → `--publish` → `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN` (a PREDICATE remains); `--discard` → disk `A[p]`.
  - ON_DISK `host-reject` PIN → `--publish` → `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN` (released only by resume, Table 3); `--discard` → disk `A[p]`.
  - a `local_only` record (`.env` restart record) → `--park` → `HOLD_NOT_PARKABLE`; `--discard` → disk `A[p]`, a stub op.
  - a record carrying an unknown guard `future-guard` → every verb refused `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN {guards: ['future-guard']}` (R3-09).
  - (Table 4, U5 resolve parts, H4, H5, H6, H7, H8.)
- `"HLD-57 (a): a record owned by another peer is refused HOLD_NOT_OWNER with nothing changed, and an id neither the registry nor the fold names is refused HOLD_NOT_OWNER"` — H1, HLD-57, contract addition 2.
- `"HLD-57 (b): a record whose folded state is terminal, or whose folded offer is accepted, is a no-op HOLD_ALREADY_RESOLVED with the registry head, the disk and the outbox unchanged"` — the accepted case: on an ordinary record (offer `none`) `offerEvent(id, 'born_leftover')`, then `offerEvent(id, 'handoff')`, then `offerEvent(id, 'accepted')` (test-only, R3-06; `handoff` is legal only from `pending_leftover`, HLD-09), mirrored and folded (H1, 05 P6 resolve part).
- `"HLD-57 (c): the resolve's hold op carries prev_rev by mirrorSync's rule — the folded rev, or the rev of r's newest allocated pending op while one is in flight — replacing a still-pending unallocated draft of the record; a forged op of the record with a stale prev_rev folds as a no-op"` — U5 (`prev_rev` races rejected by the fold), HLD-10, R3-27.
- `"HLD-57 (d): a writer MISMATCH refuses HOLD_DISK_CHANGED with the record unchanged; a share-none lock returns {kind: 'locked', paths} with nothing changed; a kept push intent refuses HOLD_DISK_CHANGED"` — `FaultFs.interpose` changes the file between the decision and the writer's check; `FaultFs.holdLock(p, 'share-none')` for `--park` and `--restore` (H1, H6, HLD-16, R3-23, Review Focus 2).
- `"HLD-57 (e): a --restore write is an observed change of the human CS: the next materialization creates no revert record for it and it publishes through the gate and T0"` — `ObservedWrites.record` consumed by the next `revertStep` (HLD-57 (e), HLD-26 (b), HLD-17).
- `"HLD-58: under FREEZE(MASS) a human --park writes, while the Claude restore is refused HOLD_FROZEN"` — `humanEvidence: true` vs `{actor: {seat, agent: null}, humanEvidence: false}` (H2, HLD-57 frozen rule, HLD-58).
- `"every refusal carries its 05 §1 next command"` — `NEXT_COMMAND`: `HOLD_NOT_OWNER` and `HOLD_RESTORE_CONFLICT` → `/cowork show <id>`, `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN` → `wait, cowork_request go-first, or --handoff`, `HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE` → `/cowork resolve <id> --publish|--discard`, `HOLD_DISK_CHANGED` → `repeat the command`, `HOLD_FROZEN` → `/cowork status`; the daemon subcommand prints the code and that command (H9, §1 refusal table).
- `"--handoff of a SHADOW or a claim PIN moves offer to offered with one mirrored op from the owner; revert, INCOMING, local_only, conflict, claim-breach, base-move, wake-scope and K7 t0 records are refused HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE"` — the K7 refusal is the COO-10 predicate answering false (H8, R3-06, HLD-09, U5).

`test/integration/resolve.publish.test.ts`
- `"--publish of an ON_DISK revert record releases revert, marks it explicit and adds its paths to the human CS; after its publish lands R1 ends it RESOLVED_PUBLISH, not SUBSUMED"` — H4, HLD-29, Table 4.
- `"--publish of an OFF_DISK CONFLICTED K4 record restores first, and a clean restore ends RESOLVED_PUBLISH once the content is in A"` — H4, HLD-17.
- `"05 I13: --restore of a K4 record over an overlapping local edit writes cowork: restore markers, adds guard markers and reports RESTORE_MARKERS {id, path, rows}; S_pub and P keep A[p] and nothing publishes; after the rows are removed the edit publishes and the record ends RESOLVED_PUBLISH"` — I13, H7, HLD-17, DAT-45.
- `"05 I13 binary: --restore of a binary record whose disk differs from both its base and A is refused HOLD_RESTORE_CONFLICT with nothing written, and a seat actor's restore returns claude_refused with nothing written"` — the K4 fixture's origin is `daemon`, so `resolve(…, 'restore', {actor: {seat, agent: null}, humanEvidence: false})` returns `{kind: 'claude_refused', reason: 'not_owner'}`; a binary-path OFF_DISK CONFLICTED record whose origin is that seat (made with `hold()`) returns `{kind: 'claude_refused', reason: 'binary'}`; neither writes `holdCode` or a log line, and `RESTORE_BINARY_HUMAN_ONLY` stays `restore()`'s operator-level outcome only (I13, H3, H7, R3-54, R3-65).
- `"--restore of a marker companion writes its rows back with guard markers"` — result code `RESTORE_MARKERS` (H7, 05 §3.4 item 3).
- `"05 U8: --publish on a portability record is refused HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN"` — U8, DAT-33 refusal, I-DAT-09 (`--publish` clause).
- `"--publish of a generated? record keeps syncing: the guard releases, keepSyncing gains the path, the path publishes with its group, and churnStep never creates another record for it"` — 5 further qualifying ticks after the publish: no record (DAT-39 answers, TC-HLD-10, contract addition 1).
- `"--publish after an exclude answer still means keep syncing"` — `generated?:exclude` released the same way (Table 3 `generated?`, glossary §25.2 item 2).
- `"05 I15 Plan 3 part: a wake-scope PIN refuses --handoff with HOLD_NOT_OFFERABLE, and --publish releases wake-scope so the whole CS publishes in one commit once T0 passes"` — the PIN from `pinCs('wake-scope', …)` with origin `wake` (I15, H8, HLD-66, HLD-52).
- `"--publish on secret-scan and filter-failure records is refused HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN"` — contract addition 3, H4.

`test/integration/resolve.discard.test.ts`
- `"--discard writes r's step-5 MASK result for an ON_DISK PATCH and A[p] for a PIN, a local_only record and a whole-path-guard record, then ends RESOLVED_DISCARD with a mirrored op, a daemon log line and a discard trace naming the held-side blobs"` — the `local_only` record's op is a stub; trace `discard {id, rev, held: [{path, blob}]}`; the writer received origin `discard:<id>`; `core.observed.peek(p, written)` is true, the private-index entry and `state:snap[p]` equal the written blob, and the registry saw `inflight[r] = {op: 'discard', …}` before the write and the final patch with it cleared after (H5, R3-28, R3-36, R3-60).
- `"a kill inside --discard's write is completed by restart as RESOLVED_DISCARD, never GONE"` — kill at `apply.group.phase2` inside the `--discard` write of an ON_DISK `revert` PATCH: after `restart(core)` (`settleHoldOps`) the record is RESOLVED_DISCARD with its mirrored op and `discard` trace, the inflight entry is cleared, and R1 never ends it GONE; a kill at the second `registry.cas` hit of the inflight commit leaves the disk unchanged and the record ON_DISK ACTIVE (05 F7, HLD-16, R3-28, INV-NOLOSS).
- `"--discard of an OFF_DISK record changes state only, and of an INCOMING lock record ends it so the next apply writes TIP once the lock clears"` — H5.
- `"--discard of an ON_DISK CONFLICTED record writes A[p]"` — two fixtures: a `revert` PATCH made CONFLICTED by a MASK conflict (an edit next to its held line) and an ON_DISK CONFLICTED K4 record whose TIP write is pending: each `--discard` writes `A[p]` through `writeLocal` (MASK step 4's result, as PARK writes), ends RESOLVED_DISCARD with a mirrored op and a `discard` trace naming its held-side blobs (Table 4, H5, contract addition 2).
- `"05 U8 generated?: --discard writes nothing, sets generated?:exclude in a new mirrored rev, releases the dependent HLD-42 PIN at once so src/use.ts publishes in the next window, keeps gen/api.ts masked, and the record ends SUBSUMED when the scope reports gen/api.ts UNMANAGED"` — `releaseDependencyGuards` called by the verb; the UNMANAGED step uses Task 7's scope seam (U8, I-DAT-20 exclude half, TC-HLD-10, DAT-39).

`test/integration/resolve.collision.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 2})`; peer 1 has an ignored `.env` holding marker `CWM_t11_1_b` and peer 0 lands a CS adding `.env` and `src/a.ts`, so peer 1 holds a `collision` record (Task 6).
- `"I-DAT-08 take: --publish captures peer 1's .env through the staged copy as a new local_only PATCH (h0 ABSENT, h1 = its bytes, OFF_DISK PARKED, displaced_by null), writes TIP's .env through writeLocal, and the collision record ends SUBSUMED at the next apply"` — the next apply sets `A[.env] := TIP[.env]` with cause `disk_equal` (R3-33); `ResolveResult.created` names the capture record (I-DAT-08 take, H4, DAT-29, D-09, TC-HLD-10).

- `"05 U8 keep: --discard sets collision:keep in a new mirrored rev, never writes .env, the snapshot keeps skipping it, and the record ends SUBSUMED when TIP drops .env or DISK_EQ(.env, TIP[.env])"` — both endings in separate fixtures; no add and no K4 for `.env` meanwhile (U8, I-DAT-08 keep, D-09).

- `"I-DAT-08 variant: an untracked, non-ignored notes.txt created inside the quiet period just before the add lands becomes a collision record and both verbs behave as above, with no bytes lost"` — the marker is on peer 1's disk or in the capture record's `h1` after either verb (I-DAT-08 variant, TC-HLD-10 "no bytes are lost in either branch").

`test/integration/resolve.copyrestore.test.ts`
- `"05 I9 copy-restore: after peer 0's revert record mirrors and peer 0's leave op folds, a fresh clone's --restore of that record creates a new local record and restores it byte-exact from held/<id>/, leaving peer 0's folded record untouched"` — the copy: origin `'human'`, `displaced_by: null`, peer 0's guards; `ResolveResult.created` is its id (I9 copy-restore, HLD-64, F7, H1 (a) exception, contract addition 4).
- `"the same restore without a folded leave op is refused HOLD_NOT_OWNER"` — HLD-57 (a), §1 refusal table.

`test/integration/daemon.resolve.test.ts`
- `"Engine.resolve never overlaps iterate(): called during an iteration it starts after it, fetches fresh first and returns the ResolveResult"` — R3-02, F-HLD-1 engine part.
- `"node dist/daemon.js resolve exits 0 for done and HOLD_ALREADY_RESOLVED, 1 for a refusal or a lock with one line naming the code and its next command, 2 for an internal error, and refuses while a live daemon holds daemon.lock"` — `runDaemon` in process with a capturing `DaemonIo` (R3-02).
- `"daemon.js resolve holds daemon.lock for its whole run and runs restart() before writing"` — a clone killed inside a PARK (kept local intent and `inflight[r]` with the disk at the park target): `daemon.js resolve --id <r> --restore` takes `daemon.lock` (a dead pid's lock is replaced, R-26), `restart(core)` resolves the kept intent and `settleHoldOps` completes the PARK before the fresh fetch, and only then does the RESTORE write; a daemon started during the resolve refuses on the held lock (R3-02, R-26, Plan 2 Task 17 carry).
- `"parseDaemonArgs accepts exactly one of --publish, --discard, --park, --restore with a ULID --id; --handoff, two verbs or a missing --id are usage errors with exit 2, and the usage text lists resolve"` — R3-02, `src/entry/daemon.ts`.
- `"EngineFixture.resolve(peer, id, verb) gives the same Table 4 outcomes through Engine.resolve as resolve(core, it, …) for one record per verb"` — the entry the SIM's `resolve` op uses (F-HLD-1 engine part).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** none.

**Notes:** `resolve` applies HLD-57 in order: (a) owner (local registry) or the copy-restore exception (fold `peers[owner].status === 'left'`), (b) the fold of the fresh fetch (`fold.holds[id]` terminal or `offer: 'accepted'` → `HOLD_ALREADY_RESOLVED`), then the verb's applicability and refusals, then writes through `holdOps` and `writeLocal(Group)` with `humanEvidence = o.humanEvidence` (an ON_DISK `--discard` with origin `discard:<id>`, its `inflight` entry, `ObservedWrites.record`, `setEntry` and R3-60's snap transition), then the registry transition whose mirror draft carries `prev_rev` by `mirrorSync`'s rule (c); a `--restore` output stays an observed change of the human CS (e). `POSTPONED` writer outcomes map to `HOLD_DISK_CHANGED` (R3-23). `--publish` releases the guards g with `guardRow(g).humanRelease`, except `filter-failure` and `secret-scan` (refused `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN`, contract addition 3) and `collision` (handled as the take, DAT-29) — that is `revert`, `conflict`, `claim-breach`, `join`, `restart`, `phantom`, `generated?` and `wake-scope` — and refuses while any other guard remains.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/config.checks.test.ts`
- `"readTeamConfig keeps a string array checks.checkpoint and gives [] for a missing or mistyped key"` — CS-12, contract addition 1.

`test/unit/status.holds.test.ts`
- `"computeStatus in the ok state takes BRANCH_REWRITTEN, else HOST_THROTTLED, else lastEvent, else CS_NO_CHECKS_CONFIGURED, else null"` — each rung with the higher ones absent (R3-46, R3-61, HLD-24, CS-12).
- `"computeStatus in the degraded state takes lastEvent, else HOLD_DIVERGED_REGIONS {n, age}, else Plan 2's degraded code, when only not_converged entries make it degraded"` — inputs whose `notConverged` holds an ON_DISK record's path: with `lastEvent` `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE` the message is it; without `lastEvent` and `diverged.n = 2` it is `HOLD_DIVERGED_REGIONS {n: 2, age}`; with `diverged` null it is Plan 2's code; a higher state (frozen, offline) keeps Plan 2's message (R3-61, R3-46, HLD-24).

`test/integration/statusHolds.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2})`, records created through the Plan 3 creators.
- `"holds_active counts non-terminal non-INCOMING records, holds_local_only those with local_only, incoming the INCOMING records plus closure paths deferred under 60 s (each path once), and cs_open/cs_ready come from the CS table"` — HLD-24, I3, CS-45, R3-46, T-06.
- `"T-10: not_converged lists every path of every ON_DISK record of each Plan 3 kind plus deferred and not-applicable paths, and no OFF_DISK record path"` — a `revert`, an `eol-bom`, a `portability`, a `markers`, a `generated?`, a `conflict` record before its TIP write, an `unreadable` PIN, a `collision:keep` and an `unmaterializable` INCOMING record, one `lock`-deferred closure, and a never-staged unreadable new file older than 60 s with no record (R3-56); a PARKED record's path is absent (T-10, HLD-24, I-DAT-08 keep variant, 05 I14, INV-CONVERGE).
- `"Plan 2's not_converged entries stay listed with Plan 3's records present"` — on `platform: 'win32'` a carried case-collision path (P2-CARRY) and a kept ambiguous push intent older than 60 s (`push_unresolved`) stay in `not_converged` while an ON_DISK `revert` record exists, and Plan 2's `oracleConverge` passes on that state (R3-46, P2-CARRY, the Plan 2 Task 14 carry to Task 17).
- `"01 #42: a ready group kept back by FREEZE(MASS) and by the regionSpec VERSION pause has its paths in not_converged as blocked_publish, and after the lift they publish and leave the list"` — 01-engine test 42, ENG-57, CS-30.
- `"T-06: a new record, a deferral, a not-converged path and MASS each appear in status.json within 2 iterations"` — the INV-NOSILENT status half.
- `"HOLD_DIVERGED_REGIONS {n: 2, age: <oldest record age in s>} is the message of the degraded state two ON_DISK records cause when no newer hold code is set, and CS_NO_CHECKS_CONFIGURED shows in the ok state when checks.checkpoint is empty and nothing outranks it"` — R3-46, R3-61, CS-12, contract addition 4.

`test/integration/loop.holds.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2})`, `rounds(n)`.
- `"iterate runs prepareIteration, resolvePushIntent, detectStep, maskStep, captureStep, applyStep, recordStep, publishStep, timerStep and sweepRing in that order, with Plan 2's step-0 gates, incident branch and throttle hand-off kept"` — a stale revert saved between materializations and a 20 s human close in one iteration: in `TraceRecorder.events` the revert record's `hold` event precedes the `state` event of the capture, and the capture's `state` event precedes the `apply` and `publish` events; with a kept push intent `resolvePushIntent` runs on the iteration's own `IterationCtx` before `applyStep` and no second fetch is made; an iteration whose `prepareIteration` returns `ok: false` with why `incident` enters or keeps the incident and runs no hold step; a 429 on the iteration fetch with nothing to publish still reaches the governor's fallback; `Writer.sweepRing` runs every iteration (E3, ENG-14, CS-25 "before any merge or ready capture", 05 B3 variant, P2-PUSHINTENT-FIRST, the Plan 2 Task 17 carries).
- `"the breaker observes every detection tick and the revert detector runs only on iterations that materialize"` — ENG-14, contract addition 6.
- `"maskStep runs only on materializing iterations and S_pub stays the MASK of the last materialized S_disk between them"` — `EngineInspection.sPub` unchanged across tick-only iterations and across `recordStep` (no re-MASK); trace `mask` once per materialization, its `registry` the registry head `maskEntries` read (HLD-20, R3-19, R3-62, D9).
- `"an assertP failure blocks only its own window's code part"` — the Task 2 M28 fixture with the ready CS already captured (M28 off), then `setMutants(p, ['M28'])` for one publish window that does not materialize and `setMutants(p, [])` after it: that window's `assertP` fails `p_range`, its `PublishReport.maskInvalid` is true and no live commit lands, `EngineCore.maskInvalid` stays false, and the next window — with no new edit, no apply and no materialization — composes and checks P again and lands the code commit (R3-57, R3-20).
- `"while maskInvalid or massResumePending holds, allowedPending reports no code pending to the governor"` — a spy `Governor` records `pending: false` for the code part in both states, and `true` again after the next passing `assertSPub` / after `massResumeStep` (R3-57, HLD-31, HLD-39).
- `"a pending green or tool checkpoint makes the loop materialize and capture"` — a scripted `checkpoint{source: 'green'}` and, separately, `{source: 'tool'}` with no other occasion: the next `iterate()` materializes (`it.mat` non-null) and `captureStep` journals the seat CS's passing pair (ENG-13, HLD-40, contract addition 5).
- `"an apply of a teammate change elsewhere in a file whose held revert row is on disk merges from S_disk: the held row survives on disk and A changes only by the verified write"` — D9, HLD-20.
- `"ENG-28 / R3-20: with mutant M28 and a revert record on row 30 after the human inserted 5 rows above it, assertSPub fails: HOLD_MASK_INVALID in status, no live commit that loop, the record's hold op still goes out board-only, and S_disk is never pushed"` — `setMutants(p, ['M28'])`; `PublishReport.maskInvalid: true` (ENG-28, R3-20, contract addition 2).
- `"recordStep is the only R1 call site: a held change undone on disk ends GONE after the next materializing iteration, a record whose H1 reached A ends SUBSUMED, and a MASK conflict makes the record CONFLICTED with HOLD_CONFLICTED in status"` — HLD-08 placement, E3.
- `"recordStep releases markers when the rows are gone and eol-bom when the path passes canon, never releases claim under openGate, releases claim when a scripted gate's claimStillBlocks answers false, and calls releaseDependencyGuards and incomingStep"` — the claim case is Task 10's I2 path bundle driven through the loop: once `claimStillBlocks` answers false, `recordStep` releases `claim:abcdefghij234567` and the next window publishes the three files in one live commit (HLD-08, HLD-52, contract addition 7).
- `"HLD-21: heldRanges('f.py') is [[12, 12]] for an ON_DISK revert PATCH holding row 12 after the materializing iteration, stays so after a save until the next materialization, and is [] for a PIN path"` — HLD-21 (`Engine.heldRanges`; the G1 deny is Plan 5's).
- `"HLD-22/23 data: records made by pinCs and shadowRecord keep origin <seat>/<agent> and cs_ids in the record and its op, and Engine declares no unacked member"` — HLD-22, HLD-23, I2, CS-46, 05 I10 (data only); the unacknowledged-item list is Plan 5's `TurnItems.unacked` (X-13).
- `"R3-30: a closure deferred by a lock for 50 s, then a daemon restart, gets its INCOMING record 60 s after the restart, and meta.pending keeps its rows while the record exists"` — R3-30, HLD-32, HLD-54.
- `"every attachState is followed by an awaited attachCsTable, so a restart with a stale cache keeps the journaled orders and passing flags"` — a kill after a capturing state commit and before `writeCsCache`, then `restart(core)`: before the first iteration starts, `inspect().csTable` has the journaled `orders`, `next_order` and `passing`, and the open-CS deltas form one `recovered` CS; the same after a lost-state rebuild, a join and a re-anchor `recover` (R3-69, HLD-36).
- `"after Engine.resume() lifts MASS, the next iterate() materializes because massResumePending is true, no live commit lands before massResumeStep's state update, and then the formatted files publish in one live commit"` — the Task 8 MASS fixture driven through `Engine.iterate`: `resume()`, then one iteration with no other materialize occasion: `it.mat` is non-null, `massResumeStep` runs in `detectStep` and drops `snap_pre_freeze` in its state update, and `publishStep` of that same iteration and of every earlier iteration since the lift sends no code commit (`PublishReport` shows the code part skipped); after 20 s one live commit carries the formatted files (HLD-31 "before anything publishes afterwards", R3-38, contract addition 5).
- `"CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE fires once per open seat CS 300 s after its oldest uncovered effect, and CS_GROUP_WAITING once per group episode after 10 min unpublished with a ready member; neither publishes anything"` — HLD-43, CS-16, contract addition 8.
- `"R3-53 in status: a blocked non-portable path and every path of the human group it keeps back are in not_converged, and PATH_NOT_PORTABLE is the status message until the name is fixed"` — Linux and macOS hosts: `src/old.py` renamed to `src/old:v2.py` and `src/other.py` edited; within 2 iterations `status.json` lists `src/old:v2.py`, `src/old.py` and `src/other.py` in `not_converged` (the last two as `blocked_publish`) and, in the `degraded` state, carries `message_code` `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE` with params `{path, rule}` (R3-61); after the rename to a portable name all are gone within 2 iterations of the landed publish (R3-53, INV-NOSILENT, INV-CONVERGE, R3-46).

`test/integration/editcheckpoint.loop.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2, ports: {editCheck: <scripted>, t0Regions: <scripted>}})`; edits are written with `writeWork` and announced with `Engine.signal`.
- `"01 #53: a passing edit checkpoint starts one out-of-cycle iteration whose candidate is journaled in one state update and published in the next governor window before the turn ends; a second passing edit replaces it and only its content publishes"` — 01-engine test 53, ENG-13, CS-09.
- `"01 #54: a failing edit leaves no ready pair, record, event or message (rev 2.5: only the memory-only last_check changes, D1); an earlier passing capture still publishes; the next passing edit publishes; a turn that ends while still failing gets the ordinary close and a K7 PIN when T0 fails"` — 01-engine test 54.
- `"01 #55: the out-of-cycle iteration starts no earlier than 300 ms after the hook-named path's mtime and captures the edited bytes; with a concurrent appender no capture holds a half-written file; ten edits within 1 s coalesce into one iteration per loop slot with at most one in flight"` — 01-engine test 55 (its TXN clause is Plan 5's).
- `"01 #56 Plan 3 part: a failing candidate leaves the state ref unchanged and its blobs referenced only by the private index"` — 01-engine test 56.
- `"Engine.signal of a stop starts one out-of-cycle iteration of steps 0–4 whose capture comes from a fresh snapshot"` — ENG-13 (b), CS-09.
- `"Engine.signal resolves only after the out-of-cycle iteration it asked for has started and the cache is written"` — `await engine.signal(stop)` then, with no further step, `cs/<enc(b)>.json` names the current state commit and `TraceRecorder` holds that iteration's `iteration` event (ENG-13, contract addition 8).
- `"a stranded restore blocks every capture: a due close and a passing edit checkpoint journal no ready pair until the restore completes"` — a `FaultFs` rule leaves one restore stranded (`Writer.strandedPaths()` non-empty), then a 20 s human close and a scripted passing `checkpoint{source: 'edit'}`: the iterations that follow neither materialize for the checkpoint nor journal any pair (the state commit is unchanged); after the stranded restore completes, the next iteration captures (R3-23, P2-STRANDED-SKIP, contract addition 5).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** none.

**Notes:** The loop's capture goes through `captureStep` from this task on (R3-40), so every pair the loop journals carries the CS table keep entry, and Task 9's push-ok update writes the table keep entry unconditionally from here on. `holdStatusInputs` extends Plan 2's `not_converged` entries (never replaces them) with Plan 3's, among them `core.cs.blocked()` and every path of each group the R3-53 gate keeps back (as `blocked_publish`) (R3-53, R3-46). `iterate()` keeps every Plan 2 Task 17 step (§Interfaces order): it calls `resolvePushIntent(core, it)` with the `IterationCtx` `prepareIteration` returned, never a second fetch, and never calls `observe()` again. `timerStep` here runs the recovered CS's 20 s quiet timer, `CS_UNPUBLISHED_NUDGE` and `CS_GROUP_WAITING`; Task 12b adds `escalationStep` and `s6Step` to it.

**Tests:**

`test/integration/escalation.test.ts`
- `"HLD-60: every record creation sets HOLD_CREATED {path, hid, REASON} on the owner's PC, and a CONFLICTED record HOLD_CONFLICTED {path, author, hid}, in status and the log"` — `REASON` from `reasonKey` (HLD-60, L1, CS-42).
- `"a binary-path revert PIN ends status on HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE after HOLD_CREATED"` — Task 8's `img.bin` stale overwrite driven through `iterate()`: after the iteration `holdCode` is `HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE {hid, path}`, raised by `escalationStep` right after that record's `HOLD_CREATED`, and the log has one `HOLD_BINARY_WHOLE_FILE` line for the record (R3-70, 05 §1 binary row).
- `"HLD-60 revert row: at age 10 min (REVERT_EVENT_AFTER_MS = 600,000) exactly one event{kind: health, reason_code: HOLD_REVERT_HELD, refs: [hold_id]} is drafted, none again after a restart because the fold holds it, and none for any other kind"` — HLD-60, INV-ONE-EVENT, T-PRO-16 shape, CS-42.
- `"age comes only from age_anchor: a +10 min wall-clock jump with 1 min of Δmono posts the event, and a restart after drain() keeps the accumulated Δmono"` — INV-CLOCK, B1 (drain flush).
- `"rev 2.5 (only when 8a's codes.ts has merged): HOLD_REVERT_HELD is in HEALTH_REASON_CODES and has an en template, so the viewer's closed-set check accepts the posted health event"` — 8a R-V43, X-104 (else this test moves to follow-up 3-V).

`test/integration/s6.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2})`; ages come from the peer's `VirtualClock`.
- `"05 I11 / TC-HLD-06: an ACTIVE revert record with local_only false is auto-parked at 24 h: OFF_DISK PARKED, displaced_by null, disk = the team version, its held/ blobs on the board, HOLD_AUTO_PARKED {path, hid, next: '/cowork resolve <id> --restore'}, and --restore brings the bytes back"` — `S6_AFTER_MS = 86_400_000` (I11, TC-HLD-06, HLD-61, L2).
- `"S6 is postponed while any FREEZE reason is set and parks in the first loop after the lift"` — HLD-61, I11.
- `"Review Focus 4: a peer offline since before the record's creation keeps it ON_DISK past 24 h; after reconnect the queued mirror lands first and S6 parks in a later loop"` — HLD-61, R3-45, I11 (OFFLINE), glossary §25.2 item 1.
- `"a HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED record and a many-path PIN whose op line exceeds 16 KiB are never auto-parked, and durabilityReport lists both in notDurable"` — a forged `validator` rejection and 300 paths (Review Focus 4, 05 F5 shape, HLD-61, R3-27, M1).
- `"I-DAT-12 variant: a record restored with markers while the remote is unreachable stays ON_DISK past 24 h; after reconnect the markers park runs in a later loop and no restore-marker row reaches any remote object"` — disk = the team version, a `local_only` marker companion holds the marker file byte-exact, `git cat-file -p` over every object reachable from `refs/cowork/*` on the bare remote shows no restore-marker row, and `--restore` of the companion brings the rows back with guard `markers` (I11 markers variant, TC-HLD-06, Review Focus 4).
- `"S6 never parks a local_only record at any age"` — a secret-like `.env` restart record and a `portability` record at 48 h (HLD-61, TC-HLD-06).
- `"S6 is postponed while a record path is in an open human CS and parks after that CS has been captured"` — CS-47, HLD-61.
- `"a PIN whose park must refresh its held side first commits the refresh rev and parks only after that rev is durable, in a later loop"` — HLD-61, R3-45.
- `"a share-none lock on the record's file at 24 h writes nothing and keeps the record ON_DISK; S6 parks after the lock is released"` — HLD-16, HLD-61, Review Focus 2.
- `"age survives a restart: 20 h, drain() (one flushAgeAnchors commit, no park), restart, 4 h more → parked"` — B1, R3-45, INV-CLOCK.
- `"S6 parking 11 durable records in one loop is attributed: no MASS, no revert record, no human CS"` — 11 ON_DISK `revert` PATCHes on 11 files of a 100-file tree (above max(10, 2 %)), all durable at 24 h: one loop parks all 11; the next three detection ticks list none of the 11 paths as changed, `breakerTick` returns null, `freeze_reasons` stays without `MASS`, `revertStep` creates no record and `cs.openPaths()` holds none of them (R3-36, ENG-37, HLD-30, HLD-61).

`test/integration/stopPrimitives.test.ts`
- `"drain() flushes age anchors in one registry commit with no hold op and never parks, also on IDLE_NO_SESSION"` — HLD-63, R3-45, B1.
- `"05 I9 engine part: durabilityReport lists every non-terminal non-local_only record in notDurable before one governed push and none after it, lists local_only records only in localOnly, and a kill at registry.cas plus git gc --prune=now loses no record or blob"` — I9 engine part, M1, HLD-62, HLD-63, INV-HLD-DURABLE.
- `"parkAllForStop(leaveOpId) parks every ON_DISK PATCH or PIN with local_only false with displaced_by = leaveOpId, parks a markers record by the markers park and lists its companion, leaves local_only records ON_DISK, and reports a record whose refresh rev is not yet durable in failed"` — HLD-63, M1, R3-45, Table 2.
- `"discardHolds ends each id RESOLVED_DISCARD through Table 4 --discard with a mirrored op"` — HLD-63, M1.
- `"discardHolds of 11 ON_DISK revert PATCHes, then three ticks and a restart: no MASS, no revert record and no human CS"` — 11 records on 11 files of a 100-file tree (above max(10, 2 %)): each write carries origin `discard:<id>`, is recorded in `ObservedWrites`, sets the private-index entry and `state:snap`; the next three detection ticks list none of the 11 paths, `breakerTick` returns null, `freeze_reasons` has no `MASS`, `revertStep` creates no record, `cs.openPaths()` holds none of them, and the same after a fresh engine's restart (R3-36, R3-60, HLD-30, HLD-63).
- `"parkAllForStop of 11 records, then a restart and 3 ticks: no MASS, no revert record and no human CS"` — `parkAllForStop(leaveOpId)` over 11 ON_DISK PATCHes, `drain()`, a fresh engine (`ObservedWrites` empty) and three iterations: the private-index entries of the 11 paths equal the parked blobs (restart reseeds them from `state:snap`, which R3-60's snap transitions updated), no tick lists them, `freeze_reasons` has no `MASS`, no `revert` record is created and the human CS stays empty (R3-36: the park family's attribution survives the restart that follows a stop; HLD-63 step 3, HLD-30, HLD-31).

`test/integration/startHolds.test.ts`
- `"HLD-64 / F7 start: a restart re-attaches the registry before the first publish, loads provenance, restores the CS table from a fresh cache, and the first window re-mirrors every non-durable record"` — F7 (start), HLD-64, HLD-01.
- `"PARKED records appear in restoreOffers only after the start's TIP has been applied, with one HOLD_PARKED log line"` — HLD-64, contract addition 3.
- `"a lost-state restart record mirrors in the next window like any record"` — F7, HLD-64 (`restart` records, R3-26).
- `"a peer joining after a teammate added TEAM_OLD starts with an empty provenance journal: its edit removing TEAM_OLD creates no revert record, while a line the teammate adds after the join stays protected; after deleting the journal and restarting, the rebuild back to provRebuildFrom gives the same verdicts"` — peer 0 genesis and a landed publish of `TEAM_OLD`, then `peers[1].engine.start()` (join) calls `load(tip, tip)` before its first apply (its state's first `laC` is `L_b`, and the first apply's advance appends nothing before the load point); restart calls `load(view.laC, await provRebuildFrom(core))` before `resolveWriteIntent`, and `provRebuildFrom` reaches back no further than the join point, the live code of the oldest reflog entry's `laB` (HLD-25, Task 8 contract addition 4, R3-59).
- `"a restart with a not-stageable non-portable name on disk publishes nothing of the human CS"` — Linux and macOS hosts: `src/old.py` renamed to `src/old:v2.py` while the daemon is down; at restart `portabilityStep` runs on the first detection tick before any publish, `blocked()` holds the name, and neither `captureLocalWork` nor the first windows publish the deletion of `src/old.py` (R3-53, DAT-32).
- `"a restart's captureLocalWork goes through captureStep once a CS table exists"` — a peer whose state holds a CS table and an unpublished human delta on disk at restart: the restart's capture journals the pair with an order the table names and the update is accepted by `StateStore.commit` (R3-40).

**Crashpoints:** none new (the I9 test kills at `registry.cas`). **Lint:** none.

**Notes:** `s6Step` candidates are ON_DISK PATCH or PIN records with `local_only: false`, not INCOMING, `ageMs ≥ S6_AFTER_MS`, `isDurable` against the fetched board tip, no path in `cs.openPaths()`, `it.freeze` empty and no kept push intent or stranded path; it calls `markersPark` for a record carrying `markers`, else `park(…, {humanEvidence: false, displacedBy: null, refreshMustBeDurable: true})`; `REFRESHED`, `LOCKED` and `POSTPONED` retry at a later loop. `parkAllForStop` uses `humanEvidence: true` and `refreshMustBeDurable: true`. Both reach `park`/`markersPark`, which record every written path in `ObservedWrites`, set its private-index entry and commit the snap transition (Task 5, R3-36, R3-60); `discardHolds` reaches Task 11's `--discard`, which does the same. `drain()` calls `flushAgeAnchors` and nothing that parks (HLD-63). Genesis and join call `core.prov.load(tip, tip)` (an empty journal) and a restart calls `load(view.laC, await provRebuildFrom(core))` — both wired by Task 8 (R3-59); this task only tests them end to end.
