*Composition (R3-47).* `holdsSetup(seed, o)` is the only place that composes the `holds` profile; run-seeds, run-shard and every holds SIM test call it. It returns `tuple = {seed, peers: o.peers ?? 3, ops: o.steps ?? PROFILES.holds.steps}` and `options = {generator, quiescence: DEFAULT_QUIESCENCE, seedFiles: o.seedFiles ?? seedFilesPlan3(seededRandom(`${seed}/files`)), peerProfile: HOLDS_PEER_PROFILE, crash: {rate: o.crashRate ?? (o.script ? 0 : 0.02)}}`, where `generator = o.script ? scriptedGenerator(o.script, markers) : holdsGenerator({...PROFILES.holds, weights: {...PROFILES.holds.weights, ...o.weights}}, markers)`, `factory = realEngine({mutants: o.mutants})`, `markers = new MarkerModel(markerRun(seed))` and `options.settled = holdTimersSettled` (also the harness default when `settled` is omitted, so `edits`/`faults` runs and Plan 2's realEngine SIM tests use it too): true only when no peer has a hold timer due within the next 40 rounds — no non-terminal `revert` record younger than 10 min (`inspect().holdAgeMs[id]`, Task 12a) whose `HOLD_REVERT_HELD` event is not yet in the fold, no INCOMING or `unreadable`/`unstable` record whose creation op `creationDue` still withholds, no `pendingAgeMs` entry below 60,000 ms — so a timer's push never lands inside the 40 extra rounds (INV-CONVERGE's zero-push clause) while `maxRounds` still bounds the wait. `HOLDS_PEER_PROFILE`: p0 `{autocrlf: false, withFilter: false}`, p1 `{autocrlf: true, withFilter: false}`, p2 `{autocrlf: false, withFilter: false, platform: 'win32'}` on every host (Windows-receiver emulation, data OQ-26); no peer has a filter (R3-47 lists none; R-31). No seat signal is ever produced (R3-05).

*`seedFilesPlan3(random)`* = `seedFilesPlan2(random)` plus: `ko.txt` — 40 LF rows `row <k> ` followed by the cp949 bytes `C7 D1 B1 DB` ("한글"), k = 1…40; `bin.dat` — 4,096 bytes from `random` with byte 0 set to `0x00` (binary by DAT-04); `big_bomcrlf.py` — `generateBigBomCrlf(26_436, random)` with every CRLF stored as LF (UTF-8 BOM, stored LF, CRLF on p1's disk: the 1.3 MB fixture of 11-testing §3.7); `.gitignore` — `*.log\nbuild/\n` (a SAVE_ONLY path, glossary §3); `form/flip.txt` — 12 LF rows `flip <k>` (touched only by `nonconforming_push`); `fmt/m00.py`…`fmt/m23.py` — 12 LF rows each, `    v<k> = <k>` (4-space indent; the formatter's input; 24 files > max(10, 2 % of tracked)).

*Weights (`PROFILES.holds`, steps 60, engineWeight 45).* new_file 3, edit_own 6, edit_band 6, edit_same_line 3, append_md 2, eol_rewrite 1, delete_own 1, temp_file 1, secret_name 1, edit_peer_line 5, stale_save 4, rewind 2, collide_ignored 2, nonportable_name 2, write_during_apply 2, lock_share_read 2, lock_share_none 1, unlock 3, age_24h 1, resolve 6, gc_prune 1, nonconforming_push 2; partition, heal, reject_transient, race_push, formatter and resume 0 (the last two are scheduled). `PROFILES.edits` and `PROFILES.faults` gain `steps: 40` and are otherwise unchanged.

*Drawing and scheduling.* `next(ctx, step)` is synchronous and draws only from `ctx.random`: the kind (a scheduled op first, else weighted, else null = one engine iteration) and the peer index; `run(ctx)` resolves targets from disk, `inspect()` and holdsView (sorted candidate lists, first eligible) and returns an op-log detail; an op whose target does not exist writes nothing and logs `<kind> p<i> skipped (<why>)`. Scheduled ops: one `formatter` at a step drawn uniformly in [10, 30] on a drawn peer; `resume` of that peer 3–8 steps later; `nonconforming_push` draws `bad_name` or `form_flip`, each at most once per seed, and schedules `bad_name_fix` 5–15 steps after `bad_name` and `form_fix` at the first step at which p0's virtual mono clock is ≥ 70,000 ms past `form_flip`; `lock_*` only at steps < 40, at most one lock per peer and two in all; `unlock` only for a lock held ≥ 60,000 ms of the holder's virtual mono clock; `age_24h` at most twice per seed, only at steps ≥ 20; forced cleanup: step 57 emits any pending `form_fix`/`bad_name_fix` and any pending `nonportable_name` fix (so no R3-53 gate or `portability` record of the op is left at quiescence by a late fix), step 58 a `resume` of every peer whose `inspect().freeze` holds `MASS`, step 59 one `unlock` of every remaining lock.

*Ops (each writes Buffers with Node fs from test code, in the file's dominant ending unless stated, and sets the written file's mtime to the peer's virtual wall clock, as Plan 2's generator does).* `params` keys accepted by `holdsOp` are named in brackets.
- Plan 2 edit kinds [path, row, text] keep their Plan 2 behaviour and add one fresh marker per written file (own line for `new_file`/`append_md`, suffix of the edited row otherwise); an explicit `path` overrides the drawn target (a script may name any path, e.g. a host-table non-portable name for `new_file`); `edit_band` [path, row, insert] inserts, in 1 of 3 draws, n ∈ [1, 40] own rows after row 7 of the peer's band (05 P7 "peer insertions of 1–40 lines"), the marker on the first inserted row.
- `edit_peer_line` [path, marker]: on peer X, a row containing a marker whose author ≠ X that is also in X's A blob of the path; the row text before its first marker gets `-x<k>` appended, its existing markers stay, a fresh marker is suffixed; informed. Expected: a `revert` PATCH on X at its next materializing iteration (HLD-27, modify of a line another writer added).
- `stale_save` [path, buffer = 'genesis' | n steps back, row, variant = 'plain' | 'hot_exit']: X writes an earlier content B of the file from its ring of the last 10 disk contents (recorded after every step that changed the file on X, for files ≤ 64 KiB, and `big_bomcrlf.py` only when a holds op wrote it) plus one new own row `x<k> <marker>` at a row ≥ 2 rows from every row that differs between B and the current content; a drawn B is eligible only when `findMarkers(current) − findMarkers(B)` contains ≥ 1 marker of another author present in X's A and none authored by X (a stale editor never drops its own text in a NOLOSS-gated run); an explicit `buffer` param of a script bypasses the eligibility rule (the script owns its consequences); `hot_exit` writes B first and B + the row after advancing X's clock 1,000 ms (F-ED-2's VS Code hot-exit restore); stale.
- `rewind` [path, buffer]: X restores B exactly (same eligibility) and appends one row `# rewind <marker>` (F-ED-5); stale.
- `formatter` [glob = 'fmt/*.py']: X rewrites every matching file, each row's leading 4 spaces → 2 spaces, and appends `# formatted <marker>` (one marker per file); informed (markers kept). Expected: `FREEZE(MASS)` at X's next detection tick (HLD-30 window rule).
- `collide_ignored` [other, name]: X appends `local/<name>` to `<X.dir>/.git/info/exclude` and writes `local/<name>` (3 rows + marker); peer Y = `other` writes `local/<name>` with 3 different rows + marker. Expected: Y publishes, the third peer applies, X gets one INCOMING `collision` record (DAT-29).
- `nonportable_name` [name, fix]: X writes the name from its host table (X's platform = `peer.opts.platform ?? process.platform`: linux `['con.py', 'trail.', 'a:b.txt', NFD]`, darwin `['con.py', 'trail.', 'a:b.txt']`, a real win32 host (`process.platform === 'win32'`, no emulation) `[HANGUL304, NFD]`, an emulated win32 peer (`peer.opts.platform === 'win32'` on another host) `[NFD]` on Linux hosts and nothing on darwin hosts (the op is skipped with why `no host name`, since git there precomposes the name), where HANGUL304 is `'np/' + '한'.repeat(100) + '-<k>.txt'` (one component over 300 UTF-8 bytes, D7 rule 2; Task 7's win32 name; Linux and macOS cap a component at 255 bytes) and NFD is the JS string `'nfd/\u1112\u1161\u11ab-<k>.txt'`, the decomposed spelling of the syllable U+D55C; macOS leaves NFD out because git there precomposes names, Windows leaves `con.py`, `trail.` and `a:b.txt` out because the host cannot create them) and `np/ok-p<i>-<k>.txt`, each 3 rows + marker, in one op (one CS). Expected for a `stageable()` name (`con.py`, `trail.`, HANGUL304): one `local_only` PATCH `portability` and one `dependency` PIN over `np/ok-…` (HLD-42). Expected for a name that is not `stageable()` (`a:b.txt`, NFD): no record, `PATH_NOT_PORTABLE {path, rule}` without an id in status, and nothing of the CS published while the file exists — `np/ok-…` waits with it (R3-53); `np/ok-…`'s marker counts at `author_disk` while its CS is open or captured and unpublished, and the bad file's marker is exempt (not a synced path, R3-48). `fix: true` (scheduled 5–15 steps later unless the record ended; always for an unrecorded name) renames the bad file to `np/fixed-<k>.txt` (same bytes; the reference model moves the marker, it does not retire it, and on the synced name it is an obligation again).
- `write_during_apply` [path, variant = 'target' | 'old_handle']: chooses on X a modified text path p with A_X[p] present and `TIP[p] ≠ A_X[p]`, arms a one-shot handler on `ctx.scheduler.onYield` (chained after any existing one) for `apply.beforePhase2`, then runs `ctx.engines[X].step(ctx)`; at the first such yield at which p's working file no longer exists (P1 renamed it) the handler writes, for `target`, X's pre-step bytes of p plus `x-dur-<k> <marker>` to p, and for `old_handle`, appends `x-late-<k> <marker>` to the newest `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/*.old` whose bytes equal X's pre-step bytes of p and calls `markers.noteLateWrite`; no such yield → `skipped (no apply)`.
- `lock_share_read` / `lock_share_none` [path]: `faultFsOf(X).holdLock(join(X.dir, p), mode)` on a tracked text path (host separators, so the stage copy `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/stage/<p>` never matches). `unlock` [path] releases it.
- `age_24h`: every peer clock and the scheduler clock advance 86,401,000 ms (mono and wall).
- `resolve` [id, verb]: X with ≥ 1 non-terminal own record (`realEngineOf(X).inspect().holds`); record priority `revert`, `collision`, `generated?`, `portability`, OFF_DISK PARKED or CONFLICTED, any other, oldest ULID first; verb: `collision` → publish | discard (1:1); `generated?` → discard; ON_DISK `revert` → publish 4 : discard 3 : park 3; a record carrying `markers` → park; OFF_DISK → restore 3 : discard 2; `portability` → discard 9 : publish 1 (expects `HOLD_GUARDS_REMAIN`); any other → publish. Calls `Engine.resolve(id, verb, {humanEvidence: true, actor: 'human'})` and logs `resolve <id> --<verb> → done <code> | refused <code> | locked <paths>`. Before the call the op reads the record at its current rev (readRegistry); after `done`: `publish` → for each path, `noteEdit(X, p, findMarkers(h1[p] or s2[p]) − findMarkers(h0[p] or s1[p]), findMarkers(h0[p] or s1[p]) − findMarkers(h1[p] or s2[p]), true, () => true)` (the human published the held change: its re-adds and its removals become informed edits); `discard` → `noteDiscard(id, findMarkers(h1[p] or s2[p]) − findMarkers(h0[p] or s1[p]))` over its paths; `park`, `restore` → no model change (bytes move between disk and record).
- `resume`: `Engine.resume()` on X (MASS lift, host-reject release; R3-38, R3-39), logged with its `ResumeReport`.
- `gc_prune`: `git gc --prune=now --quiet` in X's clone through `ctx.git` (F-FS-4).
- `nonconforming_push` [variant, name] through `teamPush` (writer ids from `seededRandom(`${seed}/team`)`, dates from p0's virtual wall clock, markers authored `team`): `bad_name` {`np/team-<k>/<name>` (name ∈ `con.py`, `a:b.txt`, `trail.`), `np/team-<k>/ok.txt`} in one commit; `bad_name_fix` deletes the bad path and adds `np/team-<k>/fixed.txt` with its bytes; `form_flip` stores `form/flip.txt` with CRLF endings plus one marker row (a stored-form violation, I-DAT-13 shape); `form_fix` stores it with LF endings plus a second marker row; `plain` [paths] (scripts only, weight 0) stores 3 conforming LF rows + one marker per named path in one commit — the way a script lands several separate incoming CSs from one source within seconds, which one peer's human CS (HLD-35, 20 s quiet) cannot do.
- Plan 2 fault kinds (`partition`, `heal`, `reject_transient`, `race_push`) are reachable through `holdsOp` (built by the same code `editGenerator` uses) for scripted tests; they have weight 0 in `holds`.

*Target exclusions.* No op targets `form/flip.txt` (except `nonconforming_push`), `np/team-*`, a tracked path that fails `portable()`, `.gitignore`, or a path under an active lock (except `unlock`); `delete_own` only deletes a file all of whose markers the deleting peer authored (so no deletion of peer-provenance lines trips HLD-30 (b) by accident).

*Reference model (TST-08, R3-48).* Each op, before returning, calls `noteEdit(peer, path, added, removed, informed, peerHad)` per written path with `added = findMarkers(after) − findMarkers(before)` and `removed = findMarkers(before) − findMarkers(after)` (bytes of that peer's file), `informed = false` exactly for `stale_save` and `rewind`, and `peerHad(m)` = m in the peer's A blob of the path (`inspect().aTree` + treeEntries/readBlob) or in its disk bytes before the write; the team writer uses peer `team` with `peerHad = () => true`. `noteEdit` with an already-live marker updates its path; with a retired marker it makes it live again for the re-adding peer. Markers written to a path that is not synced at the write — temp files, secret-like names without a record, locally excluded files (`collide_ignored`'s own file, `.git/info/exclude`), NOT_ADMITTED names without a record (R3-53) — are registered with `synced = false` and land in `exempt()`, not in the obligations (R3-48; glossary INV-NOLOSS covers synced paths), and a later move of such a marker to a synced path (`nonportable_name`'s fix) makes it an obligation.

*Test-side scope (`simScope`).* For a tracked path, the D5 rows the SIM can reach, in order: `isQuarantined(path, null)` → QUARANTINED; `!validPathSyntax(path)` → UNMANAGED; mode 160000/120000 → UNMANAGED; a `sync.exclude` glob of `.cowork/config.json` at EPOCH_BASE (lenient JSON; `wildmatch`) → UNMANAGED; basename `.gitignore` → SAVE_ONLY; size ≥ `HUGE_BYTES` → SAVE_ONLY; a path of a non-terminal ON_DISK `local_only` record (R3-21) → LOCAL_ONLY; else LIVE. It throws `unsupported scope input` when EPOCH_BASE holds `.gitattributes` or a `merge.<name>.driver` is configured (no SIM fixture has either), so an oracle never silently diverges from D5.

*Timing in tests.* Every test below that asserts a virtual-time bound (60 s records, 10 s coalescing, `pendingAgeMs`, S6 ages) drives the timed phase with `{rounds: n}` steps (every clock + 2,500 ms, every peer iterates in name order), never with drawn steps, whose 1–5 s advances and one-random-peer iterations make the observed age only a lower bound.

*Determinism (T-08, TST-03).* Every draw comes from `ctx.random`; run() picks targets from sorted lists; record ids come from the peers' seeded Random (ULIDs), `age_anchor` from the virtual clocks, registry and board commits are dated by `wallMsForDates`; the team writer's ids and dates are seeded and virtual; Plan 3 trace events carry no duration or wall time.

**Tests:**
- `"mint numbers markers per run across authors: CWM_r7_1_p0, CWM_r7_2_p1, CWM_r7_3_team; markerRun('holds-12') is 'holds12'"` — TST-08, 11-testing §2 *marker*.
- `"place: line mode inserts '<marker><EOL>' before row at, suffix mode inserts ' <marker>' before row at's EOL, in the file's dominant EOL"` — 'a\nb\nc\n' line at 1 → 'a\n' + m + '\nb\nc\n'; 'a\r\nb\r\n' line at 1 → 'a\r\n' + m + '\r\nb\r\n'; 'a\nb\nc\n' suffix at 1 → 'a\nb ' + m + '\nc\n'; at ≥ row count: line appends a final row, suffix suffixes the last row.
- `"place keeps a leading UTF-8 BOM first, a CRLF file's endings and a missing final EOL"` — EF BB BF + 'x\r\n' line at 0 → EF BB BF + m + '\r\n' + 'x\r\n'; 'a\nb' suffix at 1 → 'a\nb ' + m with no EOL added — F-PATH-2.
- `"place on a cp949 row keeps every Hangul byte pair: 'row 1 ' C7 D1 B1 DB '\n' suffix at 0 gives 'row 1 ' C7 D1 B1 DB ' ' m '\n', which TextDecoder('euc-kr', {fatal: true}) decodes"` — F-PATH-2, 11-testing §3.7 encodings.
- `"place on a binary buffer (a NUL in the first 8,000 bytes) appends '<marker>\n' after the last byte and leaves the first 4,096 bytes unchanged"`.
- `"findMarkers returns markers in byte order and ignores incomplete ones: 'x CWM_r_1_p0\nCWM_r_2_team y\nCWM_r_x_p0\n' → ['CWM_r_1_p0', 'CWM_r_2_team']"`.
- `"noteEdit retires a marker only on an informed removal by a peer whose A or disk held it"` — m = mint('p0'); noteEdit('p0','a.txt',[m],[],true,()=>false) → live has m {p0, a.txt}; noteEdit('p1','a.txt',[],[m],false,()=>true) → still live; noteEdit('p1','a.txt',[],[m],true,()=>false) → still live; noteEdit('p1','a.txt',[],[m],true,()=>true) → retired (not in live(), in retired()) — TST-08, R3-48.
- `"noteEdit of an already-live marker moves it to the new path, and re-adding a retired marker makes it live for the re-adding peer"` — m live at a.txt; noteEdit('p0','b.txt',[m],[],true) → {p0, b.txt}; after retiring it, noteEdit('p2','b.txt',[m],[],false) → {p2, b.txt}.
- `"noteDiscard removes the listed live markers into discarded() with the hold id and ignores markers that are not live; noteLateWrite marks a marker in lateWrites()"`.
- `"seedFilesPlan3 is deterministic and adds the Plan 3 fixtures with their exact shapes"` — two calls with seededRandom('f') are byte-equal; keys ⊇ seedFilesPlan2 ∪ {ko.txt, bin.dat, big_bomcrlf.py, .gitignore, form/flip.txt, fmt/m00.py…fmt/m23.py}; ko.txt: 40 LF rows, no 0x0D, decodes with TextDecoder('euc-kr', {fatal: true}), TextDecoder('utf-8', {fatal: true}) throws; bin.dat: 4,096 bytes, byte 0 = 0x00, isBinaryBytes true; big_bomcrlf.py: starts EF BB BF, no 0x0D, 26,436 LF rows, 1,100,000–1,600,000 bytes; .gitignore = '*.log\nbuild/\n'; each fmt file 12 LF rows starting with 4 spaces — X-04, F-PATH-2.
- `"PROFILES.holds has steps 60, engineWeight 45 and exactly the Task 13 weight table (partition, heal, reject_transient, race_push, formatter and resume at 0); PROFILES.edits and PROFILES.faults gain steps 40 and are otherwise unchanged"` — R3-47.
- `"holdsGenerator.next draws every weighted kind within 600 draws of a SimContext stub with seededRandom('g'), schedules exactly one formatter at a step in [10, 30] with its resume 3–8 steps later, emits the step 57–59 cleanup ops, draws lock ops only before step 40 and age_24h at most twice from step 20, and reads nothing but ctx.random"` — X-02, TST-05.
- `"every host-table name fails portable() with the rule it names (con.py 2, a:b.txt 2, trail. 2, HANGUL304 2, the NFD name 1), con.py, trail. and HANGUL304 are stageable() while a:b.txt and NFD are not, and an emulated win32 peer draws only NFD on a Linux host and skips the op (`no host name`) on a darwin host, while a real win32 host draws HANGUL304 or NFD"` — F-PATH-1, DAT-32, R3-53.
- `"realEngine binds grammarlessEditCheck, passT0Regions, openGate and noSymbols, takes EngineDeps.platform from RealEngineOptions.platform, else PeerOptions.platform, else process.platform, and realEngineOf/faultFsOf return that peer's Engine and FaultFs (null Engine before its first step)"` — R3-47 platform, R3-03, R3-04, R3-07 bindings.
- `"readRegistry equals inspect().holds field for field on a peer holding a revert PATCH and a K4 record; boardHolds of the landed Bnew equals the fold's entries and readHeld; simScope classifies .gitignore SAVE_ONLY, .github/workflows/ci.yml QUARANTINED, a sync.exclude path UNMANAGED, a local_only ON_DISK record path LOCAL_ONLY, shared.txt LIVE, and throws on an EPOCH_BASE with .gitattributes"` — holdsView (Tasks 14–16 input).
- `"teamPush lands one live commit whose blob for crlf-stored.txt keeps 'a\r\nb\r\n' verbatim, carries Cowork-CS and Cowork-CS-Paths and a leased board commit that every engine folds without an own rejection; every existing CoreFixture.pushTeamChange caller (the apply, apply.bomcrlf, publish and publish.rejections integration tests) passes unchanged through teamPush"` — R3-47 non-conforming writer.
- `"Review Focus 1, four shapes on files where p0 also made untainted edits: edit_peer_line on fmt/m01.py, stale_save on shared.txt, stale_save hot_exit on fmt/m02.py and rewind on fmt/m03.py each give exactly one revert PATCH on p0 whose h1 is A with only the tainted hunk (computed test-side by row replacement), p0's untainted rows publish in the same window, and the teammate's rows are in live_code at every later step"` — script per file: p1 appends `p1-line <m>` and publishes, {rounds: 12}; then on p0: `edit_peer_line` of that row plus a separate own row edit 3 rows away; `stale_save` / `hot_exit` with buffer 'genesis' (the save's own new row is the untainted edit); `rewind` after p0 wrote and published its own row before p1's line, with the buffer taken right after that own row; then {rounds: 12} — F-ED-2, F-ED-5, HLD-27, HLD-28, MASK step 5.
- `"Review Focus 1, attach and release: a second stale_save of shared.txt whose buffer is the first save's content (still without p1's rows) plus another untainted row creates no second record (no second 'hold' creation event, same id), resolve --publish then ends the record RESOLVED_PUBLISH after the next landed push, live_code lacks p1's reverted rows (the reference model retired them as an informed removal), and five more materializing iterations of p0 (each after an untainted p0 edit) create no record over those rows"` — HLD-07, HLD-29, Table 4 `--publish`, F-HLD-1.
- `"write_during_apply 'target' between P1 and P2 of p0's apply of p1's shared.txt edit leaves p0's marker row in shared.txt at the end of the step and on p0's disk or in a p0 record at every later step; 'old_handle' gives one OFF_DISK PARKED PATCH with no guard whose h1 holds the late marker by the next step"` — F-ED-4, ENG-35, R3-10.
- `"collide_ignored: p0's excluded local/c1.cfg stays byte-identical while p1 publishes local/c1.cfg; p0 holds one INCOMING collision record at once (INCOMING_COLLISION in status), p2 has the file; resolve --publish on p0 creates one OFF_DISK PARKED PATCH holding p0's bytes before p1's version is on p0's disk; in a second run resolve --discard keeps p0's file and the record ends SUBSUMED when p1 deletes the path"` — F-HLD-1 collision verbs, F-PATH-1 collisions, DAT-29, R3-33.
- `"nonportable_name on p0 with a stageable host-table name (con.py on Linux and macOS, HANGUL304 on Windows) and np/ok-p0-1.txt gives one local_only PATCH 'portability' (PATH_NOT_PORTABLE, board stub without held/) and one dependency PIN over np/ok-p0-1.txt; the fix op ends both records and np/fixed-1.txt and np/ok-p0-1.txt publish in one live commit"` — F-PATH-1 publish side, F-ED-9, DAT-33, HLD-42, HLD-52.
- `"nonportable_name on p0 with a name that is not stageable (a:b.txt on Linux and macOS, NFD on Linux and Windows) creates no record, shows PATH_NOT_PORTABLE without an id, publishes nothing of its CS (np/ok-p0-2.txt included) while the file exists, and the fix op publishes np/fixed-2.txt and np/ok-p0-2.txt in one live commit"` — R3-53, DAT-32, F-PATH-1; INV-NOLOSS passes every step (np/ok-…'s marker at `author_disk`, the bad file's marker exempt until the fix).
- `"nonconforming_push bad_name with con.py: every peer whose EngineDeps.platform is win32 (p2 on every host) writes np/team-1/ok.txt in that apply and holds np/team-1/con.py in its own INCOMING unmaterializable record (INCOMING_UNMATERIALIZABLE) listed in not_converged; other peers write both; bad_name_fix ends p2's record SUBSUMED and writes fixed.txt"` — F-PATH-1 receive side, R3-47, DAT-33, INV-GROUP shape.
- `"nonconforming_push form_flip: each peer defers form/flip.txt with reason eol_form at once, holds one INCOMING eol-form record from 60 virtual s (not before), and form_fix makes the next permitted apply write the LF form and end the record SUBSUMED"` — R3-47 non-conforming writer, R3-31, R3-32, I-DAT-13 shape.
- `"lock_share_read on p2's shared.txt while p1 publishes a band edit: p2 defers the closure 'locked', has one INCOMING lock record from 60 virtual s, and unlock lets the next permitted apply write it and end the record; lock_share_none on a file p0 just edited keeps it out of p0's captures and gives one 'unreadable' PIN from 60 s that ends after unlock"` — F-FS-1, HLD-32, HLD-54.
- `"age_24h: a durable ON_DISK revert record is parked by S6 at p0's next iteration (HOLD_AUTO_PARKED, disk = team version, held/ unchanged on the board), while a local_only portability record of the same age and a revert record of a partitioned peer (Plan 2 'partition' op before the jump, 'heal' after the check) stay ON_DISK; the HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED form is Task 12b's"` — F-HLD-1 S6 part, HLD-61, glossary §25.2 item 1.
- `"formatter over the 24 fmt files on p1 enters FREEZE(MASS) at p1's next detection tick (no code publish while hold ops still mirror), resume lifts it, the formatting publishes in one human CS, and the whitespace-only reindent of p0's earlier fmt rows creates no revert record on p1 (ws-equal pairs, HLD-27); the same with seedFiles adding fmt/x000.py…fmt/x179.py and glob 'fmt/**' trips once over 204 files"` — F-ED-3 (formatter over 180 files), F-HLD-1 formatter, HLD-27, HLD-30, HLD-31.
- `"generated? exclude: three consecutive p0 rewrites of 12 of the 20 rows of gen/out.txt at three detection ticks give one ON_DISK PATCH 'generated?' (GENERATED_SUSPECT); resolve --discard writes nothing, sets guard generated?:exclude, keeps gen/out.txt masked and releases the dependency PIN of its CS in that loop"` — F-HLD-1 `generated?` answer, DAT-39, U8 shape.
- `"resolve verbs on revert PATCHes: --park → OFF_DISK PARKED (HOLD_PARKED) with p0's disk equal to A on the held rows; --restore of that record → ON_DISK with the held rows back (HOLD_RESTORED); --discard on another → RESOLVED_DISCARD with one 'discard' trace event naming its h1 blob; every result code is in HOLD_CODES"` — F-HLD-1 `--publish|--discard|--park|--restore`, Table 4.
- `"gc_prune between two materializing iterations of p0 holding an ON_DISK revert PATCH: every oid named by p0's registry records and state journal exists afterwards (cat-file -e) and the next 'mask' event's step-5 result for that path equals the previous one"` — F-FS-4, RebaseCache stale-key rule.
- `"with crashRate 0.25 and 12 steps on a pinned seed at least one KilledAt ends a step, realEngineOf returns a new Engine for that peer at its next step, its registry head is the old or the new commit of any interrupted registry commit, and Plan 2's six exit oracles pass"` — the seed is pinned at implementation time: the implementer scans `holds-crash-smoke-<k>` from k = 0 and names in the test the first seed whose run ends at least one step with a `KilledAt` (whether a drawn kill fires depends on the seed, not on the engine); a later change that makes the pinned seed kill nothing fails the test with a message saying to re-pick, never silently passes — F-FS-5, 11-testing §3.3 run step 4.
- `"inspection in SIM: after p0's stale_save over p1's shared.txt row 14 materializes, inspect() shows registryHead = rev-parse of p0's registry ref, sPub['shared.txt'] = the step-5 result (≠ the snap entry, = the newest 'mask' event's map entry), ranges['shared.txt'] = [[14, 14]], csTable with p0's human CS; after p0's next landed push mirror[id] = {durable: true, rejected: null} and a 'mirror' event lists {id, rev, localOnly: false, held: 2}"` — X-03 (inspection), HLD-21, HLD-62.
- `"pendingAgeMs: with a share-read lock on p2's shared.txt and the lock phase driven by 30 consecutive {rounds: 1} steps, pendingAgeMs['shared.txt'] read after each step is ≥ 60,000 and < 62,500 at the step whose 'hold' event creates the record {holdKind: 'INCOMING', guard: 'lock', rev: 1, residence: 'ON_DISK', localOnly: false}, and < 60,000 at every earlier step"` — X-03 (inspection, widened `hold` event).
- `"TraceRecorder.monoAt gives each event the emitting peer's virtual mono time, the events carry none, and afterEveryOp oracles run after every quiescence and extra round"` — a 3-peer holds script: `monoAt(i)` equals the peer's `VirtualClock.monoMs()` read by a probe oracle at that event; no event has a time field (TST-03); a counting `afterEveryOp` oracle runs once per op step plus once per quiescence round and per extra round (oracleGroup's 10 s clause and step-59 unlock applies depend on both).
- `"holdTimersSettled holds T* back while a hold timer is due within 40 rounds"` — a scripted fresh `revert` record at the last op: T* is not declared before the record's `HOLD_REVERT_HELD` event has folded, and the 40 extra rounds then make zero push attempts (INV-CONVERGE, R-32).
- `"the 'apply' event of p1's 3-path CS on p0 carries groups [{paths: [g/a.txt, g/b.txt, g/c.txt], outcome: 'written', reason: null}], notApplicable [] and mDisk = the tree whose g/* blobs p0's disk now holds (DISK_EQ through a scratch index)"` — X-03 (apply event, INV-GROUP input).
- `"holdsSetup('holds-smoke-0') runs 3 peers (p1 core.autocrlf=true, p2 EngineDeps.platform win32), 60 steps with crash rate 0.02, reaches quiescence (K = 20 then 40 loops) and passes Plan 2's six exit oracles; its op-log names only holds catalogue kinds; the test prints its wall time, and the implementer records in the ledger that time and the wall time of a `TEST_SIM_FULL=1` 20-seed `holdsSetup` loop (`holds-time-0…19`) on their OS (the per-OS PR-leg p50/p95 that sizes the exit shards is Task 16's, R3-58)"` — 11-testing §3.3, R3-47, R3-55, R3-58, TST-02.
- `"the same holds seed run twice in different temp roots gives identical op-logs, identical trace events (remote URL normalised), identical remote ref oids and identical refs/cowork-local/<e>/holds oids on every peer; every hold, apply, mask, compose, mirror, discard, repair, form_violation, edit_check and mass event has exactly its contract fields (no duration, no wall time)"` — T-08, TST-03, TC-TST-01.

**Crashpoints:** none new (SIM kills reach the existing 14 names). **Lint:** none (test code).

### Task 14: INV-MASK, INV-GROUP and the 05 oracle rows

**Covers:** O2, O3, O4, T-02, T-03, T-04, T-06 (oracle), T-09 (M19, M25, M38, M39 firing), X-05, R3-55 (first-seed PR runs); INV-MASK, INV-GROUP, O-READY, O-AND, O-OFFDISK, O-LIVE-RECORD, O-ATOMIC-PUB, INV-NOSILENT (status half), INV-HLD-BUDGET; 05 P1 (≥ 1,000 trials), TC-HLD-01, TC-HLD-02 (mask half), TC-DAT-04 (mask half), TC-DAT-06 (exception (c)), TC-ENG-06 (SIM shape), TC-HLD-05 (SIM form), TC-TST-02 form; R3-49, R3-50, R3-63, R3-67. Depends on: Task 13. Parallel with Task 15 (disjoint files: this task never edits `test/sim/oracles.ts`, `markers.ts` or `oracleNoloss.ts`).

**Files:**
- Create: `test/sim/oraclesHolds.ts`
- Modify: nothing in `src/` (the `mask` event's `registry` field and the `compose` event this task reads are §Interfaces contract: declared by Task 1, emitted by Task 12's `maskStep` and Task 10's `publishStep`)
- Test: `test/unit/oraclesHolds.test.ts`, `test/integration/mask.fuzz.test.ts`, `test/integration/sim.oracles.holds.test.ts`, `test/integration/sim.mutants.holds.test.ts`, `test/integration/sim.regressions.holds.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 13 `holdsSetup`, `scriptedGenerator`, `holdsOp`, `ScriptStep`, `MarkerModel`, `readRegistry`, `treeEntries`, `readBlob`, `boardHolds`, `simScope`, `readStatus`, `realEngineOf`, `faultFsOf`; `TraceEvent` variants `iteration`, `apply`, `hold`, `mask` (with `registry`), `compose`, `publish`, `push`, `mirror`; `EngineInspection` (`sPub`, `lastP`, `holds`, `ready`, `csTable`, `pending`, `freeze`, `publishPause`, `mirror`, `laC`, `aTree`, `snapTree`, `epochBase`); Plan 2 `Oracle`, `SimContext`, `runSim`, `exitOracles`, `parseCsTrailers`, `decodeCsPaths`, `parseWriter`, `parseRecord`, `TERMINAL_STATES`, `MutantId`, `makeRepoFixture`, `ChangeSets` (P1 only), `maskEntries` (P1 only: P1 is a test of the engine function, not an oracle); fast-check (dev dependency).
- Produces: `oracleMask`, `oracleGroup`, `oracleReady`, `oracleAnd`, `oracleOffDisk`, `oracleLiveRecord`, `oracleAtomicPub`, `oracleNoSilent` (header signatures); `testRebase`, `testMask` (test-side operators, exported for the unit tests).

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — the `mask` event's `registry` field and the `compose` event (declared by Task 1, emitted by Tasks 12 and 10); `testRebase`, `testMask`.

**Spec:** 11-testing §3.4 rows INV-MASK, INV-GROUP, INV-NOSILENT (status half), O-READY, O-AND, O-OFFDISK, O-LIVE-RECORD, O-ATOMIC-PUB; §3.8 rows `hold/*`, `holdcat/X1–X3`, `atom/*`; §3.9 M19, M25, M38, M39 (and Plan 2's M20 for O-READY); TST-07, TST-11; glossary §21 INV-MASK, INV-GROUP, INV-NOSILENT, §20 (≤ 1 apply per 10 s per worktree); 05 §3.4 items 1–2, §5 INV-HLD-READY, INV-HLD-AND, INV-HLD-OFFDISK, INV-HLD-SINGLE, INV-HLD-ATOMIC-PUB, INV-HLD-BUDGET, INV-HLD-RECEIVE, §7 P1, P3 shape, B1; 11-testing §7 TC-HLD-01, TC-HLD-02 (mask half), TC-DAT-04 (mask half), TC-ENG-06, TC-TST-02; HLD-15, HLD-19, HLD-24, HLD-38, HLD-41, HLD-42, HLD-53, HLD-54; rulings R3-21, R3-31, R3-46, R3-49, R3-50.

**Notes:** every oracle is an `afterEveryOp` oracle reading only new trace events since its last index (`ctx.trace.since(i)`), the peers' git objects (each peer repo has the commits it fetched), working files, `status.json`, holdsView readers and `realEngineOf(sim).inspect()`; none calls `maskEntries`, `RebaseCache`, `writeGroups`, `HoldRegistry`, `isDurable`, `ChangeSets` or `evaluateOnDisk` (TST-07 independence). A peer's `binary(p)` is true for `bin.dat` and every path whose A blob has a NUL in its first 8,000 bytes (DAT-04; no SIM path is filtered). Cost (R3-49): INV-MASK recomputes only for new `mask` and `compose` events, i.e. loops that materialized or composed. Plants in the tests below are applied after a run's last step (synthetic trace events naming crafted objects in the peer repo, `inspect()` wrappers, scratch registry commits) and the oracle's `afterEveryOp` is then invoked directly on that context, so a plant never disturbs a running engine.
- **oracleMask (INV-MASK).** For each new `mask` event: records = readRegistry(peer, at = event.registry); scope = simScope(…, inspect().epochBase, records); expected = testMask(tree(sDisk), tree(aTree), records, scope, binary); the engine's map is the peer's `inspect().sPub` when the event is that peer's last `mask` of the step and `realEngineOf(peer)` is non-null (otherwise — an earlier event of the step, or a peer between a `KilledAt` and its restart — only the event's `withheld`/`partial`/`conflicted` sets are compared). Fails when (i) any path's entry differs (message names peer, path, both oids), (ii) the sets differ, (iii) a QUARANTINED/SAVE_ONLY/UNMANAGED/LOCAL_ONLY or withheld path differs from A in sPub, (iv) re-applying each ON_DISK ACTIVE PATCH to sPub in reverse ULID order (`mf(x, H0'[p], H1'[p])`) does not reproduce `sDisk[p]` on a partial path. For each new `compose` event: P_expected[p] = testMask(R)[p] of the newest pair (by `order`) covering p among groups the oracle finds composable — the path closure of the pairs' paths plus every non-terminal ON_DISK PIN with guards in `records(registry)`; a group is composable when the event marks it `ready` (the engine's readiness: the R3-53 gate and a missing passing capture are record-less, R3-63), it holds no such PIN (test-side) and every csId in the event's group is among the pairs (test-side) — and A[p] elsewhere; an engine group marked `ready` that the test side finds held fails the oracle; fails when `tree(pTree) ≠ P_expected` or a held/class path differs from A in P.
- **oracleGroup (INV-GROUP, INV-HLD-RECEIVE).** For each new `apply` event of peer X: the incoming groups are the path closure of `parseCsTrailers` over the first-parent commits in (X's laC at the previous check, event.tip] (a commit without trailers is one CS of its changed paths, HLD-37), built only over paths that `simScope` (at X's EPOCH_BASE) classes LIVE — UNMANAGED and special-mode paths are carried by scope class (HLD-53; D5 row 1a makes a tracked `np/team-k/a:b.txt` UNMANAGED: A follows TIP and nothing is written), so they are neither applicable nor "written" by an A change; applicable = those group paths minus the event's `notApplicable`. Virtual times come from `ctx.trace.monoAt(index)` (the emitting peer's mono clock, Task 13), never from the events. Fails when (i) a group has written and unwritten applicable paths (written = `A_after[p] ≠ A_before[p]` from consecutive inspections, cross-checked with the event's `written`), excluding a path a `write_during_apply` op of the same step wrote; (ii) a written path's disk stored form (scratch index from `mDisk` + `update-index --refresh` + `diff-files -z`, the oracleConverge technique) differs from `mDisk[p]`; (iii) the event's groups are not exactly the oracle's closure partition (a deferral blocking another closure, INV-HLD-RECEIVE); (iv) a not-applicable path that passes `validPathSyntax` has no non-terminal INCOMING record over exactly [p] with guard `collision`, `unmaterializable` or `quarantine-review` in X's registry by X's next `iteration` event after the apply (the records are made by `recordStep` after the `apply` event, so a `KilledAt` between the two leaves the step without them; a restart of X restarts the deadline, which then falls at X's first `iteration` event after the restart — oracleNoSilent's form); a path failing `validPathSyntax` is no record's path and is exempt (Flags item 8); (v) a deferred group older than 62,500 ms of X's virtual mono (60 s + one loop; the age from `monoAt` of the apply that first deferred it, or `inspect().pendingAgeMs`) is not covered by exactly one non-terminal INCOMING record whose paths equal its applicable paths (R3-31); (vi) two `apply` events of X with a non-empty `written` are < 10,000 ms apart by `monoAt` (glossary §20; kills M39). Every timer restarts at daemon start (Global Constraints, R3-30), so a restart of X — `realEngineOf(X)` returning a new Engine after a KilledAt — restarts the deferral ages of clause (v) at the restart and exempts from clause (vi) any pair of applies separated by it.
- **oracleReady (O-READY).** After every `apply` event of peer X (so a stale pair is caught even when ENG-39's `ReadyBaseMismatchError` later stops the publish and no `compose` event exists): every pair of `inspect().ready` has `A` equal to `inspect().aTree` on its paths, and every pair that was ready before the apply and still is has `R[p]` equal to the apply's `mDisk[p]` on each of its paths the apply wrote (HLD-41 merged/concatenated case; a displaced path cancels the group instead) — this clause kills M20. For each `compose` event followed by a landed `push` of that peer: every pair's `A` equals `aTree` on its paths and `P[p] == testMask(R)[p]` for every published ready path.
- **oracleAnd (O-AND).** For each landed ordinary live commit C of peer X (its `publish` event's `c`, with its `compose` event): for every non-terminal ON_DISK record with a non-empty guard set in `records(registry)`: a PIN, a CONFLICTED record or one with a whole-path guard (eol-bom, generated?, portability, markers) or `local_only` has `C[p] == parent[p]`; an ACTIVE PATCH's rows added by `diff -U0 (H0'[p], H1'[p])` (testRebase onto `aTree`) are not among the rows C adds to its parent, and the rows it removes are not among the rows C removes (test code may diff -U0; L-HOLD-STORES covers `src/` only).
- **oracleOffDisk (O-OFFDISK).** Every path in a `mask` event's `withheld ∪ partial` is explained test-side by an ON_DISK record or a scope class; testMask with and without the OFF_DISK records gives equal results (no `claim-breach` exists in Plan 3); no `hold` transition to GONE has an OFF_DISK previous residence unless the record is a marker companion (`local.companions`) or a collision-take capture (R3-67: test-side, a guard-less OFF_DISK PARKED PATCH with `h0` ABSENT whose path had a non-terminal `collision` record in the registry commit that created it).
- **oracleLiveRecord (O-LIVE-RECORD, INV-HLD-SINGLE).** For each landed Bnew (a `mirror` event followed by a landed push): boardHolds(Bnew): every folded hold with `local_only` false names only LIVE paths (simScope at that peer's EPOCH_BASE) and its `held/<id>/` names only those paths; every `local_only` hold has no `held/<id>/` subtree and no oid; every id in a peer's `inspect().mirror` with `rejected ≠ null` has `status.json.message_code == 'HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED'` (params.hold_id = id) or a non-ok state within 2 of that peer's `iteration` events; for every record whose newest rev appeared in a landed `mirror` event, the folded entry equals the owner's registry record on state, residence, offer, guards, paths, cs_ids and local_only; no two non-terminal records of one peer share kind, paths and held-side blobs.
- **oracleAtomicPub (O-ATOMIC-PUB, human CSs).** For each landed ordinary live commit with `Cowork-CS` trailers and its `compose` event (the last one before the push; a T0 trial composition emits none, R3-63): for every composable group (as above, the event's `ready` included) and every path p with `R[p] ≠ A_cap[p]` of its newest pair: `P[p] ≠ A[p]` and C carries it, unless (a) p's scope class is QUARANTINED/SAVE_ONLY/UNMANAGED, (b) p is a step-5 partial path (C then differs from `MASK(R)` only inside the held rows), or (c) p is in the `publish` event's `conflicted` list (K4 TAKE_TIP); no path of a non-composable group differs from A in P; base-move commits (two parents) are skipped.
- **oracleNoSilent (INV-NOSILENT, status half).** Per peer, the condition set read test-side after each step — `holds_active` = non-terminal non-INCOMING records, `holds_local_only`, `incoming` = INCOMING records' paths ∪ `inspect().pending` paths younger than 60 s (each path once), every path of every ON_DISK record and every `pending` path, and `MASS ∈ inspect().freeze` — must appear in `readStatus(peer)` (`pending.*` counts, `not_converged ⊇` those paths, `freeze_reasons ∋ 'MASS'`) by the peer's second `iteration` event after it was first observed (R3-46).

**Tests:**
- `"testMask applies 05 §3.4 item 2 steps 1–6 in order on a 9-path fixture"` — QUARANTINED `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, SAVE_ONLY `.gitignore` and a sync.exclude path take A; the path of an ON_DISK `local_only` record and of an ON_DISK `eol-bom` record take A; an ON_DISK PIN with guards and an ON_DISK CONFLICTED record take A; an ON_DISK ACTIVE revert PATCH reverse-merges only its row; an OFF_DISK PARKED PATCH and an OFF_DISK `local_only` marker companion change nothing; binary `bin.dat` under an ACTIVE PATCH takes A whole (R3-18) — 05 §3.4, R3-21.
- `"testRebase is single-step from the original pair: two A moves give the same H1' as one move to the final A; rc ≠ 0 marks the record conflicted and its path takes A; two PATCHes on one path apply in ULID order"` — HLD-13, HLD-14 (test-side).
- `"05 P1 / TC-HLD-01 exp_j_fuzz: 1,000 fast-check trials (seed 0x5031, numRuns 1,000, shrinking on) on one real repo — a 40-row LF file, a team edit set E_t and a local edit set E_l on rows ≥ 2 apart, a held subset H ⊆ E_l recorded as one ON_DISK revert-style PATCH (H0 = A_old, H1 = A_old + H), and in 1 trial of 5 a team edit identical to one held edit — give maskEntries(S_disk = A_new + E_l) == A_new + (E_l − H) and composeP of one ready pair (R = S_disk, A_cap = A_new) the same P; with M19 on the property fails and shrinks to a counterexample containing an identical team edit"` — `npm test` runs the first 100 trials and `npm run test:sim` all 1,000 (R3-55); 05 P1, TC-HLD-01 (M19), X-05 `exp_j_fuzz`, INV-MASK.
- `"every oracle of this task passes after every op on holds seeds holds-o-0, holds-o-1, holds-o-2 and on edits seed edits-o-0 and faults seed faults-o-0 (each run with Plan 2's six exit oracles followed by the eight oracles of this task)"` — `npm test` runs `holds-o-0`, `npm run test:sim` all five (R3-55); R3-49 (every profile), O2, O3, O4, T-04.
- `"TC-TST-02 form: run with the expected oracle last in the list, M19 fails INV-MASK, M25 fails INV-MASK, M38 fails INV-GROUP and M39 fails INV-GROUP, each first at the step its scenario predicts and with no earlier oracle firing; with the mutant off every scenario passes all oracles"` — scenarios: **M19** m.txt (40 rows): p1 adds Y at row 10 and Z at row 30 and publishes; p0 (applied) stale_saves the genesis buffer + an own row at 20 → revert PATCH over Y and Z; p1 deletes Y and publishes; p0 applies (REBASE clean, ACTIVE) → under M19 the step-5 merge from the unrebased pair re-adds Y in sPub; **M25** seedFiles + `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: p0 edits it and shared.txt row 5 (a capture occasion) → sPub carries the quarantined edit; **M38** p1 publishes one CS over g/a.txt, g/b.txt, g/c.txt while p0 holds a share-read lock on g/b.txt → p0 writes g/a.txt and g/c.txt; **M39** three `nonconforming_push` `plain` commits on g/a.txt, g/b.txt and g/c.txt land one {rounds: 1} apart (three CSs from one source; a peer's single human CS could not) → p0 writes two of them < 10,000 ms apart — T-09, R3-50, TC-TST-02, TC-ENG-06 (M38).
- `"each of M19, M25, M28, M33, M38 and M39 is read at exactly one site in src/ (source scan for the mutant id string)"` — R3-50 "one site each".
- `"oracleMask fails on three plants and names the clause: an inspect() wrapper that swaps one sPub entry (peer, path, both oids), a synthetic 'compose' event whose pTree changes .github/workflows/ci.yml against A, and a synthetic 'mask' event whose sDisk differs from the re-application of an ON_DISK PATCH on a partial path"` — T-02 clauses (equality, class/held paths in P, re-apply).
- `"oracleMask and oracleAtomicPub pass every step while p2's nonportable_name file waits and while a t0 PIN is released, and after the fix the group publishes"` — a holds script with p0's not-stageable host-table `nonportable_name` (`a:b.txt` on Linux and macOS, NFD on Windows; its human group not `ready` in every `compose` event, P keeping `A`), then its fix; and a scripted K7 `t0` PIN whose T0 later passes (the trial composition emits no event; the recomposition's event shows no `t0` PIN); a synthetic `compose` event that marks a group `ready` while the registry holds a guarded ON_DISK PIN over it fails oracleMask (R3-63, R3-53).
- `"oracleGroup fails on four plants and names the clause: an 'apply' event that writes 2 of a 3-path group, a deferred closure 62,501 virtual ms old with no INCOMING record, a not-applicable path without its own INCOMING record at X's next iteration event, and two written applies of one peer 9,999 virtual ms apart (by monoAt)"` — T-03 clauses.
- `"oracleGroup passes on nonconforming_push bad_name with a:b.txt on p0 and p1, and on a KilledAt between an apply event and its records"` — the team writer's `np/team-1/a:b.txt` and `np/team-1/ok.txt`: the UNMANAGED `a:b.txt` is outside every closure and no record is required for it, `ok.txt` is written; separately a `KilledAt` at `registry.cas` right after an `apply` event with a not-applicable `con.py` on p2: clause (iv) waits for p2's first `iteration` event after the restart, by which the record exists (Flags item 8, HLD-53, R3-53's receive-side counterpart).
- `"TC-ENG-06 SIM shape: p1's one CS over g/a.txt, g/b.txt and g/c.txt with g/b.txt share-read-locked on p0 leaves all three unchanged on p0, one INCOMING lock record covers exactly [g/a.txt, g/b.txt, g/c.txt] from 60 virtual s, and after unlock one apply writes all three; oracleGroup passes every step"` — TC-ENG-06, INV-GROUP, R3-31.
- `"05 I6 / INV-HLD-RECEIVE: with f.py held by a p0 revert record and book.csv locked on p0, separate incoming CSs on f.py (other rows, by p1), g.py (by p2) and book.csv (a nonconforming_push 'plain' commit) arrive: f.py and g.py are written on p0 within 2 loops, book.csv within 1 loop after unlock, and oracleGroup's closure-partition clause passes"` — O4 (INV-HLD-RECEIVE).
- `"O-READY with holdcat X1 (ready-pair rebase): p0's captured ready pair on f.txt (row 5) is rebased by p1's applied row-20 change (R[f.txt] = the apply's mDisk entry) and publishes both rows; with Plan 2's M20 on, oracleReady's after-apply clause fires at that apply (R ≠ mDisk) before any publish, whether or not the A_cap assert later stops the push"` — O-READY, TC-HLD-01 (M20), X-05 `holdcat/X1`.
- `"O-AND: synthetic 'compose'/'publish'/'push' events naming a crafted commit that adds the held row of p0's ACTIVE revert PATCH fail oracleAnd; in SIM (hold/exp_bc guard composition) a record holding guards revert and markers keeps its rows out of every landed commit until both are released, and oracleAnd passes"` — O-AND, X-05 `hold/exp_bc`, U4 shape.
- `"O-OFFDISK with holdcat X2 (conflict record residence): a K4 record born OFF_DISK on the publisher masks nothing, a later p0 edit of that path publishes within 2 loops after its human CS closes (20 s quiet), and a planted 'mask' event that withholds a path covered only by an OFF_DISK record fails oracleOffDisk"` — O-OFFDISK, 05 P3 shape, X-05 `holdcat/X2`.
- `"oracleOffDisk exempts a failed collision-take capture ending GONE and nothing else"` — a `collide_ignored` + `resolve --publish` script whose take meets a share-none lock (the capture ends GONE): oracleOffDisk passes; a planted scratch registry commit that ends an ordinary OFF_DISK PARKED `revert` PATCH GONE fails it (R3-67, R3-15).
- `"O-LIVE-RECORD: p0's portability record mirrors as a stub without held/ and no oid, its dependency PIN names only LIVE paths; an inspect() wrapper reporting mirror[id].rejected = 'validator' while status.json never shows HOLD_MIRROR_REJECTED fails oracleLiveRecord after p0's second iteration; a planted registry edit (a scratch registry commit flipping state) that disagrees with the landed fold fails the INV-HLD-SINGLE clause"` — O-LIVE-RECORD, INV-HLD-SINGLE (O4).
- `"O-ATOMIC-PUB on atom x-series shapes: a 3-file human CS publishes in one live commit; a K4 TAKE_TIP on 1 of 3 paths publishes the other 2 in that CS (exception (c), TC-DAT-06); a planted commit carrying 2 of a 3-path composable group fails oracleAtomicPub"` — O-ATOMIC-PUB, X-05 `atom/*`, TC-DAT-06.
- `"INV-NOSILENT status half: a revert record, a lock deferral, a not-applicable path and MASS each appear in status.json (counts, not_converged, freeze_reasons) by the peer's second iteration; with a FaultFs rule failing every rename of status.json with EPERM, oracleNoSilent fails at that peer's second iteration after the new revert record"` — T-06 (oracle), INV-NOSILENT, HLD-24, R3-46.

### Task 15: INV-NOLOSS and the held-bytes INV-SECRET slice

**Covers:** O1, T-01, T-05, D-26, CS-52 (INV-NOLOSS), T-09 (M05, M06, M07, M19, M28 for INV-NOLOSS); TST-07, TST-08, 05 §5 harness extension, 05 P7 (Plan 3 subset), TC-HLD-04, TC-ENG-02 (M05 shape), TC-ENG-13 (Plan 3 part), TC-HLD-06 (INV-SECRET part), TC-TST-02 (NOLOSS plant), P-DAT-05; O-NOMARKER (held side); R3-48, R3-49, R3-64. Depends on: Task 13. Parallel with Task 14 (disjoint files).

**Files:**
- Create: `test/sim/oracleNoloss.ts`
- Test: `test/integration/sim.noloss.test.ts`, `test/integration/sim.noloss.mutants.test.ts`, `test/integration/sim.heldsecret.test.ts`, `test/integration/durability.reclone.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 13 `MarkerModel` (`live`, `discarded`, `lateWrites`, `retired`, `exempt`), `findMarkers`, `holdsSetup`, `scriptedGenerator`, `holdsOp`, `readRegistry`, `boardHolds`, `treeEntries`, `readBlob`, `realEngineOf`, `faultFsOf`; `TraceEvent` variants `hold`, `mirror`, `push`, `publish`, `discard`, `apply`, `iteration`; `EngineInspection` (`publishPause`, `mirror`, `holds`); Task 11 `Engine.resolve` (copy-restore, HLD-64); Plan 2 `Oracle`, `SimContext`, `runSim`, `exitOracles`, `refNames`, `RefNamespace`, `RELEASE_NAMESPACE`, `BareRemote`, `makeEngines`, `EngineFixture`, `clonePeer`, `KilledAt`, the mutant sites M05, M06 and M07 (`MutantId`, `RealEngineOptions.mutants`), the harness rewrite fault; Plan 1 `createBareRemote`.
- Produces: `oracleNoloss`, `oracleHeldSecret` (header signatures); `NolossLocation` and `locateMarker` (exported for the tests).

**Contract additions:** folded into §Interfaces — `NolossLocation`, `locateMarker`.

**Mutant pairs (R3-50):** INV-NOLOSS is paired here with M05, M06, M07, M19 and M28 in TC-TST-02 form (INV-NOLOSS the only Plan 3 oracle that must fail); M01–M04 are Plan 7's, M37/M47 Plan 5's.

**Resurrection clause:** adopted into R3-48 by R3-64 (a retired marker that reappears in `live_code(board tip)` fails INV-NOLOSS); the M19 and "stays quiet on legitimate re-adds" tests below are unconditional.

**Spec:** 11-testing §3.4 rows INV-NOLOSS and INV-SECRET (held-bytes part: restore markers and `local_only` held bytes), O-NOMARKER (held side), TST-07, TST-08, §3.9 M19, M28; glossary §21 INV-NOLOSS (rev 2.1 scope), §9 (LIVE-only, restore markers never on a remote ref); 05 §5 INV-NOLOSS harness extension, INV-HLD-DURABLE, §7 P7, I9/I11 shapes; 03-data §5 INV-NOLOSS row, P-DAT-05, INV-DAT-NOMARKER, DAT-43, DAT-46; 11-testing §7 TC-HLD-04, TC-HLD-06 (INV-SECRET part), TC-TST-02; rulings R3-28, R3-48, R3-49.

**Notes:**
- **Obligations** are `markers.live()`. For each, `locateMarker` searches, in order: (1) `live` — the blob at the marker's path, else any blob, of `tree(live_code(board tip))` on the bare remote (`refNames(branch, ns).remoteBoard`, last parent), scanned with `findMarkers` over `cat-file --batch` (the per-tree index is cached by tree oid and updated from `diff-tree` paths, so the search is incremental); (2) `saved` — vacuous; (3) `held` — a `held/<id>/{h1,s2}/<path>` blob of the board tip whose folded hold is non-terminal (05 §5: "the H1/s2 blob … of a non-terminal record reachable from the remote board"); (4) `local_only_disk` — the owner's working file at a path of one of its non-terminal `local_only` records; (5) `author_disk` — the marker author's working file at the marker's path ("verified by reading the file"), counted only while that path belongs to an open CS of the author or to a captured but unpublished ready pair of the author (`inspect().csTable`, `inspect().ready`; R3-48, 11-testing §3.4 "a seat's open CS or human CS"): once that CS's publish lands the marker must be at `live`, `held`, `registry` or `local_only_disk`; (6) `registry` — the owner's registry (readRegistry): the `h1` of a marker companion (always), or the `h1`/`s2` of a non-terminal, non-`local_only` record within the pre-mirror window: the window for marker m in record r closes at the owner's first landed push (a `push` event with outcome `landed`) whose `mirror` event follows, in trace order, the `hold` event of the first rev of r whose held side contains m; it stays open while the owner's `inspect().publishPause` is `OFFLINE`, while `inspect().mirror[r].rejected ≠ null`, and while `creationDue(r, age)` is false — a not-applicable INCOMING record (`unmaterializable`, `collision`, `quarantine-review`) younger than 60 s (R3-29; closure INCOMING records and unreadable/unstable PINs are due at once); after it closes, a `mirror` event that does not list r at a rev ≥ that first rev fails the oracle (a missed mirror). `discard` is not searched: `noteDiscard` removed the marker from the obligations, and the oracle instead verifies each new entry of `markers.discarded()` — the owner's record is RESOLVED_DISCARD at the step and a `discard` trace event of that id names a held blob containing the marker (R3-28).
- **Late writes:** a marker in `markers.lateWrites()` must be found at `held` or `registry` (not only on a disk) from the first check after it was noted.
- **Resurrection (the M19 finding stale-hold-resurrects-team-revert):** a marker in `markers.retired()` — removed by an informed edit of a peer whose A held it and re-added by no noted edit since — that appears in `live_code(board tip)` fails the oracle: a teammate's deliberate removal was silently reverted, which is the loss of that teammate's work (TST-08's reference model makes the removal authoritative). A noted re-add (a script or generator op, or a `resolve --publish` whose held side adds it) makes the marker live again first, so legitimate re-adds never trip the clause.
- **Two stores (TST-08):** every hold id that appears in any `hold` event is in exactly one peer's registry; its folded owner (when folded) is that peer; no peer clone or the remote has a ref under `refs/cowork-held/` or `refs/worktree/cowork/`, and no clone has a directory `<gitdir>/cowork/quarantine/` or `<gitdir>/cowork/conflicts/` (L-HOLD-STORES' runtime counterpart).
- **Cadence (R3-49):** `afterEveryOp` rechecks the markers that may have moved — markers on paths written by this step's op (op-log), on paths named by this step's `apply` (`written`, `deferred`), `publish` (paths of `diff(tip, c)`), `hold`, `discard` and `repair` events, markers whose last location was `held`/`live` when the board tip changed, `registry` when that peer's registry head changed or it landed a push, and every new or late-write marker; `atQuiescence` runs a full scan of every obligation and the two-stores check over every record. A failure message names the marker, its author, path and the step it was written, and the last location it was seen at.
- **oracleHeldSecret (T-05, O-NOMARKER held side), atQuiescence:** `cat-file --batch-all-objects --batch` on the bare remote (unreachable objects included) contains no row beginning `<<<<<<< cowork:`, `||||||| cowork:` or `>>>>>>> cowork:` in any blob, and no `h1`/`s2` blob of any peer's `local_only` record (any state; portability records, marker companions) except a blob also reachable from the tree of a live code commit at a LIVE path.

**Tests:**
- `"locateMarker finds each location: a published marker → live; a K4 loser's marker after its owner's next landed push → held (h1/s2 only, never h0/s1/s3); the marker of a portability record's file → local_only_disk; an unpublished edit → author_disk; a K4 loser's marker before that push → registry; a marker companion's marker → registry (companion h1); a discarded revert marker → removed from obligations with its RESOLVED_DISCARD verified"` — TST-08 (six locations), 05 §5 harness extension, R3-48.
- `"the pre-mirror window closes at the owner's first landed push after the rev that added the marker: against a copy of the remote whose board tip is rewritten without held/<id>/ the oracle fails naming the marker, and a mirror event of that push that omits the record fails as a missed mirror"` — R3-48, INV-HLD-DURABLE ("after one governed push").
- `"the registry keeps counting indefinitely while the owner is partitioned (publishPause OFFLINE for 10 virtual minutes) and while inspect().mirror[id].rejected is 'validator' (wrapper plant), and a not-applicable INCOMING record's marker counts there until the record is 60 s old, while a closure INCOMING record's window closes at the owner's first landed push after its creation"` — R3-48, R3-29.
- `"retirement: a marker removed by an informed edit of a peer whose A held it is never searched again; the same removal tagged stale keeps it obligated and the oracle fails once no location holds it"` — TST-08 reference model.
- `"late write: a write_during_apply 'old_handle' marker is at held or registry at the next check; a plant whose handler, right after appending the late row, restores the .old to its pre-step bytes (a ring that lost the late write) fails INV-NOLOSS at that check naming the late marker"` — INV-NOLOSS `.old` clause, ENG-35.
- `"two stores: a hold id copied into a second peer's registry (scratch registry commit), a folded hold whose owner differs from the registry holding it, a ref refs/cowork-held/x in a clone and a directory <gitdir>/cowork/conflicts/ each fail the oracle with the store named"` — TST-08 one-change-two-stores.
- `"TC-TST-02 NOLOSS plant: deleting one unpublished marker row from its author's working file between two steps without telling the model fails INV-NOLOSS at that step naming the marker, while Plan 2's six stay green on that plant; Plan 2's non-FF board-move plant and a pushed-tree plant that appends one row do not fire INV-NOLOSS"` — TC-TST-02 (NOLOSS plant), TST-07 (that the Task 14 oracles also stay green on this plant is Task 16's `exitOracles` cross-check: Task 14 runs in parallel with this task).
- `"a published CS's marker no longer counts on its author's disk: a landed commit that omits one unheld marker row while the author's disk keeps it fails INV-NOLOSS"` — p0 edits `shared.txt` (marker m on an unheld row) and its human CS publishes; a plant rewrites the landed live commit's tree (on a scratch copy of the remote, then pointed at by the board tip as Plan 2's pushed-tree plants do) without m's row while p0's file still holds it: INV-NOLOSS fails naming m (not at `live`, and `author_disk` no longer counts once the CS published); the same row on p0's disk while the CS is still open passes (R3-48, TST-08, 11-testing §3.4).
- `"a marker written to a path that is not synced is exempt, not an obligation"` — a `temp_file` op's marker and `collide_ignored`'s own excluded file's marker are in `markers.exempt()`, never in `live()`; deleting those files without telling the model does not fail INV-NOLOSS (R3-48, glossary INV-NOLOSS "synced path").
- `"M19 fails INV-NOLOSS (resurrection clause): Task 14's M19 scenario on m.txt (p1 adds Y at row 10 and Z at row 30; p0's stale save removes both into one revert PATCH; p1 deletes Y, an informed removal that retires Y's marker; p0 applies, REBASE clean, the record ACTIVE over Z only) continued by an untainted p0 edit of m.txt row 20 and {rounds: 12}: under M19 p0's P re-adds Y, the landed commit puts Y's retired marker back into live_code, and INV-NOLOSS (run as the only Plan 3 oracle) fails naming it; with M19 off the run passes"` — T-09 (M19), TC-TST-02 form, finding stale-hold-resurrects-team-revert.
- `"resurrection clause stays quiet on legitimate re-adds: p1 deletes its row (marker retired), then p1's own rewind restores it (a noted stale re-add by the deleter, untainted, published) and, in a second run, p0's tainted stale re-add is released by resolve --publish; neither run fails INV-NOLOSS"` — TST-08 reference model (re-add rule).
- `"M28 fails INV-NOLOSS: p0 holds a revert PATCH on rows 21–22 of m.txt (p1's lines replaced by the genesis rows); p2 inserts 3 marker rows after row 19 and publishes; p0 applies and captures an untainted row-40 edit; under M28 step 5 writes H0's rows at positions 21–22 over p2's 2nd and 3rd inserted rows, P publishes their loss, and INV-NOLOSS fails naming those markers once p2 applies; with M28 off the run passes"` — T-09 (M28), finding intra-file-withdrawal-mangles-file.
- `"M05 fails INV-NOLOSS: a restart that seeds A from TIP loses a teammate's marker"` — TC-ENG-13 / TC-ENG-02 shape: p0 holds an unpublished own row (marker m0) in `shared.txt`; p0 is killed (`KilledAt`) while p1 publishes a row 10 lines away (marker m1); p0 restarts and publishes m0's row; under Plan 2's M05 (A seeded from TIP at restart) p0's publish carries its stale bytes for `shared.txt` over p1's row and INV-NOLOSS (run as the only Plan 3 oracle, after Plan 2's six) fails naming m1; with M05 off the run passes and both markers are in `live` (T-09, R3-50, TC-TST-02 form).
- `"M06 fails INV-NOLOSS: a raw snapshot pushed on top of TIP drops an unapplied teammate marker"` — p1 publishes a row with marker m1 in `shared.txt`; before p0 applies it, p0's human CS on another row of `shared.txt` closes and publishes; under M06 the pushed tree is p0's raw snapshot, so m1 leaves `live_code`, and INV-NOLOSS (the only Plan 3 oracle) fails naming m1; with M06 off p0's publish is the merge and both rows land (T-09, R3-50, TC-TST-02 form; finding naive-push-reverts-unapplied-paths).
- `"M07 fails INV-NOLOSS: an undetected board rewind drops a marker"` — p1 publishes a row with marker m1; a harness rewrite fault rewinds the remote's code and board refs past that commit; under M07 (no first-parent fast-forward check) p0 accepts the rewound chain and publishes on it, so m1 is in no location, and INV-NOLOSS (the only Plan 3 oracle) fails naming m1; with M07 off the rewind raises `INCIDENT` and nothing publishes on it, and the marker stays accounted for in p1's and p0's registries or disks (T-09, R3-50, TC-TST-02 form; finding ref-rewind-silent-revert).
- `"05 P7 Plan 3 subset: five pinned holds seeds with weights stale_save 8, rewind 4, edit_band 8, edit_same_line 6, edit_peer_line 6, lock_share_none 3, gc_prune 3, resolve 10 contain, across their op-logs, stale saves, rewinds, 1–40-row insertions, same-row edits, a formatter with resume, a KilledAt, gc --prune=now, a share-none lock and resolve publish, discard, park and restore, and pass INV-NOLOSS after every op and at quiescence"` — the seeds are pinned at implementation time: the implementer scans `holds-p7-<k>` from k = 0 and names in the test five seeds whose op-logs together contain every required kind (which kinds a drawn seed contains depends on the seed, not on the engine); the kind-presence assertion fails with a "re-pick the seeds" message, never silently; `npm test` runs the first of the five and `npm run test:sim` all five (R3-55); 05 P7 (Plan 3 subset), T-01, O1, CS-52; P7's claims, handoffs, stash/checkout/bisect/commit, save, stop/start, --purge and re-clone steps wait for Plans 4–7 (re-clone: TC-HLD-04 below).
- `"P-DAT-05: holds seeds holds-pdat-0…holds-pdat-4 with weights edit_same_line 15, resolve 15 and stale_save 5 pass INV-NOLOSS after every op — every LOCAL byte of each K4 conflict, clean restore, conflicted restore and discard is in live_code, a record, the disk or a RESOLVED_DISCARD"` — `npm test` runs `holds-pdat-0`, `npm run test:sim` all five (R3-55); P-DAT-05, D-26, DAT-43.
- `"conflicted restore then markers park: a K4 record restored over an overlapping p0 edit writes cowork: markers on p0's disk (guard markers); after p0 types a half-done resolution containing a fresh marker, age_24h markers-parks it: that marker is in the companion's registry h1, the record's own markers stay on the board, INV-NOLOSS passes every step, and resolve --restore of the companion brings the rows and the marker back"` — D-26, 05 §5 companion location, 05 I11 shape.
- `"oracleHeldSecret: after the conflicted-restore scenario and S6's markers park, the bare remote holds no row beginning '<<<<<<< cowork:', '||||||| cowork:' or '>>>>>>> cowork:' and no h1/s2 blob of p0's portability record or marker companion; a scratch copy of the remote with a restore-marker blob added under held/<id>/h1/ fails it, and so does one holding the companion's h1 blob"` — T-05, O-NOMARKER (held side), TC-HLD-06 (INV-SECRET part), DAT-46.
- `"TC-HLD-04 durability (makeEngines, 2 peers): p0 holds a revert PATCH, an OFF_DISK K4 PATCH, an eol-bom PATCH with the dependency PIN of its CS, and an INCOMING lock record; after one landed governed push of p0, p0 is killed (KilledAt at its next crashpoint), git gc --prune=now runs in the remote and p0's clone, p0's clone directory is deleted, and a fresh clone joins as p2: its fold lists every non-terminal non-local_only id of p0 with held/ blobs byte-equal to p0's last registry blobs; resolve(id, 'restore') on p2 before p0 has left is refused HOLD_NOT_OWNER; the test then lands p0's `leave` op with a test-written board commit under p0's writer id and its next seq (Task 4's stale-prev_rev mechanism; Plan 3 has no stop verb), and after p2 folds it resolve(id, 'restore') on p2 (HLD-64 copy-restore) writes byte-exact content under a new id; the run has no fold-rejected hold op"` — TC-HLD-04, INV-HLD-DURABLE, HLD-57 (a).
- `"TC-ENG-13 Plan 3 part: a lost state ref turns p0's unpublished marker row into a restart record that holds the marker in p0's registry until p0's next landed push and in its held/<id>/h1 blob after it"` — in `test/integration/durability.reclone.test.ts` (`makeEngines`, 2 peers): p0 writes an unpublished row carrying a fresh marker, then its state ref is deleted while the daemon runs; the lost-state rebuild (ENG-54) publishes nothing before it completes; the marker is in the `h1` of a `restart` PATCH in p0's registry (read with `readRegistry`) and, after p0's next landed push, in that record's `held/<id>/h1/<path>` blob on the board tip (`boardHolds`); the record id is in exactly one peer's registry (TC-ENG-13 Plan 3 part, INV-NOLOSS registry and held locations, R3-48).
- `"INV-NOLOSS runs on every profile: edits seed edits-n-0 and faults seed faults-n-0, whose editGenerator now carries markers, pass INV-NOLOSS after every op and at quiescence, revert records of R3-37 included"` — `npm test` runs `edits-n-0`, `npm run test:sim` both (R3-55); R3-49, R3-37.
- `"incremental and full checks agree: on holds seed holds-n-0 a full scan forced at every 10th step returns the same verdict and the same location for every obligation as the incremental check"` — runs under `npm run test:sim` only; `npm test` runs the same comparison over the first 20 steps (R3-55); R3-49 cadence correctness.
- `"RESOLVED_DISCARD accounting is checked: a noteDiscard for a record that is still ACTIVE in its owner's registry, and one whose 'discard' event names no held blob containing the marker, each fail the oracle"` — 05 §5 last sentence, R3-28.
